# Kautilya: Arthasastra \[San\]

### 1

*oṁ.namaḥ.śukra.bṛhaspatibhyāṁ --*  
*01.1.01/.pṛthivyā lābhe pālane ca yāvanty artha.śāstrāṇi pūrva.ācāryaiḥ prasthāpitāni prāyaśas tāni saṁhṛtya^ekam idam artha.śāstraṁ kṛtam //*  
*01.1.02/ tasya^ayaṁ prakaraṇa.adhikaraṇa.samuddeśaḥ //*  
*01.1.03a/ vidyā.samuddeśaḥ, vṛddha.samuddeśaḥ, indriya.jayaḥ, amātya.utpattiḥ, mantri.purohita.utpattiḥ, upadhābhiḥ śauca.aśauca.jñānam amātyānām, - -*  
*01.1.03b/ gūḍha.puruṣa.praṇidhiḥ, sva.viṣaye kṛtya.akṛtya.pakṣa.rakṣaṇam, para.viṣaye kṛtya.akṛtya.pakṣa.upagrahaḥ, -*  
*01.1.03c/ mantra.adhikāraḥ, dūta.praṇidhiḥ, rāja.putra.rakṣaṇam, aparuddha.vṛttam, aparuddhe vṛttiḥ, rāja.praṇidhiḥ, niśānta.praṇidhiḥ, ātma.rakṣitakam, --, iti vinaya.adhikārikaṁ prathamam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.04a/ janapada.niveśaḥ, bhūmic.chidra.apidhānam, durga.vidhānam, durga.niveśaḥ, samnidhātṛ.nicaya.karma, samāhartṛ.samudaya.prasthāpanam, akṣa.paṭale gāṇanikya.adhikāraḥ, -*  
*01.1.04b/ samudayasya yukta.apahṛtasya pratyānayanam, upayukta.parīkṣā, śāsana.adhikāraḥ, kośa.praveśya.ratna.parīkṣā, ākara.karma.anta.pravartanam, akṣa.śālāyāṁ suvarṇa.adhyakṣaḥ -*  
*01.1.04c/ viśikhāyāṁ sauvarṇika.pracāraḥ, koṣṭha.āgāra.adhyakṣaḥ, paṇya.adhyakṣaḥ, kupya.adhyakṣaḥ, āyudha.adhyakṣaḥ, tulā.māna.pautavam, -*  
*01.1.04d/ deśa.kāla.mānam, śulka.adhyakṣaḥ, sūtra.adhyakṣaḥ, sīta.adhyakṣaḥ, surā.adhyakṣaḥ, sūna.adhyakṣaḥ, gaṇikā.adhyakṣaḥ, -*  
*01.1.04e/ nāv.adhyakṣaḥ, go.adhyakṣaḥ, aśva.adhyakṣaḥ, hasty.adhyakṣaḥ, ratha.adhyakṣaḥ, patty.adhyakṣaḥ, senā.pati.pracāraḥ, mudrā.adhyakṣaḥ, vivīta.adhyakṣaḥ, samāhartṛ.pracāraḥ, -*  
*01.1.04f/ gṛha.patika.vaidehaka.tāpasa.vyañjanāḥ praṇidhayaḥ, nāgarika.praṇidhiḥ -- ity adhyakṣa.pracāro dvitīyam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.05a/ vyavahāra.sthāpanā, vivāda.pada.nibandhaḥ, vivāha.samyuktam, dāya.vibhāgaḥ, vāstukam, samayasya anapākarma, ṛṇa.adānam, aupanidhikam, dāsa.karma.kara.kalpaḥ, -*  
*01.1.05b/ sambhūya samutthānam, vikrīta.krīta.anuśayaḥ, dattasya anapākarma, asvāmi.vikrayaḥ, sva.svāmi.sambandhaḥ, sāhasam, vāk.pāruṣyam, daṇḍa.pāruṣyam, dyūta.samāhvayam, prakīrṇakaṁ - iti, dharma.sthīyaṁ tṛtīyam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.06a/ kāru.kara.kṣaṇam, vaidehaka.rakṣaṇam, upanipāta.pratīkāraḥ, gūḍha.ājīvināṁ rakṣā, siddha.vyañjanair māṇava.prakāśanam, śaṅkā.rūpa.karma.abhigrahaḥ, -*  
*01.1.06b/ āśu.mṛtaka.parīkṣā, vākya.karma.anuyogaḥ, sarva.adhikaraṇa.rakṣaṇaṁ -*  
*01.1.06c/ eka.aṅga.vadha.niṣkrayaḥ, śuddhaś citraś ca daṇḍa kalpaḥ, kanyā.prakarma, aticāra.daṇḍāḥ - iti kaṇṭaka.śodhanaṁ caturtham adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.07/ dāṇḍakarmikam, kośa.abhisaṁharaṇam, bhṛtya.bharaṇīyam, anujīvi.vṛttam, samaya.ācārikam, rājya.pratisaṁdhānam, eka.aiśvaryaṁ - iti yoga.vṛttaṁ pañcamam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.08/ prakṛti.sampadaḥ, śama.vyāyāmikaṁ - iti maṇḍala.yoniḥ ṣaṣṭham adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.09a/ ṣāḍguṇya.samuddeśaḥ, kṣaya.sthāna.vṛddhi.niścayaḥ, saṁśraya.vṛttiḥ, samahīna.jyāyasāṁ guṇa.abhiniveśaḥ, hīna.saṁdhayaḥ, vigṛhya āsanam, saṁdhāya āsanam, vigṛhya yānam, saṁdhāya yānam, -*  
*01.1.09b/ sambhūya prayāṇam, yātavya.amitrayor abhigraha.cintā, kṣaya.lobha.virāga.hetavaḥ prakṛtīnām, sāmavāyika.viparimarśaḥ, -*  
*01.1.09c/ saṁhita prayāṇikam, paripaṇita.aparipaṇita.apasṛtāḥ saṁdhayaḥ, dvaidhī.bhāvikāḥ saṁdhi.vikramāḥ, yātavya.vṛttiḥ, anugrāhya.mitra.viśeṣāḥ, -*  
*01.1.09d/ mitra.hiraṇya.bhūmi.karma.saṁdhayaḥ, pārṣṇi.grāha.cintā, hīna.śakti.pūraṇam, balavatā vigṛhya uparodha.hetavaḥ, daṇḍa.upanata.vṛttam, -*  
*01.1.09e/ daṇḍa.upanāyi.vṛttam, saṁdhi.karma, samādhi.mokṣaḥ, madhyama.caritam, udāsīna.caritam, maṇḍala.caritaṁ - iti ṣāḍguṇyaṁ saptamam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.10/ prakṛti.vyasana.vargaḥ, rāja.rājyayor vyasana.cintā, puruṣa.vyasana.vargaḥ, pīḍana.vargaḥ, stambha.vargaḥ, kośa.saṅga.vargaḥ, mitra.vyasana.vargaḥ - iti vyasana.ādhikārikam aṣṭamam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.11a/ śakti.deśa.kāla.bala.abala.jñānam, yātrā.kālāḥ, bala.upādāna.kālāḥ, samnāha.guṇāḥ, pratibala.karma, paścāt kopa.cintā, bāhya.ābhyantara.prakṛti.kopa.pratīkārāḥ -*  
*01.1.11b/ kṣaya.vyaya.lābha.viparimarśaḥ, bāhya.ābhyantarāś ca^āpadaḥ, duṣya.śatru.samyuktāḥ, artha.anartha.saṁśaya.yuktāḥ, tāsām upāya.vikalpajāḥ siddhayaḥ - ity abhiyāsyat karma navamam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.12/ skandha.āvāra.niveśaḥ, skandha.āvāra.prayāṇam, bala.vyasana.avaskanda.kāla.rakṣaṇam, kūṭa.yuddha.vikalpāḥ, sva.sainya.utsāhanam, sva.bala.anya.bala.vyāyogaḥ, yuddha.bhūmayaḥ, patty.aśva.ratha.hasti.karmāṇi, pakṣa.kakṣa.urasyānāṁ bala.agrato vyūha.vibhāgaḥ, sāra.phalgu.bala.vibhāgaḥ, patty.aśva.ratha.hasti.yuddhāni,*  
*daṇḍa.bhoga.maṇḍala.asaṁhata.vyūha.vyūhanam, tasya prativyūha.sthāpanaṁ - iti sāṁgrāmikaṁ daśamam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.13/ bheda.upādānāni, upāṁśu.daṇḍāḥ - iti saṁgha.vṛttam ekādaśam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.14/ dūta.karma, mantra.yuddham, senā.mukhya.vadhaḥ, maṇḍala.protsāhanam, śastra.agni.rasa.praṇidhayaḥ, vīvadha.āsāra.prasāra.vadhaḥ, yoga.atisaṁdhānam, daṇḍa.atisaṁdhānam, eka.vijayaḥ - ity ābalīyasaṁ dvādaśam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.15/ upajāpaḥ, yoga.vāmanam, apasarpa.praṇidhiḥ, paryupāsana.karma, avamardaḥ, labdha.praśamanaṁ - iti durga.lambha.upāyas trayodaśam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.16/ para.bala.ghāta.prayogaḥ, pralambhanam, sva.bala.upaghāta.pratīkāraḥ - ity aupaniṣadikaṁ caturdaśam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.17/ tantra.yuktayaḥ - iti tantra.yuktiḥ pañcadaśam adhikaraṇam //*  
*01.1.18/ śāstra.samuddeśaḥ pañcadaśa.adhikaraṇāni sa-aśīti.prakaraṇa.śataṁ sa-pañcāśad.adhyāya.śataṁ ṣaṭ.śloka.sahasrāṇi^iti //*  
*01.1.19ab/ sukha.grahaṇa.vijñeyaṁ tattva.artha.pada.niścitam /*  
*01.1.19cd/ kauṭilyena kṛtaṁ śāstraṁ vimukta.grantha.vistaram //E*  
*(Chap.2, ṣection.1: Enumeration of the sciences)*  
*((i) Establishing (the necessity of) philosophy)*  
*01.2.01/ ānvīkṣikī trayī vārttā daṇḍa.nītiś ca^iti vidyāḥ //*  
*01.2.02/ trayī vārttā daṇḍa nītiś ca^iti mānavāḥ //*  
*01.2.03/ trayī viśeṣo hy ānvīkṣikī^iti //*  
*01.2.04/ vārttā daṇḍa.nītiś ca^iti bārhaspatyāḥ //*  
*01.2.05/ saṁvaraṇa.mātraṁ hi trayī loka.yātrā.vida iti //*  
*01.2.06/ daṇḍa.nītir ekā vidyā^ity auśanasāḥ //*  
*01.2.07/ tasyāṁ hi sarva.vidyā.ārambhāḥ pratibaddhā iti //*  
*01.2.08/ catasra eva vidyā iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*01.2.09/ tābhir dharma.arthau yad vidyāt tad vidyānāṁ vidyātvam //*  
*01.2.10/ sāṁkhyaṁ yogo lokāyataṁ ca^ity ānvīkṣikī //*  
*01.2.11/ dharma.adharmau trayyām artha.anarthau vārttāyāṁ naya.anayau daṇḍa.nītyāṁ bala.abale ca etāsāṁ hetubhir anvīkṣamāṇā lokasya upakaroti vyasane^abhyudaye ca buddhim avasthāpayati prajñā.vākya.kriyā.vaiśāradyaṁ ca karoti //*  
*01.2.12ab/ pradīpaḥ sarva.vidyānām upāyaḥ sarva.karmaṇām /*  
*01.2.12cd/ āśrayaḥ sarva.dharmāṇāṁ śaśvad ānvīkṣikī matā //E*  
*(Chap.3, ṣection.1, (ī) Establishing (the necessity of) the Vedic ḷore)*  
*01.3.01/ sāma.ṛg.yajur.vedās trayas trayī //*  
*01.3.02/ atharva.veda.itihāsa.vedau ca vedāḥ //*  
*01.3.03/ śikṣā kalpo vyākaraṇaṁ niruktaṁ chando.vicitir jyotiṣam iti ca^aṅgāni //*  
*01.3.04/ eṣa trayī.dharmaś caturṇāṁ varṇānām āśramāṇāṁ ca sva.dharma.sthāpanād aupakārikaḥ //*  
*01.3.05/ svadharmo brāhmaṇasya adhyayanam adhyāpanaṁ yajanaṁ yājanaṁ dānaṁ pratigrahaś ca //*  
*01.3.06/ kṣatriyasya^adhyayanaṁ yajanaṁ dānaṁ śastra.ājīvo bhūta.rakṣaṇaṁ ca //*  
*01.3.07/ vaiśyasya^adhyayanaṁ yajanaṁ dānaṁ kṛṣi.pāśupālye vaṇijyā ca //*  
*01.3.08/ śūdrasya dvijāti.śuśrūṣā vārttā kāru.kuśīlava.karma ca //*  
*01.3.09/ gṛhasthasya svadharma.ājīvas tulyair asamāna.ṛṣibhir vaivāhyam ṛtu.gāmitvaṁ deva.pitr.atithi.pūjā bhṛtyeṣu tyāgaḥ śeṣa.bhojanaṁ ca //*  
*01.3.10/ brahma.cāriṇaḥ svādhyāyo agni.kārya.abhiṣekau bhaikṣa.vratitvam ācārye prāṇa.antikī vṛttis tad.abhāve guru.putre sa-brahma.cāriṇi vā //*  
*01.3.11/ vānaprasthasya brahmacaryaṁ bhūmau śayyā jaṭā.ajina.dhāraṇam agni.hotra.abhiṣekau devatā.pitr.atithi.pūjā vanyaś ca^āhāraḥ //*  
*01.3.12/ parivrājakasya jita.indriyatvam anārambho niṣkiṁcanatvaṁ saṅga.tyāgo bhaikṣavratam anekatra^araṇye ca vāso bāhya.ābhyantaraṁ ca śaucam //*  
*01.3.13/ sarveṣām ahiṁsā satyaṁ śaucam anasūya ānṛśaṁsyaṁ kṣamā ca //*  
*01.3.14/ svadharmaḥ svargāya^ānantyāya ca //*  
*01.3.15/ tasya^atikrame lokaḥ saṁkarād ucchidyeta //*  
*01.3.16ab/ tasmāt svadharmaṁ bhūtānāṁ rājā na vyabhicārayet /*  
*01.3.16cd/ svadharmaṁ saṁdadhāno hi pretya ca^iha ca nandati //*  
*01.3.17ab/ vyavasthita.ārya.maryādaḥ kṛta.varṇa.āśrama.sthitiḥ /*  
*01.3.17cd/ trayyā^abhirakṣito lokaḥ prasīdati na sīdati //E*  
*((īi) Establishing (the necessity of) Economics, and (iv) the ṣcience of Politics)*  
*01.4.01/ kṛṣi.pāśupālye vaṇijyā ca vārtā, dhānya.paśu.hiraṇya.kupya.viṣṭi.pradānād aupakārikī //*  
*01.4.02/ tayā sva.pakṣaṁ para.pakṣaṁ ca vaśī.karoti kośa.daṇḍābhyām //*  
*01.4.03/ ānvīkṣikī trayī vārttānāṁ yoga.kṣema.sādhano daṇḍaḥ, tasya nītir daṇḍa nītiḥ, alabdha.lābha.arthā labdha.parirakṣaṇī rakṣita.vivardhanī vṛddhasya tīrthe pratipādanī ca //*  
*01.4.04/ tasyām āyattā loka.yātrā //*  
*01.4.05/ "tasmāl loka.yātrā.arthī nityam udyata.daṇḍaḥ syāt //*  
*01.4.06/ na hy evaṁvidhaṁ vaśa.upanayanam asti bhūtānāṁ yathā daṇḍaḥ /" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*01.4.07/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*01.4.08/ tīkṣṇa.daṇḍo hi bhūtānām udvejanīyo bhavati //*  
*01.4.09/ mṛdu.daṇḍaḥ paribhūyate //*  
*01.4.10/ yathā.arha.daṇḍaḥ pūjyate //*  
*01.4.11/ suvijñāta.praṇīto hi daṇḍaḥ prajā dharma.artha.kāmair yojayati //*  
*01.4.12/ duṣpraṇītaḥ kāma.krodhābhyām avajñānād vā vānaprastha.parivrājakān api kopayati, kiṁ.aṅga punar gṛhasthān //*  
*01.4.13/ apraṇītas tu mātsya.nyāyam udbhāvayati //*  
*01.4.14/ balīyān abalaṁ hi grasate daṇḍa.dhara.abhāve //*  
*01.4.15/ sa tena guptaḥ prabhavati iti //*  
*01.4.16ab/ catur.varṇa.āśramo loko rājñā daṇḍena pālitaḥ /*  
*01.4.16cd/ svadharma.karma.abhirato vartate sveṣu vartmasu //E*  
*(ṣection.2: Association with elders)*  
*01.5.01/ tasmād daṇḍa.mūlās tisro vidyāḥ //*  
*01.5.02/ vinaya.mūlo daṇḍaḥ prāṇabhṛtāṁ yoga.kṣema.āvahaḥ //*  
*01.5.03/ kṛtakaḥ svābhāvikaś ca vinayaḥ //*  
*01.5.04/ kriyā hi dravyaṁ vinayati na^adravyam //*  
*01.5.05/ śuśrūṣā śravaṇa.grahaṇa.dhāraṇa.vijñāna.ūha.apoha.tattva.abhiniviṣṭa.buddhiṁ vidyā vinayati na^itaram //*  
*01.5.06/ vidyānāṁ tu yathāsvam ācārya.prāmāṇyād vinayo niyamaś ca //*  
*01.5.07/ vṛtta.caula.karmā lipiṁ saṁkhyānaṁ ca^upayuñjīta //*  
*01.5.08/ vṛtta.upanayanas trayīm ānvīkṣikīṁ ca śiṣṭebhyo vārttām adhyakṣebhyo daṇḍa.nītiṁ vaktṛ.prayoktṛbhyaḥ //*  
*01.5.09/ brahmacaryaṁ ca ṣoḍaśād varṣāt //*  
*01.5.10/ ato go.dānaṁ dāra.karma ca^asya //*  
*01.5.11/ nityaś ca vidyā.vṛddha.samyogo vinaya.vṛddhy.artham, tan.mūlatvād vinayasya //*  
*01.5.12/ pūrvam ahar.bhāgaṁ hasty.aśva.ratha.praharaṇa.vidyāsu vinayaṁ gacchet /*  
*01.5.13/ paścimam itihāsa.śravaṇe //*  
*01.5.14/ purāṇam itivṛttam ākhyāyika.udāharaṇaṁ dharma.śāstram artha.śāstraṁ ca^iti^itihāsaḥ //*  
*01.5.15/ śeṣam ahorātra.bhāgam apūrva.grahaṇaṁ gṛhīta.paricayaṁ ca kuryāt, agṛhītānām ābhīkṣṇya.śravaṇaṁ ca //*  
*01.5.16/ śrutādd hi prajñā^upajāyate prajñāyā yogo yogād ātmavattā^iti vidyānāṁ sāmarthyam //*  
*01.5.17ab/ vidyā.vinīto rājā hi prajānāṁ vinaye rataḥ /*  
*01.5.17cd/ ananyāṁ pṛthivīṁ bhuṅkte sarva.bhūta.hite rataḥ //E*  
*(ṣection.3: Control over the senses, (i) Casting out the group of six enemies)*  
*01.6.01/ vidyā vinaya.hetur indriya.jayaḥ kāma.krodha.lobha.māna.mada.harṣa.tyāgāt kāryaḥ //*  
*01.6.02/ karṇa.tvag.akṣi.jihvā.ghrāṇa.indriyāṇāṁ śabda.sparśa.rūpa.rasa.gandheṣv avipratipattir indriya.jayaḥ, śāstra.anuṣṭhānaṁ vā //*  
*01.6.03/ kṛtsnaṁ hi śāstram idam indriya.jayaḥ //*  
*01.6.04/ tad viruddha.vṛttir avaśya.indriyaś cāturanto api rājā sadyo vinaśyati //*  
*01.6.05/ yathā dāṇḍakyo nāma bhojaḥ kāmād brāhmaṇa.kanyām abhimanyamānaḥ sa-bandhu.rāṣṭro vinanāśa, karālaś ca vaidehaḥ //*  
*01.6.06/ kopāj janamejayo brāhmaṇeṣu vikrāntaḥ, tāla.jaṅghaś ca bhṛguṣu //*  
*01.6.07/ lobhād ailaś cāturvarṇyam atyāhārayamāṇaḥ, sauvīraś ca^ajabinduḥ /*  
*01.6.08/ mānād rāvaṇaḥ para.dārān aprayacchan, duryodhano rājyād aṁśaṁ ca //*  
*01.6.09/ madād dambhodbhavo bhūta.avamānī, haihayaś ca^arjunaḥ //*  
*01.6.10/ harṣād vātāpir agastyam atyāsādayan, vṛṣṇi.saṁghaś ca dvaipāyanam iti //*  
*01.6.11ab/ ete ca^anye ca bahavaḥ śatru.ṣaḍ.vargam āśritāḥ /*  
*01.6.11cd/ sa-bandhu.rāṣṭrā rājāno vineśur ajita.indriyāḥ //*  
*01.6.12ab/ śatru.ṣaḍ.vargam utsṛjya jāmadagnyo jita.indriyaḥ /*  
*01.6.12cd/ ambarīṣaś ca nābhāgo bubhujāte ciraṁ mahīm //E*  
*((ī) ṭhe life of a sage-like king)*  
*01.7.01/ tasmād ari.ṣaḍ.varga.tyāgena^indriya.jayaṁ kurvīta, vṛddha.samyogena prajñām, cāreṇa cakṣuḥ, utthānena yoga.kṣema.sādhanam, kārya.anuśāsanena svadharma.sthāpanam, vinayaṁ vidyā.upadeśena, loka.priyatvam artha.samyogena vṛttim //*  
*01.7.02/ evaṁ vaśya.indriyaḥ para.strī.dravya.hiṁsāś ca varjayet, svapnaṁ laulyam anṛtam uddhata.veṣatvam anarthya.samyogam adharma.samyuktam anartha.samyuktaṁ ca vyavahāram //*

*01.8.01/ "saha.adhyāyino amātyān kurvīta, dṛṣṭa.śauca.sāmarthyatvāt" iti bhāradvājaḥ //*  
*01.8.02/ "te hy asya viśvāsyā bhavanti" iti //*  
*01.8.03/ na^iti viśāla.akṣaḥ //*  
*01.8.04/ "saha.krīḍitatvāt paribhavanty enam //*  
*01.8.05/ ye hy asya guhya.sadharmāṇas tān amātyān kurvīta, samāna.śīla.vyasanatvāt //*  
*01.8.06/ te hy asya marmajña.bhayān na^aparādhyanti" iti //*  
*01.8.07/ "sādhāraṇa^eṣa doṣaḥ" iti pārāśarāḥ //*  
*01.8.08/ "teṣām api marmajña.bhayāt kṛta.akṛtāny anuvarteta //*

*01.8.10/ ya^enam āpatsu prāṇa.ābādha.yuktāsv anugṛhṇīyus tān amātyān kurvīta, dṛṣṭa.anurāgatvāt" iti //*  
*01.8.11/ na^iti piśunaḥ //*  
*01.8.12/ "bhaktir eṣā na buddhi.guṇaḥ //*  
*01.8.13/ saṁkhyāta.artheṣu karmasu niyuktā ye yathā.ādiṣṭam arthaṁ saviśeṣaṁ vā kuryus tān amātyān kurvīta, dṛṣṭa.guṇatvāt" iti //*  
*01.8.14/ na^iti kauṇapadantaḥ //*  
*01.8.15/ "anyair amātya.guṇair ayuktā hy ete //*  
*01.8.16/ pitṛ.paitāmahān amātyān kurvīta, dṛṣṭa.avadānatvāt //*  
*01.8.17/ te hy enam apacarantam api na tyajanti, sagandhatvāt //*

*01.8.20/ na^iti vātavyādhiḥ //*  
*01.8.21/ "te hy asya sarvam avagṛhya svāmivat pracaranti //*  
*01.8.22/ tasmān nītivido navān amātyān kurvīta //*

*01.8.24/ na^iti bāhu.dantī putraḥ //*  
*01.8.25/ "śāstravid adṛṣṭa.karmā karmasu viṣādaṁ gacchet //*  
*01.8.26/ tasmād abhijana.prajñā.śauca.śaurya.anurāga.yuktān amātyān kurvīta, guṇa.prādhānyāt" iti //*

*01.9.01/ jānapado abhijātaḥ svavagrahaḥ kṛta.śilpaś cakṣuṣmān prājño dhārayiṣṇur dakṣo vāgmī pragalbhaḥ pratipattimān utsāha.prabhāva.yuktaḥ kleśa.sahaḥ śucir maitro dṛḍha.bhaktiḥ śīla.bala.ārogya.sattva.yuktaḥ stambha.cāpala.hīnaḥ sampriyo vairāṇām akartā^ity amātya.sampat //*

*01.10.08/ pratyākhyāne śuciḥ / iti kāma.upadhā //*

*01.13.06/ dhānya.ṣaḍ.bhāgaṁ paṇya.daśa.bhāgaṁ hiraṇyaṁ ca^asya bhāga.dheyaṁ prakalpayām-āsuḥ //*  
*01.13.07/ tena bhṛtā rājānaḥ prajānāṁ yoga.kṣema.āvahāḥ //*  
*01.13.08/ teṣāṁ kilbiṣam adaṇḍa.karā haranty ayoga.kṣema.āvahāś ca prajānām //*  
*01.13.09/ tasmād uñcha.ṣaḍ.bhāgam āraṇyakā^api nirvapanti - "tasya^etad bhāga.dheyaṁ yo^asmān gopāyati" iti //*  
*01.13.10/ indra.yama.sthānam etad rājānaḥ pratyakṣa.heḍa.prasādāḥ //*  
*01.13.11/ tān avamanyamānān daivo^api daṇḍaḥ spṛśati //*  
*01.13.12/ tasmād rājāno na^avamantavyāḥ //*  
*01.13.13/ ity evaṁ kṣudrakān pratiṣedhayet //*  
*01.13.14/ kiṁ.vadantīṁ ca vidyuḥ //*  
*01.13.15/ ye ca^asya dhānya.paśu.hiraṇyāny ājīvanti, tair upakurvanti vyasane^abhyudaye vā, kupitaṁ bandhuṁ rāṣṭraṁ vā vyāvartayanti, amitram āṭavikaṁ vā pratiṣedhayanti, teṣāṁ muṇḍa.jaṭila.vyañjanās tuṣṭa.atuṣṭatvaṁ vidyuḥ //*  
*01.13.16/ tuṣṭān bhūyo^artha.mānābhyāṁ pūjayet //*  
*01.13.17/ atuṣṭāṁs tuṣṭi.hetos tyāgena sāmnā ca prasādayet //*  
*01.13.18/ parasparād vā bhedayed enān, sāmanta.āṭavika.tat.kulīna.aparuddhebhyaś ca //*  
*01.13.19/ tathā^apy atuṣyato daṇḍa.kara.sādhana.adhikāreṇa janapada.vidveṣaṁ grāhayet //*  
*01.13.20/ viviṣṭān upāṁśu.daṇḍena janapada.kopena vā sādhayet //*  
*01.13.21/ gupta.putra.dārān ākara.karma.anteṣu vā vāsayet pareṣām āspada.bhayāt //*  
*01.13.22/ kruddha.lubdha.bhīta.māninas tu pareṣāṁ kṛtyāḥ //*  
*01.13.23/ teṣāṁ kārtāntika.naimittika.mauhūrtika.vyañjanāḥ paraspara.abhisambandham amitra.āṭavika.sambandhaṁ vā vidyuḥ //*  
*01.13.24/ tuṣṭān artha.mānābhyāṁ pūjayet /*  
*01.13.25/ atuṣṭān sāma.dāna.bheda.daṇḍaiḥ sādhayet //*  
*01.13.26ab/ evaṁ sva.viṣaye kṛtyān akṛtyāṁś ca vicakṣaṇaḥ /*  
*01.13.26cd/ para.upajāpāt samrakṣet pradhānān kṣudrakān api //E*  
*(Winning over the seducible and non-seducible parties in the enemy"s territory)*  
*01.14.01/ kṛtya.akṛtya.pakṣa.upagrahaḥ sva.viṣaye vyākhyātaḥ, para.viṣaye vācyaḥ //*  
*01.14.02/ saṁśrutya^arthān vipralabdhaḥ, tulya.kāriṇoḥ śilpe vā^upakāre vā vimānitaḥ, vallabha.avaruddhaḥ, samāhūya parājitaḥ, pravāsa.upataptaḥ, kṛtvā vyayam alabdha.kāryaḥ, svadharmād dāyādyād vā^uparuddhaḥ, māna.adhikārābhyāṁ bhraṣṭaḥ, kulyair antarhitaḥ, prasabha.abhimṛṣṭa.strīkaḥ, kāra.abhinyastaḥ, para.ukta.daṇḍitaḥ, mithyā.ācāra.vāritaḥ, sarva.svam*  
*āhāritaḥ, bandhana.parikliṣṭaḥ, pravāsita.bandhuḥ - iti kruddha.vargaḥ //*  
*01.14.03/ svayam upahataḥ, viprakṛtaḥ, pāpa.karma.abhikhyātaḥ, tulya.doṣa.daṇḍena^udvignaḥ, paryātta.bhūmiḥ, daṇḍena^upanataḥ, sarva.adhikaraṇasthaḥ, sahasā.upacita.arthaḥ, tat.kulīna.upāśaṁsuḥ, pradviṣṭo rājñā, rāja.dveṣī ca - iti bhīta.vargaḥ //*  
*01.14.04/ parikṣīṇaḥ, anya.ātta.svaḥ, kadaryaḥ, vyasanī, atyāhita.vyavahāraś ca - iti lubdha.vargaḥ //*  
*01.14.05/ ātma.sambhāvitaḥ, māna.kāmaḥ, śatru.pūjā.amarṣitaḥ, nīcair upahitaḥ, tīkṣṇaḥ, sāhasikaḥ, bhogena^asaṁtuṣṭaḥ - iti māni.vargaḥ //*  
*01.14.06/ teṣāṁ muṇḍa.jaṭila.vyañjanair yo yad.bhaktiḥ kṛtya.pakṣīyas taṁ tena^upajāpayet //*  
*01.14.07/ "yathā mada.andho hastī mattena^adhiṣṭhito yad yad āsādayati tat sarvaṁ pramṛdnāti, evam ayam aśāstra.cakṣur andho rājā paura.jānapada.vadhāya^abhyutthitaḥ, śakyam asya pratihasti.protsāhanena^apakartum, amarṣaḥ kriyatām" iti kruddha.vargam upajāpayet //*  
*01.14.08/ "yathā līnaḥ sarpo yasmād bhayaṁ paśyati tatra viṣam utsṛjati, evam ayaṁ rājā jāta.doṣa.āśaṅkas tvayi purā krodha.viṣam utsṛjati, anyatra gamyatām" iti bhīta.vargaṁ.upajāpayet //*  
*01.14.09/ "yathā śva.gaṇināṁ dhenuḥ śvabhyo duhyate na brāhmaṇebhyaḥ, evam ayaṁ rājā sattva.prajñā.vākya.śakti.hīnebhyo duhyate na^ātma.guṇa.sampannebhyaḥ, asau rājā puruṣa.viśeṣajñaḥ, tatra gamyatām" iti lubdha.vargaṁ.upajāpayet //*  
*01.14.10/ "yathā caṇḍāla.uda.pānaś caṇḍālānām eva^upabhogyo na^anyeṣām, evam ayaṁ rājā nīco nīcānām eva^upabhogyo na tvadvidhānām āryāṇām, asau rājā puruṣa.viśeṣajñaḥ, tatra gamyatām" iti māni.vargam upajāpayet //*  
*01.14.11ab/ tathā^iti pratipannāṁs tān saṁhitān paṇa.karmaṇā /*  
*01.14.11cd/ yojayeta yathā.śakti sa-apasarpān sva.karmasu //*  
*01.14.12ab/ labheta sāma.dānābhyāṁ kṛtyāṁś ca para.bhūmiṣu /*  
*01.14.12cd/ akṛtyān bheda.daṇḍābhyāṁ para.doṣāṁś ca darśayan //E*  
*(ṭhe topic of counsel)*  
*01.15.01/ kṛta.sva.pakṣa.para.pakṣa.upagrahaḥ kārya.ārambhāṁś cintayet //*  
*01.15.02/ mantra.pūrvāḥ sarva.ārambhāḥ //*  
*01.15.03/ tad.uddeśaḥ saṁvṛtaḥ kathānām anihśrāvī pakṣibhir apy anālokyaḥ syāt /*  
*01.15.04/ śrūyate hi śuka.sārikābhir mantro bhinnaḥ, śvabhir apy anyaiś ca tiryag.yonibhir iti //*  
*01.15.05/ tasmān mantra.uddeśam anāyukto na^upagacchet //*  
*01.15.06/ ucchidyeta mantra.bhedī //*  
*01.15.07/ mantra.bhedo hi dūta.amātya.svāminām iṅgita.ākārābhyām //*  
*01.15.08/ iṅgitam anyathā.vṛttiḥ //*  
*01.15.09/ ākṛti.grahaṇam ākāraḥ //*  
*01.15.10/ tasya saṁvaraṇam āyukta.puruṣa.rakṣaṇam ā.kārya.kālād iti //*  
*01.15.11/ teṣāṁ hi pramāda.mada.supta.pralāpāḥ, kāma.ādir utsekaḥ, pracchanno^avamato vā mantraṁ bhinatti //*  
*01.15.12/ tasmād ādrakṣen mantram //*  
*01.15.13/ "mantra.bhedo hy ayoga.kṣema.karo rājñas tad.āyukta.puruṣāṇāṁ ca //*  
*01.15.14/ tasmād guhyam eko mantrayeta" iti bhāradvājaḥ //*  
*01.15.15/ "mantriṇām api hi mantriṇo bhavanti, teṣām apy anye //*  
*01.15.16/ sā^eṣā mantri.paramparā mantraṁ bhinatti //*  
*01.15.17ab/ "tasmān na^asya pare vidyuḥ karma kiṁcic cikīrṣitam /*  
*01.15.17cd/ ārabdhāras tu jānīyur ārabdhaṁ kṛtam eva vā //*  
*01.15.18/ "na^ekasya mantra.siddhir asti" iti viśāla.akṣaḥ //*  
*01.15.19/ "pratyakṣa.parokṣa.anumeyā hi rāja.vṛttiḥ //*  
*01.15.20/ anupalabdhasya jñānam upalabdhasya niścita.bala.ādhānam artha.dvaidhasya saṁśayac.chedanam eka.deśa.dṛṣṭasya śeṣa.upalabdhir iti mantri.sādhyam etat //*  
*01.15.21/ tasmād buddhi.vṛddhaiḥ sārdham adhyāsīta mantram //*  
*01.15.22ab/ "na kaṁcid avamanyeta sarvasya śṛṇuyān matam /*  
*01.15.22cd/ bālasya^apy arthavad.vākyam upayuñjīta paṇḍitaḥ //"*  
*01.15.23/ "etan mantra.jñānam, na^etan mantra.rakṣaṇam" iti pārāśarāḥ //*  
*01.15.24/ "yad asya kāryam abhipretaṁ tat.pratirūpakaṁ mantriṇaḥ pṛcchet - "kāryam idam evam āsīt, evaṁ vā yadi bhavet, tat kathaṁ kartavyam" iti //*  
*01.15.25/ te yathā brūyus tat kuryāt //*  
*01.15.26/ evaṁ mantra.upalabdhiḥ saṁvṛtiś ca bhavati" iti //*  
*01.15.27/ na^iti piśunaḥ //*  
*01.15.28/ "mantriṇo hi vyavahitam arthaṁ vṛttam avṛttaṁ vā pṛṣṭā anādareṇa bruvanti prakāśayanti vā //*  
*01.15.29/ sa doṣaḥ //*  
*01.15.30/ tasmāt karmasu ye yeṣv abhipretās taiḥ saha mantrayeta //*  
*01.15.31/ tair mantrayamāṇo hi mantra.siddhiṁ guptiṁ ca labhate" iti //*  
*01.15.32/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*01.15.33/ anavasthā hy eṣā //*  
*01.15.34/ mantribhis tribhiś caturbhir vā saha mantrayeta //*  
*01.15.35/ mantrayamāṇo hy ekena^artha.kṛcchreṣu niścayaṁ na^adhigacchet //*  
*01.15.36/ ekaś ca mantrī yathā.iṣṭam anavagrahaś carati //*  
*01.15.37/ dvābhyāṁ mantrayamāṇo dvābhyāṁ saṁhatābhyām avagṛhyate, vigṛhītābhyāṁ vināśyate //*  
*01.15.38/ tat triṣu catuṣu vā kṛcchreṇa^upapadyate //*  
*01.15.39/ mahā.doṣam upapannaṁ tu bhavati //*  
*01.15.40/ tataḥ pareṣu kṛcchreṇa^artha.niścayo gamyate, mantro vā rakṣyate //*  
*01.15.41/ deśa.kāla.kārya.vaśena tv ekena saha dvābhyām eko vā yathā.sāmarthyaṁ mantrayeta //(alterṇative views approved)*  
*01.15.42/ karmaṇām ārambha.upāyaḥ puruṣa.dravya.sampad deśa.kāla.vibhāgo vinipāta.pratīkāraḥ kārya.siddhir iti pañca.aṅgo mantraḥ //*  
*01.15.43/ tān ekaikaśaḥ pṛcchet samastāṁś ca //*  
*01.15.44/ hetubhiś ca^eṣāṁ mati.pravivekān vidyāt //*  
*01.15.45/ avāpta.arthaḥ kālaṁ na^atikrāmayet //*  
*01.15.46/ na dīrgha.kālaṁ mantrayeta, na teṣāṁ pakṣīyair yeṣām apakuryāt //*  
*01.15.47/ "mantri.pariṣadaṁ dvādaśa.amātyān kurvīta" iti mānavāḥ //*  
*01.15.48/ "ṣoḍaśa" iti bārhaspatyāḥ //*  
*01.15.49/ "viṁśatim" ity auśanasāḥ //*  
*01.15.50/ yathā.sāmarthyam iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*01.15.51/ te hy asya sva.pakṣaṁ para.pakṣaṁ ca cintayeyuḥ //*  
*01.15.52/ akṛta.ārambham ārabdha.anuṣṭhānam anuṣṭhita.viśeṣaṁ niyoga.sampadaṁ ca karmaṇāṁ kuryuḥ //*  
*01.15.53/ āsannaiḥ saha karmāṇi paśyet //*  
*01.15.54/ anāsannaiḥ saha pattra.sampreṣaṇena mantrayeta //*  
*01.15.55/ indrasya hi mantri.pariṣad.ṛṣīṇāṁ sahasram //*  
*01.15.56/ sa tac cakṣuḥ //*  
*01.15.57/ tasmād imaṁ dvy.akṣaṁ sahasra.akṣam āhuḥ //*  
*01.15.58/ ātyayike kārye mantriṇo mantri.pariṣadaṁ ca^āhūya brūyāt //*  
*01.15.59/ tatra yad.bhūyiṣṭhā brūyuḥ kārya.siddhi.karaṁ vā tat kuryāt //*

*01.16.01/ udvṛtta.mantro dūta.praṇidhiḥ //*  
*01.16.02/ amātya.sampadā^upeto nisṛṣṭa.arthaḥ //*  
*01.16.03/ pāda.guṇa.hīnaḥ parimita.arthaḥ //*  
*01.16.04/ ardha.guṇa.hīnaḥ śāsana.haraḥ //*  
*01.16.05/ suprativihita.yāna.vāhana.puruṣa.parivāpaḥ pratiṣṭheta //*  
*01.16.06/ śāsanam evaṁ vācyaḥ paraḥ, sa vakṣyaty evam, tasya^idaṁ prativākyam, evam atisaṁdhātavyam, ity adhīyāno gacchet //*  
*01.16.07/ aṭavy.anta.pāla.pura.rāṣṭra.mukhyaiś ca pratisaṁsargaṁ gacchet //*  
*01.16.08/ anīka.sthāna.yuddha.pratigraha.apasāra.bhūmīr ātmanaḥ parasya ca^avekṣeta //*  
*01.16.09/ durga.rāṣṭra.pramāṇaṁ sāra.vṛtti.guptic.chidrāṇi ca^upalabheta //*  
*01.16.10/ para.adhiṣṭhānam anujñātaḥ praviśet //*  
*01.16.11/ śāsanaṁ ca yathā.uktaṁ brūyāt, prāṇa.ābādhe^api dṛṣṭe //*  
*01.16.12/ parasya vāci vaktre dṛṣṭyāṁ ca prasādaṁ vākya.pūjanam iṣṭa.paripraśnaṁ guṇa.kathā.saṅgam āsannam āsanaṁ satkāram iṣṭeṣu smaraṇaṁ viśvāsa.gamanaṁ ca lakṣayet tuṣṭasya, viparītam atuṣṭasya //*  
*01.16.13/ taṁ brūyāt - "dūta.mukhā hi rājānaḥ, tvaṁ ca^anye ca //*  
*01.16.14/ tasmād udyateṣv api śastreṣu yathā.uktaṁ vaktāro dūtāḥ //*  
*01.16.15/ teṣām anta.avasāyino^apy avadhyāḥ, kim aṅga punar brāhmaṇāḥ //*  
*01.16.16/ parasya^etad vākyam //*  
*01.16.17/ eṣa dūta.dharmaḥ" iti //*  
*01.16.18/ vased avisṛṣṭaḥ pūjayā na^utsiktaḥ //*  
*01.16.19/ pareṣu balitvaṁ na manyeta //*  
*01.16.20/ vākyam aniṣṭaṁ saheta //*  
*01.16.21/ striyaḥ pānaṁ ca varjayet //*  
*01.16.22/ ekaḥ śayīta //*  
*01.16.23/ supta.mattayor hi bhāva.jñānaṁ dṛṣṭam //*  
*01.16.24/ kṛtya.pakṣa.upajāpam akṛtya.pakṣe gūḍha.praṇidhānaṁ rāga.aparāgau bhartari randhraṁ ca prakṛtīnāṁ tāpasa.vaidehaka.vyañjanābhyām upalabheta, tayor antevāsibhiś cikitsaka.pāṣaṇḍa.vyañjana.ubhaya.vetanair vā //*  
*01.16.25/ teṣām asambhāṣāyāṁ yācaka.matta.unmatta.supta.pralāpaiḥ puṇya.sthāna.deva.gṛha.citra.lekhya.saṁjñābhir vā cāram upalabheta //*  
*01.16.26/ upalabdhasya^upajāpam upeyāt //*

*01.16.28/ "sarvaṁ veda bhavān" iti brūyāt, kārya.siddhi.karaṁ vā //*  
*01.16.29/ kāryasya^asiddhāv uparudhyamānas tarkayet - "kiṁ bhartur me vyasanam āsannaṁ paśyan, svaṁ vā vyasanaṁ pratikartu.kāmaḥ, pārṣṇi.grāham āsāram antaḥ.kopam āṭavikaṁ vā samutthāpayitu.kāmaḥ, mitram ākrandaṁ vā vyāghātayitu.kāmaḥ, svaṁ vā parato vigraham antaḥ.kopam āṭavikaṁ vā pratikartu.kāmaḥ, saṁsiddhaṁ vā me bhartur yātrā.kālam abhihantu.kāmaḥ,*  
*sasya.paṇya.kupya.saṁgrahaṁ durga.karma bala.samuddānaṁ vā kartu.kāmaḥ, sva.sainyānāṁ vā vyāyāmasya deśa.kālāv ākāṅkṣamāṇaḥ, paribhava.pramādābhyāṁ vā, saṁsarga.anubandha.arthī vā, mām uparuṇaddhi" iti //*

*01.17.01/ rakṣito rājā rājyaṁ rakṣaty āsannebhyaḥ parebhyaś ca, pūrvaṁ dārebhyaḥ putrebhyaś ca //*  
*01.17.02/ dāra.rakṣaṇaṁ niśānta.praṇidhau vakṣyāmaḥ //*

*01.17.04/ "janma.prabhṛti rāja.putrān rakṣet //*

*01.17.12/ "aurabhraṁ bhayam etad" iti piśunaḥ //*  
*01.17.13/ "pratyāpatter hi tad eva kāraṇaṁ jñātvā^anta.pāla.sakhaḥ syāt //*  
*01.17.14/ tasmāt sva.viṣayād apakṛṣṭe sāmanta.durge vāsaḥ śreyān" iti //*  
*01.17.15/ "vatsa.sthānam etad" iti kauṇapadantaḥ //*  
*01.17.16/ "vatsena^iva hi dhenuṁ pitaram asya sāmanto duhyāt //*  
*01.17.17/ tasmān mātṛ.bandhuṣu vāsaḥ śreyān" iti //*  
*01.17.18/ "dhvaja.sthānam etad" iti vāta.vyādhiḥ //*  
*01.17.19/ "tena hi dhvajena^aditi.kauśikavad asya mātṛ.bāndhavā bhikṣeran //*  
*01.17.20/ tasmād grāmya sukheṣv enam avasṛjet //*  
*01.17.21/ sukha.uparuddhā hi putrāḥ pitaraṁ na^abhidruhyanti" iti //*  
*01.17.22/ jīvan.maraṇam etad iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*01.17.23/ kāṣṭham iva ghuṇa.jagdhaṁ rāja.kulam avinīta.putram abhiyukta.mātraṁ bhajyeta //*  
*01.17.24/ tasmād ṛtumatyāṁ mahiṣyām ṛtvijaś carum aindrābārhaspatyaṁ nirvapeyuḥ //*  
*01.17.25/ āpanna.sattvāyāḥ kaumāra.bhṛtyo garbha.bharmaṇi prasave ca viyateta //*  
*01.17.26/ prajātāyāḥ putra.saṁskāraṁ purohitaḥ kuryāt //*  
*01.17.27/ samarthaṁ tadvido vinayeyuḥ //*  
*01.17.28/ "sattriṇām ekaś ca^enaṁ mṛgayā.dyūta.madya.strībhiḥ pralobhayet "pitari vikramya rājyaṁ gṛhāṇa" iti //*  
*01.17.29/ tam anyaḥ sattrī pratiṣedhayet" ity āmbhīyāḥ //*  
*01.17.30/ mahā.doṣam abuddha.bodhanam it kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*01.17.31/ navaṁ hi dravyaṁ yena yena^artha.jātena^upadihyate tat tad ācūṣati //*  
*01.17.32/ evam ayaṁ nava.buddhir yad yad ucyate tat tat.śāstra.upadeśam iva^abhijānāti //*  
*01.17.33/ tasmād dharmyam arthyaṁ ca^asya^upadiśen na^adharmyam anarthyaṁ ca //*  
*01.17.34/ sattriṇas tv enaṁ "tava smaḥ" iti vadantaḥ pālayeyuḥ //*  
*01.17.35/ yauvana.utsekāt para.strīṣu manaḥ kurvāṇam āryā.vyañjanābhiḥ strībhir amedhyābhiḥ śūnya.āgāreṣu rātrāv udvejayeyuḥ //*  
*01.17.36/ madya.kāmaṁ yoga.pānena^udvejayeyuḥ //*  
*01.17.37/ dyūta.kāmaṁ kāpaṭikair udvejayeyuḥ //*  
*01.17.38/ mṛgayā.kāmaṁ pratirodhaka.vyañjanais trāsayeyuḥ //*  
*01.17.39/ pitari vikrama.buddhiṁ "tathā" ity anupraviśya bhedayeyuḥ - "aprārthanīyo rājā, vipanne ghātaḥ, sampanne naraka.pātaḥ, saṁkrośaḥ, prajābhir eka.loṣṭa.vadhaś ca" iti //*  
*01.17.40/ virāgaṁ vedayeyuḥ //*  
*01.17.41/ priyam eka.putraṁ badhnīyāt //*  
*01.17.42/ bahu.putraḥ pratyantam anya.viṣayaṁ vā preṣayed yatra garbhaḥ paṇyaṁ ḍimbo vā na bhavet //*  
*01.17.43/ ātma.sampannaṁ saināpatye yauvarājye vā sthāpayet //*  
*01.17.44/ buddhimān.āhārya.buddhir durbuddhir iti putra.viśeṣāḥ //*  
*01.17.45/ śiṣyamāṇo dharma.arthāv upalabhate ca^anutiṣṭhati ca buddhimān //*  
*01.17.46/ upalabhamāno na^anutiṣṭhaty āhārya.buddhiḥ //*  
*01.17.47/ apāya.nityo dharma.artha.dveṣī ca^iti durbuddhiḥ //*  
*01.17.48/ sa yady eka.putraḥ putra.utpattāv asya prayateta //*  
*01.17.49/ putrikā.putrān utpādayed vā //*  
*01.17.50/ vṛddhas tu vyādhito vā rājā mātṛ.bandhu.kulya.guṇavat.sāmantānām anyatamena kṣetre bījam utpādayet //*  
*01.17.51/ na ca^eka.putram avinītaṁ rājye sthāpayet //*  
*01.17.52ab/ bahūnām eka.samrodhaḥ pitā putra.hito bhavet /*  
*01.17.52cd/ anyatra^āpada aiśvaryaṁ jyeṣṭha.bhāgi tu pūjyate //*  
*01.17.53ab/ kulasya vā bhaved rājyaṁ kula.saṁgho hi durjayaḥ /*  
*01.17.53cd/ arāja.vyasana.ābādhaḥ śaśvad āvasati kṣitim //E*  
*(ṭhe conduct of a prince in disfavour)*  
*(Behaviour towards a prince in disfavour)*  
*01.18.01/ vinīto rāja.putraḥ kṛcchra.vṛttir asadṛśe karmaṇi niyuktaḥ pitaram anuvarteta, anyatra prāṇa.ābādhaka.prakṛti.kopaka.pātakebhyaḥ //*  
*01.18.02/ puṇye karmaṇi niyuktaḥ puruṣam adhiṣṭhātāraṁ yācet //*  
*01.18.03/ puruṣa.adhiṣṭhitaś ca saviśeṣam ādeśam anutiṣṭhet //*  
*01.18.04/ abhirūpaṁ ca karma.phalam aupāyanikaṁ ca lābhaṁ pitur upanāyayet //*  
*01.18.05/ tathā^apy atuṣyantam anyasmin putre dāreṣu vā snihyantam araṇyāya^āpṛccheta //*  
*01.18.06/ bandha.vadha.bhayād vā yaḥ sāmanto nyāya.vṛttir dhārmikaḥ satya.vāg.avisaṁvādakaḥ pratigrahītā mānayitā ca^abhipannānāṁ tam āśrayeta //*  
*01.18.07/ tatrasthaḥ kośa.daṇḍa.sampannaḥ pravīra.puruṣa.kanyā.sambandham aṭavī.sambandhaṁ kṛtya.pakṣa.upagrahaṁ ca kuryāt //*  
*01.18.08/ eka.caraḥ suvarṇa.pāka.maṇi.rāga.hema.rūpya.paṇya.ākara.karma.antān ājīvet //*  
*01.18.09/ pāṣaṇḍa.saṁgha.dravyam aśrotriya.upabhogyaṁ vā deva.dravyam āḍhya.vidhavā.dravyaṁ vā gūḍham anupraviśya sārtha.yāna.pātrāṇi ca madana.rasa.yogena^atisaṁdhāya^apaharet //*  
*01.18.10/ pāragrāmikaṁ vā yogam ātiṣṭhet //*  
*01.18.11/ mātuḥ parijana.upagraheṇa vā ceṣṭeta //*  
*01.18.12/ kāru.śilpi.kuśīlava.cikitsaka.vāg.jīvana.pāṣaṇḍac.chadmabhir vā naṣṭa.rūpas tad.vyañjana.sakhaś.chidreṣu praviśya rājñaḥ śastra.rasābhyāṁ prahṛtya brūyāt - "aham asau kumāraḥ, saha.bhogyam idaṁ rājyam, eko na^arhati bhoktum, ye kāmayante māṁ bhartuṁ tān ahaṁ dviguṇena bhakta.vetanena^upasthāsyāmi" iti // ity aparuddha.vṛttam /*  
*01.18.13/ aparuddhaṁ tu mukhya.putra.apasarpāḥ pratipādya^ānayeyuḥ, mātā vā pratigṛhītā //*  
*01.18.14/ tyaktaṁ gūḍha.puruṣāḥ śastra.rasābhyāṁ hanyuḥ //*  
*01.18.15/ atyaktaṁ tulya.śīlābhiḥ strībhiḥ pānena mṛgayayā vā prasañjayitvā rātrāv upagṛhya^ānayeyuḥ //*  
*01.18.16ab/ upasthitaṁ ca rājyena mad.ūrdhvam iti sāntvayet /*  
*01.18.16cd/ ekastham atha samrundhyāt putravāṁs tu pravāsayet //E*  
*(ṛules for the king)*  
*01.19.01/ rājānam utthitam anūttiṣṭhante bhṛtyāḥ //*  
*01.19.02/ pramādyantam anupramādyanti //*  
*01.19.03/ karmāṇi ca^asya bhakṣayanti //*  
*01.19.04/ dviṣadbhiś ca^atisaṁdhīyate /*  
*01.19.05/ tasmād utthānam ātmanaḥ kurvīta //*  
*01.19.06/ nālikābhir ahar aṣṭadhā rātriṁ ca vibhajet, chāyā.pramāṇena vā //*  
*01.19.07/ tripauruṣī pauruṣī catur.aṅgulā naṣṭac.chāyo madhya.ahna^iti catvāraḥ pūrve divasasya^aṣṭa.bhāgāḥ //*  
*01.19.08/ taiḥ paścimā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*01.19.09/ tatra pūrve divasasya^aṣṭa.bhāge rakṣā.vidhānam āya.vyayau ca śṛṇuyāt //*  
*01.19.10/ dvitīye paura.jānapadānāṁ kāryāṇi paśyet //*  
*01.19.11/ tṛtīye snāna.bhojanaṁ seveta, svādhyāyaṁ ca kurvīta //*  
*01.19.12/ caturthe hiraṇya.pratigraham adhyakṣāṁś ca kurvīta //*  
*01.19.13/ pañcame mantri.pariṣadā pattra.sampreṣaṇena mantrayeta, cāra.guhya.bodhanīyāni ca budhyeta //*  
*01.19.14/ ṣaṣṭhe svaira.vihāraṁ mantraṁ vā seveta //*  
*01.19.15/ saptame hasty.aśva.ratha.āyudhīyān paśyet //*  
*01.19.16/ aṣṭame senā.pati.sakho vikramaṁ cintayet //*  
*01.19.17/ pratiṣṭhite^ahani saṁdhyām upāsīta //*  
*01.19.18/ prathame rātri.bhāge gūḍha.puruṣān paśyet //*  
*01.19.19/ dvitīye snāna.bhojanaṁ kurvīta, svādhyāyaṁ ca //*  
*01.19.20/ tṛtīye tūrya.ghoṣeṇa saṁviṣṭaś caturtha.pañcamau śayīta //*  
*01.19.21/ ṣaṣṭhe tūrya.ghoṣeṇa pratibuddhaḥ śāstram itikartavyatāṁ ca cintayet //*  
*01.19.22/ saptame mantram adhyāsīta, gūḍha.puruṣāṁś ca preṣayet //*  
*01.19.23/ aṣṭame ṛtvig.ācārya.purohita.svastyayanāni pratigṛhṇīyāt, cikitsaka.māhānasika.mauhūrtikāṁś ca paśyet //*  
*01.19.24/ savastāṁ dhenuṁ vṛṣabhaṁ ca pradakṣiṇī.kṛtya^upasthānaṁ gacchet //*  
*01.19.25/ ātma.bala.ānukūlyena vā niśā.ahar.bhāgān pravibhajya kāryāṇi seveta //*  
*01.19.26/ upasthāna.gataḥ kārya.arthinām advāra.āsaṅgaṁ kārayet //*  
*01.19.27/ durdarśo hi rājā kārya.akārya.viparyāsam āsannaiḥ kāryate //*  
*01.19.28/ tena prakṛti.kopam ari.vaśaṁ vā gacchet //*  
*01.19.29/ tasmād devatā.āśrama.pāṣaṇḍa.śrotriya.paśu.puṇya.sthānānāṁ bāla.vṛddha.vyādhita.vyasany.anāthānāṁ strīṇāṁ ca krameṇa kāryāṇi paśyet, kārya.gauravād ātyayika.vaśena vā //*  
*01.19.30ab/ sarvam ātyayikaṁ kāryaṁ śṛṇuyān na^atipātayet /\[ś\]*  
*01.19.30cd/ kṛcchra.sādhyam atikrāntam asādhyaṁ vā^api jāyate //\[ś\]*  
*01.19.31ab/ agny.agāra.gataḥ kāryaṁ paśyed vaidya.tapasvinām /\[ś\]*  
*01.19.31cd/ purohita.ācārya.sakhaḥ pratyutthāya^abhivādya ca //\[ś\]*  
*01.19.32ab/ tapasvināṁ tu kāryāṇi traividyaiḥ saha kārayet /\[ś\]*  
*01.19.32cd/ māyā.yogavidāṁ caiva na svayaṁ kopa.kāraṇāt //\[ś\]*  
*01.19.33ab/ rājño hi vratam utthānaṁ yajñaḥ kārya.anuśāsanam /\[ś\]*  
*01.19.33cd/ dakṣiṇā vṛtti.sāmyaṁ tu dīkṣā tasya^abhiṣecanam //\[ś\]*  
*01.19.34ab/ prajā.sukhe sukhaṁ rājñaḥ prajānāṁ ca hite hitam /\[ś\]*  
*01.19.34cd/ na^ātma.priyaṁ hitaṁ rājñaḥ prajānāṁ tu priyaṁ hitam //\[ś\]*  
*01.19.35ab/ tasmān nitya.utthito rājā kuryād artha.anuśāsanam /*  
*01.19.35cd/ arthasya mūlam utthānam anarthasya viparyayaḥ //\[ś\]*  
*01.19.36ab/ anutthāne dhruvo nāśaḥ prāptasya^anāgatasya ca /\[ś\]*  
*01.19.36cd/ prāpyate phalam utthānāl labhate ca^arthasampadam //\[ś\]*  
*ṛegulations for the royal resideñce*  
*01.20.01/ vāstuka.praśaste deśe saprākāra.parikhā.dvāram aneka.kakṣyā.parigatam antaḥpuraṁ kārayet //*  
*KA01.20.02/ kośagṛha.vidhānena madhye vāsa.gṛham, gūḍha.bhitti.saṁcāraṁ mohana.gṛhaṁ tan.madhye vā vāsa.gṛham, bhūmi.gṛhaṁ vā^āsanna.caitya.kāṣṭha.devatā.apidhāna.dvāram aneka.suruṅgā.saṁcāraṁ tasya^upari prāsādaṁ gūḍha.bhitti.sopānaṁ suṣira.stambha.praveśa.apasāraṁ vā vāsa.gṛhaṁ yantra.baddha.tala.avapātaṁ kārayet, āpat.pratīkāra.artham āpadi vā //*  
*01.20.03/ ato^anyathā vā vikalpayet, saha.adhyāyi.bhayāt //*  
*01.20.04/ mānuṣeṇa^agninā trir apasavyaṁ parigatam antaḥpuram agnir anyo na dahati, na ca^atra^anyo^agnir jvalati, vaidyutena bhasmanā mṛt.samyuktena karaka.vāriṇā^avaliptaṁ ca //*  
*01.20.05/ jīvantī.śvetā.muṣkaka.puṣpa.vandākābhir akṣīve jātasya^aśvatthasya pratānena guptaṁ sarpā viṣāṇi vā na prabhavanti //*  
*01.20.06/ mayūra.nakula.pṛṣata.utsargaḥ sarpān bhakṣayati //*  
*01.20.07/ śukaḥ sārikā bhṛṅga.rājo vā sarpa.viṣa.śaṅkāyāṁ krośati //*  
*01.20.08/ krauñco viṣa.abhyāśe mādyati, glāyati jīvaṁ.jīvakaḥ, mriyate matta.kokilaḥ, cakorasya^akṣiṇī virajyete //*  
*01.20.09/ ity evam agni.viṣa.sarpebhyaḥ pratikurvīta //*  
*01.20.10/ pṛṣṭhataḥ kakṣyā.vibhāge strī.niveśo garbha.vyādhi.saṁsthā vṛkṣa.udaka.sthānaṁ ca //*  
*01.20.11/ bahiḥ kanyā.kumāra.puram //*  
*01.20.12/ purastād alaṅkāra.bhūmir mantra.bhūmir upasthānaṁ kumāra.adhyakṣa.sthānaṁ ca //*  
*01.20.13/ kakṣya.antareṣv antarvaṁśika.sainyaṁ tiṣṭhet //*  
*01.20.14/ antar.gṛha.gataḥ sthavira.strī.pariśuddhāṁ devīṁ paśyet //*  
*01.20.15/ devī.gṛhe līno hi bhrātā bhadrasenaṁ jaghāna, mātuḥ śayyā.antargataś ca putraḥ kārūṣam //*  
*01.20.16/ lājān madhunā^iti viṣeṇa paryasya devī kāśi.rājam, viṣa.digdhena nūpreṇa vairantyam, mekhalā.maṇinā sauvīram, jālūtham ādarśena, veṇyāṁ gūḍhaṁ śastraṁ kṛtvā devī vidūrathaṁ jaghāna //*  
*01.20.17/ tasmād etāny āspadāni pariharet //*  
*01.20.18/ muṇḍa.jaṭila.kuhaka.pratisaṁsargaṁ bāhyābhiś ca dāsībhiḥ pratiṣedhayet //*  
*01.20.19/ na ca^enāḥ kulyāḥ paśyeyuḥ, anyatra garbha.vyādhi.saṁsthābhyaḥ //*  
*01.20.20/ rūpa.ājīvāḥ snāna.pragharṣa.śuddha.śarīrāḥ parivartita.vastra.alaṁkārāḥ paśyeyuḥ //*  
*01.20.21/ aśītikāḥ puruṣāḥ pañcāśatkāḥ striyo vā mātā.pitṛ.vyañjanāḥ sthavira.varṣadhara.abhyāgārikāś ca^avarodhānāṁ śauca.āśaucaṁ vidyuḥ, sthāpayeyuś ca svāmi.hite, //*  
*01.20.22ab/ sva.bhūmau ca vaset sarvaḥ para.bhūmau na saṁcaret /\[ś\]*  
*01.20.22cd/ na ca bāhyena saṁsargaṁ kaścid ābhyantaro vrajet //\[ś\]*  
*01.20.23ab/ sarvaṁ ca^avekṣitaṁ dravyaṁ nibaddha.āgama.nirgamam /\[ś\]*  
*01.20.23cd/ nirgacched abhigacched vā mudrā.saṁkrānta.bhūmikam //\[ś\] E*  
*(Concerning the protection of (the king"s) own person)*  
*01.21.01/ śayanād utthitaḥ strī.gaṇair dhanvibhiḥ parigṛhyate, dvitīyasyāṁ kakṣyāyāṁ kañcuka.uṣṇīṣibhir varṣa.dhara.abhyāgārikaiḥ, tṛtīyasyāṁ kubja.vāmana.kirātaiḥ, caturthyāṁ mantribhiḥ sambandhibhir dauvārikaiś ca prāsa.pāṇibhiḥ //*  
*01.21.02/ pitṛ.paitāmahaṁ sambandha.anubaddhaṁ śikṣitam anuraktaṁ kṛta.karmāṇaṁ ca janam āsannaṁ kurvīta, na^anyato.deśīyam akṛta.artha.mānaṁ sva.deśīyaṁ vā^apy apakṛtya^upagṛhītam //*  
*01.21.03/ antar.vaṁśika.sainyaṁ rājānam antaḥpuraṁ ca rakṣet //*  
*01.21.04/ gupte deśe māhānasikaḥ sarvam āsvāda.bāhulyena karma kārayet //*  
*01.21.05/ tad rajā tathaiva pratibhuñjīta pūrvam agnaye vayobhyaś ca baliṁ kṛtvā //*  
*01.21.06/ agner jvālā.dhūma.nīlatā śabda.sphoṭanaṁ ca viṣa.yuktasya, vayasāṁ vipattiś ca //*  
*01.21.07a/ annasya ūṣmā mayūra.grīva.ābhaḥ śaityam āśu kliṣṭasya^iva vaivarṇyaṁ sa-udakatvam aklinnatvaṁ ca -*  
*01.21.07b/ vyañjanānām āśu śuṣkatvaṁ ca kvātha.dhyāma.phena.paṭala.vicchinna.bhāvo gandha.sparśa.rasa.vadhaś ca -*  
*01.21.07c/ draveṣu hīna.atiriktac.chāyā.darśanaṁ phena.paṭala.sīmanta.ūrdhva.rājī.darśanaṁ ca -*  
*01.21.07d/ rasasya madhye nīlā rājī, payasas tāmrā, madya.toyayoḥ kālī, dadhnaḥ śyāmā, madhunaḥ śvetā, dravyāṇām ārdrāṇām āśu pramlānatvam utpakva.bhāvaḥ kvātha.nīla.śyāvatā ca -*  
*01.21.07e/ śuṣkāṇām āśu śātanaṁ vaivarṇyaṁ ca, -*  
*01.21.07f/ kaṭhinānāṁ mṛdutvaṁ mṛdūnāṁ ca kaṭhinatvam, tad.abhyāśe kṣudra.sattva.vadhaś ca, -*  
*01.21.07g/ āstaraṇa.pravaraṇānāṁ dhyāma.maṇḍalatā tanturoma.pakṣma.śātanaṁ ca, -*  
*01.21.07h/ loha.maṇimayānāṁ paṅkama.lopadehatā sneha.rāga.gaurava.prabhāva.varṇa.sparśavadhaś ca - iti viṣayuktasya liṅgāni //*  
*01.21.08/ viṣa.pradasya tu śuṣka.śyāva.vaktratā vāk.saṅgaḥ svedo vijṛmbhaṇaṁ ca^atimātraṁ vepathuḥ praskhalanaṁ vākya.viprekṣaṇam āvegaḥ karmaṇi sva.bhūmau ca^anavasthānam iti //*  
*01.21.09/ tasmād asya jāṅgulīvido bhiṣajaś ca^āsannāḥ syuḥ //*  
*01.21.10/ bhiṣag.bhaiṣajya.agārād āsvāda.viśuddham auṣadhaṁ gṛhītvā pācaka.peṣakābhyām ātmanā ca pratisvādya rājñe prayacchet //*  
*01.21.11/ pānaṁ pānīyaṁ ca^auṣadhena vyākhyātam //*  
*01.21.12/ kalpaka.prasādhakāḥ snāna.śuddha.vastra.hastāḥ samudram upakaraṇam antarvaṁśika.hastād ādāya paricareyuḥ //*  
*01.21.13/ snāpaka.saṁvāhaka.āstaraka.rajaka.mālā.kāra.karma dāsyaḥ prasiddha.śaucāḥ kuryuḥ, tābhir adhiṣṭhitā vā śilpinaḥ //*  
*01.21.14/ ātma.cakṣuṣi niveśya vastra.mālyaṁ dadyuḥ, snāna.anulepana.pragharṣa.cūrṇa.vāsa.snānīyāni ca sva.vakṣo.bāhuṣu ca //*  
*01.21.15/ etena parasmād āgatakaṁ vyākhyātam //*  
*01.21.16/ kuśīlavāḥ śastra.agni.rasa.krīḍā.varjaṁ narmayeyuḥ //*  
*01.21.17/ ātodyāni ca^eṣām antas tiṣṭheyuḥ, aśva.ratha.dvipa.alaṁkārāś ca //*  
*01.21.18/ āpta.puruṣa.adhiṣṭhitaṁ yāna.vāhanam ārohet, nāvaṁ ca^āpta.nāvika.adhiṣṭhitam //*  
*01.21.19/ anya.nau.pratibaddhāṁ vāta.vega.vaśāṁ ca na^upeyāt //*  
*01.21.20/ udaka.ante sainyam āsīta //*  
*01.21.21/ matsya.grāha.viśuddham udakam avagāheta //*  
*01.21.22/ vyāla.grāha.viśuddham udyānaṁ gacchet //*  
*01.21.23/ lubdhaka.śva.gaṇibhir apāsta.stena.vyāla.para.ābādha.bhayaṁ cala.lakṣya.paricaya.arthaṁ mṛga.araṇyaṁ gacchet //*  
*01.21.24/ āpta.śastra.grāha.adhiṣṭhitaḥ siddha.tāpasaṁ paśyet, mantri.pariṣadā saha sāmanta.dūtam //*  
*01.21.25/ samnaddho^aśvaṁ hastinaṁ vā^ārūḍhaḥ samnaddham anīkaṁ paśyet //*  
*01.21.26/ niryāṇe^abhiyāne ca rāja.mārgam ubhayataḥ kṛta.ārakṣaṁ śastribhir daṇḍibhiś ca^apāsta.śastra.hasta.pravrajita.vyaṅgaṁ gacchet //*  
*01.21.27/ na puruṣa.sambādham avagāheta //*  
*01.21.28/ yātrā.samāja.utsava.prahavaṇāni ca daśa.vargika.adhiṣṭhitāni gacchet //*  
*01.21.29ab/ yathā ca yoga.puruṣair anyān rājā^adhitiṣṭhati /\[ś\]*  
*01.21.30cd/ tathā^ayam anya.ābādhebhyo rakṣed ātmānam ātmavān //\[ś\] E*  
*(Book ṭwo: ṭhe activity of the heads of departments)*  
*(Chap, 1, ṣec, 19: ṣettlement of the countryside)*

### 2

*02.01.01/ bhūta.pūrvam abhūta.pūrvaṁ vā jana.padaṁ para.deśa.apavāhanena sva.deśa.abhiṣyanda.vamanena vā niveśayet //*  
*02.01.02/ śūdra.karṣaka.prāyaṁ kula.śata.avaraṁ pañca.kula.śata.paraṁ grāmaṁ krośad.vikrośa.sīmānam anyonya.ārakṣaṁ niveśayet //*  
*02.01.03/ nalī.śaila.vana.bhṛṣṭi.darī.setu.bandha.śamī.śālmalī.kṣīra.vṛkṣān anteṣu sīmnāṁ sthāpayet //*  
*02.01.04/ aṣṭaśata.grāmyā madhye sthānīyam, catuhśata.grāmyā droṇa.mukham, dviśata.grāmyāḥ kārvaṭikam, daśa.grāmī.saṁgraheṇa saṁgrahaṁ sthāpayet //*  
*02.01.05/ anteṣv anta.pāla.durgāṇi jana.pada.dvārāṇy anta.pāla.adhiṣṭhitāni sthāpayet //*  
*02.01.06/ teṣām antarāṇi vāgurika.śabara.pulinda.caṇḍāla.araṇya.carā rakṣeyuḥ //*  
*02.01.07a/ ṛtvig.ācārya.purohita.śrotriyebhyo brahma.deyāny adaṇḍa.karāṇy abhirūpa.dāyādakāni prayacchet -*  
*02.01.07b/ adhyakṣa.saṁkhyāyaka.ādibhyo gopa.sthānika.anīkastha.cikitsaka.aśva.damaka.jaṅghākārikebhyaś ca vikraya.ādhāna.varjāni //*  
*02.01.08/ karadebhyaḥ kṛta.kṣetrāṇy aikapuruṣikāṇi prayacchet //*  
*02.01.09/ akṛtāni kartṛbhyo na^ādeyāni //*  
*02.01.10/ akṛṣatām āchidya^anyebhyaḥ prayacchet //*  
*02.01.11/ grāma.bhṛtaka.vaidehakā vā kṛṣeyuḥ //*  
*02.01.12/ akṛṣanto vā^avahīnaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*02.01.13/ dhānya.paśu.hiraṇyaiś ca^etān anugṛhṇīyāt //*  
*02.01.14/ tāny anu sukhena dadyuḥ //*  
*02.01.15/ anugraha.parihārau ca^etebbhyaḥ kośa.vṛddhi.karau dadyāt, kośa.upaghātakau varjayet //*  
*02.01.16/ alpa.kośo hi rājā paura.jānapadān eva grasate //*  
*02.01.17/ niveśa.sama.kālaṁ yathā.āgatakaṁ vā parihāraṁ dadyāt //*  
*02.01.18/ nivṛtta.parihārān pitā^iva^anugṛhṇīyāt //*  
*02.01.19/ ākara.karma.anta.dravya.hasti.vana.vraja.vaṇik.patha.pracārān vāri.sthala.patha.paṇya.pattanāni ca niveśayet //*  
*02.01.20/ saha.udakam āhārya^udakaṁ vā setuṁ bandhayet //*  
*02.01.21/ anyeṣāṁ vā badhnatāṁ bhūmi.mārga.vṛkṣa.upakaraṇa.anugrahaṁ kuryāt, puṇya.sthāna.ārāmāṇāṁ ca //*  
*02.01.22/ sambhūya.setu.bandhād apakrāmataḥ karmakara.balīvardāḥ karma kuryuḥ //*  
*02.01.23/ vyayakarmaṇi ca bhāgī syāt, na ca^aṁśaṁ labheta //*  
*02.01.24/ matsya.plava.hari.tapaṇyānāṁ setuṣu rājā svāmyaṁ gacchet //*  
*02.01.25/ dāsa.āhitaka.bandhūn aśṛṇvato rājā vinayaṁ grāhayet //*  
*02.01.26/ bāla.vṛddha.vyasany.anāthāṁś ca rājā bibhṛyāt, striyam aprajātāṁ prajātāyaś ca putrān //*  
*02.01.27/ bāla.dravyaṁ grāma.vṛddhā vardhayeyur ā vyavahāra.prāpaṇāt, deva.dravyaṁ ca //*  
*02.01.28/ apatya.dāraṁ mātā.pitarau bhrātṛṛn aprāpta.vyavahārān bhaginīḥ kanyā vidhavāś ca^abibhrataḥ śaktimato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra patitebhyaḥ, anyatra mātuḥ //*  
*02.01.29/ putra.dāram apratividhāya pravrajataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, striyaṁ ca pravrājayataḥ //*  
*02.01.30/ lupta.vyāyāmaḥ pravrajed āpṛcchya dharmasthān //*  
*02.01.31/ anyathā niyamyeta //*  
*02.01.32/ vānaprasthād anyaḥ pravrajita.bhāvaḥ, sajātād anyaḥ saṁghaḥ, sāmutthāyikād anyaḥ samaya.anubandho vā na^asya jana.padam upaniviśeta //*  
*02.01.33/ na ca tatra^ārāmā vihāra.arthā vā śālāḥ syuḥ //*  
*02.01.34/ naṭa.nartaka.gāyana.vādaka.vāg.jīvana.kuśīlavā na karma.vighnaṁ kuryuḥ //*  
*02.01.35/ nirāśrayatvād grāmāṇāṁ kṣetra.abhiratatvāc ca puruṣāṇāṁ kośa.viṣṭi.dravya.dhānya.rasa.vṛddhir bhavati //*  
*02.01.36ab/ para.cakra.aṭavī.grastaṁ vyādhi.durbhikṣa.pīḍitam /\[ś\]*  
*02.01.36cd/ deśaṁ parihared rājā vyaya.krīḍāś ca vārayet //\[ś\]*  
*02.01.37ab/ daṇḍa.viṣṭi.kara.ābādhai rakṣed upahatāṁ kṛṣim /\[ś\]*  
*02.01.37cd/ stena.vyāla.viṣa.grāhair vyādhibhiś ca paśu.vrajān //\[ś\]*  
*02.01.38ab/ vallabhaiḥ kārmikaiḥ stenair anta.pālaiś ca pīḍitam /\[ś\]*  
*02.01.38cd/ śodhayet paśu.saṁghaiś ca kṣīyamāṇaṁ vaṇik.patham //\[ś\]*  
*02.01.39ab/ evaṁ dravya.dvi.pavanaṁ setu.bandham atha^ākarān /\[ś\]*  
*02.01.39cd/ rakṣet pūrva.kṛtān rājā navāṁś ca^abhipravartayet //\[ś\] E*  
*(ḍisposal of non-agricultural land)*  
*02.2.01/ akṛṣyāyāṁ bhūmau paśubhyo vivītāni prayacchet //*  
*02.2.02/ pradiṣṭa.abhaya.sthāvara.jaṅgamāni ca brahma.soma.araṇyāni tapasvibhyo go.ruta.parāṇi prayacchet //*  
*02.2.03/ tāvan.mātram eka.dvāraṁ khāta.guptaṁ svādu.phala.gulma.guccham akaṇṭaki.drumam uttāna.toya.āśayaṁ dānta.mṛga.catuṣpadaṁ bhagna.nakha.daṁṣṭra.vyālaṁ mārgayuka.hasti.hastinīka.labhaṁ mṛga.vanaṁ vihāra.arthaṁ rājñaḥ kārayet //*  
*02.2.04/ sarva.atithi.mṛgaṁ pratyante ca^anyan.mṛga.vanaṁ bhūmi.vaśena vā niveśayet //*  
*02.2.05/ kupya.pradiṣṭānāṁ ca dravyāṇām eka.ekaśo vanāni niveśayet, dravya.vana.karma.antān aṭavīś ca dravya.vana.apāśrayāḥ //*  
*02.2.06/ pratyante hasti.vanam aṭavy.ārakṣaṁ niveśayet //*  
*02.2.07/ nāga.vana.adhyakṣaḥ pārvataṁ na^ādeyaṁ sāra.sama.anūpaṁ ca nāga.vanaṁ vidita.paryanta.praveśa.niṣkāsaṁ nāga.vana.pālaiḥ pālayet //*  
*02.2.08/ hasti.ghātinaṁ hanyuḥ //*  
*02.2.09/ danta.yugaṁ svayaṁ.mṛtasya^āharataḥ sapāda.catuṣpaṇo lābhaḥ //*  
*02.2.10/ nāga.vana.pālā hastipaka.pāda.pāśika.saimika.vana.caraka.pārikarmika.sakhā hasti.mūtra.purīṣac.channa.gandhā bhallātakī.śākhā.pracchannāḥ pañcabhiḥ saptabhir vā hasti.bandhakībhiḥ saha carantaḥ śayyā.sthāna.padyā.leṇḍa.kūla.ghāta.uddeśena hasti.kula.paryagraṁ vidyuḥ //*  
*02.2.11/ yūtha.caram eka.caraṁ niryūthaṁ yūtha.patiṁ hastinaṁ vyālaṁ mattaṁ potaṁ bandha.muktaṁ ca nibandhena vidyuḥ //*  
*02.2.12/ anīkastha.pramāṇaiḥ praśasta.vyañjana.ācārān hastino gṛhṇīyuḥ //*  
*02.2.13/ hasti.pradhānaṁ vijayo rājñaḥ //*  
*02.2.14/ para.anīka.vyūha.durga.skandha.āvāra.pramardanā hy atipramāṇa.śarīrāḥ prāṇa.hara.karmāṇo hastinaḥ //*  
*02.2.15ab/ kāliṅga.aṅgarajāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ prācyāś cedi.karūṣajāḥ /\[ś\]*  
*02.2.15cd/ dāśārṇāś ca^apara.antāś ca dvipānāṁ madhyamā matāḥ //\[ś\]*  
*02.2.16ab/ saurāṣṭrikāḥ pāñcanadās teṣāṁ pratyavarāḥ smṛtāḥ /\[ś\]*  
*02.2.16cd/ sarveṣāṁ karmaṇā vīryaṁ javas tejaś ca vardhate //\[ś\] E*  
*(Construction of forts)*  
*02.3.01/ caturdiśaṁ jana.pada.ante sāmparāyikaṁ daiva.kṛtaṁ durgaṁ kārayet, antar.dvīpaṁ sthalaṁ vā nimna.avaruddham audakam, prāstaraṁ guhāṁ vā pārvatam, nirudaka.stambam iriṇaṁ vā dhānvanam, khañjana.udakaṁ stamba.gahanaṁ vā vana.durgam //*  
*02.3.02/ teṣāṁ nadī.parvata.durgaṁ jana.pada.ārakṣa.sthānam, dhānvana.vana.durgam aṭavī.sthānam āpady apasāro vā //*  
*02.3.03/ jana.pada.madhye samudaya.sthānaṁ sthānīyaṁ niveśayet, vāstuka.praśaste deśe nadī.saṅgame hradasya^aviśoṣasya^aṅke sarasas taṭākasya vā, vṛttaṁ dīrghaṁ catur.aśraṁ vā vāstu.vaśena vā pradakṣiṇa.udakaṁ paṇya.puṭa.bhedanam aṁsapatha.vāri.pathābhyām upetam //*  
*02.3.04/ tasya parikhās tisro daṇḍa.antarāḥ kārayet caturdaśa dvādaśa daśa^iti daṇḍān vistīrṇāḥ, vistārād avagāḍhāḥ pāda.ūnam ardhaṁ vā, tribhāga.mūlāḥ, mūla.catur.aśrā vā, pāṣāṇa.upahitāḥ pāṣāṇa.iṣṭakā.baddha.pārśvā vā, toya.antikīr āgantu.toya.pūrṇā vā saparivāhāḥ padma.grāhavatīś ca //*  
*02.3.05/ caturdaṇḍa.apakṛṣṭaṁ parikhāyāḥ ṣaḍdaṇḍa.ucchritam avaruddhaṁ tad.dviguṇa.viṣkambhaṁ khātād vapraṁ kārayed ūrdhva.cayaṁ mañca.pṛṣṭhaṁ kumbha.kukṣikaṁ vā hastibhir gobhiś ca kṣuṇṇaṁ kaṇṭaki.gulma.viṣa.vallī.pratānavantam //*  
*02.3.06/ pāṁsu.śeṣeṇa vāstuc.chidraṁ rāja.bhavanaṁ vā pūrayet //*  
*02.3.07a/ vaprasya^upari prākāraṁ viṣkambha.dviguṇa.utsedham aiṣṭakaṁ dvādaśa.hastād ūrdhvam ojaṁ yugmaṁ vā ā caturviṁśati.hastād iti kārayet -*  
*02.3.07b/ ratha.caryā.saṁcāraṁ tāla.mūlaṁ murajakaiḥ kapi.śīrṣakaiś ca^ācita.agram //*  
*02.3.08/ pṛthu.śilā.saṁhataṁ vā śailaṁ kārayet, na tv eva kāṣṭamayam //*  
*02.3.09/ agnir avahito hi tasmin vasati //*  
*02.3.10/ viṣkambha.catur.aśram aṭṭālakam utsedha.sama.avakṣepa.sopānaṁ kārayet triṁśad.daṇḍa.antaraṁ ca //*  
*02.3.11/ dvayor aṭṭālakayor madhye saharmya.dvi.talām adhyardhāya.āyāmāṁ pratolīṁ kārayet //*  
*02.3.12/ aṭṭālaka.pratolī.madhye tri.dhānuṣka.adhiṣṭhānaṁ sa-apidhānac.chidra.phalaka.saṁhatam indra.kośaṁ kārayet //*  
*02.3.13/ antareṣu dvihasta.viṣkambhaṁ pārśve catur.guṇa.āyāmaṁ deva.pathaṁ kārayet //*  
*02.3.14/ daṇḍa.antarā dvi.daṇḍa.antarā vā caryāḥ kārayet, agrāhye deśe pradhāvanikāṁ niṣkira.dvāraṁ ca //*  
*02.3.15/ bahir.jānu.bhañjanī.śūla.prakara.kūpa.kūṭa.avapāta.kaṇṭaka.pratisara.ahi.pṛṣṭha.tāla.pattra.śṛṅga.aṭaka.śva.daṁṣṭra.argala.upaskandana.pāduka.ambarīṣa.uda.pānakaiḥ praticchannaṁ channa.pathaṁ kārayet //*  
*02.3.16/ prākāram ubhayato meṇḍhakam adhyardha.daṇḍaṁ kṛtvā pratolī.ṣaṭ.tulā.antaraṁ dvāraṁ niveśayet pañca.daṇḍād eka.uttaram ā.aṣṭa.daṇḍād iti catur.aśraṁ ṣaḍ.bhāgam āyāmād.adhikam aṣṭa.bhāgaṁ vā //*  
*02.3.17/ pañca.daśa.hastād eka.uttaram ā.aṣṭādaśa.hastād iti tala.utsedhaḥ //*  
*02.3.18/ stambhasya parikṣepaḥ ṣaḍ.āyāmo, dviguṇo nikhātaḥ, cūlikāyāś catur.bhāgaḥ //*  
*02.3.19/ ādi.talasya pañca.bhāgāḥ śālā vāpī sīmā.gṛhaṁ ca //*  
*02.3.20/ daśa.bhāgikau dvau pratimañcau, antaram āṇī.harmyaṁ ca //*  
*02.3.21/ samucchrayād ardha.tale sthūṇā.bandhaś ca //*  
*02.3.22/ ardha.vāstukam uttama.agāram, tribhāga.antaraṁ vā, iṣṭakā.avabaddha.pārśvam, vāmataḥ pradakṣiṇa.sopānaṁ gūḍha.bhitti.sopānam itarataḥ //*  
*02.3.23/ dvi.hastaṁ toraṇa.śiraḥ //*  
*02.3.24/ tri.pañca.bhāgikau dvau kapāṭa.yogau //*  
*02.3.25/ dvau parighau //*  
*02.3.26/ aratnir indra.kīlaḥ //*  
*02.3.27/ pañca.hastam āṇi.dvāram //*  
*02.3.28/ catvāro hasti.parighāḥ //*  
*02.3.29/ niveśa.ardhaṁ hasti.nakham //*  
*02.3.30/ mukha.samaḥ saṁkramaḥ saṁhāryo bhūmimayo vā nirudake //*  
*02.3.31/ prākāra.samaṁ mukham avasthāpya tri.bhāga.godhā.mukhaṁ gopuraṁ kārayet //*  
*02.3.32/ prākāra.madhye vāpīṁ kṛtvā puṣkariṇī.dvāram, catuḥ.śālam adhyardha.antaraṁ sāṇikaṁ kumārī.puram, muṇḍa.harmya.dvi.talaṁ muṇḍaka.dvāram, bhūmi.dravya.vaśena vā niveśayet //*  
*02.3.33/ tri.bhāga.adhika.āyāmā bhāṇḍa.vāhinīḥ kulyāḥ kārayet //*  
*02.3.34ab/ tāsu pāṣāṇa.kuddālāḥ kuṭhārī.kāṇḍa.kalpanāḥ /\[ś\]*  
*02.3.34cd/ muṣuṇḍhī.mudgarā daṇḍāś cakra.yantra.śataghnayaḥ //\[ś\]*  
*02.3.35ab/ kāryāḥ kārmārikāḥ śūlā vedhana.agrāś ca veṇavaḥ /\[ś\]*  
*02.3.35cd/ uṣṭra.grīvyo^agni.samyogāḥ kupya.kalpe ca yo vidhiḥ //\[ś\] E*  
*(ḷay-out of the fortified city)*  
*02.4.01/ trayaḥ prācīnā rāja.mārgās traya udīcīnā iti vāstu.vibhāgaḥ //*  
*02.4.02/ sa dvādaśa.dvāro yukta.udaka.bhramac.channa.pathaḥ //*  
*02.4.03/ catur.daṇḍa.antarā rathyāḥ //*  
*02.4.04/ rāja.mārga.droṇa.mukha.sthānīya.rāṣṭra.vivīta.pathāḥ samyānīya.vyūha.śmaśāna.grāma.pathāś ca^aṣṭa.daṇḍāḥ //*  
*02.4.05/ catur.daṇḍaḥ setu.vana.pathaḥ, dvi.daṇḍo hasti.kṣetra.pathaḥ, pañca.aratnayo ratha.pathaḥ, catvāraḥ paśu.pathaḥ, dvau kṣudra.paśu.manuṣya.pathaḥ //*  
*02.4.06/ pravīre vāstuni rāja.niveśaś cāturvarṇya.samājīve //*  
*02.4.07/ vāstu.hṛdayād uttare nava.bhāge yathā.ukta.vidhānam antaḥpuraṁ prān.mukham udan.mukhaṁ vā kārayet //*  
*02.4.08/ tasya pūrva.uttaraṁ bhāgam ācārya.purohita.ijyā.toya.sthānaṁ mantriṇaś ca^āvaseyuḥ, pūrva.dakṣiṇaṁ bhāgm mahānasaṁ hasti.śālā koṣṭha.agāraṁ ca //*  
*02.4.09/ tataḥ paraṁ gandha.mālya.rasa.paṇyāḥ prasādhana.kāravaḥ kṣatriyāś ca pūrvāṁ diśam adhivaseyuḥ //*  
*02.4.10/ dakṣiṇa.pūrvaṁ bhāgaṁ bhāṇḍa.agāram akṣa.paṭalaṁ karma.niṣadyāś ca, dakṣiṇa.paścimaṁ bhāgaṁ kupya.gṛham āyudha.agāraṁ ca //*  
*02.4.11/ tataḥ paraṁ nagara.dhānya.vyāvahārika.kārmāntika.bala.adhyakṣāḥ pakva.anna.surā.māṁsa.paṇyā rūpājīvās tālāvacarā vaiśyāś ca dakṣiṇāṁ diśam adhivaseyuḥ //*  
*02.4.12/ paścima.dakṣiṇaṁ bhāgaṁ khara.uṣṭra.gupti.sthānaṁ karma.gṛhaṁ ca, paścima.uttaraṁ bhāgaṁ yāna.ratha.śālāḥ //*  
*02.4.13/ tataḥ param ūrṇā.sūtra.veṇu.carma.varma.śastra.āvaraṇa.kāravaḥ śūdrāś ca paścimāṁ diśam adhivaseyuḥ //*  
*02.4.14/ uttara.paścimaṁ bhāgaṁ paṇya.bhaiṣajya.gṛham, uttara.pūrvaṁ bhāgaṁ kośo gava.aśvaṁ ca //*  
*02.4.15/ tataḥ paraṁ nagara.rāja.devatā.loha.maṇi.kāravo brāhmaṇāś ca^uttarāṁ diśam adhivaseyuḥ //*  
*02.4.16/ vāstuc.chidra.anuśāleṣu śreṇī.prapaṇi.nikāyā āvaseyuḥ //*  
*02.4.17/ apara.ajita.apratihata.jayanta.vaijayanta.koṣṭhān śiva.vaiśravaṇa.aśvi.śrī.madirā.gṛhāṇi ca pura.madhye kārayet //*  
*02.4.18/ yathā.uddeśaṁ vāstu.devatāḥ sthāpayet //*  
*02.4.19/ brāhma.aindra.yāmya.saināpatyāni dvārāṇi //*  
*02.4.20/ bahiḥ parikhāyā dhanuḥ.śata.apakṛṣṭāś caitya.puṇya.sthāna.vana.setu.bandhāḥ kāryāḥ, yathā.diśaṁ ca dig.devatāḥ //*  
*02.4.21/ uttaraḥ pūrvo vā śmaśāna.bhāgo varṇa.uttamānām, dakṣiṇena śmaśānaṁ varṇa.avarāṇām //*  
*02.4.22/ tasya^atikrame pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.4.23/ pāṣaṇḍa.caṇḍālānāṁ śmaśāna.ante vāsaḥ //*  
*02.4.24/ karma.anta.kṣetra.vaśena kuṭumbināṁ sīmānaṁ sthāpayet //*  
*02.4.25/ teṣu puṣpa.phala.vāṭān dhānya.paṇya.nicayāṁś ca^anujñātāḥ kuryuḥ //*  
*02.4.26/ daśa.kulī.vāṭaṁ kūpa.sthānam //*  
*02.4.27/ sarva.sneha.dhānya.kṣāra.lavaṇa.gandha.bhaiṣajya.śuṣka.śāka.yavasa.vallūra.tṛṇa.kāṣṭha.loha.carma.aṅgāra.snāyu.viṣa.viṣāṇa.veṇu.valkala.sāra.dāru.praharaṇa.āvaraṇa.aśma.nicayān aneka.varṣa.upabhoga.sahān kārayet //*  
*02.4.28/ navena^anavaṁ śodhayet //*  
*02.4.29/ hasti.aśva.ratha.pādātam aneka.mukhyam avasthāpayet //*  
*02.4.30/ aneka.mukhyaṁ hi paraspara.bhayāt para.upajāpaṁ na^upaiti //*  
*02.4.31/ etena^anta.pāla.durga.saṁskārā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*02.4.32ab/ na ca bāhirikān kuryāt pure rāṣṭra.upaghātakān /\[ś\]*  
*02.4.32cd/ kṣipej jana.pade ca^etān sarvān vā dāpayet karān //\[ś\] E*  
*(ṭhe work of store-keeping by the director of stories)*  
*02.5.01/ samnidhātā kośa.gṛhaṁ paṇya.gṛhaṁ koṣṭha.agāraṁ kupya.gṛham āyudha.agāraṁ bandhana.agāraṁ ca kārayet //*  
*02.5.02/ catur.aśrāṁ vāpīm an-udaka.upasnehāṁ khānayitvā pṛthu.śilābhir ubhayataḥ pārśvaṁ mūlaṁ ca pracitya sāra.dāru.pañjaraṁ bhūmi.samaṁ tri.talam aneka.vidhānaṁ kuṭṭima.deśa.sthāna.talam eka.dvāraṁ yantra.yukta.sopānaṁ bhūmi.gṛhaṁ kārayet //*  
*02.5.03/ tasya^upari^ubhayato.niṣedhaṁ sa-pragrīvam aiṣṭakaṁ bhāṇḍa.vāhinī.parikṣiptaṁ kośa.gṛhaṁ kārayet, prāsādaṁ vā //*  
*02.5.04/ jana.pada.ante dhruva.nidhim āpad.artham abhityaktaiḥ kārayet //*  
*02.5.05a/ pakva.iṣṭakā.stambhaṁ catuḥ.śālam eka.dvāram aneka.sthāna.talaṁ vivṛta.stambha.apasāram ubhayataḥ paṇya.gṛhaṁ koṣṭha.agāraṁ ca -*  
*02.5.05b/ dīrgha.bahu.śālaṁ kakṣya.āvṛta.kuḍyam antaḥ kupya.gṛham, tad eva bhūmi.gṛha.yuktam āyudha.agāraṁ -*  
*02.5.05c/ pṛthag.dharma.sthīyaṁ mahā.mātrīyaṁ vibhakta.strī.puruṣa.sthānam apasārataḥ sugupta.kakṣyaṁ bandhana.agāraṁ kārayet //*  
*02.5.06/ sarveṣāṁ śālāḥ khāta.uda.pāna.varca.snāna.gṛha.agni.viṣa.trāṇa.mārjāra.nakula.ārakṣā.sva.daivata.pūjana.yuktāḥ kārayet //*  
*02.5.07/ koṣṭha.agāre varṣamānam aratni.mukhaṁ kuṇḍaṁ sthāpayet //*  
*02.5.08/ tat.jāta.karaṇa.adhiṣṭhitaḥ purāṇaṁ navaṁ ca ratnaṁ sāraṁ phalgu kupyaṁ vā pratigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*02.5.09/ tatra ratna.upadhāv uttamo daṇḍaḥ kartuḥ kārayituś ca sāra.upadhau madhyamaḥ, phalgu.kupya.upadhau tat^ ca tāvat^ ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.5.10/ rūpa.darśaka.viśuddhaṁ hiraṇyaṁ pratigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*02.5.11/ aśuddhaṁ chedayet //*  
*02.5.12/ āhartuḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.5.13/ śuddhaṁ pūrṇam abhinavaṁ ca dhānyaṁ pratigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*02.5.14/ viparyaye mūlya.dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.5.15/ tena paṇyaṁ kupyam āyudhaṁ ca vyākhyātam //*  
*02.5.16/ sarva.adhikaraṇeṣu yukta.upayukta.tatpuruṣāṇāṁ paṇa.ādi.catuṣ.paṇa.parama.apahāreṣu pūrva.madhyama.uttama.vadhā daṇḍāḥ //*  
*02.5.17/ kośa.adhiṣṭhitasya kośa.avacchede ghātaḥ //*  
*02.5.18/ tad.vaiyāvṛtya.karāṇām ardha.daṇḍāḥ //*  
*02.5.19/ paribhāṣaṇam avijñāte //*  
*02.5.20/ corāṇām abhipradharṣaṇe citro ghātaḥ //*  
*02.5.21/ tasmād āpta.puruWa.adhiṣṭhitaḥ samnidhātā nicayān anutiṣṭhet //02.5.22a/ bāhyam abhyantaraṁ cāyaṁ vidyād varṣa.śatād api / \[ś\]*  
*02.5.22b/ yathā pṛṣṭo na sajjeta vyaye śeṣe ca saṁcaye // \[ś\] E*  
*Chapter 6 section24 ṭhe settiṅg up of revenue by the administration*  
*02.6.01/ samāhartā durgaṁ rāṣṭraṁ khaniṁ setuṁ vanaṁ vrajaṁ vaṇik.pathaṁ ca^avekṣeta //*  
*02.6.02/ śulkaṁ daṇḍaḥ pautavaṁ nāgariko lakṣaṇa.adhyakṣo mudrā.adhyakṣaḥ surā sūnā sūtraṁ tailaṁ ghṛtaṁ kṣāraḥ sauvarṇikaḥ paṇya.saṁsthā veśyā dyūtaṁ vāstukaṁ kāru.śilpi.gaṇo devatā.adhyakṣo dvāra.bahirikā.ādeyaṁ ca durgam //*  
*02.6.03/ sītā bhāgo baliḥ karo vaṇik nadī.pālas taro nāvaḥ pattanaṁ vivicitaṁ vartanī rajjuś cora.rajjuś ca rāṣṭram //*  
*02.6.04/ suvarṇa.rajata.vajra.maṇi.muktā.pravāla.śaṅkha.loha.lavaṇa.bhūmi.prastara.rasa.dhātavaḥ khaniḥ //*  
*02.6.05/ puṣpa.phala.vāṭa.ṣaṇḍa.kedāra.mūla.vāpāḥ setuḥ //*  
*02.6.06/ paśu.mṛga.dravya.hasti.vana.parigraho vanam //*  
*02.6.07/ go.mahiṣam aja.avikaṁ khara.uṣtram aśva.aśvataraṁ ca vrajaḥ //*  
*02.6.08/ sthala.patho vāri.pathaś ca vaṇik.pathaḥ //*  
*02.6.09/ ity āya.śarīram //*  
*02.6.10/ mūlyaṁ bhāgo vyājī parighaḥ klptam(klṛptam) rūpikam atyayaś ca^āya.mukham //*  
*02.6.11/ deva.pitṛ.pūjā.dāna.artham, svasti.vācanam, antaḥpuram, mahānasam, dūta.prāvartimam, koṣṭha.agāram, āyudha.agāram, paṇya.gṛham, kupya.gṛham, karma.anto, viṣṭiḥ, patti.aśva.ratha.dvipa.parigraho, go.maṇḍalam, paśu.mṛga.pakṣi.vyāla.vāṭāḥ, kāṣṭha.tṛṇa.vāṭāś ca^iti vyaya.śarīram //*  
*02.6.12/ rāja.varṣaṁ māsaḥ pakṣo divasaś ca vyuṣṭam, varṣā.hemanta.grīṣmāṇāṁ tṛtīya.saptamā divasa.ūnāḥ pakṣāḥ śeṣāḥ pūrṇāḥ, pṛthag.adhimāsakaḥ, iti kālaḥ //*  
*02.6.13/ karaṇīyaṁ siddhaṁ śeṣam āya.vyayau nīvī ca //*  
*02.6.14/ saṁsthānaṁ pracāraḥ śarīra.avasthāpanam ādānaṁ sarva.samudaya.piṇḍaḥ saṁjātaṁ - etat karaṇīyam //*  
*02.6.15/ kośa.arpitaṁ rāja.hāraḥ pura.vyayaś ca praviṣṭaṁ parama.saṁvatsara.anuvṛttaṁ śāsana.muktaṁ mukha.ājñaptaṁ ca^apātanīyaṁ - etat siddham //*  
*02.6.16/ siddhi.karma.yogaḥ daṇḍa.śeṣam āharaṇīyaṁ balāt.kṛta.pratiṣṭabdham avamṛṣṭaṁ ca praśodhyaṁ - etat^śeṣam, asāram alpa.sāraṁ ca //*  
*02.6.17/ vartamānaḥ paryuṣito^anya.jātaś ca^āyaḥ //*  
*02.6.18/ divasa.anuvṛtto vartamānaḥ //*  
*02.6.19/ parama.sāṁvatsarikaḥ para.pracāra.saṁkrānto vā paryuṣitaḥ //*  
*02.6.20/ naṣṭa.prasmṛtam āyukta.daṇḍaḥ pārśvaṁ pārihīṇikam aupāyanikaṁ ḍamara.gataka.svam aputrakaṁ nidhiś ca^anya.jātaḥ //*  
*02.6.21/ vikṣepa.vyādhita.antara.ārambha.śeṣaṁ ca vyaya.pratyāyaḥ //*  
*02.6.22/ vikriye paṇyānām argha.vṛddhir upajā, māna.unmāna.viśeṣo vyājī, kraya.saṁgharṣe vārdha.vṛddhiḥ - ity āyaḥ //*  
*02.6.23/ nityo nitya.utpādiko lābho lābha.utpādika iti vyayaḥ //*  
*02.6.24/ divasa.anuvṛtto nityaḥ //*  
*02.6.25/ pakṣa.māsa.saṁvatsara.lābho lābhaḥ //*  
*02.6.26/ tayor utpanno nitya.utpādiko lābha.utpādika iti vyayaḥ //*  
*02.6.27/ saṁjātād āya.vyaya.viśuddhā nīvī, prāptā ca^anuvṛttā ca //*  
*02.6.28ab/ evaṁ kuryāt samudayaṁ vṛddhiṁ ca^āyasya darśayet /\[ś\]*  
*02.6.28cd/ hrāsaṁ vyayasya ca prājñaḥ sādhayec ca viparyayam //\[ś\] E*  
*(ṭhe topic of accounts in the records and audit office)*  
*02.7.01/ akṣa.paṭalam adhyakṣaḥ prān.mukham udan.mukhaṁ vā vibhakta.upasthānaṁ nibandha.pustaka.sthānaṁ kārayet //*  
*02.7.02/ tatra^adhikaraṇānāṁ saṁkhyā.pracāra.saṁjāta.agram, karma.antānāṁ dravya.prayoga.vṛddhi.kṣaya.vyaya.prayāma.vyājī.yoga.sthāna.vetana.viṣṭi.pramāṇam, ratna.sāra.phalgu.kupyānām argha.prativarṇaka.māna.pratimāna.unmāna.avamāna.bhāṇḍam, deśa.grāma.jāti.kula.saṁghānāṁ dharma.vyavahāra.caritra.saṁsthānam, rāja.upajīvināṁ*  
*pragraha.pradeśa.bhoga.parihāra.bhakta.vetana.lābham, rājñaś ca patnī.putrāṇāṁ ratna.bhūmi.lābhaṁ nirdeśa.utpātika.pratīkāra.lābham, mitra.amitrāṇāṁ ca saṁdhi.vigraha.pradāna.ādānaṁ nibandha.pustakasthaṁ kārayet //*  
*02.7.03/ tataḥ sarva.adhikaraṇānāṁ karaṇīyaṁ siddhaṁ śeṣam āya.vyayau nīvīm upasthānaṁ pracāraṁ caritraṁ saṁsthānaṁ ca nibandhena prayacchet //*  
*02.7.04/ uttama.madhyama.avareṣu ca karmasu taj.jātikam adhyakṣaṁ kuryāt, sāmudayikeṣv avaklṛptikam(avakḷptikam) yam upahatya rājā na^anutapyeta //*  
*02.7.05/ sahagrāhiṇaḥ pratibhuvaḥ karma.upajīvinaḥ putrā bhrātaro bhāryā duhitaro bhṛtyāś ca^asya karmac.chedaṁ vaheyuḥ //*  
*02.7.06/ tri.śataṁ catuḥ.pañcāśat^ ca^ahorātrāṇāṁ karma.saṁvatsaraḥ //*  
*02.7.07/ tam āṣāḍhī.paryavasānam ūnaṁ pūrṇaṁ vā dadyāt //*  
*02.7.08/ karaṇa.adhiṣṭhitam adhimāsakaṁ kuryāt //*  
*02.7.09/ apasarpa.adhiṣṭhitaṁca pracāram //*  
*02.7.10/ pracāra.caritra.saṁsthānāny anupalabhamāno hi prakṛtaḥ samudayam ajñānena parihāpayati, utthāna.kleśa.asahatvād ālasyena, śabdādiṣv indriya.artheṣu prasaktaḥ pramādena, saṁkrośa.adharma.anartha.bhīru.bhāyena, kārya.arthiṣv anugraha.buddhiḥ kāmena, hiṁsā.buddhiḥ kopena, vidyā.dravya.vallabha.apāśrayād darpeṇa, tulā.māna.tarka.gaṇita.antara.upadhānāt^*  
*lobhena //*  
*02.7.11/ "teṣām ānupūrvyā yāvān artha.upaghātas tāvān eka.uttaro daṇḍaḥ" iti mānavāḥ //*  
*02.7.12/ "sarvatra^aṣṭa.guṇaḥ" iti pārāśarāḥ //*  
*02.7.13/ "daśa.guṇaḥ" iti bārhaspatyāḥ //*  
*02.7.14/ "viṁśati.guṇaḥ" ity auśanasāḥ //*  
*02.7.15/ yathā.aparādham iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*02.7.16/ gāṇanikyāni āṣāḍhīm āgaccheyuḥ //*  
*02.7.17/ āgatānāṁ samudra.pustaka.bhāṇḍa.nīvīkānām ekatra.asambhāṣā.avarodhaṁ kārayet //*  
*02.7.18/ āya.vyaya.nīvīnām agrāṇi śrutvā nīvīm avahārayet //*  
*02.7.19/ yac ca^agrād āyasya^antara.parṇe nīvyāṁ vardheta vyayasya vā yat parihāpayet, tad aṣṭa.guṇam adhyakṣaṁ dāpayet //*  
*02.7.20/ viparyaye tam eva prati syāt //*  
*02.7.21/ yathā.kālam anāgatānām apustaka.bhāṇḍa.nīvīkānāṁ vā deya.daśa.bandho daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.7.22/ kārmike ca^upasthite kāraṇikasya^apratibadhnataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.7.23/ viparyaye kārmikasya dvi.guṇaḥ //*  
*02.7.24/ pracāra.samaṁ mahā.mātrāḥ samagrāḥ śrāvayeyur aviṣama.mantrāḥ //*  
*02.7.25/ pṛthag.bhūto mithyā.vādī ca^eṣām uttamaṁ daṇḍaṁ dadyāt //*  
*02.7.26/ akṛta.aho.rūpa.haraṁ māsam ākāṅkṣeta //*  
*02.7.27/ māsād ūrdhvaṁ māsa.dviśata.uttaraṁ daṇḍaṁ dadyāt //*  
*02.7.28/ alpa.śeṣa.lekhya.nīvīkaṁ pañca.rātram ākāṅkṣeta //*  
*02.7.29/ tataḥ paraṁ kośa.pūrvam aho.rūpa.haraṁ dharma.vyavahāra.caritra.saṁsthāna.saṁkalana.nirvartana.anumāna.cāra.prayogair avekṣeta //*  
*02.7.30/ divasa.pañca.rātra.pakṣa.māsa.cāturmāsya.saṁvatsaraiś ca pratisamānayet //*  
*02.7.31/ vyuṣṭa.deśa.kāla.mukha.utpatti.anuvṛtti.pramāṇa.dāyaka.dāpaka.nibandhaka.pratigrāhakaiś ca^ayaṁ samānayet //*  
*02.7.32/ vyuṣṭa.deśa.kāla.mukha.lābha.kāraṇa.deya.yoga.pramāṇa.ājñāpaka.uddhāraka.vidhātṛka.pratigrāhakaiś ca vyayaṁ samānayet //*  
*02.7.33/ vyuṣṭa.deśa.kāla.mukha.anuvartana.rūpa.lakṣaṇa.pramāṇa.nikṣepa.bhājana.gopāyakaiś ca nīvīṁ samānayet //*  
*02.7.34/ rāja.arthe kāraṇikasya^apratibadhnataḥ pratiṣedhayato vā^ājñāṁ nibandhād āya.vyayam anyathā nīvīm avalikhato dvi.guṇaḥ //*  
*02.7.35/ krama.avahīnam utkramam avijñātaṁ punar.uktaṁ vā vastukam avalikhato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.7.36/ nīvīm avalikhato dvi.guṇaḥ //*  
*02.7.37/ bhakṣayato^aṣṭa.guṇaḥ //*  
*02.7.38/ nāśayataḥ pañca.bandhaḥ pratidānaṁ ca //*  
*02.7.39/ mithyā.vāde steya.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.7.40/ paścāt.pratijñāte dvi.guṇaḥ, prasmṛta.utpanne ca //*  
*02.7.41ab/ aparādhaṁ saheta^alpaṁ tuṣyed alpe^api ca^udaye / \[ś\]*  
*02.7.41cd/ mahā.upakāraṁ ca^adhyakṣaṁ pragraheṇa^abhipūjayet //\[ś\] E*  
*(ṛecovery of revenue misappropriated by state employees)*  
*02.8.01/ kośa.pūrvāḥ sarva.ārambhāḥ //*  
*02.8.02/ tasmāt pūrvaṁ kośam avekṣeta //*  
*02.8.03/ pracāra.samṛddhiś caritra.anugrahaś cora.nigraho yukta.pratiṣedhaḥ sasya.sampat paṇya.bāhulyam upasarga.pramokṣaḥ parihāra.kṣayo hiraṇya.upāyanam iti kośa.vṛddhiḥ //*  
*02.8.04/ pratibandhaḥ prayogo vyavahāro^avastāraḥ parihāpaṇam upabhogaḥ parivartanam apahāraś ca^iti kośa.kṣayaḥ //*  
*02.8.05/ siddhīnām asādhanam anavatāraṇam apraveśanaṁ vā pratibandhaḥ //*  
*02.8.06/ tatra daśa.bandho daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.8.07/ kośa.dravyāṇāṁ vṛddhi.prayogāḥ prayogaḥ //*  
*02.8.08/ paṇya.vyavahāro vyavahāraḥ //*  
*02.8.09/ tatra phala.dvi.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.8.10/ siddhaṁ kālam aprāptaṁ karoti^ aprāptaṁ prāptaṁ vā^ity avastāraḥ //*  
*02.8.11/ tatra pañca.bandho daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.8.12/ klṛptam(kḷptam) āyaṁ parihāpayati vyayaṁ vā vivardhayati^iti parihāpaṇam //*  
*02.8.13/ tatra hīna.catur.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.8.14/ svayam anyair vā rāja.dravyāṇām upabhojanam upabhogaḥ //*  
*02.8.15/ tatra ratna.upabhoge ghātaḥ, sāra.upabhoge madhyamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, phalgu.kupya.upabhoge tac ca tāvat^ ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.8.16/ rāja.dravyāṇām anya.dravyena^ādānaṁ parivartanam //*  
*02.8.17/ tad upabhogena vyākhyātam //*  
*02.8.18/ siddham āyaṁ na praveśayati, nibaddhaṁ vyayaṁ na prayacchati, prāptāṁ nīvīṁ vipratijānīta ity apahāraḥ //*  
*02.8.19/ tatra dvādaśa.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.8.20/ teṣāṁ haraṇa.upāyāś catvāriṁśat //*  
*02.8.21a/ pūrvaṁ siddhaṁ paścād avatāritam, paścāt siddhaṁ pūrvam avatāritam, sādhyaṁ na siddham, asādhyaṁ siddham, siddham asiddhaṁ kṛtam, asiddhaṁ siddhaṁ kṛtam, alpa.siddhaṁ bahu kṛtam, bahu.siddham alpaṁ kṛtam, anyat siddham anyat kṛtam, anyataḥ siddham anyataḥ kṛtam,-*  
*02.8.21b/ deyaṁ na dattam, adeyaṁ dattam, kāle na dattam, akāle dattam, alpaṁ dattaṁ bahu kṛtam, bahu dattam alpaṁ kṛtam, anyad dattam anyat kṛtam, anyato dattam anyataḥ kṛtam,-*  
*02.8.21c/ praviṣṭam apraviṣṭaṁ kṛtam, apraviṣṭaṁ praviṣṭaṁ kṛtam, kupyam adatta.mūlyaṁ praviṣṭam, datta.mūlyaṁ na praviṣṭaṁ -*  
*02.8.21d/ saṁkṣepo vikṣepaḥ kṛtaḥ, vikṣepaḥ saṁkṣepo vā, mahā.argham alpa.argheṇa parivartitam, alpa.arghaṁ mahā.argheṇa vā -*  
*02.8.21e/ samāropito^arghaḥ, pratyavaropito vā, saṁvatsaro māsa.viṣamaḥ kṛtaḥ, māso divasa.viṣamo vā, samāgama.viṣamaḥ, mukha.viṣamaḥ, kārmika.viṣamaḥ -*  
*02.8.21f/ nirvartana.viṣamaḥ, piṇḍa.viṣamaḥ, varṇa.viṣamaḥ, argha.viṣamaḥ, māna.viṣamaḥ, māpana.viṣamaḥ, bhājana.viṣamaḥ - iti haraṇa.upāyāḥ // -*  
*02.8.22/ tatra^upayukta.nidhāyaka.nibandhaka.pratigrāhaka.dāyaka.dāpaka.mantri.vaiyāvṛtya.karān eka.ekaśo^anuyuñjīta //*  
*02.8.23/ mithyā.vāde ca^eṣāṁ yukta.samo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.8.24/ pracāre ca^avaghoṣayet "amunā prakṛtena^upahatāḥ prajñāpayantu" iti //*  
*02.8.25/ prajñāpayato yathā.upaghātaṁ dāpayet //*  
*02.8.26/ anekeṣu ca^abhiyogeṣv apavyayamānaḥ sakṛd eva para.uktaḥ sarvaṁ bhajeta //*  
*02.8.27/ vaiṣamye sarvatra^anuyogaṁ dadyāt //*  
*02.8.28/ mahaty artha.apahāre ca^alpena^api siddhaḥ sarvaṁ bhajeta //*  
*02.8.29/ kṛta.pratighāta.avasthaḥ sūcako niṣpanna.arthaḥ ṣaṣṭham aṁśaṁ labheta, dvādaśam aṁśaṁ bhṛtakaḥ //*  
*02.8.30/ prabhūta.abhiyogād alpa.niṣpattau niṣpannasya^aṁśaṁ labheta //*  
*02.8.31/ aniṣpanne śārīraṁ hairaṇyaṁ vā daṇḍaṁ labheta, na ca^anugrāhyaḥ //*  
*02.8.32ab/ niṣpattau nikṣiped vādam ātmānaṁ vā^apavāhayet /\[ś\]*  
*02.8.32cd/ abhiyukta.upajāpāt tu sūcako vadham āpnuyāt //\[ś\] E*  
*(Inspection of the ṛork of officers)*  
*02.9.01/ amātya.sampadā^upetāḥ sarva.adhyakṣāḥ śaktitaḥ karmasu niyojyāḥ //*  
*02.9.02/ karmasu ca^eṣāṁ nityaṁ parīkṣāṁ kārayet, citta.anityatvāt^ manuṣyānām //*  
*02.9.03/ aśva.sadharmāṇo hi manuṣyā niyuktāḥ karmasu vikurvate //*  
*02.9.04/ tasmāt kartāraṁ karaṇaṁ deśaṁ kālaṁ kāryaṁ prakṣepam udayaṁ ca^eṣu vidyāt //*  
*02.9.05/ te yathā.saṁdeśam asaṁhatā avigṛhītāḥ karmāṇi kuryuḥ //*  
*02.9.06/ saṁhatā bhakṣayeyuḥ, vigṛhītā vināśayeyuḥ //*  
*02.9.07/ na ca^anivedya bhartuḥ kaṁcid ārambhaṁ kuryuḥ, anyatra^āpat.pratīkārebhyaḥ //*  
*02.9.08/ pramāda.sthāneṣu ca^eṣām atyayaṁ sthāpayed divasa.vetana.vyaya.dvi.guṇam //*  
*02.9.09/ yaś ca^eṣāṁ yathā.ādiṣṭam arthaṁ saviśeṣaṁ vā karoti sa sthāna.mānau labheta //*  
*02.9.10/ "alpa.āyatiś cet^ mahā.vyayo bhakṣayati //*  
*02.9.11/ viparyaye yathā.āyati.vyayaś ca na bhakṣayati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*02.9.12/ apasarpeṇa^eva^upalabhyeta^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*02.9.13/ yaḥ samudayaṁ parihāpayati sa rāja.arthaṁ bhakṣayati //*  
*02.9.14/ sa ced ajñāna.ādibhiḥ parihāpayati tad enaṁ yathā.guṇaṁ dāpayet //*  
*02.9.15/ yaḥ samudayaṁ dvi.guṇam udbhāvayati sa jana.padaṁ bhakṣayati //*  
*02.9.16/ sa ced rāja.artham upanayaty alpa.aparādhe vārayitavyaḥ, mahati yathā.aparādhaṁ daṇḍayitavyaḥ //*  
*02.9.17/ yaḥ samudayaṁ vyayam upanayati sa puruṣa.karmāṇi bhakṣayati //*  
*02.9.18/ sa karma.divasa.dravya.mūlya.puruṣa.vetana.apahāreṣu yathā.aparādhaṁ daṇḍayitavyaḥ //*  
*02.9.19/ tasmād asya yo yasminn adhikaraṇe śāsanasthaḥ sa tasya karmaṇo yāthātathyam āya.vyayau ca vyāsa.samāsābhyām ācakṣīta //*  
*02.9.20/ mūla.hara.tādātvika.kadaryāṁś ca pratiṣedhayet //*  
*02.9.21/ yaḥ pitṛ.paitāmaham artham anyāyena bhakṣayati sa mūla.haraḥ //*  
*02.9.22/ yo yad yad utpadyate tat tad bhakṣayati sa tādātvikaḥ //*  
*02.9.23/ yo bhṛtya.ātma.pīḍābhyām upacinoty arthaṁ sa kadaryaḥ //*  
*02.9.24/ sa pakṣavāṁś ced anādeyaḥ, viparyaye paryādātavyaḥ //*  
*02.9.25/ yo mahaty artha.samudaye sthitaḥ kadaryaḥ samnidhatte^avanidhatte^avasrāvayati vā - samnidhatte sva.veśmani, avanidhatte paura.jānapadeṣu, avasrāvayati para.viṣaye - tasya sattrī mantri.mitra.bhṛtya.bandhu.pakṣam āgatiṁ gatiṁ ca dravyāṇām upalabheta //*  
*02.9.26/ yaś ca^asya para.viṣaye saṁcāraṁ kuryāt tam anupraviśya mantraṁ vidyāt //*  
*02.9.27/ suvidite śatru.śāsana.apadeśena^enaṁ ghātayet //*  
*02.9.28/ tasmād asya^adhyakṣāḥ saṁkhyāyaka.lekhaka.rūpa.darśaka.nīvī.grāhaka.uttara.adhyakṣa.sakhāḥ karmaṇi kuryuḥ //*  
*02.9.29/ uttara.adhyakṣā hasti.aśva.ratha.ārohāḥ //*  
*02.9.30/ teṣām antevāsinaḥ śilpa.śauca.yuktāḥ saṁkhyāyaka.ādīnām apasarpāḥ //*  
*02.9.31/ bahu.mukhyam anityaṁ ca^adhikaraṇaṁ sthāpayet //*  
*02.9.32ab/ yathā hy anāsvādayituṁ na śakyaṁ jihvā.talasthaṁ madhu7 vā viṣaṁ vā / \[ś\]*  
*02.9.32cd/ arthas tathā hy artha.careṇa rājñaḥ svalpo^apy anāsvādayituṁ na śakyaḥ //\[ś\]*  
*02.9.33ab/ matsyā yathā^antaḥ salile caranto jñātuṁ na śakyāḥ salilaṁ pibantaḥ /\[ś\]*  
*02.9.33cd/ yuktās tathā kārya.vidhau niyuktā jñātuṁ na śakyā dhanam ādadānāḥ //\[ś\]*  
*02.9.34ab/ api śakyā gatir jñātuṁ patatāṁ khe patatriṇām /\[ś\]*  
*02.9.34cd/ na tu pracchanna.bhāvānāṁ yuktānāṁ caratāṁ gatiḥ //\[ś\]*  
*02.9.35ab/ āsrāvayec ca^upacitān viparyasyec ca karmasu /\[ś\]*  
*02.9.35cd/ yathā na bhakṣayanty arthaṁ bhakṣitaṁ nirvamanti vā //\[ś\]*  
*02.9.36ab/ na bhakṣayanti ye tv arthān nyāyato vardhayanti ca /\[ś\]*  
*02.9.36cd/ nitya.adhikārāḥ kāryās te rājñaḥ priya.hite ratāḥ //\[ś\] E*  
*(On edicts)*  
*ArrayAZ02.10.01/ śāsane śāsanam ity ācakṣate //*  
*02.10.02/ śāsana.pradhānā hi rājānaḥ, tan.mūlatvāt saṁdhi.vigrahayoḥ //*  
*02.10.03/ tasmād amātya.sampadā^upetaḥ sarva.samayavid āśu.granthaś cāru.akṣaro lekhana.vācana.samartho lekhakaḥ syāt //*  
*02.10.04/ so^avyagra.manā rājñaḥ saṁdeśaṁ śrutvā niścita.arthaṁ lekhaṁ vidadhyāt deśa.aiśvarya.vaṁśa.nāmadheya.upacāram īśvarasya, deśa.nāmadheya.upacāram anīśvarasya //*  
*02.10.05ab/ jātiṁ kulaṁ sthāna.vayaḥ.śrutāni karma.ṛddhi.śīlāny atha deśa.kālau /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.05cd/ yauna.anubandhaṁ ca samīkṣya kārye lekhaṁ vidadhyāt puruṣa.anurūpam //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.06/ artha.kramaḥ sambandhaḥ paripūrṇatā mādhuryam audāryaṁ spaṣṭatvam iti lekha.sampat //*  
*02.10.07/ tatra yathāvad anupūrva.kriyā pradhānasya^arthasya pūrvam abhiniveśa ity artha.kramaḥ //*  
*02.10.08/ prastutasya^arthasya^anuparodhād uttarasya vidhānam ā.samāpter iti sambandhaḥ //*  
*02.10.09/ artha.pada.akṣarāṇām anyūna.atiriktatā hetu.udāharaṇa.dṛṣṭāntair artha.upavarṇanā^aśrānta.padatā^iti paripūrṇatā //*  
*02.10.10/ sukha.upanīta.cāru.artha.śabda.abhidhānaṁ mādhuryam //*  
*02.10.11/ agrāmya.śabda.abhidhānam audāryam //*  
*02.10.12/ pratīta.śabda.prayogaḥ spaṣṭatvam iti //*  
*02.10.13/ a.kāra.ādayo varṇās triṣaṣṭiḥ //*  
*02.10.14/ varṇa.saṁghātaḥ padam //*  
*02.10.15/ tac caturvidhaṁ nāma.ākhyāta.upasarga.nipātāś ca^iti //*  
*02.10.16/ tatra nāma sattva.abhidhāyi //*  
*02.10.17/ aviśiṣṭa.liṅgam ākhyātaṁ kriyā.vāci //*  
*02.10.18/ kriyā.viśeṣakāḥ pra.ādaya upasargāḥ //*  
*02.10.19/ avyayāś ca.ādayo nipātāḥ //*  
*02.10.20/ pada.samūho vākyam artha.parisamāptau //*  
*02.10.21/ eka.pada.avaras tri.pada.paraḥ para.pada.artha.anuparodhena vargaḥ kāryaḥ //*  
*02.10.22/ lekha.parisaṁharaṇa.artha iti.śabdo vācikam asya^iti ca //*  
*02.10.23ab/ nindā praśaṁsā pṛcchā ca tathā^ākhyānam atha^arthanā /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.23cd/ pratyākhyānam upālambhaḥ pratiṣedho^atha codanā //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.24ab/ sāntvam abhyupapattiś ca bhartsana.anunayau tathā /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.24cd/ eteṣv arthāḥ pravartante trayodaśasu lekhajāḥ //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.25/ tatra^abhijana.śarīra.karmaṇāṁ doṣa.vacanaṁ nindā //*  
*02.10.26/ guṇa.vacanam eteṣām eva praśaṁsā //*  
*02.10.27/ "katham etad" iti pṛcchā //*  
*02.10.28/ "evam" ity ākhyānam //*  
*02.10.29/ "dehi" ity arthanā //*  
*02.10.30/ "na prayacchāmi" iti pratyākhyānam //*  
*02.10.31/ "ananurūpaṁ bhavataḥ" ity upālambhaḥ //*  
*02.10.32/ "mā kārṣīḥ" iti pratiṣedhaḥ //*  
*02.10.33/ "idaṁ kriyatām" iti codanā //*  
*02.10.34/ "yo^ahaṁ sa bhavān, yan mama dravyaṁ tad bhavataḥ" ity upagrahaḥ sāntvam //*  
*02.10.35/ vyasana.sāhāyyam abhyupapattiḥ //*  
*02.10.36/ sadoṣam āyati.pradarśanam abhibhartsanam //*  
*02.10.37/ anunayas trividho^artha.kṛtāv atikrame puruṣa.ādi.vyasane ca^iti //*  
*02.10.38ab/ prajñāpana.ājñā.paridāna.lekhās tathā parīhāra.nisṛṣṭi.lekhau /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.38cd/ prāvṛttikaś ca pratilekha eva sarvatragaś ca^iti hi śāsanāni //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.39ab/ anena vijñāpitam evam āha tad dīyatāṁ ced yadi tattvam asti /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.39cd/ rājñaḥ samīpe vara.kāram āha prajñāpanā^eṣā vividhā^upadiṣṭā //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.40ab/ bhartur ājñā bhaved yatra nigraha.anugrahau prati /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.40cd/ viśeṣeṇa tu bhṛtyeṣu tad.ājñā.lekha.lakṣaṇam //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.41ab/ yathā.arha.guṇa.samyuktā pūjā yatra^upalakṣyate /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.41cd/ apy ādhau paridāne vā bhavatas tāv upagrahau //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.42ab/ jāter viśeṣeṣu pareṣu caiva grāmeṣu deśeṣu ca teṣu teṣu /\[ś\]*  
*02/10.42cd/ anugraho yo nṛpter nideśāt taj.jñaḥ parīhāra iti vyavasyet //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.43ab/ nisṛṣṭisthā^āpanā kārya.karaṇe vacane tathā /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.43cd/ eṣa vācika.lekhaḥ syād bhaven naisṛṣṭiko^api vā //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.44ab/ vividhāṁ daiva.samyuktāṁ tattvajāṁ caiva mānuṣīm /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.44cd/ dvi.vidhāṁ tāṁ vyavasyanti pravṛttiṁ śāsanaṁ prati //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.45ab/ dṛṣṭvā lekhaṁ yathā.tattvaṁ tataḥ pratyanubhāṣya ca /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.45cd/ pratilekho bhavet kāryo yathā rāja.vacas tathā //\[ś\]*  
*02.10.46ab/ yatra^īśvarāṁś ca^adhikṛtāṁś ca rājā rakṣā.upakārau pathika.artham āha /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.46cd/ sarvatrago nāma bhavet sa mārge deśe ca sarvatra ca veditavyaḥ //*  
*02.10.47/ upāyāḥ sāma.upapradāna.bheda.daṇḍāḥ //*  
*02.10.48/ tatra sāma pañcavidhaṁ - guṇa.saṁkīrtanam, sambandha.upākhyānam, paraspara.upakāra.saṁdarśanam, āyati.pradarśanam, ātma.upanidhānam iti //*  
*02.10.49/ tatra^abhijana.śarīra.karma.prakṛti.śruta.dravya.ādīnāṁ guṇa.grahaṇaṁ praśaṁsā stutir guṇa.saṁkīrtanam //*  
*02.10.50/ jñāti.yauna.maukha.srauva.kula.hṛdaya.mitra.saṁkīrtanaṁ sambandha.upākhyānam //*  
*02.10.51/ sva.pakṣa.para.pakṣayor anyonya.upakāra.saṁkīrtanaṁ paraspara.upakāra.saṁdarśanam //*  
*02.10.52/ "asminn evaṁ kṛta idam āvayor bhavati" ity āśā.jananam āyati.pradarśanam //*  
*02.10.53/ "yo^ahaṁ sa bhavān, yan mama dravyaṁ tad bhavatā sva.kṛtyeṣu prayojyatām" ity ātma.upanidhānam / iti //*  
*02.10.54/ upapradānam artha.upakāraḥ //*  
*02.10.55/ śaṅkā.jananaṁ nirbhartsanaṁ ca bhedaḥ //*  
*02.10.56/ vadhaḥ parikleśo^artha.haraṇaṁ daṇḍaḥ / iti //*  
*02.10.57/ akāntir vyāghātaḥ punar.uktam apaśabdaḥ samplava iti lekha.doṣaḥ //*  
*02.10.58/ tatra kāla.pattrakam acāru.viṣam avirāga.akṣaratvam akāntiḥ //*  
*02.10.59/ pūrveṇa paścimasya^anupapattir vyāghātaḥ //*  
*02.10.60/ uktasya^aviśeṣeṇa dvitīyam uccāraṇaṁ punar.uktam //*  
*02.10.61/ liṅga.vacana.kāla.kārakāṇām anyathā.prayogo^apaśabdaḥ //*  
*02.10.62/ avarge varga.karaṇaṁ ca^avarga.kriyā guṇa.viparyāsaḥ samplavaḥ / iti //*  
*02.10.63ab/ sarva.śāstrāṇy anukramya prayogam upalabhya ca /\[ś\]*  
*02.10.63cd/ kauṭilyena nara.indra.arthe śāsanasya vidhiḥ kṛtaḥ //\[ś\] E*  
*(Examination of the precious articles to be received into the treasury)*  
*02.11.01/ kośa.adhyakṣaḥ kośa.praveśyaṁ ratnaṁ sāraṁ phalguṁ kupyaṁ vā taj.jāta.karaṇa.adhiṣṭhitaḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt //*
