*02.12.27/ khany.adhyakṣaḥ śaṅkha.vajra.maṇi.muktā.pravāla.kṣāra.karma.antān kārayet, paṇana.vyavahāraṁ ca //*  
*02.12.28/ lavaṇa.adhyakṣaḥ pāka.muktaṁ lavaṇa.bhāgaṁ prakrayaṁ ca yathā.kālaṁ saṁgṛhṇīyād, vikrayāc ca mūlyaṁ rūpaṁ vyājīṁ ca //*  
*02.12.29/ āgantu.lavaṇaṁ ṣaḍ.bhāgaṁ dadyāt //*  
*02.12.30/ datta.bhāga.vibhāgasya vikrayaḥ, pañcakaṁ śataṁ vyājīṁ rūpaṁ rūpikaṁ ca //*  
*02.12.31/ kretā śulkaṁ rāja.paṇyac.cheda.anurūpaṁ ca vaidharaṇaṁ dadyāt, anyatra kretā ṣaṭ.chatam atyayaṁ ca //*  
*02.12.32/ vilavaṇam uttamaṁ daṇḍaṁ dadyād, aniṣṛṣṭa.upajīvī ca^anyatra vānaprasthebhyaḥ //*  
*02.12.33/ śrotriyās tapasvino viṣṭayaś ca bhakta.lavaṇaṁ hareyuḥ //*  
*02.12.34/ ato^anyo lavaṇa.kṣāra.vargaḥ śulkaṁ dadyāt //*  
*02.12.35ab/ evaṁ mūlyaṁ ca bhāgaṁ ca vyājīṁ parigham atyayam /*  
*02.12.35cd/ śulkaṁ vaidharaṇaṁ daṇḍaṁ rūpaṁ rūpikam eva ca //*  
*02.12.36ab/ khanibhyo dvādaśa.vidhaṁ dhātuṁ paṇyaṁ ca saṁharet /*  
*02.12.36cd/ evaṁ sarveṣu paṇyeṣu sthāpayen mukha.saṁgraham //*  
*02.12.37ab/ ākara.prabhaḥ kośaḥ kośād daṇḍaḥ prajāyate /*  
*02.12.37cd/ pṛthivī kośa.daṇḍābhyāṁ prāpyate kośa.bhūṣaṇā //E*  
*(ṣuperintendent of gold in the ṛorkshop)*  
*02.13.01/ suvarṇa.adhyakṣaḥ suvarṇa.rajata.karma.antānām asambandha.āveśana.catuḥ.śālām eka.dvārām akṣa.śālāṁ kārayet //*  
*02.13.02/ viśikhā.madhye sauvarṇikaṁ śilpavantam abhijātaṁ prātyayikaṁ ca sthāpayet //*  
*02.13.03/ jāmbūnadaṁ śātakumbhaṁ hāṭakaṁ vaiṇavaṁ śṛṅga.śuktijaṁ jāta.rūpaṁ rasa.viddham ākara.udgataṁ ca suvarṇam //*  
*02.13.04/ kiñjalka.varṇaṁ mṛdu snigdham anādi bhrājiṣṇu ca śreṣṭham, rakta.pītakaṁ madhyamam, raktam avaram //*  
*02.13.05/ śreṣṭhānāṁ pāṇḍu śvetaṁ ca^aprāptakam //*  
*02.13.06/ tad yena^aprāptakaṁ tac catur.guṇena sīsena śodhayet //*  
*02.13.07/ sīsa.anvayena bhidyamānaṁ śuṣka.paṭalair dhmāpayet //*  
*02.13.08/ rūkṣatvād bhidyamānaṁ taila.gomaye niṣecayet //*  
*02.13.09/ ākara.udgataṁ sīsa.anvayena bhidyamānaṁ pāka.pattrāṇi kṛtvā gaṇḍikāsu kuṭṭayet, kadalī.vajra.kanda.kalke vā niṣecayet //*  
*02.13.10/ tuttha.udgataṁ gauḍikaṁ kāmbukaṁ cākravālikaṁ ca rūpyam //*  
*02.13.11/ śvetaṁ snigdhaṁ mṛdu ca śreṣṭham //*  
*02.13.12/ viparyaye sphoṭanaṁ ca duṣṭam //*  
*02.13.13/ tat.sīsa.catur.bhāgena śodhayet //*  
*02.13.14/ udgata.cūlikam acchaṁ bhrājiṣṇu dadhi.varṇaṁ ca śuddham //*  
*02.13.15/ śuddhasya^eko hāridrasya suvarṇo varṇakaḥ //*  
*02.13.16/ tataḥ śulba.kākaṇy.uttara.apasāritā ā.catuḥ.sīma.antād iti ṣoḍaśa varṇakāḥ //*  
*02.13.17/ suvarṇaṁ pūrvaṁ nikaṣya paścād varṇikāṁ nikaṣayet //*  
*02.13.18/ sama.rāga.lekham animna.unnate deśe nikaṣitam, parimṛditaṁ parilīḍhaṁ nakha.antarād vā gairikeṇa.avacūrṇitam upadhiṁ vidyāt //*  
*02.13.19/ jāti.hiṅgulukena puṣpakā.sīsena vā go.mūtra.bhāvitena digdhena^agra.hastena saṁspṛṣṭaṁ suvarṇaṁ śvetī.bhavati //*  
*02.13.20/ sa-kesaraḥ snigdho mṛdur bhājiṣṇuś ca nikaṣa.rāgaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ //*  
*02.13.21/ kāliṅgakas tāpī.pāṣāṇo vā mudga.varṇo nikaṣaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ //*  
*02.13.22/ sama.rāgī vikraya.kraya.hitaḥ //*  
*02.13.23/ hastic.chavikaḥ saharitaḥ prati.rāgī vikraya.hitaḥ //*  
*02.13.24/ sthiraḥ paruṣo viṣama.varṇaś ca^apratirāgī kraya.hitaḥ //*  
*02.13.25/ chedaś cikkaṇaḥ sama.varṇaḥ ślakṣṇo mṛdur bhājiṣṇuś ca śreṣṭhaḥ //*  
*02.13.26/ tāpo bahir.antaś ca samaḥ kiñjalka.varṇaḥ kuraṇḍaka.puṣpa.varṇo vā śreṣṭhaḥ //*  
*02.13.27/ śyāvo nīlaś ca^aprāptakaḥ //*  
*02.13.28/ tulā.pratimānaṁ pautava.adhyakṣe vakṣyāmaḥ //*  
*02.13.29/ tena^upadeśena rūpya.suvarṇaṁ dadyād ādadīta ca //*  
*02.13.30/ akṣa.śālām anāyukto na^upagacchet //*  
*02.13.31/ abhigacchann ucchedyaḥ //*  
*02.13.32/ āyukto vā sarūpya.suvarṇas tena^eva jīyeta //*  
*02.13.33/ vicita.vastra.hasta.guhyāḥ kāñcana.pṛṣata.tvaṣṭṛ.tapanīya.kāravo dhmāyaka.caraka.pāṁsu.dhāvakāḥ praviśeyur niṣkaseyuś ca //*  
*02.13.34/ sarvaṁ ca^eṣām upakaraṇam aniṣṭhitāś ca prayogās tatra^eva^avatiṣṭheran //*  
*02.13.35/ gṛhītaṁ suvarṇaṁ dhṛtaṁ ca prayogaṁ karaṇa.madhye dadyāt //*  
*02.13.36/ sāyaṁ prātaś ca lakṣitaṁ kartṛ.kārayitṛ.mudrābhyāṁ nidadhyāt //*  
*02.13.37/ kṣepaṇo guṇaḥ kṣudrakam iti karmāṇi //*  
*02.13.38/ kṣepaṇaḥ kāca.arpaṇa.ādīni //*  
*02.13.39/ guṇaḥ sūtra.vāna.ādīni //*  
*02.13.40/ ghanaṁ suṣiraṁ pṛṣata.ādi.yuktaṁ kṣudrakam iti //*  
*02.13.41/ arpayet kāca.karmaṇaḥ pañca.bhāgaṁ kāñcanaṁ daśa.bhāgaṁ kaṭu.mānam //*  
*02.13.42/ tāmra.pāda.yuktaṁ rūpyaṁ rūpya.pāda.yuktaṁ vā suvarṇaṁ saṁskṛtakam, tasmād rakṣet //*  
*02.13.43/ pṛṣata.kāca.karmaṇaḥ trayo hi bhāgāḥ paribhāṇḍaṁ dvau vāstukam, catvāro vā vāstukaṁ trayaḥ paribhāṇḍam //*  
*02.13.44/ tvaṣṭṛ.karmaṇaḥ śulba.bhāṇḍaṁ sama.suvarṇena samyūhayet //*  
*02.13.45/ rūpya.bhāṇḍaṁ ghanaṁ suṣiraṁ vā suvarṇa.ardhena^avalepayet //*  
*02.13.46/ catur.bhāga.suvarṇaṁ vā vālukā.hiṅgulukasya rasena cūrṇena vā vāsayet /*  
*02.13.47/ tapanīyaṁ jyeṣṭhaṁ suvarṇaṁ surāgaṁ sama.sīsa.atikrāntaṁ pāka.pattra.pakvaṁ saindhavikayā^ujjvālitaṁ nīla.pīta.śveta.harita.śuka.pattra.varṇānāṁ prakṛtir bhavati //*  
*02.13.48/ tīkṣṇaṁ ca^asya mayūra.grīva.ābhaṁ śveta.bhaṅgaṁ cimicimāyitaṁ pīta.cūrṇitaṁ kākaṇikaḥ suvarṇa.rāgaḥ //*  
*02.13.49/ tāram upaśuddhaṁ vā - asthi.tutthe catuḥ sama.sīse catuḥ śuṣka.tutthe catuḥ kapāle trir gomaye dvir evaṁ sapta.daśa.tuttha.atikrāntaṁ saindhavikayā^ujjvālitam //*  
*02.13.50/ etasmāt kākaṇy.uttaramād vimāṣād iti suvarṇe deyam, paścād rāga.yogaḥ, śveta.tāraṁ bhavati /*  
*02.13.51/ trayo^aṁśās tapanīyasya dvātriṁśad.bhāga.śveta.tāram ūrcchitāḥ tat śveta.lohitakaṁ bhavati //*  
*02.13.52/ tāmraṁ pītakaṁ karoti //*  
*02.13.53/ tapanīyam ujjvālya rāga.tri.bhāgaṁ dadyāt, pīta.rāgaṁ bhavati //*  
*02.13.54/ śveta.tāra.bhāgau dvāv ekas tapanīyasya mudga.varṇaṁ karoti //*  
*02.13.55/ kāla.ayasasya^ardha.bhāga.abhyaktaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ bhavati //*  
*02.13.56/ pratilepinā rasena dvi.guṇa.abhyaktaṁ tapanīyaṁ śuka.pattra.varṇaṁ bhavati //*  
*02.13.57/ tasya.ārambhe rāga.viśeṣeṣu prativarṇikāṁ gṛhṇīyāt //*  
*02.13.58/ tīkṣṇa.tāmra.saṁskāraṁ ca budhyeta //*  
*02.13.59/ tasmād vajra.maṇi.muktā.pravāla.rūpāṇām apaneyi.mānaṁ ca rūpya.suvarṇa.bhāṇḍa.bandha.pramāṇāni ca //*  
*02.13.60ab/ sama.rāgaṁ sama.dvandvam asakta.pṛṣataṁ sthiram /*  
*02.13.60cd/ supramṛṣṭam asampītaṁ vibhaktaṁ dhāraṇe sukham //*  
*02.13.61ab/ abhinītaṁ prabhā.yuktaṁ saṁsthānam adhuraṁ samam /*  
*02.13.61cd/ mano.netra.abhirāmaṁ ca tapanīya.guṇāḥ smṛtāḥ //E*  
*(Activity of the goldsmith in the market-highṛay)*  
*02.14.01/ sauvarṇikaḥ paura.jāna.padānāṁ rūpya.suvarṇam āveśanibhiḥ kārayet //*  
*02.14.02/ nirdiṣṭa.kāla.kāryaṁ ca karma kuryuḥ, anirdiṣṭa.kālaṁ kārya.apadeśam //*  
*02.14.03/ kāryasya.anyathā.karaṇe vetana.nāśaḥ, tad.dvi.guṇaś ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.14.04/ kāla.atipātane pāda.hīnaṁ vetanaṁ tad.dvi.guṇaś ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.14.05/ yathā.varṇa.pramāṇaṁ nikṣepaṁ gṛhṇīyus tathā.vidham eva^arpayeyuḥ //*  
*02.14.06/ kāla.antarād api ca tathā.vidham eva pratigṛhṇīyuḥ, anyatra kṣīṇa.pariśīrṇābhyām //*  
*02.14.07/ āveśanibhiḥ suvarṇa.pudgala.lakṣaṇa.prayogeṣu tat.taj jānīyāt //*  
*02.14.08/ tapta.kala.dhautakayoḥ kākaṇikaḥ suvarṇe kṣayo deyaḥ //*  
*02.14.09/ tīkṣṇa.kākaṇī - rūpya.dvi.guṇaḥ - rāga.prakṣepaḥ, tasya ṣaḍ.bhāgaḥ kṣayaḥ //*  
*02.14.10/ varṇa.hīne māṣa.avare pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, pramāṇa.hīne madhyamaḥ, tulā.pratimāna.upadhāv uttamaḥ, kṛta.bhāṇḍa.upadhau ca //*  
*02.14.11/ sauvarṇikena^adṛṣṭam anyatra vā prayogaṁ kārayato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.14.12/ kartur dvi.guṇaḥ sa-apasāraś cet //*  
*02.14.13/ anapasāraḥ kaṇṭaka.śodhanāya nīyeta //*  
*02.14.14/ kartuś ca dvi.śato daṇḍaḥ paṇac.chedanaṁ vā //*  
*02.14.15/ tulā.pratimāna.bhāṇḍaṁ pautava.hastāt krīṇīyuḥ //*  
*02.14.16/ anyathā dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.14.17/ ghanaṁ suṣiraṁ samyūhyam avalepyaṁ saṁghātyaṁ vāsitakaṁ ca kāru.karma //*  
*02.14.18/ tulā.viṣamam apasāraṇaṁ visrāvaṇaṁ peṭakaḥ piṅkaś ca^iti haraṇa.upāyāḥ //*  
*02.14.19/ samnāminy utkīrṇikā bhinna.mastaka.upakaṇṭhī kuśikyā sakaṭu.kakṣyā parivelyā^ayas.kāntā ca duṣṭa.tulāḥ //*  
*02.14.20/ rūpyasya dvau bhāgāv ekaḥ śulbasya tripuṭakam //*  
*02.14.21/ tena^akarod gatam apasāryate tat.tripuṭaka.apasāritam //*  
*02.14.22/ śulbena śulba.apasāritam, vellakena vellaka.apasāritam, śulba.ardha.sāreṇa hemnā hema.apasāritam //*  
*02.14.23/ mūka.mūṣā pūti.kiṭṭaḥ karaṭuka.mukhaṁ nālī saṁdaṁśo joṅganī suvarcikā.lavaṇaṁ tad eva suvarṇam ity apasāraṇa.mārgāḥ //*  
*02.14.24/ pūrva.praṇihitā vā piṇḍa.vālukā mūṣā.bhedād agniṣṭhād uddhriyante //*  
*02.14.25/ paścād bandhane ācitaka.pattra.parīkṣāyāṁ vā rūpya.rūpeṇa parivartanaṁ visrāvaṇam, piṇḍa.vālukānāṁ loha.piṇḍa.vālukābhir vā //*  
*02.14.26/ gāḍhaś ca^abhyuddhāryaś ca peṭakaḥ samyūhya^avalepya.saṁghātyeṣu kriyate //*  
*02.14.27/ sīsa.rūpaṁ suvarṇa.pattreṇa^avaliptam abhyantaram aṣṭakena baddhaṁ gāḍha.peṭakaḥ //*  
*02.14.28/ sa eva paṭala.sampuṭeṣv abhyuddhāryaḥ //*  
*02.14.29/ pattram āśliṣṭaṁ yamakapattraṁ vā^avalepyeṣu kriyate //*  
*02.14.30/ śulbaṁ tāraṁ vā garbhaḥ pattrāṇāṁ saṁghātyeṣu kriyate //*  
*02.14.31/ śulba.rūpaṁ suvarṇa.pattra.saṁhataṁ pramṛṣṭaṁ supārśvam, tad eva yamaka.pattra.saṁhataṁ pramṛṣṭaṁ tāmra.tāra.rupaṁ ca^uttara.varṇakaḥ //*  
*02.14.32/ tad ubhayaṁ tāpani.kaṣābhyāṁ nihśabda.ullekhanābhyāṁ vā vidyāt //*  
*02.14.33/ abhyuddhāryaṁ badara.āmle lavaṇa.udake vā sādayanti // iti peṭakaḥ //*  
*02.14.34/ ghane suṣire vā rūpe suvarṇa.mṛn.mālukā.hiṅguluka.kalpo vā tapto^avatiṣṭhate //*  
*02.14.35/ dṛḍha.vāstuke vā rūpe vālukā.miśraṁ jatu gāndhāra.paṅko vā tapto^avatiṣṭhate //*  
*02.14.36/ tayos tāpanam avadhvaṁsanaṁ vā śuddhiḥ //*  
*02.14.37/ sa-paribhāṇḍe vā rūpe lavaṇam ulkayā kaṭu.śarkarayā taptam avatiṣṭhate //*  
*02.14.38/ tasya kvāthanaṁ śuddhiḥ //*  
*02.14.39/ abhra.paṭalam aṣṭakena dvi.guṇa.vāstuke vā rūpe badhyate //*  
*02.14.40/ tasya^apihita.kācakasya^udake nimajjata eka.deśaḥ sīdati, paṭala.antareṣu vā sūcyā bhidyate //*  
*02.14.41/ maṇayo rūpyaṁ suvarṇaṁ vā ghana.suṣirāṇāṁ piṅkaḥ //*  
*02.14.42/ tasya tāpanam avadhvaṁsanaṁ vā śuddhiḥ / iti piṅkaḥ //*  
*02.14.43/ tasmād vajra.maṇi.muktā.pravāla.rūpāṇāṁ jāti.rūpa.varṇa.pramāṇa.pudgala.lakṣaṇāny upalabheta //*  
*02.14.44/ kṛta.bhāṇḍa.parīkṣāyāṁ purāṇa.bhāṇḍa.pratisaṁskāre vā catvāro haraṇa.upāyāḥ - parikuṭṭanam avacchedanam ullekhanaṁ parimardanaṁ vā //*  
*02.14.45/ peṭaka.apadeśena pṛṣataṁ guṇaṁ piṭakāṁ vā yat pariśātayanti tat.parikuṭṭanam //*  
*02.14.46/ yad.dvi.guṇa.vāstukānāṁ vā rūpe sīsa.rūpaṁ prakṣipya^ābhyantaram avacchindanti tad avacchedanam //*  
*02.14.47/ yad ghanānāṁ tīkṣṇena^ullikhanti tad ullekhanam //*  
*02.14.48/ hari.tāla.manaḥ.śilā.hiṅguluka.cūrṇānām anyatamena kuru.vinda.cūrṇena vā vastraṁ samyūhya yat parimṛdnanti tat parimardanam //*  
*02.14.49/ tena sauvarṇa.rājatāni bhāṇḍāni kṣīyante, na ca^eṣāṁ kiṁcid avarugṇaṁ bhavati //*  
*02.14.50/ bhagna.khaṇḍa.ghṛṣṭānāṁ samyūhyānāṁ sadṛśena^anumānaṁ kuryāt //02.14.51/ avalepyānāṁ yāvad utpāṭitaṁ tāvad utpāṭya^anumānaṁ kuryāt //*  
*02.14.52/ virūpāṇāṁ vā tāpanam udaka.peṣaṇaṁ ca bahuśaḥ kuryāt //*  
*02.14.53/ avakṣepaḥ pratimānam agnir gaṇḍikā bhaṇḍika.adhikaraṇī piñchaḥ sūtraṁ cellaṁ bollanaṁ śira utsaṅgo makṣikā sva.kāya.īkṣā dṛtir udaka.śarāvam agniṣṭham iti kācaṁ vidyāt //*  
*02.14.54/ rājatānāṁ visraṁ mala.grāhi paruṣaṁ prastīnaṁ vivarṇaṁ vā duṣṭam iti vidyāt //*  
*02.14.55ab/ evaṁ navaṁ ca jīrṇaṁ ca virūpaṁ ca^api bhāṇḍakam /*  
*02.14.55cd/ parīkṣeta^atyayaṁ ca^eṣāṁ yathā.uddiṣṭaṁ prakalpayet //E*  
*(ṣuperintendent of the magaśin)*  
*02.15.01/ koṣṭha.agāra.adhyakṣaḥ sītā.rāṣṭra.krayima.parivartaka.prāmityaka.āpamityaka.saṁhanika.anya.jāta.vyaya.pratyāya.upasthānāny upalabhet //*  
*02.15.02/ sītā.adhyakṣa.upanītaḥ sasya.varṇakaḥ sītā //*  
*02.15.03/ piṇḍa.karaḥ ṣaḍ.bhāgaḥ senā.bhaktaṁ baliḥ kara utsaṅgaḥ pārśvaṁ pārihīṇikam aupāyanikaṁ kauṣṭheyakaṁ ca rāṣṭram //*  
*02.15.04/ dhānya.mūlyaṁ kośa.nirhāraḥ prayoga.pratyādānaṁ ca krayimam //*  
*02.15.05/ sasya.varṇānām argha.antareṇa vinimayaḥ parivartakaḥ //*  
*02.15.06/ sasya.yācanam anyataḥ prāmityakam //*  
*02.15.07/ tad eva pratidāna.artham āpamityakam //*  
*02.15.08/ kuṭṭaka.rocaka.saktu.śukta.piṣṭa.karma taj.jīvaneṣu taila.pīḍana.maudra.cākrikeṣv ikṣūṇāṁ ca kṣāra.karma saṁhanikā //*  
*02.15.09/ naṣṭa.prasmṛta.ādir anya.jātaḥ //*  
*02.15.10/ vikṣepa.vyādhita.antara.ārambha.śeṣaṁ ca vyaya.pratyāyaḥ //*

*02.16.05/ ubhayaṁ ca prajānām anugraheṇa vikrāpayet //*

*02.19.11/ ṣaḍaṅgulād ūrdhvam aṣṭa.aṅgula.uttarā daśa tulāḥ kārayet loha.palād ūrdhvam eka.pala.uttarāḥ, yantram ubhayataḥ.śikyaṁ vā //*  
*02.19.12/ pañca.triṁśat.palalohāṁ dvi.saptaty.aṅgula.āyāmāṁ sama.vṛttāṁ kārayet //*

*02.19.14/ tataḥ karṣa.uttaraṁ palaṁ pala.uttaraṁ daśa.palaṁ dvādaśa pañcadaśa viṁśatir iti padāni kārayet //*

*02.19.16/ akṣeṣu nāndī.pinaddhaṁ kārayet //*  
*02.19.17/ dvi.guṇa.lohāṁ tulām ataḥ ṣaṇṇavaty.aṅgula.āyāmāṁ parimāṇīṁ kārayet //*  
*02.19.18/ tasyāḥ śata.padād ūrdhvaṁ viṁśatiḥ pañcāśat śatam iti padāni kārayet //*  
*02.19.19/ viṁśati.tauliko bhāraḥ //*  
*02.19.20/ daśa.dhāraṇikaṁ palam //*  
*02.19.21/ tat.pala.śatam āya.mānī //*  
*02.19.22/ pañca.pala.avarā vyāvahārikī bhājany antaḥ.pura.bhājanī ca //*  
*02.19.23/ tāsām ardha.dharaṇa.avaraṁ palam, dvi.pala.avaram uttara.loham, ṣaḍ.aṅgula.avarāś ca^āyāmāḥ //*  
*02.19.24/ pūrvayoḥ pañca.palikaḥ prayāmo māṁsa.loha.lavaṇa.maṇi.varjam //*  
*02.19.25/ kāṣṭha.tulā aṣṭa.hastā padavatī pratimānavatī mayūra.pada.adhiṣṭhitā //*  
*02.19.26/ kāṣṭha.pañcaviṁśati.palaṁ taṇḍula.prastha.sādhanam //*  
*02.19.27/ eṣa pradeśo bahv.alpayoḥ //*  
*02.19.28/ iti tulā.pratimānaṁ vyākhyātam //*  
*02.19.29/ atha dhānya.māṣa.dvi.pala.śataṁ droṇam āya.mānam, sapta.aśīti.pala.śatam ardha.palaṁ ca vyāvahārikam, pañca.saptati.pala.śataṁ bhājanīyam, dvi.ṣaṣṭi.pala.śatam ardha.palaṁ ca^antaḥ.pura.bhājanīyam //*  
*02.19.30/ teṣām āḍhaka.prastha.kuḍubāś catur.bhāga.avarāḥ //*  
*02.19.31/ ṣoḍaśa.droṇā khārī //*  
*02.19.32/ viṁśati.droṇikaḥ kumbhaḥ //*  
*02.19.33/ kumbhair daśabhir vahaḥ //*  
*02.19.34/ śuṣka.sāra.dāru.mayaṁ samaṁ catur.bhāga.śikhaṁ mānaṁ kārayet, antaḥ.śikhaṁ vā //*  
*02.19.35/ rasasya tu surāyāḥ puṣpa.phalayos tuṣa.aṅgārāṇāṁ sudhāyāś ca śikhā.mānaṁ dvi.guṇa.uttarā vṛddhiḥ //*  
*02.19.36/ sa-pāda.paṇo droṇa.mūlyam āḍhakasya pāda.ūnaḥ, ṣaṇ.māṣakāḥ prasthasya, māṣakaḥ kuḍubasya //*  
*02.19.37/ dvi.guṇaṁ rasa.ādīnāṁ māna.mūlyam //*  
*02.19.38/ viṁśati.paṇāḥ pratimānasya //*  
*02.19.39/ tulā.mūlyaṁ tri.bhāgaḥ //*  
*02.19.40/ catur.māsikaṁ prātivedhanikaṁ kārayet //*  
*02.19.41/ apratividdhasya^atyayaḥ sa-pādaḥ sapta.viṁśati.paṇaḥ //*  
*02.19.42/ prātivedhanikaṁ kākaṇīkam ahar ahaḥ pautava.adhyakṣāya dadyuḥ //*  
*02.19.43/ dvātriṁśad.bhāgas tapta.vyājī sarpiṣaḥ, catuḥ.ṣaṣṭi.bhāgas tailasya //*  
*02.19.44/ pañcāśad bhāgo māna.srāvo dravāṇām //*  
*02.19.45/ kuḍuba.ardha.catur.aṣṭa.bhāgāni mānāni kārayet //*  
*02.19.46/ kuḍubāś catur.aśītir vārakaḥ sarpiṣo mataḥ //*  
*02.19.47/ catuḥ.ṣaṣṭis tu tailasya pādaś ca ghaṭikā^anayoḥ //*  
*(ṁeasures of space and time)*  
*02.20.01/ māna.adhyakṣyo deśa.kāla.mānaṁ vidyāt //*  
*02.20.02/ aṣṭau parama.aṇavo ratha.cakra.vipruṭ //*  
*02.20.03/ tā aṣṭau likṣā //*  
*02.20.04/ tā aṣtau yūkā //*  
*02.20.05/ tā aṣṭau yava.madhyaḥ //*  
*02.20.06/ aṣṭau yava.madhyā aṅgulam //*  
*02.20.07/ madhyamasya puruṣasya madhyamāyā anugulyā madhya.prakarṣo vā^aṅgulam //*  
*02.20.08/ catur.aṅgulo dhanur.grahaḥ //*  
*02.20.09/ aṣṭa.aṅgulā dhanur.muṣṭiḥ //*  
*02.20.10/ dvādaśa.aṅgulā vitastiḥ, chāyā.pauruṣaṁ ca //*  
*02.20.11/ catur.daśa.aṅgulaṁ śamaḥ śalaḥ parīrayaḥ padaṁ ca //*  
*02.20.12/ dvi.vitastir aratniḥ prājāpatyo hastaḥ //*  
*02.20.13/ sa-dhanur.grahaḥ pautava.vivīta.mānam //*  
*02.20.14/ sa-dhanur.muṣṭiḥ kuṣkuḥ kaṁso vā //*  
*02.20.15/ dvi.catvāriṁśad.aṅgulas takṣṇaḥ krākacanika.kiṣkuḥ skandha.āvāra.durga.rāja.parigraha.mānam //*  
*02.20.16/ catuṣ.pañcāśad.aṅgulaḥ kūpya.vana.hastaḥ //*  
*02.20.17/ catur.aśīty.aṅgulo vyāmo rajju.mānaṁ khāta.pauruṣaṁ ca //*  
*02.20.18/ catur.aratnir daṇḍo dhanur.nālikā pauruṣaṁ ca gārhapatyam //*  
*02.20.19/ aṣṭa.śata.aṅgulaṁ dhanuḥ pathi.prākāra.mānaṁ pauruṣaṁ ca^agni.cityānām //*  
*02.20.20/ ṣaṭ.kaṁso daṇḍo brahma.deya.ātithya.mānam //*  
*02.20.21/ daśa.daṇḍo rajjuḥ //*  
*02.20.22/ dvi.rajjukaḥ parideśaḥ //*  
*02.20.23/ tri.rajjukaṁ nivartanam ekataḥ //*  
*02.20.24/ dvi.daṇḍa.adhiko bāhuḥ //*  
*02.20.25/ dvi.dhanuḥ.sahasraṁ go.rutam //*  
*02.20.26/ catur.go.rutaṁ yojanam //*  
*02.20.27/ iti deśa.mānam //*  
*02.20.28/ kāla.mānam ata ūrdhvam //*  
*02.20.29/ tuṭo lavo nimeṣaḥ kāṣṭhā kallā nālikā muhūrtaḥ pūrva.apara.bhāgau divaso rātriḥ pakṣo māsa ṛtur ayanaṁ saṁvatsaro yugam iti kālāḥ //*  
*02.20.30/ dvau tuṭau lavaḥ //*  
*02.20.31/ dvau lavau nimeṣaḥ //*  
*02.20.32/ pañca.nimeṣāḥ kāṣṭhāḥ //*  
*02.20.33/ triṁśat.kāṣṭhāḥ kalāḥ //*  
*02.20.34/ catvāriṁśat.kalāḥ nālikā //*  
*02.20.35/ suvarṇa.māṣakāś catvāraś catur.aṅgula.āyāmāḥ kumbhac.chidram āḍhakam ambhaso vā nālikā //*  
*02.20.36/ dvi.nāliko muhūrtaḥ //*  
*02.20.37/ pañca.daśa.muhūrto divaso rātriś ca caitre ca^āśvayuje ca māsi bhavataḥ //*  
*02.20.38/ tataḥ paraṁ tribhir muhūrtair anyataraḥ ṣaṇ.māsaṁ vardhate hrasate ca^iti //*  
*02.20.39/ chāyāyām aṣṭa.pauruṣyām aṣṭādaśa.bhāgaś chedaḥ, ṣaṭ.pauruṣyāṁ catur.daśa.bhāgaḥ, tri.pauruṣyām aṣṭa.bhāgaḥ, dvi.pauruṣyāṁ ṣaḍ.bhāgaḥ, pauruṣyāṁ catur.bhāgaḥ, aṣṭa.aṅgulāyāṁ trayo daśa.bhāgāḥ, catur.aṅgulāyāṁ trayo^aṣṭa.bhāgāḥ, acchāyo madhya.ahna iti //*  
*02.20.40/ parāvṛtte divase śeṣam evaṁ vidyāt //*  
*02.20.41/ āṣāḍhe māsi naṣṭac.chāyo madhya.ahno bhavati //*  
*02.20.42/ ataḥ paraṁ śrāvaṇa.ādīnāṁ ṣaṇ.māsānāṁ dvy.aṅgula.uttarā māgha.ādīnāṁ dvy.aṅgula.avarā chāyā iti //*  
*02.20.43/ pañcadaśa.aho.rātrāḥ pakṣaḥ //*  
*02.20.44/ soma.āpyāyanaḥ śuklaḥ //*  
*02.20.45/ soma.avacchedano bahulaḥ //*  
*02.20.46/ dvi.pakṣo māsaḥ //*  
*02.20.47/ triṁśad.aho.rātraḥ karma.māsaḥ //*  
*02.20.48/ sa-ardhaḥ sauraḥ //*  
*02.20.49/ ardha.nyūnaś cāndra.māsaḥ //*  
*02.20.50/ sapta.viṁśatir nākṣatra.māsaḥ //*  
*02.20.51/ dvātriṁśad bala.māsaḥ //*  
*02.20.52/ pañcatriṁśad aśva.vāhāyāḥ //*  
*02.20.53/ catvāriṁśadd.hasti.vāhāyāḥ //*  
*02.20.54/ dvau māsāv ṛtuḥ //*  
*02.20.55/ śrāvaṇaḥ prauṣṭhapadaś ca varṣāḥ //*  
*02.20.56/ āśvayujaḥ kārttikaś ca śarat //*  
*02.20.57/ mārga.śīrṣaḥ pauṣaś ca hemantaḥ //*  
*02.20.58/ māghaḥ phālgunaś ca śiśiraḥ //*  
*02.20.59/ caitro vaiśākhaś ca vasantaḥ //*  
*02.20.60/ jyeṣṭhāmūlīya āṣāḍhaś ca grīṣmaḥ //*  
*02.20.61/ śiśira.ādy uttara.ayaṇam //*  
*02.20.62/ varṣa.ādi dakṣiṇa.ayanam //*  
*02.20.63/ dvy.ayanaḥ saṁvatsaraḥ //*  
*02.20.64/ pañca.saṁvatsaro yugam / iti //*  
*02.20.65ab/ divasasya haraty arkaḥ ṣaṣṭi.bhāgam ṛtau tataḥ /*  
*02.20.65cd/ karoty ekam ahaś.chedaṁ tathā^eva^ekaṁ ca candramāḥ //*  
*02.20.66ab/ evam ardha.tṛtīyānām abdānām adhimāsakam /*  
*02.20.66cd/ grīṣme janayataḥ pūrvaṁ pañca.abda.ante ca paścimam //E*  
*(Collector of costums and tolls)*  
*02.21.01/ śulka.adhyakṣaḥ śulka.śālāṁ dhvajaṁ ca prān.mukham udan.mukhaṁ vā mahā.dvāra.abhyāśe niveśayet //*  
*02.21.02/ śulka.ādāyinaś catvāraḥ pañca vā sārtha.upayātān vaṇijo likheyuḥ - ke kutastyāḥ kiyat.paṇyāḥ kva ca^abhijñānaṁ mudrā vā kṛtā iti //*  
*02.21.03/ amudrāṇām atyayo deya.dvi.guṇaḥ //*  
*02.21.04/ kūṭa.mudrāṇāṁ śulka.aṣṭa.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.21.05/ bhinna.mudrāṇām atyayo ghaṭikā.sthāne sthānam //*  
*02.21.06/ rāja.mudrā.parivartane nāma.kṛte vā sa-pāda.paṇikaṁ vahanaṁ dāpayet //*  
*02.21.07/ dhvaja.mūla.upasthitasya pramāṇam arghaṁ ca vaidehikāḥ paṇyasya brūyuḥ "etat.pramāṇena^argheṇa paṇyam idaṁ kaḥ kretā" iti //*  
*02.21.08/ tri.ruddha.uṣitam arthibhyo dadyāt //*  
*02.21.09/ kretṛ.saṁgharṣe mūlya.vṛddhiḥ sa-śulkā kośaṁ gacchet //*  
*02.21.10/ śulka.bhayāt paṇya.pramāṇa mūlyaṁ vā hīnaṁ bruvatas tad atiriktaṁ rājā haret //*  
*02.21.11/ śulkam aṣṭa.guṇaṁ vā dadyāt //*  
*02.21.12/ tad eva niviṣṭa.paṇyasya bhāṇḍasya hīna.prativarṇakena^argha.apakarṣaṇe sāra.bhāṇḍasya phalgu.bhāṇḍena praticchādane ca kuryāt //*  
*02.21.13/ pratikretṛ.bhayād vā paṇya.mūlyād upari mūlyaṁ vardhayato mūlya.vṛddhiṁ rājā haret, dvi.guṇaṁ vā śulkaṁ kuryāt //*  
*02.21.14/ tad eva^aṣṭa.guṇam adhyakṣasyac. chādayataḥ //*  
*02.21.15/ tasmād vikrayaḥ paṇyānāṁ dhṛto mito gaṇito vā kāryaḥ, tarkaḥ phalgu.bhāṇḍānām ānugrāhikāṇāṁ ca //*  
*02.21.16/ dhvaja.mūlam atikrāntānāṁ ca^akṛta.śulkānāṁ śulkād aṣṭa.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.21.17/ pathika.utpathikās tad vidyuḥ //*  
*02.21.18/ vaivāhikam anvāyanam aupāyikaṁ yajña.kṛtya.prasava.naimittikaṁ deva^ijyā.caula.upanayana.go.dāna.vrata.dīkṣā.ādiṣu kriyā.viśeṣeṣu bhāṇḍam ucchulkaṁ gacchet //*  
*02.21.19/ anyathā.vādinaḥ steya.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.21.20/ kṛta.śulkena^akṛta.śulkaṁ nirvāhayato dvitīyam eka.mudrayā bhittvā paṇya.puṭam apaharato vaidehakasya tac^ca tāvac ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.21.21/ śulka.sthānād gomaya.palālaṁ pramāṇaṁ kṛtvā^apaharata uttamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.21.22/ śastra.varma.kavaca.loha.ratha.ratna.dhānya.paśūnām anyatamam anirvāhyaṁ nirvāhayato yathā^avaghuṣito daṇḍaḥ paṇya.nāśaś^ca //*  
*02.21.23/ teṣām anyatamasya^ānayane bahir eva^ucchulko vikrayaḥ //*  
*02.21.24/ anta.pālaḥ sa-pāda.paṇikāṁ vartanīṁ gṛhṇīyāt paṇya.vahanasya, paṇikām eka.khurasya, paśūnām ardha.paṇikāṁ kṣudra.paśūnāṁ pādikām, aṁsa.bhārasya māṣikām //*  
*02.21.25/ naṣṭa.apahṛtaṁ ca pratividadhyāt //*  
*02.21.26/ vaideśyaṁ sārthaṁ kṛta.sāra.phalgu.bhāṇḍa.vicayanam abhijñānaṁ mudrāṁ ca dattvā preṣayed adhyakṣasya //*  
*02.21.27/ vaidehaka.vyañjano vā sārtha.pramāṇaṁ rājñaḥ preṣayet //*  
*02.21.28/ tena pradeśena rājā śulka.adhyakṣasya sārtha.pramāṇam upadiśet sarvajña.khyāpana^artham //*  
*02.21.29/ tataḥ sārtham adhyakṣo^abhigamya brūyāt "idam amuṣyām uṣya ca sāra.bhāṇḍaṁ phalgu.bhāṇḍaṁ ca, na nihūhitavyam, eṣa rājñaḥ prabhāvaḥ" iti //*  
*02.21.30/ nihūhataḥ phalgu.bhāṇḍaṁ śulka.aṣṭa.guṇo daṇḍaḥ, sāra.bhāṇḍaṁ sarva.apahāraḥ //*  
*02.21.31ab/ rāṣṭra.pīḍā.karaṁ bhāṇḍam ucchindyād aphalaṁ ca yat /*  
*02.21.31cd/ mahā.upakāram ucchulkaṁ kuryād bījaṁ ca durlabham //E*  
*(ṭariff of duties and tolls)*  
*02.22.01/ bāhyam ābhyantaraṁ ca^ātithyam //*  
*02.22.02/ niṣkrāmyaṁ praveśyaṁ ca śulkam //*  
*02.22.03/ praveśyānāṁ mūlya.pañca.bhāgaḥ //*  
*02.22.04/ puṣpa.phala.śāka.mūla.kanda.vāllikya.bīja.śuṣka.matsya.māṁsānāṁ ṣaḍ.bhāgaṁ gṛhṇīyāt //*  
*02.22.05/ śaṅkha.vajra.maṇi.muktā.pravāla.hārāṇāṁ taj.jāta.puruṣaiḥ kārayet kṛta.karma.pramāṇa.kāla.vetana.phala.niṣpattibhiḥ //*  
*02.22.06/ kṣauma.dukūla.krimi.tāna.kaṅkaṭa.hari.tāla.manaḥ.śilā.añjana.hiṅguluka.loha.varṇa.dhātūnāṁ candana.aguru.kaṭuka.kiṇva.avarāṇāṁ carma.danta.āstaraṇa.prāvaraṇa.krimi.jātānām āja.eḍakasya ca daśa.bhāgaḥ pañca.daśa.bhāgo vā //*  
*02.22.07/ vastra.catuṣpada.dvipada.sūtra.kārpāsa.gandha.bhaiṣajya.kāṣṭha.veṇu.valkala.carma.mṛdbha.aṇḍānāṁ dhānya.sneha.kṣāra.lavaṇa.madya.pakvān nādīnāṁ ca viṁśati.bhāgaḥ pañca.viṁśati.bhāgo vā//*  
*02.22.08/ dvārādeyaṁ śulkaṁ pañca.bhāgaḥ ānugrāhikaṁ vā yathā.deśa.upakāraṁ sthāpaytet//*  
*02.22.09/ jāti.bhūmiṣu ca paṇyānāṁ vikrayaḥ//*  
*02.22.10/ khanibhyo dhātu.paṇyādāne ṣaṭ.chatam atyayaḥ//*  
*02.22.11/ puṣpa.phala.vāṭebhyaḥ puṣpa.phala.ādāne catuṣ.pañcāśat.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.22.12 ṣaṇḍebhyaḥ śāka.mūla.kanda.ādāne pāda.ūnaṁ dvi.pañcāśat.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.22.13/ kṣetrebhyaḥ sarva.sasya.ādāne tri.pañcāśat.paṇaḥ //*  
*02.22.14/ paṇo^adhyardha.paṇaś ca sītā.atyayaḥ //*  
*02.22.15ab/ ato nava.purāṇāṁ deśa.jāti.caritrataḥ /*  
*02.22.15cd/ paṇyānāṁ sthāpayec^śuklam atyayaṁ ca^apakārataḥ //E*  
*(ṣuperintendent of yarns (and teṣtiles))*  
*02.23.01/ sūtra.adhyakṣaḥ sūtra.varma.vastra.rajju.vyavahāraṁ taj.jāta.puruṣaiḥ kārayet //*  
*02.23.02/ ūrṇā.valka.kārpāsa.tūla.śaṇa.kṣaumāṇi ca vidhavā.nyaṅgā.kanyā.pravrajitā.daṇḍa.pratikāriṇībhī rūpa.ājīvā.mātṛkābhir vṛddha.rāja.dāsībhir vyuparata.upasthāna.deva.dāsībhiś ca kartayet //*  
*02.23.03/ ślakṣṇa.sthūla.madhyatāṁ ca sūtrasya viditvā vetanaṁ kalpayet, bahv.alpatāṁ ca //*  
*02.23.04/ sūtra.pramāṇa jñātvā taila.āmalaka.udvartanair etā anugṛhṇīyāt //*  
*02.23.05/ tithiṣu pratimāna.dānaiś ca karma kārayitavyāḥ //*  
*02.23.06/ sūtra.hrāse vetana.hrāso dravya.sārāt //*  
*02.23.07/ kṛta.karma.pramāṇa.kāla.vetana.phala.niṣpattibhiḥ kārubhiś ca karma kārayet, pratisaṁsargaṁ ca gacchet //*  
*02.23.08/ kṣauma.dukūla.krimi.tāna.rāṅkava.kārpāsa.sūtra.vāna.karma.antāṁś ca prayuñjāno gandha.mālya.dānair anyaiś ca^aupagrāhikair ārādhayet //*  
*02.23.09/ vastra.āstaraṇa.prāvaraṇa.vikalpān utthāpayet //*  
*02.23.10/ kaṅkaṭa.karma.antāṁś ca taj.jāta.kāru.śilpibhiḥ kārayet //*  
*02.23.11/ yāś ca^aniṣkāsinyaḥ proṣitā vidhavā nyaṅgāḥ kanyakā vā^ātmānaṁ bibhṛyuḥ tāḥ sva.dāsībhir anusārya sa-upagrahaṁ karma kārayitavyāḥ //*  
*02.23.12/ svayam āgacchantīnāṁ vā sūtra.śālāṁ pratyuṣasi bhāṇḍa.vetana.vinimayaṁ kārayet //*  
*02.23.13/ sūtra.parīkṣā.artha.mātraḥ pradīpaḥ //*

*02.24.01/ sītā.adhyakṣaḥ kṛṣi.tantra.śulba.vṛkṣa.āyur.vedajñas taj.jña.sakho vā sarva.dhānya.puṣpa.phala.śāka.kanda.mūla.vāllikya.kṣauma.kārpāsa.bījāni yathā.kālaṁ gṛhṇīyāt //*

*02.24.06/ varṣa.tri.bhāgaḥ pūrva.paścima.māsayoḥ, dvau tri.bhāgau madhyamayoḥ - suṣamā.rūpam //*

*02.24.23/ yathā.svaṁ bhūmiṣu ca sthālyāś ca^ānūpyāś ca^oṣadhīḥ sthāpayet //*

*02.25.01/ surā.adhyakṣaḥ surā.kiṇva.vyavahārān durge jana.pade skandha.āvāre vā taj.jāta.surā.kiṇva.vyavahāribhiḥ kārayed, eka.mukham aneka.mukhaṁ vā vikraya.kraya.vaśena vā //*  
*02.25.02/ ṣaṭ.śatam atyayam anyatra kartṛ.kretṛ.vikretṛṛṇāṁ sthāpayet //*  
*02.25.03/ grāmād anirṇayaṇam asampātaṁ ca surāyāḥ, pramāda.bhayāt karmasu ñjirdiṣṭānām, maryāda.atikrama.bhayād āryāṇām, utsāha.bhayāc ca tīṣkṇānām //*  
*02.25.04/ lakṣitam alpaṁ vā catur.bhāgam ardha.kuḍubaṁ kuḍubam ardha.prasthaṁ prasthaṁ vā^iti jñāta.śaucā nirhareyuḥ //*  
*02.25.05/ pāna.agāreṣu vā pibeyur asaṁcāriṇaḥ //*  
*02.25.06/ nikṣepa.upanidhi.prayoga.apahṛtānām aniṣṭa.upagatānāṁ ca dravyāṇāṁ jñāna.artham asvāmikaṁ kupyaṁ hiraṇyaṁ ca^upalabhya niṣkeptāram anyatra vyapadeśena grāhayed, ativyaya.kartāram anāyati.vyayaṁ ca //*  
*02.25.07/ na ca^anargheṇa kālikāṁ vā surāṁ dadyād, anyatra duṣṭa.surāyāḥ //*  
*02.25.08/ tām anyatra vikrāpayet //*  
*02.25.09/ dāsa.karma.karebhyo vā vetanaṁ dadyāt //*  
*02.25.10/ vāhana.pratipānaṁ sūkara.poṣaṇaṁ vā dadyāt //*  
*02.25.11/ pāna.agārāṇy.aneka.kakṣyāṇi vibhakta.śayana.āsanavanti pāna.uddeśāni gandha.mālya.udakavanti ṛtu.sukhāni kārayet //*  
*02.25.12/ tatrasthāḥ prakṛty.autpattikau vyayau gūḍhā vidyuḥ, āgantūṁś ca //*  
*02.25.13/ kretṛṛṇāṁ matta.suptānām alaṅkārāt^chādana.hiraṇyāni ca vidyuḥ //*  
*02.25.14/ tan.nāśe vaṇijas tac ca tāvac ca daṇḍaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*02.25.15/ vaṇijaśs tu saṁvṛteṣu kakṣyā.vibhāgeṣu sva.dāsībhiḥ peśala.rūpābhir āgantūnāṁ vāstavyānāṁ ca^ārya.rūpāṇāṁ matta.suptānāṁ bhāvaṁ vidyuḥ //*  
*02.25.16/ medaka.prasanna.āsava.ariṣṭa.maireya.madhūnām //*  
*02.25.17/ udaka.droṇaṁ taṇḍulānām ardha.āḍhakaṁ trayaḥ prasthāḥ kiṇvasya^iti medaka.yogaḥ //*

*02.25.19/ kapittha.tulā phāṇitaṁ pañca.taulikaṁ prastho madhuna ity āsava.yogaḥ //*

*02.25.27/ pāṭhā.loghra.tejovaty.elā.vāluka.madhuka.madhu.rasā.priyaṅgu.dāru.haridrā.marica.pippalīnāṁ ca pañca.kārṣikaḥ sambhāra.yogo medakasya prasannāyāś ca //*  
*02.25.28/ madhuka.niryūha.yuktā kaṭa.śarkarā varṇa.prasādanī ca //*

*02.26.01/ sūnā.adhyakṣaḥ pradiṣṭa.abhayānām abhaya.vana.vāsināṁ ca mṛga.paśu.pakṣi.matsyānāṁ bandha.vadha.hiṁsāyām uttamaṁ daṇḍaṁ kārayet, kuṭumbinām abhaya.vana.parigraheṣu madhyamam //*  
*02.26.02/ apravṛtta.vadhānāṁ matsya.pakṣiṇāṁ bandha.vadha.hiṁsāyāṁ pāda.ūna.sapta.viṁśati.paṇam atyayaṁ kuryāt, mṛga.paśūnāṁ dvi.guṇam //*  
*02.26.03/ pravṛtta.hiṁsānām aparigṛhītānāṁ ṣaḍ.bhāgaṁ gṛhṇīyāt, matsya.pakṣiṇāṁ daśa.bhāgaṁ vā^adhikam, mṛga.paśūnāṁ śulkaṁ vā^adhikam //*  
*02.26.04/ pakṣi.mṛgāṇāṁ jīvat ṣaḍ.bhāgam abhaya.vaneṣu pramuñcet //*  
*02.26.05/ sāmudra.hasty.aśva.puruṣa.vṛṣa.gardabha.ākṛtayo matsyāḥ sārasā na^ādeyās taṭāka.kulyā.udbhavā vā krauñca.utkrośaka.dātyūha.haṁsa.cakravāka.jīvan.jīvaka.bhṛṅga.rāja.cakora.matta.kokila.mayūra.śuka.madana.śārikā vihāra.pakṣiṇo maṅgalyāś ca^anye^api prāṇinaḥ pakṣi.mṛgā hiṁsā.bādhebhyo rakṣyāḥ //*  
*02.26.06/ rakṣā.atikrame pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.26.07/ mṛga.paśūnām anasthi.māṁsaṁ sadyo.hataṁ vikrīṇīran //*

*02.26.09/ tulā.hīne hīna.aṣṭa.guṇam //*

*02.26.11/ ghnataḥ pañcāśatko daṇḍaḥ, kliṣṭa.ghātaṁ ghātayataś ca //*

*02.27.01/ gaṇikā.adhyakṣo gaṇikā.anvayām agaṇikā.anvayāṁ vā rūpa.yauvana.śilpa.sampannāṁ sahasreṇa gaṇikāṁ kārayet, kuṭumba.ardhena pratigaṇikām //*  
*02.27.02/ niṣpatitā.pretayor duhitā bhaginī vā kuṭumbaṁ bhareta, mātā vā pratigaṇikāṁ sthāpayet //*  
*02.27.03/ tāsām abhāve rājā haret //*  
*02.27.04/ saubhāgya.alaṁkāra.vṛddhyā sahasreṇa vāraṁ kaniṣṭhaṁ madhyamam uttamaṁ vā^āropayet chatra.bhṛṅgāra.vyajana.śibikā.pīṭhikā.ratheṣu ca viśeṣa.artham //*  
*02.27.05/ saubhāgya.bhaṅge mātṛkāṁ kuryāt //*  
*02.27.06/ niṣkrayaś catur.viṁśati.sāhasro gaṇikāyāḥ, dvādaśa.sāhasro gaṇikā.putrasya //*  
*02.27.07/ aṣṭa.varṣāt prabhṛti rājñaḥ kuśīlava.karma kuryāt //*  
*02.27.08/ gaṇikā.dāsī bhagna.bhogā koṣṭha.agāre mahānase vā karma kuryāt //*  
*02.27.09/ aviśantī sapāda.paṇam avaruddhā māsa.vetanaṁ dadyāt //*  
*02.27.10/ bhogaṁ dāyamāyaṁ vyayam āyatiṁ ca gaṇikāyā nibandhayet, ati.vyaya.karma ca vārayet //*  
*02.27.11/ mātṛ.hastād anyatra abharaṇa.nyāse sa.pāda.catuṣ.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.27.12/ svāpateyaṁ vikrayam ādhānaṁ vā nayantyāḥ sa.pāda.pañcāśat.paṇaḥ paṇo^ardha.paṇa.cchedane //*  
*02.27.13/ akāmāyāḥ kumāryā vā sāhase uttamo daṇḍaḥ, sa.kāmāyāḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.27.14/ gaṇikām akāmāṁ rundhato niṣpātayato vā vraṇa.vidāraṇena vā rūpaṁ.upaghnataḥ sahasraṁ daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.27.15/ sthān.viśeṣeṇa vā daṇḍa.vṛddhiḥ ā.niṣkraya.dvi.guṇāt //*  
*02.27.16/ prāpta.adhikāraṁ gaṇikāṁ ghatayato niṣkraya.tri.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.27.17/ mātṛkā.duhitṛkā.rūpa.dāsīnāṁ ghāte uttamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.27.18/ sarvatra prathame^aparādhe prathamaḥ, dvitīye dvi.guṇaḥ, tṛtīye tri.guṇaḥ, caturthe yathā.kāmī syāt //*  
*02.27.19/ rāja.ājñayā puruṣam anabhigacchantī gaṇikā śiphā.sahasraṁ labheeta, pañca.sahasraṁ vā daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.27.20/ bhogaṁ gṛhītvā dviṣatyā bhoga.dvi.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*

*02.30.01/ aśva.adhyakṣaḥ paṇya.āgārikaṁ kraya.upāgatam āhava.labdham ājātaṁ sāhāyya.āgatakaṁ paṇa.sthitaṁ yāvat.kālikaṁ vā^aśva.paryagraṁ kula.vayo.varṇa.cihna.varga.āgamair lekhayet //*  
*02.30.02/ apraśasta.nyaṅga.vyādhitāṁś ca^āvedayet //*  
*02.30.03/ kośa.koṣṭha.agārābhyāṁ ca gṛhītvā māsa.lābham aśva.vāhaś cintayet //*  
*02.30.04/ aśva.vibhavena^āyatām aśvāyām advi.guṇa.vistārāṁ catur.dvāra.upāvartana.madhyāṁ sa-pragrīvāṁ pradvāra.āsana.phalaka.yuktānāṁ vānara.mayūra.pṛṣata.nakula.cakora.śuka.sārika.ākīrṇāṁ śālāṁ niveśayet //*  
*02.30.05/ aśvāyām acatur.aśra.ślakṣṇa.phalaka.āstāraṁ sa-khādana.koṣṭhakaṁ sa-mūtra.purīṣa.utsargam eka.ekaśaḥ prān.mukham udan.mukhaṁ vā sthānaṁ niveśayet //*  
*02.30.06/ śālā.vaśena vā dig.vibhāgaṁ kalpayet //*  
*02.30.07/ vaḍavā.vṛṣa.kiśorāṇām eka.anteṣu //*  
*02.30.08/ vaḍavāyāḥ prajatāyās tri.rātraṁ ghṛta.prasthaḥ pānam //*  
*02.30.09/ ata ūrdhvaṁ saktu.prasthaḥ sneha.bhaiṣajya.pratipānaṁ daśa.rātram //*  
*02.30.10/ tataḥ pulāko yavasam ārtavaś ca^āhāraḥ //*  
*02.30.11/ daśa.rātrād ūrdhvaṁ kiśorasya ghṛta.catur.bhāgaḥ saktu.kuḍubaḥ kṣīra.prasthaś ca^āhāra ā.ṣaṇ.māsāt //*  
*02.30.12/ tataḥ paraṁ māsa.uttaram ardha.vṛddhir yava.prastha ā.tri.varṣāt, droṇa ā.catur.varṣāt //*  
*02.30.13/ ata ūrdhvaṁ catur.varṣaḥ pañca.varṣo vā karmaṇyaḥ pūrṇa.pramāṇaḥ //*  
*02.30.14/ dvātriṁśad.aṅgulaṁ mukham uttama.aśvasya, pañca.mukhāny āyāmo, viṁśaty.aṅgulā jaṅghā, catur.jaṅgha utsedhaḥ //*  
*02.30.15/ try.aṅgula.avaraṁ madhyama.avarayoḥ //*  
*02.30.16/ śata.aṅgulaḥ pariṇāhaḥ //*  
*02.30.17/ pañca.bhāga.avaro madhyama.avarayoḥ //*  
*02.30.18/ uttama.aśvasya dvi.droṇaṁ śāli.vrīhi.yava.priyaṅgūṇām ardha.śuṣkam ardha.siddhaṁ vā mudga.māṣāṇāṁ vā pulākaḥ sneha.prasthaś ca, pañca.palaṁ lavaṇasya, māṁsaṁ pañcāśat.palikaṁ rasasya^āḍhakaṁ dvi.guṇaṁ vā dadhnaḥ piṇḍa.kledana.artham, kṣāra.pañca.palikaḥ surāyāḥ prasthaḥ payaso vā dvi.guṇaḥ pratipānam //*  
*02.30.19/ dīrgha.patha.bhāra.klāntānāṁ ca khādana.arthaṁ sneha.prastho^anuvāsanaṁ kuḍubo nasya.karmaṇaḥ, yavasasya^ardha.bhāras tṛṇasya dvi.guṇaḥ ṣaḍ.aratni.parikṣepaḥ puñjīla.graho vā //*  
*02.30.20/ pāda.avaram etan madhyama.avarayoḥ //*

*02.30.22/ madhyama.samaś ca^avaraḥ //*  
*02.30.23/ pāda.hīnaṁ vaḍavānāṁ pāraśamānāṁ ca //*  
*02.30.24/ ato^ardhaṁ kiśorāṇāṁ ca //*  
*02.30.25/ iti vidhā.yogaḥ //*  
*02.30.26/ vidhā.pācaka.sūtra.grāhaka.cikitsakāḥ pratisvāda.bhājaḥ //*  
*02.30.27/ yuddha.vyādhi.jarā.karma.kṣīṇāḥ piṇḍa.gocarikāḥ syuḥ //02.30.28/ asamara.prayogyāḥ paura.jānapadānām arthena vṛṣā vaḍavāsv āyojyāḥ //*  
*02.30.29/ prayogyānām uttamāḥ kāmboja.saindhava.āraṭṭa.vanāyujāḥ, madhyamā bāhlīka.pāpeyaka.sauvīraka.taitalāḥ, śeṣāḥ pratyavarāḥ //*  
*02.30.30/ teṣāṁ tīṣkṇa.bhadra.manda.vaśena sāmnāhyam aupavāhyakaṁ vā karma prayojayet //*  
*02.30.31/ catur.aśraṁ karma.aśvasya sāmnāhyam //*  
*02.30.32/ valgano nīcair gato laṅghano ghoraṇo nāroṣṭraś ca^aupavāhyāḥ //*  
*02.30.33/ tatra.aupaveṇuko vardhamānako yamaka ālīḍha.plutaḥ pṛthug.astrika.cālī ca valganaḥ //*  
*02.30.34/ sa eva śiraḥ.karṇa.viśuddho nīcair gataḥ, ṣoḍaśa.mārgo vā //*  
*02.30.35/ prakīrṇakaḥ prakīrṇa.uttaro niṣaṇṇaḥ pārśva.anuvṛtta ūrmi.mārgaḥ śarabha.krīḍitaḥ śarabha.plutas tri.tālo bāhya.anuvṛttaḥ pañca.pāṇiḥ siṁha.āyataḥ svādhūtaḥ kliṣṭaḥ śliṅgito bṛṁhitaḥ puṣpa.abhikīrṇaś ca^iti nīcair gata.mārgaḥ //*  
*02.30.36/ kapi.pluto bheka.plut^eṇa.pluta^eka.pāda.plutaḥ kokila.saṁcāry.urasyo baka.cārī ca laṅghanaḥ //*  
*02.30.37/ kāṅko vāri.kāṅko māyūro^ardha.māyūro nākulo^rdha.nākulo vārāho^ardha.vārāhaś ca^iti dhoraṇaḥ //*  
*02.30.38/ saṁjñā.pratikāro nāra.uṣṭra^iti //*  
*02.30.39/ ṣaṇṇava dvādaśa^iti yojanāny dhvā rathyānām, pañca yojanāny ardha.aṣṭamāni daśa^iti pṛṣṭha.vāhinām aśvānām adhvā //*  
*02.30.40/ vikramo bhadra.aśvāso bhāra.vāhya iti mārgāḥ //*  
*02.30.41/ vikramo valgitam upakaṇṭham upajavo javaś ca dhārāḥ //*  
*02.30.42/ teṣāṁ bandhana.upakaraṇaṁ yogya.ācāryāḥ pratidiśeyuḥ, sāṁgrāmikaṁ ratha.aśva.alaṁkāraṁ ca sūtāḥ //*  
*02.30.43/ aśvānāṁ cikitsakāḥ śarīra.hrāsa.vṛddhi.pratīkāram ṛtu.vibhaktaṁ ca^āhāram //*  
*02.30.44/ sūtra.grāhaka.aśva.bandhaka.yāvasika.vidhā.pācaka.sthāna.pāla.keśa.kāra.jāṅgulīvidaś ca sva.karmabhir aśvān ārādhayeyuḥ //*  
*02.30.45/ karma.atikrame ca^eṣāṁ divasa.vetanac.chedanaṁ kuryāt //*  
*02.30.46/ nīrājana.uparuddhaṁ vāhayataś cikitsaka.uparuddhaṁ vā dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.30.47/ kriyā.bhaiṣajya.saṅgena vyādhi.vṛddhau pratīkāra.dvi.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.30.48/ tad.aparādhena vailomye pattra.mūlyaṁ daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.30.49/ tena go.maṇḍalaṁ khara.uṣṭra.mahiṣam aja.avikaṁ ca vyākhyātam //*  
*02.30.50ab/ dvir ahnaḥ snānam aśvānāṁ gandha.mālyaṁ ca dāpayet /*  
*02.30.50cd/ kṛṣṇa.saṁdhiṣu bhūta.ijyāḥ śukleṣu svasti.vācanam //*  
*02.30.51ab/ nīrājanām āśvayuje kārayen navame^ahani /*  
*02.30.51cd/ yātrā.ādāv avasāne vā vyādhau vā śāntike rataḥ //E*  
*(ṣuperintendent of elephants)*  
*02.31.01/ hasty.adhyakṣo hasti.vana.rakṣāṁ damya.karma.kṣāntānāṁ hasti.hastinī.kalabhānāṁ śālā.sthāna.śayyā.karma.vidhā.yavasa.pramāṇaṁ karmasv āyogaṁ bandhana.upakaraṇaṁ sāṁgrāmikam alaṁkāraṁ cikitsaka.anīkastha.aupasthāyika.vargaṁ ca^anutiṣṭhet //*  
*02.31.02/ hasty.āyāma.dvi.guṇa.utsedha.viṣkambha.āyāmāṁ hastinī.sthāna.adhikāṁ sapragrīvāṁ kumārī.saṁgrahāṁ prān.mukhīm udan.mukhīṁ vā śālāṁ niveśayet //*  
*02.31.03/ hasty.āyāma.catur.aśra.ślakṣṇa.ālāna.stambha.phalaka.āstarakaṁ sa-mūtra.purīṣa.utsargaṁ sthānaṁ niveśayet //*  
*02.31.04/ sthāna.samāṁ śayyām ardha.apāśrayāṁ durge sāmnāhya.aupavāhyānāṁ bahir damya.vyālānām //*  
*02.31.05/ prathama.saptama aṣṭama.bhāgāv ahnaḥ snāna.kālau, tad.anantaraṁ vidhāyāḥ //*  
*02.31.06/ pūrva.ahne vyāyāma.kālaḥ, paśca.ahnaḥ pratipāna.kālaḥ //*  
*02.31.07/ rātri.bhāgau dvau svapna.kālā, tri.bhāgaḥ saṁveśana.utthānikaḥ //*  
*02.31.08/ grīṣme grahaṇa.kālaḥ //*  
*02.31.09/ viṁśati.varṣo grāhyaḥ //*  
*02.31.10/ vikko moḍho makkaṇo vyāthito garbhiṇī dhenukā hastinī ca^agrāhyāḥ //*  
*02.31.11/ sapta.aratni utsedho nava.āyāmo daśa pariṇāhaḥ pramāṇataś catvāriṁśad.varṣo bhavaty uttamaḥ, triṁśad.varṣo madhyamaḥ, pañca.viṁśati.varṣo^avaraḥ //*  
*02.31.12/ tayoḥ pāda.avaro vidhā.vidhiḥ //*  
*02.31.13/ aratnau taṇula.droṇaḥ, ardha.āḍhakaṁ tailasya, sarpiṣas trayaḥ prasthāḥ, daśa.palaṁ lavaṇasya, māṁsaṁ pañcāśat.palikam, rasasya^āḍhakaṁ dvi.guṇaṁ vā dadhnaḥ piṇḍa.kledana.artham, kṣāra.daśa.palikaṁ madyasya^āḍhakaṁ dvi.guṇaṁ vā payasaḥ pratipānam, gātra.avasekas taila.prasthaḥ, śiraso^aṣṭa.bhāgaḥ prādīpikaś ca, yavasasya dvau bhārau sa-pādau,*  
*śaṣpasya śuṣkasya^ardha.tṛtīyo bhāraḥ, kaḍaṅkarasya^aniyamaḥ //*  
*02.31.14/ sapta.aratninā tulya.bhojano^aṣṭa.aratnir atyarālaḥ //*  
*02.31.15/ yathā.hastam avaśeṣaḥ ṣaḍ.aratniḥ pañca.aratniś ca //*  
*02.31.16/ kṣīra.yāvasiko vikkaḥ krīḍā.arthaṁ grāhyaḥ //*  
*02.31.17/ saṁjāta.lohitā praticchannā samlipta.pakṣā sama.kakṣyā vyatikīrṇa.māṁsā sama.talpa.talā jāta.droṇikā^iti śobhāḥ //*  
*02.31.18ab/ śobhā.vaśena vyāyāmaṁ bhadrma mandaṁ ca kārayet /*  
*02.31.18cd/ mṛgaṁ saṁkīrṇa.liṅgaṁ ca karmasv ṛtu.vaśena vā //*  
*(Activity of elephants)*  
*02.32.01/ karma.skandhāś catvāro damyaḥ sāmnāhya aupavāhyo vyālaś ca //*  
*02.32.02/ tatra damyaḥ pañca.vidhaḥ skandha.gataḥ stambha.gato vāri.gato^avapāta.gato yūtha.gataś ca^iti //*  
*02.32.03/ tasya^upavicāro vikka.karma //*  
*02.32.04/ sāmnāhyaḥ sapta.kriyā.patha upasthānaṁ saṁvartanaṁ samyānaṁ vadha.āvadho hasti.yuddhaṁ nāga.rāyaṇaṁ sāṁgrāmikaṁ ca //*  
*02.32.05/ tasya^upavicāraḥ kakṣyā.karma graiveya.karma yūtha.karma ca //*  
*02.32.06/ aupavāhyo^aṣṭa.vidha ācaraṇaḥ kuñjara.aupavāhyo dhoraṇa ādhāna.gatiko yaṣṭy.upavāhyas totra.upavāhyaḥ śuddha.upavāhyo mārgayukaś ca^iti //*  
*02.32.07/ tasya^upavicāraḥ śārada.karma hīna.karma nāra.uṣṭra.karma ca //*  
*02.32.08/ vyāla^eka.kriyā.pathaḥ śaṅkito^avaruddho viṣamaḥ prabhinnaḥ prabhinna.viniścayo mada.hetu.viniścayaś ca //*  
*02.32.09/ tasya-upavicāra āyamya^eka.rakṣā.karma //*  
*02.32.10/ kriyā.vipanno vyālaḥ śuddhaḥ su.vrato viṣamaḥ sarva.doṣa.praduṣṭaś ca //*  
*02.32.11/ teṣāṁ bandhana.upakaraṇam anīka.stha.pramāṇam //*  
*02.32.12/ ālāna.graiveya.kakṣyā.pāra.ayaṇa.parikṣepa.uttara.ādikaṁ bandhanam //*  
*02.32.13/ aṅkuśa.veṇu.yantra.ādikam upakaraṇam //*  
*02.32.14/ vaijayantī.kṣura.pramāla.āstaraṇa.kuthā.ādikaṁ bhūṣaṇam //*  
*02.32.15/ varma.tomara.śara.āvāpa.yantra.ādikaḥ sāṁgrāmika.alaṁkāraḥ //*  
*02.32.16/ cikitsaka.anīkastha.ārohaka.ādhoraṇa.hastipa.kaupacārika.vidhā.pācaka.yāvasika.pādapāśika.kuṭīr.rakṣaka.aupaśayaika.ādir aupasthāyika.vargaḥ //*  
*02.32.17/ cikitsaka.kuṭī.rakṣa.vidhā.pācakāḥ prastha.odanaṁ sneha.prasṛtiṁ kṣāra.lavaṇayoś ca dvi.palikaṁ hareyuḥ, daśa.palaṁ māṁsasya, anyatra cikitsakebhyaḥ //*  
*02.32.18/ pathi.vyādhi.karma.mada.jarā.abhitaptānāṁ cikitsakāḥ pratikuryuḥ //*  
*02.32.19/ sthānasya^aśuddhir yavasasya^agrahaṇaṁ sthale śāyanam abhāge ghātaḥ para.ārohaṇam akāle yānam abhūmāv atīrthe^avatāraṇaṁ taru.ṣaṇḍa ity atyaya.sthānāni //*  
*02.32.20/ tam eṣāṁ bhakta.vetanād ādadīta //*  
*02.32.21ab/ tisro nīrājanāḥ kāryāś cāturmāsya.ṛtu.saṁdhiṣu /*  
*02.32.21cd/ bhūtānāṁ kṛṣṇa.saṁdhī^ijyāḥ senānyaḥ śukla.saṁdhuṣu //*  
*02.32.22ab/ danta.mūla.parīṇāha.dvi.guṇaṁ projjhya kalpayet /*  
*02.32.22cd/ abde dvy.ardhe nadī.jānāṁ pañca.abde parvata.okasām //E*  
*(ṣuperintendent of chariots)*

*02.33.01/ aśva.adhyakṣeṇa ratha.adhyakṣo vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*02.33.02/ sa ratha.karma.antān kārayet //*  
*02.33.03/ daśa.puruṣo dvādaśa.antaro rathaḥ //*  
*02.33.04/ tasmād eka.antara.avarā ā.ṣaḍ.antarād iti sapta rathāḥ //*  
*02.33.05/ deva.ratha.puṣya.ratha.sāṁgrāmika.pāriyāṇika.para.pura.abhiyānika.vainayikāṁś ca rathān kārayet //*  
*02.33.06/ iṣv.astra.praharaṇa.āvaraṇa.upakaraṇa.kalpanāḥ sārathi.rathika.rathyānāṁ ca karmasv āyogaṁ vidyāt, ā.karmabhyaś ca bhakta.vetanaṁ bhṛtānām abhṛtānāṁ ca yogyā.rakṣā.anuṣṭhānam artha.māna.karma ca //*  
*02.33.07/ etena patty.adhyakṣo vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*02.33.08/ sa maula.bhṛta.śreṇi.mitra.amitra.aṭavī.balānāṁ sāra.phalgutāṁ vidyāt, nimna.sthala.prakāśa.kūṭa.khanaka.ākāśa.divā.rātri.yuddha.vyāyāmaṁ ca, āyogam ayogaṁ ca karmasu //*  
*02.33.09/ ted eva senā.patiḥ sarva.yuddha.praharaṇa.vidyā.vinīto hasty.aśva.ratha.caryā.saṁghuṣṭaś catur.aṅgasya balasya^anuṣṭhāna.adhiṣṭhānaṁ vidyāt //*  
*02.33.10/ sva.bhūmiṁ yuddha.kālaṁ pratyanīkam abhinna.bhedanaṁ bhinna.saṁdhānaṁ saṁhata.bhedanaṁ bhinna.vadhaṁ durga.vadhaṁ yātrā.kālaṁ ca paśyet //*  
*02.33.11ab/ tūrya.dhvaja.patākābhir vyūha.saṁjñāḥ prakalpayet /*  
*02.33.11cd/ sthāne yāne praharaṇe sainyānāṁ vinaye rataḥ //E*  
*(ṣuperintendent of passports)*  
*(ṣuperintendent of pasture lands)*  
*02.34.01/ mudrā.adhyakṣo mudrāṁ māṣakeṇa dadyāt //*  
*02.34.02/ sa-mudro jana.padaṁ praveṣṭuṁ niṣkramituṁ vā labheta //*  
*02.34.03/ dvādaśa.paṇam amudro jānapado dadyāt //*  
*02.34.04/ kūṭa.mudrāyāṁ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ tiro.jana.padasya^uttamaḥ //*  
*02.34.05/ vivīta.adhyakṣo mudrāṁ paśyet //*  
*02.34.06/ grāma.antareṣu ca vivītaṁ sthāpayet //*  
*02.34.07/ cora.vyāla.bhayān.nimna.araṇyāni śodhayet //*  
*02.34.08/ anudake kūpa.setu.bandha.utsān sthāpayet, puṣpa.phala.vāṭāṁś ca //*  
*02.34.09/ lubdhaka.śva.gaṇinaḥ parivrajeyur araṇyāni //*  
*02.34.10/ taskara.amitra.abhyāgame śaṅkha.dundubhi.śabdam agrāhyāḥ kuryuḥ śaila.vṛkṣa.adhirūḍhā vā śīghra.vāhanā vā //*  
*02.34.11/ amitra.aṭavī.saṁcāraṁ ca rājño gṛha.kapotair mudrā.yuktair hārayet, dhūma.agni.paramparayā vā //*  
*02.34.12ab/ dravya.hasti.vana.ājīvaṁ vartanīṁ cora.rakṣaṇam /*  
*02.34.12cd/ sārtha.ativāhyaṁ go.rakṣyaṁ vyavahāraṁ ca kārayet //E*  
*(Activity of the adiministrator)*  
*(ṣecret agents in the disguise of householders, traders, and ascetics)*  
*02.35.01/ samāhartā caturdhā jana.apdaṁ vibhajya jyeṣṭha.madhyama.kaniṣṭha.vibhāgena grāma.agraṁ parihārakam āyudhīyaṁ dhānya.paśu.hiraṇya.kupya.viṣṭi.pratikaram idam etāvad iti nibandhayet //*  
*02.35.02/ tat.pradiṣṭaḥ pañca.grāmīṁ daśa.grāmīṁ vā gopaś cintayet //*  
*02.35.03/ sīma.avarodhena grāma.agram, kṛṣṭa.akṛṣṭa.sthala.kedāra.ārāma.ṣaṇḍa.vāṭa.vana.vāstu.caitya.deva.gṛha.setu.bandha.śmaśāna.sattra.prapā.puṇya.sthāna.vivīta.pathi.saṅkhyānena kṣetra.agram, tena sīmnāṁ kṣetrāṇāṁ ca karada.akarada.saṅkhyānena //*  
*02.35.04/ teṣu ca^etāvac^cātur.vārṇyam, etāvantaḥ karṣaka.go.rakṣaka.vaidehaka.kāru.karma.kara.dāsāś ca, etāvac ca dvi.pada.catuṣ.padam, idaṁ ca^eṣu hiraṇyalviṣṭi.śulka.daṇḍaṁ samuttiṣṭhati^iti //*  
*02.35.05/ kulānāṁ ca strī.puruṣāṇāṁ bāla.vṛddha.karma.caritra.ājīva.vyaya.parimāṇaṁ vidyāt //*  
*02.35.06/ evaṁ ca jana.pada.catur.bhāgaṁ sthānikaś cintayet //*  
*02.35.07/ gopa.sthānika.sthāneṣu pradeṣṭāraḥ kārya.karaṇaṁ bali.pragrahaṁ ca kuryuḥ //*  
*02.35.08/ samāhartṛ.pradiṣṭāś ca gṛha.patika.vyañjanā yeṣu grāmeṣu praṇihitās teṣāṁ grāmāṇāṁ kṣetra.gṛha.kula.agraṁ vidyuḥ, māna.saṁjātābhyāṁ kṣetrāṇi bhoga.parihārābhyāṁ gṛhāṇi varṇa.karmabhyāṁ kulāni ca //*  
*02.35.09/ teṣāṁ jaṅgha.agram āya.vyayau ca vidyuḥ //*  
*02.35.10/ prasthita.āgatānāṁ ca pravāsa.āvāsa.kāraṇam, anarthyānāṁ ca strī.puruṣāṇāṁ cāra.pracāraṁ ca vidyuḥ //*  
*02.35.11/ evaṁ vaidehaka.vyañjanāḥ sva.bhūmijānāṁ rāja.paṇyānāṁ khani.setu.vana.karma.anta.kṣetrajānāṁ pramāṇam arghaṁ ca vidyuḥ //*  
*02.35.12/ para.bhūmi.jātānāṁ vāri.sthala.patha.upayātānāṁ sāra.phalgu.puṇyānāṁ karmasu ca śulka.vartany.ātivāhika.gulma.tara.deya.bhāga.bhakta.paṇya.agāra.pramāṇaṁ vidyuḥ //*  
*02.35.13/ evaṁ samāhartṛ.pradiṣṭās tāpasa.vyañjanāḥ karṣaka.go.rakṣaka.vaidehakānām adhyakṣāṇāṁ ca śauca.āśaucaṁ vidyuḥ //*  
*02.35.14/ purāṇa cora.vyañjanāś ca^antevāsinaś caitya.catuṣpatha.śūnya.pada.uda.pāna.nadī.nipāna.tīrtha.āyatana.āśrama.araṇya.śaila.vana.gahaneṣu stena.amitra.pravīra.puruṣāṇāṁ ca praveśana.sthāna.gamana.prayojanāny upalabheran //*  
*02.35.15ab/ samāhartā jana.padaṁ cintayed evam utthitaḥ /*  
*02.35.15cd/ cintayeyuś ca saṁsthās tāḥ saṁsthāś ca^anyāḥ sva.yonayaḥ //E*  
*(ṛules for the city-superintendent)*  
*02.36.01/ samāhartṛvan nāgariko nagaraṁ cintayet //*  
*02.36.02/ daśa.kulīṁ gopo viṁśati.kulīṁ catvāriṁśat.kulīṁ vā //*  
*02.36.03/ sa tasyāṁ strī.puruṣāṇāṁ jāti.gotra.nāma.karmabhiḥ jaṅgha.agram āya.vyayau ca vidyāt //*  
*02.36.04/ evaṁ durga.catur.bhāgaṁ sthānikaś cintayet //*  
*02.36.05/ dharma.āvasathinaḥ pāṣaṇḍi.pathikān āvedya vāsayeyuḥ, sva.pratyayāś ca tapasvinaḥ śrotriyāṁś ca //*  
*02.36.06/ kāru.śilpinaḥ sva.karma.sthāneṣu sva.janaṁ vāsayeyuḥ, vaidehakāś ca^anyonyaṁ sva.karma.sthāneṣu //*  
*02.36.07/ paṇyānām adeśa.kāla.vikretāram asvakaraṇaṁ ca nivedayeyuḥ //*  
*02.36.08/ śauṇḍika.pākva.māṁsika.audanika.rūpa.ājīvāḥ parijñātam āvāsayeyuḥ //*  
*02.36.09/ ativyaya.kartāram atyāhita.karmāṇaṁ ca nivedayeyuḥ //*  
*02.36.10/ cikitsakaḥ pracchanna.vraṇa.pratīkāra.kārayitāram apathya.kāriṇaṁ ca gṛha.svāmī ca nivedya gopa.sthānikayor mucyeta, anyathā tulya.doṣaḥ syāt //*  
*02.36.11/ prasthita.āgatau ca nivedayet, anyathā rātri.doṣaṁ bhajeta //*  
*02.36.12/ kṣema.rātriṣu tri.paṇaṁ dadyāt //*  
*02.36.13/ pathika.utpathikāś ca bahir.antaś ca nagarasya deva.gṛha.puṇya.sthāna.vana.śmaśāneṣu sa-vraṇam aniṣṭa.upakaraṇam udbhāṇḍī.kṛtam āvignam atisvapnam adhva.klāntam apūrvaṁ vā gṛhṇīyuḥ //*  
*02.36.14/ evam abhyantare śūnya.niveśa.āveśana.śauṇḍika.audanika.pākva.māṁsika.dyūta.pāṣaṇḍa.āvāseṣu vicayaṁ kuryuḥ //*  
*02.36.15/ agni.pratīkāraṁ ca grīṣme //*  
*02.36.16/ madhyamayor ahnaś catur.bhāgayor aṣṭa.bhāgo^agni.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.36.17/ bahir.adhiśrayaṇaṁ vā kuryuḥ //*  
*02.36.18/ pādaḥ pañca.ghaṭīnāṁ kumbha.droṇi.nihśreṇī.paraśu.śūrpa.aṅkuśa.kaca.grahaṇī.dṛtīnāṁ ca^akaraṇe //*  
*02.36.19/ tṛṇa.kaṭac.channāny apanayet //*  
*02.36.20/ agni.jīvina ekasthān vāsayet //*  
*02.36.21/ sva.gṛha.pradvāreṣu gṛha.svāmino vaseyuḥ asampātino rātrau //*  
*02.36.22/ rathyāsu kuṭa.vrajāḥ sahasraṁ tiṣṭheyuḥ, catuṣpatha.dvāra.rāja.parigraheṣu ca //*  
*02.36.23/ pradīptam anabhidhāvato gṛha.svāmino dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, ṣaṭ.paṇo^avakrayiṇaḥ //*  
*02.36.24/ pramādād dīpteṣu catuṣ.pañcāśat.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.36.25/ pradīpiko^agninā vadhyaḥ //*  
*02.36.26/ pāṁsu.nyāse rathyāyām aṣṭa.bhāgo daṇḍaḥ, paṅka.udaka.samnirodhe pādaḥ //*  
*02.36.27/ rāja.mārge dvi.guṇaḥ //*  
*02.36.28/ paṇya.sthāna.udaka.sthāna.deva.gṛha.rāja.parigraheṣu paṇa.uttarā viṣṭā.daṇḍāḥ, mūtreṣv ardha.daṇḍāḥ //*  
*02.36.29/ bhaiṣajya.vyādhi.bhaya.nimittam adaṇḍyāḥ //*  
*02.36.30/ mārjāra.śva.nakula.sarpa.pretānāṁ nagarasya.antar.utsarge tri.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, khara.uṣṭra.aśvatara.aśva.pretānāṁ ṣaṭ.paṇaḥ, manuṣya.pretānāṁ pañcāśat.paṇaḥ //*  
*02.36.31/ mārga.viparyāse śava.dvārād anyataś ca śava.nirṇayane pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.36.32/ dvāḥ.sthānāṁ dviśatam //*  
*02.36.33/ śmaśānād anyatra nyāse dahane ca dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.36.34/ viṣaṇṇa.alikam ubhayator ātraṁ yāma.tūryam //*  
*02.36.35/ tūrya.śabde rājño gṛha.abhyāśe sa.pāda.paṇaṁ.akṣaṇa.tāḍanaṁ prathama.paścima.yāmikam, madhyama.yāmikaṁ dvi.guṇam, antaś.catur.guṇam //*  
*02.36.36/ śaṅkanīye deśe liṅge pūrva.apadāne ca gṛhītam anuyuñjīta //*  
*02.36.37/ rāja.parigraha.upagamane nagara.rakṣā.ārohaṇe ca madhyamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.36.38/ sūtikā.cikitsaka.preta.pradīpa.yāna.nāgarika.tūrya.prekṣā.agni.nimittaṁ mudrābhiś ca^agrāhyāḥ //*  
*02.36.39/ cāra.rātriṣu pracchanna.viparīta.veṣāḥ pravrajitā daṇḍa.śastra.hastāś ca manuṣyā doṣato daṇḍyāḥ //*  
*02.36.40/ rakṣiṇām avāryaṁ vārayatāṁ vāryaṁ ca^āvārayatāṁ kṣaṇa.dvi.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*02.36.41/ striyaṁ dāsīm adhimehayatāṁ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, adāsīṁ madhyamaḥ, kṛta.avarodhām uttamaḥ, kula.striyaṁ vadhaḥ //*  
*02.36.42/ cetana.acetanikaṁ rātri.doṣam aśaṁsato nāgarikasya doṣa.anurūpo daṇḍaḥ, pramāda.sthāne ca //*  
*02.36.43/ nityam udaka.sthāna.mārga.bhramac.channa.patha.vapra.prākāra.rakṣā.avekṣaṇaṁ naṣṭa.prasmṛta.apasṛtānāṁ ca rakṣaṇam //*  
*02.36.44/ bandhana.agāre ca bāla.vṛddha.vyādhita.anāthānāṁ jāta.nakṣatra.paurṇamāsīṣu visargaḥ //*  
*02.36.45/ paṇya.śīlāḥ samaya.anubaddhā vā doṣa.niṣkrayaṁ dadyuḥ //*

### 3

*03.1.01/ dharmasthās trayas trayo^amātyā jana.pada.saṁdhi.saṁgrahaṇa.droṇa.mukha.sthānīyeṣu vyāvahārikān arthān kuryuḥ //*  
*03.1.02/ tirohita.antar.agāra.nakta.araṇya.upadhy.upahvara.kṛtāṁś ca vyavahārān pratiṣedhayeyuḥ //*  
*03.1.03/ kartuḥ kārayituḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.1.04/ śrotṛṛṇām eka.ekaṁ pratyardha.daṇḍāḥ //*  
*03.1.05/ śraddheyānāṁ tu dravya.vyapanayaḥ //*  
*03.1.06/ parokṣeṇa^adhika.ṛṇa.grahaṇam avaktavya.karā vā tirohitāḥ sidhyeyuḥ //*  
*03.1.07/ dāya.nikṣepa.upanidhi.vivāha.yuktāḥ strīṇām aniṣkāsinīnāṁ vyādhitānāṁ ca^amūḍha.saṁjñānām antar.agāra.kṛtāḥ sidhyeyuḥ //*  
*03.1.08/ sāhasa.anupraveśa.kalaha.vivāha.rāja.niyoga.yuktāḥ pūrva.rātra.vyavahāriṇāṁ ca rātri.kṛtāḥ sidhyeyuḥ //*  
*03.1.09/ sārtha.vraja.āśrama.vyādha.cāraṇa.madhyeṣv araṇya.carāṇām araṇya.kṛtāḥ sidhyeyuḥ //*  
*03.1.10/ gūḍha.ājīviṣu ca^upadhi.kṛtāḥ sidhyeyuḥ //*  
*03.1.11/ mithaḥ.samavāye ca^upahvara.kṛtāḥ sidhyeyuḥ //*  
*03.1.12/ ato^anyathā na sidhyeyuḥ, apāśrayavadbhiś ca kṛtāḥ, pitṛmatā putreṇa, pitrā putravatā, niṣkulena bhrātrā, kaniṣṭhena^avibhakta.aṁśena, patimatyā putravatyā ca striyā, dāsa.āhitakābhyām, aprāpta.atīta.vyavahārābhyām, abhiśasta.pravrajita.nyaṅga.vyasanibhiś ca, anyatra niṣṛṣṭa.vyavahārebhyaḥ //*  
*03.1.13/ tatra^api kruddhena^ārtena mattena.unmattena^avagṛhītena vā kṛtā vyavahārā na sidhyeyuḥ //*  
*03.1.14/ kartṛ.kārayitṛ.śrotṛṛṇāṁ pṛthag yathā.uktā daṇḍāḥ //*  
*03.1.15/ sve sve tu varge deśe kāle ca sva.karaṇa.kṛtāḥ sampūrṇa.ācārāḥ śuddha.deśā dṛṣṭa.rūpa.lakṣaṇa.pramāṇa.guṇāḥ sarva.vyavahārāḥ sidhyeyuḥ //*  
*03.1.16/ paścimaṁ ca^eṣāṁ karaṇam ādeśa.ādhivarjaṁ śraddheyam // iti vyavahāra.sthāpanā /*  
*03.1.17/ saṁvatsaram ṛtuṁ māsaṁ pakṣaṁ divasaṁ karaṇam adhikaraṇam ṛṇaṁ vedaka.āvedakayoḥ kṛta.samartha.avasthayor deśa.grāma.jāti.gotra.nāma.karmāṇi ca^abhilikhya vādi.prativādi.praśnān artha.ānupūrvyā niveśayet //*  
*03.1.18/ niviṣṭāṁś ca^avekṣeta //*  
*03.1.19/ nibaddhaṁ vādam utsṛjya^anyaṁ vādaṁ saṁkrāmati, pūrva.uktaṁ paścimena^arthena na^abhisaṁdhatte, para.vākyam anabhigrāhyam abhigrāhya^avatiṣṭhate, pratijñāya deśaṁ nirdiśa^ity ukte na nirdiśati, hīna.deśam adeśaṁ vā nirdiśati, nirdiṣṭād deśād anyaṁ deśam upasthāpayati, upasthite deśe^artha.vacanaṁ na^evam ity apavyayate, sākṣibhir avadhṛtaṁ na^icchati,*

*03.2.01/ vivāha.pūrvo vyavahāraḥ //*  
*03.2.02/ kanyā.dānaṁ kanyām alaṁkṛtya brāhmo vivāhaḥ //*  
*03.2.03/ saha.dharma.caryā prājāpatyaḥ //*  
*03.2.04/ go.mithuna.ādānād ārṣaḥ //*  
*03.2.05/ antar.vedyām ṛtvije dānād daivaḥ //*  
*03.2.06/ mithaḥ.samavāyād gāndharvaḥ //*  
*03.2.07/ śulka.ādānād āsuraḥ //*  
*03.2.08/ prasahya.ādānād rākṣasaḥ //*  
*03.2.09/ supta.matta.ādānāt paiśācaḥ //*

*03.2.11/ tau hi śulka.harau duhituḥ, anyatara.abhāve^anyataro vā //*  
*03.2.12/ dvitīyaṁ śulkaṁ strī hareta //*

*03.2.14/ vṛttir ābandhyaṁ vā strī.dhanam //*  
*03.2.15/ para.dvi.sāhasrā sthāpyā vṛttiḥ, ābandhya.aniyamaḥ //*  
*03.2.16/ tad ātma.putra.snuṣā.bharmaṇi pravāsa.apratividhāne ca bhāryāyā bhoktum adoṣaḥ, pratirodhaka.vyādhi.durbhikṣa.bhaya.pratīkāre dharma.kārye ca patyuḥ, sambhūya vā dampatyor mithunaṁ prajātayoḥ //*  
*03.2.17/ tri.varṣa.upabhuktaṁ ca dharmiṣṭheṣu vivāheṣu na^anuyuñjīta //*  
*03.2.18/ gāndharva.āsura.upabhuktaṁ sa-vṛddhikam ubhayaṁ dāpyeta, rākṣasa.paiśāca.upabhuktaṁ steyaṁ dadyāt //*  
*03.2.19/ mṛte bhartari dharma.kāmā tadānīm eva sthāpya^ābharaṇaṁ śulka.śeṣaṁ ca labheta //*  
*03.2.20/ labdhvā vā vindamānā sa-vṛddhikam ubhayaṁ dāpyeta //*  
*03.2.21/ kuṭumba.kāmā tu śvaśura.pati.dattaṁ niveśa.kāle labheta //*  
*03.2.22/ niveśa.kālaṁ hi dīrgha.pravāse vyākhyāsyāmaḥ //*  
*03.2.23/ śvaśura.prātilomyena vā niviṣṭā śvaśura.pati.dattaṁ jīyeta //*  
*03.2.24/ jñāti.hastād.abhimṛṣṭāyā jñātayo yathā.gṛhītaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*03.2.25/ nyāya.upagatāyāḥ pratipattā strī.dhanaṁ gopayet //*  
*03.2.26/ pati.dāyaṁ vindamānā jīyeta //*  
*03.2.27/ dharma.kāmā bhuñjīta //*  
*03.2.28/ putravatī vindamānā strī.dhanaṁ jīyeta //*  
*03.2.29/ tat tu strī.dhanaṁ putrā hareyuḥ //*  
*03.2.30/ putra.bharaṇa.arthaṁ vā vindamānā putra.arthaṁ sphātī.kuryāt //*  
*03.2.31/ bahu.puruṣa.prajānāṁ putrāṇāṁ yathā.pitṛ.dattaṁ strī.dhanam avasthāpayet //*  
*03.2.32/ kāma.karaṇīyam api strī.dhanaṁ vindamānā putra.saṁsthaṁ kuryāt //*  
*03.2.33/ aputrā pati.śayanaṁ pālayantī guru.samīpe strī.dhanam āyuḥ.kṣayād bhuñjīta //*  
*03.2.34/ āpad.arthaṁ hi strī.dhanam //*  
*03.2.35/ ūrdhvaṁ dāyādaṁ gacchet //*  
*03.2.36/ jīvati bhartari mṛtāyāḥ putrā duhitaraś ca strī.dhanaṁ vibhajeran, aputrāyā duhitaraḥ, tad.abhāve bhartā //*  
*03.2.37/ śulkam anvādheyam anyad vā bandhubhir dattaṁ bāndhavā hareyuḥ // iti strī.dhana.kalpaḥ /*  
*03.2.38/ varṣāṇy aṣṭāv aprajāyamānām aputrāṁ vandhyāṁ ca^ākāṅkṣeta, daśa nindum, dvādaśa kanyā.prasavinīm //*  
*03.2.39/ tataḥ putra.arthī dvitīyāṁ vindeta //*

*03.2.41/ śulkaṁ strī.dhanam aśulka.strī.dhanāyās tat.pramāṇam ādhivedanikam anurūpāṁ ca vṛttiṁ dattvā bahvīr api vindeta //*  
*03.2.42/ putra.arthā hi striyaḥ //*

*03.2.44/ tīrtha.gūhana.āgamane ṣaṇ.ṇavatir daṇḍaḥ //*

*03.2.47/ strī tu putra.artham evaṁ.bhūtaṁ vā^upagacchet //*

*03.5.01/ anīśvarāḥ pitṛmantaḥ sthita.pitṛ.mātṛkāḥ putrāḥ //*

*03.5.05/ tāvad avicchinnaḥ piṇḍo bhavati //*  
*03.5.06/ vicchinna.piṇḍāḥ sarve samaṁ vibhajeran //*  
*03.5.07/ apitṛ.dravyā vibhakta.pitṛ.dravyā vā saha jīvantaḥ punar vibhajeran //*  
*03.5.08/ yataś ca^uttiṣṭheta sa dvy.aṁśaṁ labheta //*  
*03.5.09/ dravyam aputrasya sodaryā bhrātaraḥ saha.jīvino vā hareyuḥ kanyāś ca //*  
*03.5.10/ rikthaṁ putravataḥ putrā duhitaro vā dharmiṣṭheṣu vivāheṣu jātāḥ //*  
*03.5.11/ tad.abhāve pitā dharamāṇaḥ //*  
*03.5.12/ pitr.abhāve bhrātaro bhrātṛ.putrāś ca //*

*03.5.16/ jīvad.vibhāge pitā na^ekaṁ viśeṣayet //*  
*03.5.17/ na ca^ekam akāraṇān nirvibhajeta //*

*03.6.01/ eka.strī.putrāṇāṁ jyeṣṭha.aṁśaḥ - brāhmaṇānām ajāḥ, kṣatriyāṇām aśvāḥ, vaiśyānāṁ gāvaḥ, śūdrāṇām avayaḥ //*  
*03.6.02/ kāṇa.laṅgās teṣāṁ madhyama.aṁśaḥ, bhinna.varṇāḥ kaniṣṭha.aṁśaḥ //*  
*03.6.03/ catuṣpada.abhāve ratna.varjānāṁ daśānāṁ bhāgaṁ dravyāṇām ekaṁ jyeṣṭho haret //*  
*03.6.04/ pratimukta.svadhā.pāśo hi bhavati //*  
*03.6.05/ ity auśanaso vibhāgaḥ //*  
*03.6.06/ pituḥ parivāpād yānam ābharaṇaṁ ca jyeṣṭha.aṁśaḥ, śayana.āsanaṁ bhukta.kāṁsyaṁ ca madhyama.aṁśaḥ, kṛṣṇaṁ dhānya.āyasaṁ gṛha.parivāpo go.śakaṭaṁ ca kaniṣṭha.aṁśaḥ //*  
*03.6.07/ śeṣa.dravyāṇām eka.dravyasya vā samo vibhāgaḥ //*  
*03.6.08/ adāyādā bhaginyaḥ, mātuḥ parivāpād bhukta.kāṁsya.ābharaṇa.bhāginyaḥ //*  
*03.6.09/ mānuṣa.hīno jyeṣṭhas tṛtīyam aṁśaṁ jyeṣṭha.aṁśāl labheta, caturtham anyāya.vṛttiḥ, nivṛtta.dharma.kāryo vā //*  
*03.6.10/ kāma.ācāraḥ sarvaṁ jīyeta //*  
*03.6.11/ tena madhyama.kaniṣṭhau vyākhyātau //*  
*03.6.12/ tayor mānuṣa.upeto jyeṣṭha.aṁśād ardhaṁ labheta //*  
*03.6.13/ nānā.strī.putrāṇāṁ tu saṁskṛta.asaṁskṛtayoḥ kanyā.kṛta.kṣatayor abhāve ca ekasyāḥ putrayor yamayor vā pūrva.janmanā jyeṣṭha.bhāvaḥ //*  
*03.6.14/ sūta.māgadha.vrātya.ratha.kārāṇām aiśvaryato vibhāgaḥ //*  
*03.6.15/ śeṣās tam upajīveyuḥ //*  
*03.6.16/ anīśvarāḥ sama.vibhāgāḥ //*  
*03.6.17/ cāturvarṇya.putrāṇāṁ brāhmaṇī.putraś caturo^aṁśān haret, kṣatriyā.putra.strīn aṁśān, vaiśyā.putro dvāv aṁśau, ekaṁ śūdrā.putraḥ //*  
*03.6.18/ tena tri.varṇa.dvi.varṇa.putra.vibhāgaḥ kṣatriya.vaiśyayor vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*03.6.19/ brāhmaṇasya^anantarā.putras tulya.aṁśaḥ //*  
*03.6.20/ kṣatriya.vaiśyayor ardha.aṁśaḥ tulya.aṁśo vā mānuṣa.upetaḥ //*  
*03.6.21/ tulya.atulyayor eka.putraḥ sarvaṁ haret, bandhūṁś ca bibhṛyāt //*  
*03.6.22/ brāhmaṇānāṁ tu pāraśavas tṛtīyam aṁśaṁ labheta, dvāv aṁśau sapiṇḍaḥ kulyo vā^āsannaḥ, svadhā.dāna.hetoḥ //*

*03.7.30/ ta ete pratilomāḥ svadharma.atikramād rājñaḥ sambhavanti //*  
*03.7.31/ ugrān naiṣādyāṁ kukkuṭaḥ, viparyaye pulkasaḥ //*  
*03.7.32/ vaidehikāyām ambaṣṭhād vaiṇaḥ, viparyaye kuśīlavaḥ //*

*03.10.01/ karma.udaka.mārgam ucitaṁ rundhataḥ kurvato^anucitaṁ vā pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, setu.kūpa.puṇya.sthāna.caitya.deva.āyatanāni ca para.bhūmau niveśayataḥ //*  
*03.10.02/ pūrva.anuvṛttaṁ dharma.setum ādhānaṁ vikrayaṁ vā nayato nāyayato vā madhyamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, śrotṛṛṇām uttamaḥ, anyatra bhagna.utsṛṣṭāt //*  
*03.10.03/ svāmy abhāve grāmāḥ puṇya.śīlā vā pratikuryuḥ //*  
*03.10.04/ pathi.pramāṇaṁ durga.niveśe vyākhyātam //*  
*03.10.05/ kṣudra.paśu.manuṣya.pathaṁ rundhato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, mahā.paśu.pathaṁ catur.viṁśati.paṇaḥ, hasti.kṣetra.pathaṁ catuṣ.pañcāśat.paṇaḥ, setu.vana.pathaṁ ṣaṭ.śataḥ, śmaśāna.grāma.pathaṁ dviśataḥ, droṇa.mukha.pathaṁ pañca.śataḥ, sthānīya.rāṣṭra.vivīta.pathaṁ sāhasraḥ //*  
*03.10.06/ atikarṣaṇe ca^eṣāṁ daṇḍa.caturthā daṇḍāḥ //*  
*03.10.07/ karṣaṇe pūrva.uktāḥ //*  
*03.10.08/ kṣetrikasya^akṣipataḥ kṣetram upavāsasya vā tyajato bīja.kāle dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra doṣa.upanipāta.aviṣahyebhyaḥ //*  
*03.10.09/ karadāḥ karadeṣv ādhānaṁ vikrayaṁ vā kuryuḥ, brahma.deyikā brahma.deyikeṣu //*  
*03.10.10/ anyathā pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.10.11/ karadasya vā^akarada.grāmaṁ praviśataḥ //*  
*03.10.12/ karadaṁ tu praviśataḥ sarva.dravyeṣu prākāmyaṁ syāt, anyatra^agārāt //*  
*03.10.13/ tad apy asmai dadyāt //*  
*03.10.14/ anādeyam akṛṣato^anyaḥ pañca.varṣāṇy upabhujya prayāsa.niṣkrayeṇa dadyāt //*  
*03.10.15/ akaradāḥ paratra vasanto bhogam upajīveyuḥ //*  
*03.10.16/ grāma.arthena grāmikaṁ vrajantam upavāsāḥ paryāyeṇa^anugaccheyuḥ //*  
*03.10.17/ ananugacchantaḥ paṇa.ardha.paṇikaṁ yojanaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*03.10.18/ grāmikasya grāmād astena.pāradārikaṁ nirasyataś catur.viṁśati.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, grāmasya^uttamaḥ //*  
*03.10.19/ nirastasya praveśo hy abhigamena vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*03.10.20/ stambhaiḥ samantato grāmād dhanuḥ.śata.apakṛṣṭam upasālaṁ kārayet //*  
*03.10.21/ paśu.pracāra.arthaṁ vivītam ālavanena^upajīveyuḥ //*  
*03.10.22/ vivītaṁ bhakṣayitvā^apasṛtānām uṣṭra.mahiṣāṇāṁ pādikaṁ rūpaṁ gṛhṇīyuḥ, gava.aśva.kharāṇāṁ ca^ardha.pādikam, kṣudra.paśūnāṁ ṣoḍaśa.bhāgikam //*  
*03.10.23/ bhakṣayitvā niṣaṇṇānām eta eva dvi.guṇā daṇḍāḥ, parivasatāṁ catur.guṇāḥ //*  
*03.10.24/ grāma.deva.vṛṣā vā^anirdaśa.ahā vā dhenur ukṣāṇo go.vṛṣāś ca^adaṇḍyāḥ //*  
*03.10.25/ sasya.bhakṣaṇe sasya.upaghātaṁ niṣpattitaḥ parisaṁkhyāya dvi.guṇaṁ dāpayet //*  
*03.10.26/ svāminaś ca^anivedya cārayato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇdaḥ, pramuñcataś catur.viṁśati.paṇaḥ //*  
*03.10.27/ pālinām ardha.daṇḍāḥ //*

*03.10.37/ prekṣāyām anaṁśadaḥ, sa-sva.jano na prekṣeta //*

*03.10.39/ sarva.hitam ekasya bruvataḥ kuryur ājñām //*

*03.11.01/ sapāda.paṇā dharmyā māsa.vṛddhiḥ paṇa.śatasya, pañca.paṇā vyāvahārikī, daśa.paṇā kāntāragāṇām, viṁśati.paṇā sāmudrāṇām //*  
*03.11.02/ tataḥ paraṁ kartuḥ kārayituś ca pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, śrotṛṛṇām eka.ekaṁ pratyardha.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.11.03/ rājany ayoga.kṣema.āvahe tu dhanika.dhāraṇikayoś caritram avekṣeta //*  
*03.11.04/ dhānya.vṛddhiḥ sasya.niṣpattāv upārdhā, paraṁ mūlya.kṛtā vardheta //*  
*03.11.05/ prakṣepa.vṛddhir udayād ardhaṁ samnidhāna.sannā vārṣikī deyā //*  
*03.11.06/ cira.pravāsaḥ stambha.praviṣṭo vā mūlya.dvi.guṇaṁ dadyāt //*  
*03.11.07/ akṛtvā vṛddhiṁ sādhayato vardhayato vā, mūlyaṁ vā vṛddhim āropya śrāvayato bandha.catur.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.11.08/ tuccha.śrāvaṇāyām abhūta.catur.guṇaḥ //*  
*03.11.09/ tasya tri.bhāgam ādātā dadyāt, śeṣaṁ pradātā //*  
*03.11.10/ dīrgha.sattra.vyādhi.guru.kula.uparuddhaṁ bālam asāraṁ vā na^ṛṇam anuvardheta //*  
*03.11.11/ mucyamānam ṛṇam apratigṛhṇato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.11.12/ kāraṇa.apadeśena nivṛtta.vṛddhikam anyatra tiṣṭhet //*  
*03.11.13/ daśa.varṣa.upekṣitam ṛṇam apratigrāhyam, anyatra bāla.vṛddha.vyādhita.vyasani.proṣita.deśa.tyāga.rājya.vibhramebhyaḥ //*  
*03.11.14/ pretasya putrāḥ kusīdaṁ dadyuḥ, dāyādā vā riktha.harāḥ, saha.grāhiṇaḥ, pratibhuvo vā //*  
*03.11.15/ na prātibhāvyam anyat //*  
*03.11.16/ asāraṁ bāla.prātibhāvyam //*  
*03.11.17/ asaṁkhyāta.deśa.kālaṁ tu putrāḥ pautrā dāyādā vā rikthaṁ haramāṇā dadyuḥ //*  
*03.11.18/ jīvita.vivāha.bhūmi.prātibhāvyam asaṁkhyāta.deśa.kālaṁ tu putrāḥ pautrā vā vaheyuḥ //*  
*03.11.19/ nānā^ṛṇa.samavāye tu na^ekaṁ dvau yugapad abhivadeyātām, anyatra pratiṣṭhamānāt //*  
*03.11.20/ tatra^api gṛhīta.ānupūrvyā rāja.śrotriya.dravyaṁ vā pūrvaṁ pratipādayet //*  
*03.11.21/ dampatyoḥ pitā.putrayoḥ bhrātṛṛṇāṁ ca^avibhaktānāṁ paraspara..kṛtam ṛṇam asādhyam //*  
*03.11.22/ agrāhyāḥ karma.kāleṣu karṣakā rāja.puruṣāś ca //*  
*03.11.23/ strī ca^apratiśrāviṇī pati.kṛtam ṛṇam, anyatra go.pālaka.ardha.sītikebhyaḥ //*  
*03.11.24/ patis tu grāhyaḥ strī.kṛtam ṛṇam, aprati.vidhāya proṣita iti //*  
*03.11.25/ sampratipattāv uttamaḥ //*  
*03.11.26/ asmapratipattau tu sākṣiṇaḥ pramāṇaṁ prātyayikāḥ śucayo^anumatā vā trayo^avara.ardhyāḥ //*  
*03.11.27/ pakṣa.anumatau vā dvau, ṛṇaṁ prati na tv eva^ekaḥ //*  
*03.11.28/ pratiṣiddhāḥ syāla.sahāya.anvarthi.dhanika.dhāraṇika.vairi.nyaṅga.dhṛta.daṇḍāḥ, pūrve ca^avyavahāryāḥ //*  
*03.11.29/ rāja.śrotriya.grāma.bhṛtaka.kuṣṭhi.vraṇinaḥ patita.caṇḍāla.kutsita.karmāṇo^andha.badhira.mūka.ahaṁ.vādinaḥ strī.rāja.puruṣāś ca, anyatra sva.vargebhyaḥ //*  
*03.11.30/ pāruṣya.steya.saṁgrahaṇeṣu tu vairi.syāla.sahāya.varjāḥ //*  
*03.11.31/ rahasya.vyavahāreṣv ekā strī puruṣa upaśrotā upadraṣṭā vā sākṣī syād rāja.tāpasa.varjam //*  
*03.11.32/ svāmino bhṛtyānām ṛtvig.ācāryāḥ śiṣyāṇāṁ mātā.pitarau putrāṇāṁ ca^anigraheṇa sākṣyaṁ kuryuḥ, tesām itare vā //*  
*03.11.33/ paraspara.abhiyoge ca^eṣām uttamāḥ parā.uktā daśa.bandhaṁ dadyuḥ, avarāḥ pañca.bandham // iti sākṣy.adhikāraḥ //*  
*03.11.34/ brāhmaṇa.uda.kumbha.agni.sakāśe sākṣiṇaḥ parigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*03.11.35/ tatra brāhmaṇaṁ brūyāt "satyaṁ brūhi" iti //*  
*03.11.36/ rājanyaṁ vaiśyaṁ vā "mā tava^iṣṭā.pūrta.phalam, kapāla.hastaḥ śatru.kulaṁ bhikṣā.arthī gaccheḥ" iti //*  
*03.11.37/ śūdraṁ "janma.maraṇa.antare yad vaḥ puṇya.phalaṁ tad rājānaṁ gacched, rājñaś ca kilbiṣaṁ yuṣmān anyathā.vāde, daṇḍaś ca^anubaddhaḥ, paścād api jñāyeta yathā.dṛṣṭa.śrutam, eka.mantrāḥ satyam upaharata" iti //*  
*03.11.38/ anupaharatāṁ sapta.rātrād ūrdhvaṁ dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, tri.pakṣād ūrdhvam abhiyogaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*03.11.39/ sākṣi.bhede yato bahavaḥ śucayo^anumatā vā tato niyaccheyuḥ, madhyaṁ vā gṛhṇīyuḥ //*  
*03.11.40/ tad vā dravyaṁ rājā haret //*  
*03.11.41/ sākṣiṇaś ced abhiyogād ūnaṁ brūyur atiriktasya^abhiyoktā bandhaṁ dadyāt //*  
*03.11.42/ atiriktaṁ vā brūyus tad.atiriktaṁ rājā haret //*  
*03.11.43/ bāliśyād abhiyoktur vā duhśrutaṁ durlikhitaṁ preta.abhiniveśaṁ vā samīkṣya sākṣi.pratyayam eva syāt //*  
*03.11.44/ "sākṣi.bāliṣyeṣv eva pṛthag.anuyoge deśa.kāla.kāryāṇāṁ pūrva.madhyama.uttamā daṇḍāḥ" ity auśanasāḥ //*  
*03.11.45/ "kūṭa.sākṣiṇo yam artham abhūtaṁ kuryur bhūtaṁ vā nāśayeyus tad daśa.guṇaṁ daṇḍaṁ dadyuḥ" iti mānavāḥ //*  
*03.11.46/ "bāliśyād vā visaṁvādayatāṁ citro ghātaḥ" iti bārhaspatyāḥ //*  
*03.11.47/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*03.11.48/ dhruvaṁ hi sākṣibhiḥ śrotavyam //*  
*03.11.49/ aśṛṇvatāṁ catur.viṁśati.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, tato^ardham abruvāṇānām //*  
*03.11.50ab/ deśa.kāla.avidūrasthān sākṣiṇaḥ pratipādayet /*  
*03.11.50cd/ dūrasthān aprasārān vā svāmi.vākyena sādhayet //E*  
*(ḍeposits)*  
*03.12.01/ upanidhir ṛṇena vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*03.12.02/ para.cakra.āṭavikābhyāṁ durga.rāṣṭra.vilope vā, pratirodhakair vā grāma.sārtha.vraja.vilope, cakra.yukta.nāśe vā, grāma.madhya.agny.udaka.ābādhe jvālā.vega.uparuddhe vā, nāvi nimagnāyāṁ muṣitāyāṁ vā svayam uparūḍho na^upanidhim abhyāvahet //*  
*03.12.03/ upanidhi.bhoktā deśa.kāla.anurūpaṁ bhoga.vetanaṁ dadyāt, dvādaśa.paṇaṁ ca daṇḍam //*  
*03.12.04/ upabhoga.nimittaṁ naṣṭaṁ vinaṣṭaṁ vā^abhyāvahet, catur.viṁśati.paṇaś ca daṇḍaḥ, anyathā vā niṣpatane //*  
*03.12.05/ pretaṁ vyasana.gataṁ vā na^upanidhim abhyāvahet //*  
*03.12.06/ ādhāna.vikraya.apavyayaneṣu ca^asya catur.guṇa.pañca.bandho daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.12.07/ parivartane niṣpātane vā mūlya.samaḥ //*  
*03.12.08/ tena^ādhi.praṇāśa.upabhoga.vikraya.ādhāna.apahārā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*03.12.09/ na^ādhiḥ sa-upakāraḥ sīdet, na ca^asya mūlyaṁ vardheta, anyatra nisargāt //*  
*03.12.10/ nirupakāraḥ sīdet, mūlyaṁ ca^asya vardheta //*  
*03.12.11/ upasthitasya^ādhim aprayacchato dvādaśaṇpaṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.12.12/ prayojaka.asamnidhāne vā grāma.vṛddheṣu sthāpayitvā niṣkrayam ādhiṁ pratipadyeta //*  
*03.12.13/ nivṛtta.vṛddhiko vā^ādhis tat.kāla.kṛta.mūlyas tatra^eva^avatiṣṭheta, anāśa.vināśa.karaṇa.adhiṣṭhito vā //*  
*03.12.14/ dhāraṇika.asamnidhāne vā vināśa.bhayād udgata.arghaṁ dharmastha.anujñāto vikrīṇīta, ādhi.pāla.pratyayo vā //*  
*03.12.15/ sthāvaras tu prayāsa.bhogyaḥ phala.bhogyo vā prakṣepa.vṛddhi.mūlya.śuddham ājīvam amūlya.kṣayeṇa^upanayet //*  
*03.12.16/ anisṛṣṭa.upabhoktā mūlya.śuddham ājīvaṁ bandhaṁ ca dadyāt //*  
*03.12.17/ śeṣam upanidhinā vyākhyātam //*  
*03.12.18/ etena^ādeśo^anvādhiś ca vyākhyātau //*  
*03.12.19/ sārthena^anvādhi.hasto vā pradiṣṭāṁ bhūmim aprāptaś corair bhagna.utsṛṣṭo vā na^anvādhim abhyāvahet //*  
*03.12.20/ antare vā mṛtasya dāyādo^api na^abhyāvahet //*  
*03.12.21/ śeṣam upanidhinā vyakahyātam //*  
*03.12.22/ yācitakam avakrītakaṁ vā yathā.vidhaṁ gṛhṇīyus tathā.vidham eva^arpayeyuḥ //*  
*03.12.23/ bhreṣa.upanipātābhyāṁ deśa.kāla.uparodhi dattaṁ naṣṭaṁ vinaṣṭaṁ vā na^abhyāvaheyuḥ //*  
*03.12.24/ śeṣam upanidhinā vyākhyātam //*  
*03.12.25/ vaiyāvṛtya.vikrayas tu - vaiyāvṛtya.karā yathā.deśa.kālaṁ vikrīṇānāḥ paṇyaṁ yathā.jātaṁ mūlyam udayaṁ ca dadyuḥ //*  
*03.12.26/ deśa.kāla.atipātane vā parihīṇaṁ sampradāna.kālikena^argheṇa mūlyam udayaṁ ca dadyuḥ //*  
*03.12.27/ yathā.sambhāṣitaṁ vā vikrīṇānā na^udayam adhigaccheyuḥ, mūlyam eva dadyuḥ //*  
*03.12.28/ argha.patane vā parihīṇaṁ yathā.parihīṇaṁ mūlyam ūnaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*03.12.29/ sāṁvyavahārikeṣu vā prātyayikeṣv arāja.vācyeṣu bhreṣa.upanipātābhyāṁ naṣṭaṁ vinaṣṭaṁ vā mūlyam api na dadyuḥ //*  
*03.12.30/ deśa.kāla.antaritānāṁ tu paṇyānāṁ kṣaya.vyaya.viśuddhaṁ mūlyam udayaṁ ca dadyuḥ, paṇya.samavāyānāṁ ca pratyaṁśam //*  
*03.12.31/ śeṣam upanidhinā vyākhyātam //*  
*03.12.32/ etena vaiyāvṛtya.vikrayo vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*03.12.33/ nikṣepaś ca^upanidhinā //*  
*03.12.34/ tam anyena nikṣpitam anyasya^arpayato hīyeta //*  
*03.12.35/ nikṣepa.apahāre pūrva.apadānaṁ nikṣeptāraś ca pramāṇam //*  
*03.12.36/ aśucayo hi kāravaḥ //*  
*03.12.37/ na^eṣāṁ karaṇa.pūrvo nikṣepa.dharmaḥ //*  
*03.12.38/ karaṇa.hīnaṁ nikṣepam apavyayamānaṁ gūḍha.bhitti.nyastān sākṣiṇo nikṣeptā rahasi praṇipātena prajñāpayet, vana.ante vā madya.prahavaṇa.viśvāsena //*  
*03.12.39/ rahasi vṛddho vyādhito vā vaidehakaḥ kaścit kṛta.lakṣaṇaṁ dravyam asya haste nikṣipya^apagacchet //*  
*03.12.40/ tasya pratideśena putro bhrātā vā^abhigamya nikṣepaṁ yāceta //*  
*03.12.41/ dāne śuciḥ, anyathā nikṣepaṁ steya.daṇḍaṁ ca dadyāt //*  
*03.12.42/ pravrajyā.abhimukho vā śraddheyaḥ kaścit kṛta.lakṣaṇaṁ dravyam asya haste nikṣipya pratiṣṭheta //*  
*03.12.43/ tataḥ kāla.antara.āgato yāceta //*  
*03.12.44/ dāne śuciḥ, anyathā nikṣepaṁ steya.daṇḍaṁ ca dadyāt //*  
*03.12.45/ kṛta.lakṣaṇena vā dravyeṇa pratyānayed enam //*  
*03.12.46/ bāliśa.jātīyo vā rātrau rāja.dāyikā.kṣaṇa.bhītaḥ sāram asya haste nikṣipya^apagacchet //*  
*03.12.47/ sa enaṁ bandhana.agāra.gato yāceta //*  
*03.12.48/ dāne śuciḥ, anyathā nikṣepaṁ steya.daṇḍaṁ ca dadyāt //*  
*03.12.49/ abhijñānena ca^asya gṛhe janam ubhayaṁ yāceta //*  
*03.12.50/ anyatarta.ādāne yathā.uktaṁ purastāt //*  
*03.12.51/ dravya.bhogānām āgamaṁ ca^asya^anuyuñjīta, tasya ca^arthasya vyavahāra.upaliṅganam, abhiyoktuś ca^artha.sāmarthyam //*  
*03.12.52/ etena mithaḥ.samavāyo vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*03.12.53ab/ tasmāt sākṣimad acchannaṁ kuryāt samyag.vibhāṣitam /*  
*03.12.53cd/ sve pare vā jane kāryaṁ deśa.kāla.agra.varṇataḥ //E*  
*(ḷaw concerning slaves and labourers)*  
*03.13.01/ udara.dāsa.varjam ārya.prāṇam aprāpta.vyavahāraṁ śūdraṁ vikraya.ādhānaṁ nayataḥ sva.janasya dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, vaiśyaṁ dvi.guṇaḥ, kṣatriyaṁ tri.guṇaḥ, brāhmaṇaṁ catur.guṇaḥ //*  
*03.13.02/ para.janasya pūrva.madhyama.uttama.vadhā daṇḍāḥ, kretṛ.śrotṛṛṇāṁ ca //*  
*03.13.03/ mlecchānām adoṣaḥ prajāṁ vikretum ādhātuṁ vā //*  
*03.13.04/ na tv eva^āryasya dāsa.bhāvaḥ //*  
*03.13.05/ athavā^āryam ādhāya kula.bandhana āryāṇām āpadi, niṣkrayaṁ ca^adhigamya bālaṁ sāhāyya.dātāraṁ vā pūrvaṁ niṣkrīṇīran //*  
*03.13.06/ sakṛd.ātma.ādhātā niṣpatitaḥ sīdet, dvir anyena^āhitakaḥ, sakṛd ubhau para.viṣaya.abhimukhau //*  
*03.13.07/ vitta.apahāriṇo vā dāsasya^ārya.bhāvam apaharato^ardha.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.13.08/ niṣpatita.preta.vyasaninām ādhātā mūlyaṁ bhajeta //*  
*03.13.09/ preta.viṇ.mūtra.ucchiṣṭa.grāhaṇam āhitasya nagna.snāpanaṁ daṇḍa.preṣaṇam atikramaṇaṁ ca strīṇāṁ mūlya.nāśa.karaṁ, dhātrī.paricārika.ardha.sītika.upacārikāṇāṁ ca mokṣa.karam //*  
*03.13.10/ siddham upacārakasya^abhiprajātasya^apakramaṇam //*  
*03.13.11/ dhātrīm āhitikāṁ vā^akāmāṁ sva.vaśāṁ gacchataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, para.vaśāṁ madhyamaḥ //*  
*03.13.12/ kanyām āhitikāṁ vā svayam anyena vā duṣayato mūlya.nāśaḥ śulkaṁ tad.dvu.guṇaś ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.13.13/ ātma.vikrayiṇaḥ prajām āryāṁ vidyāt //*  
*03.13.14/ ātma.adhigataṁ svāmi.karma.aviruddhaṁ labheta, pitryaṁ ca dāyam //*  
*03.13.15/ mūlyena ca^āryatvaṁ gacchet //*  
*03.13.16/ tena^udara.dāsa.āhitakau vyākhyātau //*  
*03.13.17/ prakṣepa.anurūpaś ca^asya niṣkrayaḥ //*  
*03.13.18/ daṇḍa.praṇītaḥ karmaṇā daṇḍam upanayet //*  
*03.13.19/ ārya.prāṇo dhvaja.āhṛtaḥ karma.kāla.anurūpeṇa mūlya.ardhena vā vimucyeta //*  
*03.13.20/ gṛhe.jāta.dāya.āgata.labdha.krītānām anyatamaṁ dāsam ūna.aṣṭa.varṣaṁ vibandhum akāmaṁ nīce karmaṇi videśe dāsīṁ vā sagarbhām aprativihita.garbha.bharmaṇyāṁ vikraya.ādhānaṁ nayataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, kretṛ.śrotṛṛṇāṁ ca //*  
*03.13.21/ dāsam anurūpeṇa niṣkrayeṇa^āryam akurvato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, saṁrodhaś ca^ā.karaṇāt //*  
*03.13.22/ dāsa.dravyasya jñātayo dāyādāḥ, teṣām abhāve svāmī //*  
*03.13.23/ svāminaḥ svasyāṁ dāsyāṁ jātaṁ samātṛkam adāsaṁ vidyāt //*  
*03.13.24/ gṛhyā cet kuṭumba.artha.cintanī mātā bhrātā bhaginī ca^asyā adāsāḥ syuḥ //*  
*03.13.25/ dāsaṁ dāsīṁ vā niṣkrīya punar vikraya.ādhānaṁ nayato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra svayaṁ.vādibhyaḥ // iti dāsa.kalpaḥ /*  
*03.13.26/ karma.karasya karma.sambandham āsannā vidyuḥ //*  
*03.13.27/ yathā.sambhāṣitaṁ vetanaṁ labheta, karma.kāla.anurūpam asambhāṣita.vetanaḥ //*  
*03.13.28/ karṣakaḥ sasyānāṁ go.pālakaḥ sarpiṣāṁ vaidehakaḥ paṇyānām ātmanā vyavahṛtānāṁ daśa.bhāgam asambhāṣita.vetano labheta //*  
*03.13.29/ sambhāṣita.vetanas tu yathā.sambhāṣitam //*  
*03.13.30/ kāru.śilpi.kuśīlava.cikitsaka.vāg.jīvana.paricāraka.ādir āśā.kārika.vargas tu yathā^anyas tad.vidhaḥ kuryād yathā vā kuśalāḥ kalpayeyus tathā vetanaṁ labheta //*  
*03.13.31/ sākṣi.pratyayam eva syāt //*  
*03.13.32/ sākṣiṇām abhāve yataḥ karma tato^anuyuñjīta //*  
*03.13.33/ vetana.ādāne daśa.bandho daṇḍaḥ, ṣaṭ.paṇo vā //*  
*03.13.34/ apavyayamāne dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, pañca.bandho vā //*  
*03.13.35/ nadī.vega.jvālā.stena.vyāla.uparuddhaḥ sarva.sva.putra.dāra.ātma.dānena^ārtas trātāram āhūya niṣtīrṇaḥ kuśala.pradiṣṭaṁ vetanaṁ dadyāt //*  
*03.13.36/ tena sarvatra^ārta.dāna.anuśayā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*03.13.37ab/ labheta puṁścalī bhogaṁ saṁgamasya^upaliṅganāt /*  
*03.13.37cd/ atiyācnā tu jīyeta daurmatya.avinayena vā //E*  
*(ḍuties of servants)*  
*(Undertaking in partnership)*  
*03.14.01/ gṛhītvā vetanaṁ karma^akurvato bhṛtakasya dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, saṁrodhaś ca^ā.karaṇāt //*  
*03.14.02/ aśaktaḥ kutsite karmaṇi vyādhau vyasane vā^anuśayaṁ labheta, pareṇa vā kārayitum //*  
*03.14.03/ tasya.vyaya.karmaṇā labheta bhartā vā kārayitum //*  
*03.14.04/ "na^anyas tvayā kārayitavyo, mayā vā na^anyasya kartavyam" ity avarodhe bhartur akārayato bhṛtakasya^akurvato vā dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.14.05/ karma.niṣṭhāpane bhartur anyatra gṛhīta.vetano na^asakāmaḥ kuryāt //*  
*03.14.06/ "upasthitam akārayataḥ kṛtam eva vidyād" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*03.14.07/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*03.14.08/ kṛtasya vetanaṁ na^akṛtasya^asti //*  
*03.14.09/ sa ced alpam api kārayitvā na kārayet kṛtam eva^asya vidyāt //*  
*03.14.10/ deśa.kāla.atipātanena karmaṇām anyathā.karaṇe vā na^asakāmaḥ kṛtam anumanyeta //*  
*03.14.11/ sambhāṣitād adhika.kriyāyāṁ prayāsaṁ na moghaṁ kuryāt //*  
*03.14.12/ tena saṁgha.bhṛtā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*03.14.13/ teṣām ādhiḥ sapta.rātram āsīta //*  
*03.14.14/ tato^anyam upasthāpayet, karma.niṣpākaṁ ca //*  
*03.14.15/ na ca^anivedya bhartuḥ saṁghaḥ kaṁcit parihared upanayed vā //*  
*03.14.16/ tasya^atikrame catur.viṁśati.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.14.17/ saṁghena parihṛtasya^ardha.daṇḍaḥ // iti bhṛtaka.adhikāraḥ //*  
*03.14.18/ saṁgha.bhṛtāḥ sambhūya.samutthātāro vā yathā.sambhāṣitaṁ vetanaṁ samaṁ vā vibhajeran //*  
*03.14.19/ karṣaṇa.vaidehakā vā sasya.paṇya.ārambha.paryavasāna.antare sannasya yathā.kṛtasya karmaṇaḥ pratyaṁśaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*03.14.20/ puruṣa.upasthāne samagram aṁśaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*03.14.21/ saṁsiddhe tu^uddhṛta.paṇye sannasya tadānīm eva pratyaṁśaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*03.14.22/ sāmānyā hi pathi.siddhiś ca^asiddhiś ca //*  
*03.14.23/ prakrānte tu karmaṇi svasthasya^apakrāmato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.14.24/ na ca prākāmyam apakramaṇe //*  
*03.14.25/ coraṁ tv abhaya.pūrvaṁ karmaṇaḥ pratyaṁśena grāhayed, dadyāt pratyaṁśam abhayaṁ ca //*  
*03.14.26/ punaḥ.steye pravāsanam, anyatra.gamane ca //*  
*03.14.27/ mahā.aparādhe tu dūṣyavad ācaret //*  
*03.14.28/ yājakāḥ svā.pracāra.dravya.varjaṁ yathā.sambhāṣitaṁ vetanaṁ samaṁ vā vibhajeran //*

*03.14.30/ mādhyandināt savanād ūrdhvaṁ samagram aṁśaṁ labheta //*  
*03.14.31/ nītā hi dakṣiṇā bhavanti //*

*03.15.01/ vikrīya paṇyam aprayacchato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra doṣa.upanipāta.aviṣahyebhyaḥ //*  
*03.15.02/ paṇya.doṣo doṣaḥ //*  
*03.15.03/ rāja.cora.agny.udaka.bādha upanipātaḥ //*  
*03.15.04/ bahu.guṇa.hīnam ārta.kṛtaṁ vā^aviṣahyam //*  
*03.15.05/ vaidehakānām eka.rātram anuśayaḥ, karṣakāṇāṁ tri.rātraṁ, go.rakṣakāṇāṁ pañca.rātram //*  
*03.15.06/ vyāmiśrāṇām uttamānāṁ ca varṇānāṁ vṛtti.vikraye sapta.rātram //*  
*03.15.07/ ātipātikānāṁ paṇyānāṁ "anyatra.avikreyam" ity avarodhena^anuśayo deyaḥ //*  
*03.15.08/ tasya^atikrame catur.viṁśati.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, paṇya.daśa.bhāgo vā //03.15.09/ krītvā paṇyam apratigṛhṇato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra doṣa.upanipāta.aviṣahyebhyaḥ //*  
*03.15.10/ samānaś ca^anuśayo vikretur anuśayena //*  
*03.15.11/ vivāhānāṁ tu trayāṇāṁ pūrveṣāṁ varṇānāṁ pāṇi.grahaṇāt siddham upāvartanaṁ, śūdrāṇāṁ ca prakarmaṇaḥ //*  
*03.15.12/ vṛtta.pāṇi.grahaṇayor api doṣam aupaśāyikaṁ dṛṣṭvā siddham upāvartanam //*  
*03.15.13/ na tv eva^abhiprajātayoḥ //*  
*03.15.14/ kanyā.doṣam aupaśāyikam anākhyāya prayacchataḥ kanyāṁ ṣaṇ.ṇavatir daṇḍaḥ, śulka.strī.dhana.pratidānaṁ ca //*  
*03.15.15/ varayitur vā vara.doṣam anākhyāya vindato dvi.guṇaḥ, śulka.strī.dhana.nāśaś ca //*  
*03.15.16/ dvipada.catuṣpadānāṁ tu kuṇṭha.vyādhita.aśucīnām utsāha.svāsthya.śucīnām ākhyāne dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.15.17/ ā.tri.pakṣād iti catuṣpadānām upāvartanam, ā.saṁvatsarād iti manuṣyāṇām //*  
*03.15.18/ tāvatā hi kālena śakyaṁ śauca.āśauce jñātum //*

*03.16.01/ dattasya^apradānam ṛṇa.ādānena vyākhyātam //*  
*03.16.02/ dattam avyavahāryam ekatra^anuśaye varteta //*  
*03.16.03/ sarva.svaṁ putra.dāram ātmānaṁ vā pradāya^anuśayinaḥ prayacchet //*  
*03.16.04/ dharma.dānam asādhuṣu karmasu ca^aupaghātikeṣu vā, artha.dānam anupakāriṣv apakāriṣu vā, kāma.dānam anarheṣu ca //*  
*03.16.05/ yathā ca dātā pratigrahītā ca na^upahatau syātāṁ tathā^anuśayaṁ kuśalāḥ kalpayeyuḥ //*  
*03.16.06/ daṇḍa.bhayād ākrośa.bhayād anartha.bhayād vā bhaya.dānaṁ pratigṛhṇataḥ steya.daṇḍaḥ, prayacchataś ca //*  
*03.16.07/ roṣa.dānaṁ para.hiṁsāyāṁ, rājñām upari darpa.dānaṁ ca //*  
*03.16.08/ tatra^uttamo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.16.09/ prātibhāvyaṁ daṇḍa.śulka.śeṣam ākṣikaṁ saurikaṁ ca na^akāmaḥ putro dāyādo vā riktha.haro dadyāt // iti dattasya^anapākarma /*  
*03.16.10/ asvāmi.vikrayas tu - naṣṭa.apahṛtam āsādya svāmī dharmasthena grāhayet //*  
*03.16.11/ deśa.kāla.atipattau vā svayaṁ gṛhītvā^upaharet //*  
*03.16.12/ dharmasthaś ca svāminam anuyuñjīta "kutas te labdham" iti //*  
*03.16.13/ sa ced ācāra.kramaṁ darśayeta, na vikretāraṁ, tasya dravyasya^atisargeṇa mucyeta //*  
*03.16.14/ vikretā ced dṛśyeta, mūlyaṁ steya.daṇḍaṁ ca dadyāt //*  
*03.16.15/ sa ced apasāram adhigacched apasared ā.apasāra.kṣayāt //*  
*03.16.16/ kṣaye mūlyaṁ steya.daṇḍaṁ ca dadyāt //*  
*03.16.17/ nāṣṭikaś ca sva.karaṇaṁ kṛtvā naṣṭa.pratyāhṛtaṁ labheta //*  
*03.16.18/ sva.karaṇa.abhāve pañca.bandho daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.16.19/ tac ca dravyaṁ rāja.dharmyaṁ syāt //*  
*03.16.20/ naṣṭa.apahṛtam anivedya^utkarṣataḥ svāminaḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.16.21/ śulka.sthāne naṣṭa.apahṛta.utpannaṁ tiṣṭhet //*  
*03.16.22/ tri.pakṣād ūrdhvam anabhisāraṁ rājā haret, svāmī vā sva.karaṇena //*  
*03.16.23/ pañca.paṇikaṁ dvipada.rūpasya niṣkrayaṁ dadyāt, catuṣpaṇikam eka.khurasya, dvipaṇikaṁ gomahiṣasya, pādikaṁ kṣudra.paśūnām //*  
*03.16.24/ ratna.sāra.phalgu.kupyānāṁ pañcakaṁ śataṁ dadyāt //*  
*03.16.25/ para.cakra.aṭavī.hṛtaṁ tu pratyānīya rājā yathā.svaṁ prayacchet //*  
*03.16.26/ cora.hṛtam avidyamānaṁ sva.dravyebhyaḥ prayacchet, pratyānetum aśakto vā //*  
*03.16.27/ svayaṁ.grāheṇa^āhṛtaṁ pratyānīya tan.niṣkrayaṁ vā prayacchet //*  
*03.16.28/ para.viṣayād vā vikrameṇa^ānītaṁ yathā.pradiṣṭaṁ rājñā bhuñjīta, anyatra^ārya.prāṇebhyo deva.brāhmaṇa.tapasvi.dravyebhyaś ca // ity asvāmi.vikrayaḥ /*  
*03.16.29/ sva.svāmi.sambandhas tu - bhoga.anuvṛttir ucchinna.deśānāṁ yathā.svaṁ dravyāṇām //*  
*03.16.30/ yat svaṁ dravyam anyair bhujyamānaṁ daśa varṣāṇy upekṣeta, hīyeta^asya, anyatra bāla.vṛddha.vyādhita.vyasani.proṣita.deśa.tyāga.rājya.vibhramebhyaḥ //*  
*03.16.31/ viṁśati.varṣa.upekṣitam anavasitaṁ vāstu na^anuyuñjīta //*  
*03.16.32/ jñātayaḥ śrotriyāḥ pāṣaṇḍā vā rājñām asaṁnidhau para.vāstuṣu vivasanto na bhogena hareyuḥ, upanidhim ādhiṁ nidhiṁ nikṣepaṁ striyaṁ sīmānaṁ rāja.śrotriya.dravyāṇi ca //*  
*03.16.33/ āśramiṇaḥ pāṣaṇḍā vā mahaty avakāśe parasparam abādhamānā vaseyuḥ //*  
*03.16.34/ alpāṁ bādhāṁ saheran //*  
*03.16.35/ pūrva.āgato vā vāsa.paryāyaṁ dadyāt //*  
*03.16.36/ apradātā nirasyeta //*

*03.16.38/ vivāda.padeṣu ca^eṣāṁ yāvantaḥ paṇā daṇḍās tāvatī rātrīḥ kṣapaṇa.abhiṣeka.agni.kārya.mahā.kaccha.vardhanāni rājñaś careyuḥ //*

*03.17.06/ "puṣpa.phala.śāka.mūla.kanda.pakva.anna.carma.veṇu.mṛd.bhāṇḍa.ādīnāṁ kṣudraka.dravyāṇāṁ dvādśa.paṇa.avaraś caturviṁśati.paṇa.paro daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.17.07/ kāla.āyasa.kāṣṭha.rajju.dravya.kṣudra.paśu.paṭa.ādīnāṁ sthūlaka.dravyāṇāṁ caturviṁśati.paṇa.avaro^aṣṭa.catvāriṁśat.paṇa.paro daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.17.08/ tāmra.vṛtta.kaṁsa.kāca.danta.bhāṇḍa.ādīnāṁ sthūlaka.dravyāṇām aṣṭa.catvāriṁśat.paṇa.avaraḥ ṣaṇ.ṇavati.paraḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.17.09/ mahā.paśu.manuṣya.kṣetra.gṛha.hiraṇya.suvarṇa.sūkṣma.vastra.ādīnāṁ sthūlaka.dravyāṇāṁ dviśata.avaraḥ pañca.śata.paro madhyamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.17.10/ striyaṁ puruṣaṁ vā^abhiṣahya badhnato bandhayato bandhaṁ vā mokṣayataḥ pañca.śata.avaraḥ sahasra.para uttamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ // ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*03.17.11/ "yaḥ sāhasaṁ "pratipattā" iti kārayati sa dvi.guṇaṁ dadyāt //*

*03.17.14/ sa cet kopaṁ madaṁ mohaṁ vā^apadiśed yathā.uktavad daṇḍam enaṁ kuryād iti kauṭilyaḥ //*
