*03.18.01/ vāk.pāruṣyam upavādaḥ kutsanam abhibhartsanam iti //*  
*03.18.02/ śarīra.prakṛti.śruta.vṛtti.jana.padānāṁ śarīra.upavāde kāṇa.khañja.ādibhiḥ satye tri.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, mithyā.upavāde ṣaṭ.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.18.03/ "śobhana.akṣimantaḥ" iti kāṇa.khañja.ādīnāṁ stuti.nindāyāṁ dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.18.04/ kuṣṭha.unmāda.klaibya.ādibhiḥ kutsāyāṁ ca satya.mithyā.stuti.nindāsu dvādaśa.paṇa.uttarā daṇḍās tulyeṣu //*  
*03.18.05/ viśiṣṭeṣu dvi.guṇāḥ, hīneṣv ardha.daṇḍāḥ, para.strīṣu dvi.guṇāḥ, pramāda.mada.moha.ādibhir ardha.daṇḍāḥ //*  
*03.18.06/ kuṣṭha.unmādayoś cikitsakāḥ saṁnikṛṣṭā pumāṁsaś ca pramāṇaṁ, klība.bhāve striyo mūtra.pheno^apsu viṣṭhā.nimajjanaṁ ca //*  
*03.18.07/ prakṛty.upavāde brāhmaṇa.kṣatriya.vaiśya.śūdra.anta.avasāyinām apareṇa pūrvasya tri.paṇa.uttarā daṇḍāḥ, pūrveṇa^aparasya dvi.paṇa.adharāḥ, kubrāhmaṇa.ādibhiś ca kutsāyām //*  
*03.18.08/ tena śruta.upavādo vāg.jīvanānāṁ, kāru.kuśīlavānāṁ vṛtty.upavādaḥ, prājjūṇaka.gāndhāra.ādīnāṁ ca jana.pada.upavādā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*03.18.09/ yaḥ paraṁ "evaṁ tvāṁ kariṣyāmi" iti karaṇena^abhibhartsayed, akaraṇe yas tasya karaṇe daṇḍas tato^ardha.daṇḍaṁ dadyāt //*  
*03.18.10/ aśaktaḥ kopaṁ madaṁ mohaṁ vā^apadiśed dvādaśa.paṇaṁ daṇḍaṁ dadyāt //*  
*03.18.11/ jāta.vaira.āśayaḥ śaktaś ca^apakartuṁ yāvaj.jīvika.avasthaṁ dadyāt //*  
*03.18.12ab/ sva.deśa.grāmayoḥ pūrvaṁ madhyamaṁ jāti.saṁghayoḥ /*  
*03.18.12cd/ ākrośād deva.caityānām uttamaṁ daṇḍam arhati //E*  
*(Physical injury)*  
*03.19.01/ daṇḍa.pāruṣyaṁ sparśanam avagūrṇaṁ prahatam iti //*  
*03.19.02/ nābher adhaḥ.kāyaṁ hasta.paṅka.bhasma.pāṁsubhir iti spṛśatas tri.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, tair eva^amedhyaiḥ pāda.ṣṭhīvikābhyāṁ ca ṣaṭ.paṇaḥ, chardi.mūtra.purīṣa.ādibhir dvādaśa.paṇaḥ //*  
*03.19.03/ nābher upari dvi.guṇāḥ, śirasi catur.guṇāḥ sameṣu //*  
*03.19.04/ viśiṣṭeṣu dvi.guṇāḥ, hīneṣv ardha.daṇḍāḥ, para.strīṣu dvi.guṇāḥ, pramāda.mada.moha.ādibhir ardha.daṇḍāḥ //*  
*03.19.05/ pāda.vastra.hasta.keśa.avalambaneṣu ṣaṭ.paṇa.uttarā daṇḍāḥ //*  
*03.19.06/ pīḍana.āveṣṭana.añcana.prakarṣaṇa.adhyāsaneṣu pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.19.07/ pātayitvā^apakrāmato^ardha.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.19.08/ śūdro yena^aṅgena brāhmaṇam abhihanyāt tad asyac^chedayet //*  
*03.19.09/ avagūrṇe niṣkrayaḥ, sparśe^ardha.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.19.10/ tena caṇḍāla.aśucayo vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*03.19.11/ hastena^avagūrṇe tri.paṇa.avaro dvādaśa.paṇa.paro daṇḍaḥ, pādena dvi.guṇaḥ, duḥkha.utpādanena dravyeṇa pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, prāṇa.ābādhikena madhyamaḥ //*  
*03.19.12/ kāṣṭha.loṣṭa.pāṣāṇa.loha.daṇḍa.rajju.dravyāṇām anyatamena duḥkham aśoṇitam utpādayataś caturviṁśati.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, śoṇita.utpādane dvi.guṇaḥ, anyatra duṣṭa.śoṇitāt //*  
*03.19.13/ mṛta.kalpam aśoṇitaṁ ghnato hasta.pāda.pārañcikaṁ vā kurvataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, pāṇi.pāda.danta.bhaṅge karṇa.nāsa.ācchedane vraṇa.vidāraṇe cca, anyatra duṣṭa.vraṇebhyaḥ //*  
*03.19.14/ sakthi.grīva.ābhañjane netra.bhedane vā vākya.ceṣṭā.bhojana.uparodheṣu ca madhyamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ samutthāna.vyayaś ca //*  
*03.19.15/ vipattau kaṇṭaka.śodhanāya nīyeta //*  
*03.19.16/ mahā.janasya^ekaṁ ghnataḥ pratyekaṁ dvi.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.19.17/ "paryuṣitaḥ kalaho^anupraveśo vā na^abhiyojyaḥ" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*03.19.18/ na^asty apakāriṇo mokṣa iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*03.19.19/ "kalahe pūrva.āgato jayati, akṣamamāṇo hi pradhāvati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*03.19.20/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*03.19.21/ pūrvaṁ paścād vā^abhigatasya sākṣiṇaḥ pramāṇam, asākṣike ghātaḥ kalaha.upaliṅganaṁ vā //*  
*03.19.22/ ghāta.abhiyogam apratibruvatas tad ahar eva paścāt.kāraḥ //*  
*03.19.23/ kalahe dravyam apaharato daśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, kṣudraka.dravya.hiṁsāyāṁ tac ca tāvac ca daṇḍaḥ, sthūlaka.dravya.hiṁsāyāṁ tac ca dvi.guṇaś ca daṇḍaḥ, vastra.ābharaṇa.hiraṇya.suvarṇa.bhāṇḍa.hiṁsāyāṁ tac ca pūrvaś ca sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.19.24/ para.kuḍyam abhighātena kṣobhayatas tri.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, chedana.bhedane ṣaṭ.paṇaḥ, pratīkāraś ca //*  
*03.19.25/ duḥkha.utpādanaṁ dravyam anya.veśmani prakṣipato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, prāṇa.ābādhikaṁ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.19.26/ kṣudra.paśūnāṁ kāṣṭha.ādibhir duḥkha.utpādane paṇo dvi.guṇo vā daṇḍaḥ, śoṇita.utpādane dvi.guṇaḥ //*  
*03.19.27/ mahā.paśūnām eteṣv eva sthāneṣv dvi.guṇo daṇḍaḥ samutthāna.vyayaś ca //*  
*03.19.28/ pura.upavana.vanaspatīnāṁ puṣpa.phalac.chāyāvatāṁ prarohac.chedane ṣaṭ.paṇaḥ, kṣudra.śākhāc.chedane dvādaśa.paṇaḥ, pīna.śākhāc.cchedane catur.viṁśati.paṇaḥ, skandha.vadhe pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, samucchittau madhyamaḥ //*  
*03.19.29/ puṣpa.phalac.chāyāvad.gulma.latāsv ardha.daṇḍāḥ, puṇya.sthāna.tapo.vana.śmaśāna.drumeṣu ca //*  
*03.19.30ab/ sīma.vṛkṣeṣu caityeṣu drumeṣv ālakṣiteṣu ca /*  
*03.19.30cd/ ta eva dvi.guṇā daṇḍāḥ kāryā rāja.vaneṣu ca //E*  
*(ṅambling and betting)*  
*(ṁiscellaneous)*  
*03.20.01/ dyūta.adhyakṣo dyūtam eka.mukhaṁ kārayet //*  
*03.20.02/ anyatra dīvyato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍo gūḍha.ājīvi.jñāpana.artham //*  
*03.20.03/ "dyūta.abhiyoge jetuḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, parājitasya madhyamaḥ //*  
*03.20.04/ bāliśa.jātīyo hy eṣa jetu.kāmaḥ parājayaṁ na kṣamate" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*03.20.05/ na^ity kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*03.20.06/ parājitaś ced dvi.guṇa.daṇḍaḥ kriyeta na kaścana rājānam abhisariṣyati //*  
*03.20.07/ prāyaśo hi kitavāḥ kūṭa.devinaḥ //*  
*03.20.08/ teṣām adhyakṣāḥ śuddhāḥ kākaṇīr akṣāṁś ca sthāpayeyuḥ //*  
*03.20.09/ kākaṇy.akṣāṇām anya.upadhāne dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, kūṭa.karmaṇi pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍo jita.pratyādānam, upadhau steya.daṇḍaś ca //*  
*03.20.10/ jita.dravyād adhyakṣaḥ pañcakaṁ śatam ādadīta, kākaṇy.akṣa.arālā.śalākā.avakrayam udaka.bhūmi.karma.krayaṁ ca //*  
*03.20.11/ dravyāṇām ādhānaṁ vikrayaṁ ca kuryāt //*  
*03.20.12/ akṣa.bhūmi.hasta.doṣāṇāṁ ca^apratiṣedhane dvi.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.20.13/ tena samāhvayo vyākhyātaḥ, anyatra vidyā.śilpa.samāhvayāt / iti //*  
*03.20.14/ prakīrṇakaṁ tu - yācitaka.avakrītaka.āhitaka.nikṣepakāṇāṁ yathā.deśa.kālam adāne, yāmac.chāyā.samupaveśa.saṁsthitīnāṁ vā deśa.kāla.atipātane, gulmatara.deyaṁ brāhmaṇaṁ sādhayataḥ, prativeśa.anuveśayor upari nimantraṇe ca dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.20.15/ saṁdiṣṭam artham aprayacchato, bhrātṛ.bhāryāṁ hastena laṅghayato, rūpa.ājīvām anya.uparuddhāṁ gacchataḥ, para.vaktavyaṁ paṇyaṁ krīṇānasya, samudraṁ gṛham udbhindataḥ, sāmanta.catvāriṁśat.kulya.ābādhām ācarataś ca^aṣṭa.catvāriṁśat.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.20.16/ kula.nīvī.grāhakasya^apavyayane, vidhavāṁ chanda.vāsinīṁ prasahya^adhicarataḥ, caṇḍālasya^āryāṁ spṛśataḥ, pratyāsannam āpady anabhidhāvato, niṣkāraṇam abhidhāvanaṁ kurvataḥ, śākya.ājīvaka.ādīn vṛṣala.pravrajitān deva.pitṛ.kāryeṣu bhojayataḥ śatyo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.20.17/ śapatha.vākya.anuyogam aniṣṛṣṭaṁ kurvataḥ, yukta.karma ca^ayuktasya, kṣudra.paśu.vṛṣāṇāṁ puṁstva.upaghātinaḥ, dāsyā garbham auṣadhena pātayataś ca pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.20.18/ pitā.putrayor dampatyor bhrātṛ.bhaginyor mātula.bhagineyayoḥ śiṣya.ācāryayor vā parasparam apatitaṁ tyajataḥ, sārtha.ābhiprayātaṁ grāma.madhye vā tyajataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, kāntāre madhyamaḥ, tan.nimittaṁ bhreṣayata uttamaḥ, saha.prasthāyiṣv anyeṣv ardha.daṇḍāḥ //*  
*03.20.19/ puruṣam abandhanīyaṁ badhnato bandhayato bandhaṁ vā mokṣayato, bālam aprāpta.vyavahāraṁ badhnato bandhayato vā sahasraṁ daṇḍaḥ //*  
*03.20.20/ puruṣa.aparādha.viśeṣeṇa daṇḍa.viśeṣaḥ kāryaḥ //*  
*03.20.21/ tīrtha.karas tapasvī vyādhitaḥ kṣut.pipāsā.adhva.klāntas tiro.jana.pado daṇḍa.khedī niṣkiṁcanaś ca^anugrāhyāḥ //*  
*03.20.22/ deva.brāhmaṇa.tapasvi.strī.bāla.vṛddha.vyādhitānām anāthānām anabhisaratāṁ dharmasthāḥ kāryāṇi kuryuḥ, na ca deśa.kāla.bhogac.chalena^atihareyuḥ //*  
*03.20.23/ pūjyā vidyā.buddhi.pauruṣa.abhijana.karma.atiśayataś ca puruṣāḥ //*  
*03.20.24ab/ evaṁ kāryāṇi dharmasthāḥ kuryur acchala.darśinaḥ /*  
*03.20.24cd/ samāḥ sarveṣu bhāveṣu viśvāsyā loka.sampriyāḥ //E*

### 4

*04.1.01/ pradeṣṭāras trayas trayo^amātyāḥ kaṇṭaka.śodhanaṁ kuryuḥ //*  
*04.1.02/ arthya.pratīkārāḥ kāru.śāsitāraḥ saṁnikṣeptāraḥ sva.vitta.kāravaḥ śreṇī.pramāṇā nikṣepaṁ gṛhṇīyuḥ //*  
*04.1.03/ vipattau śreṇī nikṣepaṁ bhajeta //*  
*04.1.04/ nirdiṣṭa.deśa.kāla.kāryaṁ ca karma kuryuḥ, anirdiṣṭa.deśa.kālaṁ kārya.apadeśam //*  
*04.1.05/ kāla.atipātane pāda.hīnaṁ vetanaṁ tad.dvi.guṇaś ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.1.06/ anyatra bhreṣa.upanipātābhyāṁ naṣṭaṁ vinaṣṭaṁ vā^abhyāvaheyuḥ //*  
*04.1.07/ kāryasya^anyathā.karaṇe vetana.nāśas tad.dvi.guṇaś ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.1.08/ tantu.vāyā daśa.ekādaśikaṁ sūtraṁ vardhayeyuḥ //*  
*04.1.09/ vṛddhic.chede cheda.dvi.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.1.10/ sūtra.mūlyaṁ vāna.vetanaṁ, kṣauma.kauśeyānām adhyardha.guṇaṁ, pattra.ūrṇā.kambala.dukūlānāṁ dvi.guṇam //*  
*04.1.11/ māna.hīne hīna.avahīnaṁ vetanaṁ tad.dvi.guṇaś ca daṇḍaḥ, tulā.hīne hīna.catur.guṇo daṇḍaḥ, sūtra.parivartane mūlya.dvi.guṇaḥ //*  
*04.1.12/ tena dvi.paṭa.vānaṁ vyākhyātam //*  
*04.1.13/ ūrṇā.tulāyāḥ pañca.paliko vihananac.chedo romac.chedaś ca //*  
*04.1.14/ rajakāḥ kāṣṭha.phalaka.ślakṣṇa.śilāsu vastrāṇi nenijyuḥ //*  
*04.1.15/ anyatra nenijato vastra.upaghātaṁ ṣaṭ.paṇaṁ ca daṇḍaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*04.1.16/ mudgara.aṅkād anyad vāsaḥ paridadhānās tri.paṇaṁ daṇḍaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*04.1.17/ para.vastra.vikraya.avakraya.ādhāneṣu ca dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, parivartane mūlya.dvi.guṇo vastra.dānaṁ ca //*  
*04.1.18/ mukula.avadātaṁ śilā.paṭṭa.śuddhaṁ dhauta.sūtra.varṇaṁ pramṛṣṭa.śvetaṁ ca^eka.rātra.uttaraṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*04.1.19/ pañca.rātrikaṁ tanu.rāgaṁ, ṣaḍ.rātrikaṁ nīlaṁ, puṣpa.lākṣā.mañjiṣṭhā.raktaṁ guru.parikarma yatna.upacāryaṁ jātyaṁ vāsaḥ sapta.rātrikam //*  
*04.1.20/ tataḥ paraṁ vetana.hāniṁ prāpnuyuḥ //*  
*04.1.21/ śraddheyā rāga.vivādeṣu vetanaṁ kuśalāḥ kalpayeyuḥ //*  
*04.1.22/ parārdhyānāṁ paṇo vetanaṁ, madhyamānām ardha.paṇaḥ, pratyavarāṇāṁ pādaḥ, sthūlakānāṁ māṣaka.dvi.māṣakaṁ, dvi.guṇaṁ raktakānām //*  
*04.1.23/ prathama.nejane catur.bhāgaḥ kṣayaḥ, dvitīye pañca.bhāgaḥ //*  
*04.1.24/ tena^uttaraṁ vyākhyātam //*  
*04.1.25/ rajakais tunna.vāyā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*04.1.26/ suvarṇa.kārāṇām aśuci.hastād rūpyaṁ suvarṇam anākhyāya sarūpaṁ krīṇatāṁ dvādaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ, virūpaṁ catur.viṁśati.paṇaḥ, cora.hastād aṣṭa.catvāriṁśat.paṇaḥ //*  
*04.1.27/ pracchanna.virūpa.mūlya.hīna.krayeṣu steya.daṇḍaḥ, kṛta.bhāṇḍa.upadhau ca //*  
*04.1.28/ suvarṇān māṣakam apaharato dvi.śato daṇḍaḥ, rūpya.dharaṇān māṣakam apaharato dvādaśa.paṇaḥ //*  
*04.1.29/ tena^uttaraṁ vyākhyātam //*

*04.1.45/ vyājī.pariśuddhau paṇa.yātrā //*

*04.1.49/ caraka.pāṁsu.dhāvakāḥ sāra.tri.bhāgaṁ, dvau rājā ratnaṁ ca //*  
*04.1.50/ ratna.apahāra uttamo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.1.51/ khani.ratna.nidhi.nivedaneṣu ṣaṣṭham aṁśaṁ nivettā labheta, dvādaśam aṁśaṁ bhṛtakaḥ //*  
*04.1.52/ śata.sahasrād ūrdhvaṁ rāja.gāmī nidhiḥ //*  
*04.1.53/ ūne ṣaṣṭham aṁśaṁ dadyāt //*  
*04.1.54/ paurvapauruṣikaṁ nidhiṁ jānapadaḥ śuciḥ sva.karaṇena samagraṁ labheta //*  
*04.1.55/ sva.karaṇa.abhāve pañca.śato daṇḍaḥ, pracchanna.ādāne sahasram //*

*04.1.58/ kuśīlavā varṣā.rātram ekasthā vaseyuḥ //*

*04.1.60/ tasya^atikrame dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*

*04.2.01/ saṁsthā.adhyakṣaḥ paṇya.saṁsthāyāṁ purāṇa.bhāṇḍānāṁ sva.karaṇa.viśuddhānām ādhānaṁ vikrayaṁ vā sthāpayet //*  
*04.2.02/ tulā.māna.bhāṇḍāni ca^avekṣeta pautava.apacārāt //*  
*04.2.03/ parimāṇī.droṇayor ardha.pala.hīna.atiriktam adoṣaḥ //*  
*04.2.04/ pala.hīna.atirikte dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.2.05/ tena pala.uttarā daṇḍa.vṛddhir vyākhyātā //*  
*04.2.06/ tulāyāḥ karṣa.hīna.atiriktam adoṣaḥ //*  
*04.2.07/ dvi.karṣa.hīna.atirikte ṣaṭ.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.2.08/ tena karṣa.uttarā daṇḍa.vṛddhir vyākhyātā //*  
*04.2.09/ āḍhakasya^ardha.karṣa.hīna.atiriktam adoṣaḥ //*  
*04.2.10/ karṣa.hīna.atirikte tri.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.2.11/ tena karṣa.uttarā daṇḍa.vṛddhir vyākhyātā //*  
*04.2.12/ tulā.māna.viśeṣāṇām ato^anyeṣām anumānaṁ kuryāt //*  
*04.2.13/ tulā.mānābhyām atiriktābhyāṁ krītvā hīnābhyāṁ vikrīṇānasya ta eva dvi.guṇā daṇḍāḥ //*  
*04.2.14/ gaṇya.paṇyeṣv aṣṭa.bhāgaṁ paṇya.mūlyeṣv apaharataḥ ṣaṇ.ṇavatir daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.2.15/ kāṣṭha.loha.maṇi.mayaṁ rajju.carma.mṛṇ.mayaṁ sūtra.valka.roma.mayaṁ vā jātyam ity ajātyaṁ vikraya.ādhānaṁ nayato mūlya.aṣṭa.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.2.16/ sāra.bhāṇḍam ity asāra.bhāṇḍaṁ taj.jātam ity ataj.jātaṁ rādhā.yuktam ity upadhiyuktaṁ samudga.parivartimaṁ vā vikraya.ādhānaṁ nayato hīna.mūlyaṁ catuṣpañcāśat.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, paṇa.mūlyaṁ dvi.guṇo, dvi.paṇa.mūlyaṁ dvi.śataḥ //*  
*04.2.17/ tena^argha.vṛddhau daṇḍa.vṛddhir vyākhyātā //*  
*04.2.18/ kāru.śilpināṁ karma.guṇa.apakarṣam ājīvaṁ vikraya.kraya.upaghātaṁ vā sambhūya samutthāpayatāṁ sahasraṁ daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.2.19/ vaidehakānāṁ vā sambhūya paṇyam avarundhatām anargheṇa vikrīṇatāṁ vā sahasraṁ daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.2.20/ tulā.māna.antaram argha.varṇa.antaraṁ vā - dharakasya māyakasya vā paṇa.mūlyād aṣṭa.bhāgaṁ hasta.doṣeṇa^ācarato dvi.śato daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.2.21/ tena dvi.śata.uttarā daṇḍa.vṛddhir vyākhyātā //*  
*04.2.22/ dhānya.sneha.kṣāra.lavaṇa.gandha.bhaiṣajya.dravyāṇāṁ sama.varṇa.upadhāne dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.2.23/ yan.niṣṛṣṭam upajīveyus tad eṣāṁ divasa.saṁjātaṁ saṁkhyāya vaṇik sthāpayet //*  
*04.2.24/ kretṛ.vikretror antara.patitam ādāyād anyad bhavati //*  
*04.2.25/ tena dhānya.paṇya.nicayāṁś ca^anujñātāḥ kuryuḥ //*  
*04.2.26/ anyathā.nicitam eṣāṁ paṇya.adhyakṣo gṛhṇīyāt //*  
*04.2.27/ tena dhānya.paṇya.vikraye vyavahareta^anugraheṇa prajānām //*  
*04.2.28/ anujñāta.krayād upari ca^eṣāṁ sva.deśīyānāṁ paṇyānāṁ pañcakaṁ śatam ājīvaṁ sthāpayet, para.deśīyānāṁ daśakam //*  
*04.2.29/ tataḥ param arghaṁ vardhayatāṁ kraye vikraye vā bhāvayatāṁ paṇa.śate pañca.paṇād dvi.śato daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.2.30/ tena^argha.vṛddhau daṇḍa.vṛddhir vyākhyātā //*  
*04.2.31/ sambhūya.kraye ca^eṣām avikrīte na^anyaṁ sambhūya.krayaṁ dadyāt //*  
*04.2.32/ paṇya.upaghāte ca^eṣām anugrahaṁ kuryāt //*  
*04.2.33/ paṇya.bāhulyāt paṇya.adhyakṣaḥ sarva.paṇyāny eka.mukhāni vikrīṇīta //*  
*04.2.34/ teṣv avikrīteṣu na^anye vikrīṇīran //*  
*04.2.35/ tāni divasa.vetanena vikrīṇīrann anugraheṇa prajānām //*  
*04.2.36ab/ deśa.kāla.antaritānāṁ tu paṇyānāṁ - prakṣepaṁ paṇya.niṣpattiṁ śulkaṁ vṛddhim avakrayam /*  
*04.2.36cd/ vyayān anyāṁś ca saṁkhyāya sthāpayed argham arghavit //E*  
*(ṛemedial measures during calamities)*  
*04.3.01/ daivāny aṣṭau mahā.bhayāni - agnir udakaṁ vyādhir durbhikṣaṁ mūṣikā vyālāḥ sarpā rakṣāṁsi^iti //*  
*04.3.02/ tebhyo jana.padaṁ rakṣet //*  
*04.3.03/ grīṣme bahir.adhiśrayaṇaṁ grāmāḥ kuryuḥ, daśa.mūlī.saṁgraheṇa^adhiṣṭhitā vā //*  
*04.3.04/ nāgarika.praṇidhāv agni.pratiṣedho vyākhyātaḥ, niśānta.praṇidhau rāja.parigrahe ca //*  
*04.3.05/ bali.homa.svasti.vācanaiḥ parvasu ca^agni.pūjāḥ kārayet //*  
*04.3.06/ varṣā.rātram ānūpa.grāmāḥ pūra.velām utsṛjya vaseyuḥ //*  
*04.3.07/ kāṣṭha.veṇu.nāvaś ca^upagṛhṇīyuḥ //*  
*04.3.08/ uhyamānam alābu.dṛti.plava.gaṇḍikā.veṇikābhis tārayeyuḥ //*  
*04.3.09/ anabhisaratāṁ dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, anyatra plava.hīnebhyaḥ //*  
*04.3.10/ parvasu ca nadī.pūjāḥ kārayet //*  
*04.3.11/ māyā.yogavido vedavido vā varṣam abhicareyuḥ //*  
*04.3.12/ varṣa.avagrahe śacī.nātha.gaṅgā.parvata.mahā.kaccha.pūjāḥ kārayet //*  
*04.3.13/ vyādhi.bhayam aupaniṣadikaiḥ pratīkāraiḥ pratikuryuḥ, auṣadhaiś cikitsakāḥ śānti.prāyaścittair vā siddha.tāpasāḥ //*  
*04.3.14/ tena marako vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*04.3.15/ tīrtha.abhiṣecanaṁ mahā.kaccha.vardhanaṁ gavāṁ śmaśāna.avadohanaṁ kabandha.dahanaṁ deva.rātriṁ ca kārayet //*  
*04.3.16/ paśu.vyādhi.marake sthāna.artha.nīrājanaṁ sva.daivata.pūjanaṁ ca kārayet //*  
*04.3.17/ durbhikṣe rājā bīja.bhakta.upagrahaṁ kṛtvā^anugrahaṁ kuryāt, durga.setu.karma vā bhakta.anugraheṇa, bhakta.saṁvibhāgaṁ vā, deśa.nikṣepaṁ vā //*  
*04.3.18/ mitrāṇi vā vyapāśrayeta, karśanaṁ vamanaṁ vā kuryāt //*  
*04.3.19/ niṣpanna.sasyam anya.viṣayaṁ vā sajana.pado yāyāt, samudra.saras.taṭākāni vā saṁśrayeta //*  
*04.3.20/ dhānya.śāka.mūla.phala.āvāpān vā setuṣu kurvīta, mṛga.paśu.pakṣi.vyāla.matsya.ārambhān vā //*  
*04.3.21/ mūṣika.bhaye mārjāra.nakula.utsargaḥ //*  
*04.3.22/ teṣāṁ grahaṇa.hiṁsāyāṁ dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, śunām anigrahe ca^anyatra^araṇya.carebhyaḥ //*  
*04.3.23/ snuhi.kṣīra.liptāni dhānyāni visṛjed, upaniṣad.yoga.yuktāni vā //*  
*04.3.24/ mūṣika.karaṁ vā prayuñjīta //*  
*04.3.25/ śāntiṁ vā siddha.tāpasāḥ kuryuḥ //*  
*04.3.26/ parvasu ca mūṣika.pūjāḥ kārayet //*  
*04.3.27/ tena śalabha.pakṣi.krimi.bhaya.pratīkārā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*04.3.28/ vyāla.bhaye madana.rasa.yuktāni paśu.śavāni prasṛjet, madana.kodrava.pūrṇāny audaryāṇi vā //*  
*04.3.29/ lubdhakāḥ śva.gaṇino vā kūṭa.pañjara.avapātaiś careyuḥ //*  
*04.3.30/ āvaraṇinaḥ śastra.pāṇayo vyālān abhihanyuḥ //*  
*04.3.31/ anabhisartur dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.3.32/ sa eva lābho vyāla.ghātinaḥ //*  
*04.3.33/ parvasu ca parvata.pūjāḥ kārayet //*  
*04.3.34/ tena mṛga.paśu.pakṣi.saṁgha.grāha.pratīkārā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*04.3.35/ sarpa.bhaye mantrair oṣadhibhiś ca jāṅgulīvidaś careyuḥ //*  
*04.3.36/ sambhūya vā^api sarpān hanyuḥ //*  
*04.3.37/ atharva.vedavido vā^abhicareyuḥ //*  
*04.3.38/ parvasu ca nāga.pūjāḥ kārayet //*  
*04.3.39/ tena^udaka.prāṇi.bhaya.pratīkārā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*04.3.40/ rakṣo.bhaye rakṣo.ghnāny atharva.vedavido māyā.yogavido vā karmāṇi kuryuḥ //*  
*04.3.41/ parvasu ca vitardic.chatra.ullopikā.hasta.patākāc.chāga.upahāraiś caitya.pūjāḥ kārayet //*  
*04.3.42/ "caruṁ vaś carāmaḥ" ity evaṁ sarva.bhayeṣv aho.rātraṁ careyuḥ //*  
*04.3.43/ sarvatra ca^upahatān pitā^iva^anugṛhṇīyāt //*  
*04.3.44ab/ māyā.yogavidas tasmād viṣaye siddha.tāpasāḥ /*  
*04.3.44cd/ vaseyuḥ pūjitā rājñā daiva.āpat.pratikāriṇaḥ //E*  
*(ṅuarding against persons with secret means of income)*  
*04.4.01/ samāhartṛ.praṇidhau jana.pada.rakṣaṇam uktam //*  
*04.4.02/ tasya kaṇṭaka.śodhanaṁ vakṣyāmaḥ //*  
*04.4.03/ samāhartā jana.pade siddha.tāpasa.pravrajita.cakra.cara.cāraṇa.kuhaka.pracchandaka.kārtāntika.naimittika.mauhūrtika.cikitsaka.unmatta.mūka.badhira.jaḍa.andha.vaidehaka.kāru.śilpi.kuśīlava.veśa.śauṇḍika.āpūpika.pākva.māṁsika.audanika.vyañjanān praṇidadhyāt //*  
*04.4.04/ te grāmāṇām adhyakṣāṇāṁ ca śauca.āśaucaṁ vidyuḥ //*  
*04.4.05/ yaṁ ca^atra gūḍha.ājīvinaṁ śaṅketa taṁ sattriṇā^apasarpayet //*  
*04.4.06/ dharmasthaṁ viśvāsa.upagataṁ sattrī brūyāt - "asau me bandhur abhiyuktaḥ, tasya^ayam anarthaḥ pratikriyatām, ayaṁ ca^arthaḥ pratigṛhyatām" iti //*  
*04.4.07/ sa cet tathā kuryād upadā.grāhaka iti pravāsyeta //*  
*04.4.08/ tena pradeṣṭāro vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*04.4.09/ grāma.kūṭam adhyakṣaṁ vā sattrī brūyāt - "asau jālmaḥ prabhūta.dravyaḥ, tasya^ayam anarthaḥ, tena^enam āhārayasva" iti //*  
*04.4.10/ sa cet tathā kuryād utkocaka iti pravāsyeta //*  
*04.4.11/ kṛtaka.abhiyukto vā kūṭa.sākṣiṇo^abhijñāta.anartha.vaipulyena^ārabheta //*  
*04.4.12/ te cet tathā kuryuḥ kūṭa.sākṣiṇa iti pravāsyeran //*  
*04.4.13/ tena kūṭa.śrāvaṇa.kārakā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*04.4.14/ yaṁ vā mantra.yoga.mūla.karmabhiḥ śmāśānikair vā saṁvadana.karakaṁ manyeta taṁ sattrī brūyāt - "amuṣya bhāryāṁ snuṣāṁ duhitaraṁ vā kāmaye, sā māṁ pratikāmayatām, ayaṁ ca^arthaḥ pratigṛhyatām" iti //*  
*04.4.15/ sa cet tathā kuryāt saṁvadana.kāraka iti pravāsyeta //*  
*04.4.16/ tena kṛtya.abhicāra.śīlau vyākhyātau //*  
*04.4.17/ yaṁ vā rasasya kartāraṁ kretāraṁ vikretāraṁ bhaiṣajya.āhāra.vyavahāriṇaṁ vā rasadaṁ manyeta taṁ sattrī brūyāt - "asau me śatruḥ, tasya^upaghātaḥ kriyatām, ayaṁ ca^arthaḥ pratigṛhyatām" iti //*  
*04.4.18/ sa cet tathā kuryād rasada iti pravāsyeta //*  
*04.4.19/ tena madana.yoga.vyavahārī vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*04.4.20/ yaṁ vā nānā.loha.kṣārāṇām aṅgāra.bhasma.asaṁdaṁśa.muṣṭika.adhikaraṇī.bimba.ṭaṅka.mūṣāṇām abhīkṣṇa.kretāraṁ maṣī.bhasma.dhūma.digdha.hasta.vastra.liṅgaṁ karmāra.upakaraṇa.saṁsargaṁ kūṭa.rūpa.kārakaṁ manyeta taṁ sattrī śiṣyatvena saṁvyavahāreṇa ca^anupraviśya prajñāpayet //*  
*04.4.21/ prajñātaḥ kūṭa.rūpa.kāraka iti pravāsyeta //*  
*04.4.22/ tena rāgasya^apahartā kūṭa.suvarṇa.vyavahārī ca vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*04.4.23ab/ ārabdhāras tu hiṁsāyāṁ gūḍha.ājīvās trayodaśa /*  
*04.4.23cd/ pravāsyā niṣkraya.arthaṁ vā dadyur doṣa.viśeṣataḥ //E*  
*(ḍetection of criminals through secret agents in the disguise of holy men)*  
*04.5.01/ sattri.prayogād ūrdhvaṁ siddha.vyañjanā māṇavān māṇava.vidyābhiḥ pralobhayeyuḥ, prasvāpana.antar.dhāna.dvāra.apoha.mantreṇa pratirodhakān, saṁvadana.mantreṇa pāratalpikān //*  
*04.5.02/ teṣāṁ kṛta.utsāhānāṁ mahāntaṁ saṁgham ādāya rātrāv anyaṁ grāmam uddiśya^anyaṁ grāmaṁ kṛtaka.strī.puruṣaṁ gatvā brūyuḥ - "iha^eva vidyā.prabhāvo dṛśyatāṁ, kṛcchraḥ para.grāmo gantum" iti //*  
*04.5.03/ tato dvāra.apoha.mantreṇa dvārāṇy apohya "praviśyatām" iti brūyuḥ //*  
*04.5.04/ antar.dhāna.mantreṇa jāgratām ārakṣiṇāṁ madhyena māṇavān atikrāmayeyuḥ //*  
*04.5.05/ prasvāpana.mantreṇa prasvāpayitvā rakṣiṇaḥ śayābhir māṇavaiḥ saṁcārayeyuḥ //*  
*04.5.06/ saṁvadana.mantreṇa bhāryā.vyañjanāḥ pareṣāṁ māṇavaiḥ sammodayeyuḥ //*  
*04.5.07/ upalabdha.vidyā.prabhāvāṇāṁ puraścaraṇā.ādy ādiśeyur abhijñāna.artham //*  
*04.5.08/ kṛta.lakṣaṇa.dravyeṣu vā veśmasu karma kārayeyuḥ //*  
*04.5.09/ anupraviṣṭā vā^ekatra grāhayeyuḥ //*  
*04.5.10/ kṛta.lakṣaṇa.dravya.kraya.vikraya.ādhāneṣu yoga.surā.mattān vā grāhayeyuḥ //*  
*04.5.11/ gṛhītān pūrva.apadāna.sahāyān anuyuñjīta //*  
*04.5.12/ purāṇa.cora.vyañjanā vā corān anupraviṣṭās tathā^eva karma kārayeyur grāhayeyuś ca //*  
*04.5.13/ gṛhītān samāhartā paura.jānapadānāṁ darśayet - "cora.grahaṇīṁ vidyām adhīte rājā, tasya^upadeśād ime corā gṛhītāḥ, bhūyaś ca grahīṣyāmi, vārayitavyo vaḥ sva.janaḥ pāpa.ācārah^" iti //*  
*04.5.14/ yaṁ ca^atra^apasarpa.upadeśena śamyā.pratoda.ādīnām apahartāraṁ jānīyāt tam eṣāṁ pratyādiśet "eṣa rājñaḥ prabhāvaḥ" iti //*  
*04.5.15/ purāṇa.cora.go.pālaka.vyādha.śva.gaṇinaś ca vana.cora.āṭavikān anupraviṣṭāḥ prabhūta.kūṭa.hiraṇya.kupya.bhāṇḍeṣu sārtha.vraja.grāmeṣv enān abhiyojayeyuḥ //*  
*04.5.16/ abhiyoge gūḍha.balair ghātayeyuḥ, madana.rasa.yuktena vā pathy.adanena //*  
*04.5.17/ gṛhīta.loptra.bhārān āyata.gata.pariśrāntān prasvapataḥ prahavaṇeṣu yoga.surā.mattān vā grāhayeyuḥ //*  
*04.5.18ab/ pūrvavac ca gṛhītvā^enān samāhartā prarūpayet /*  
*04.5.18cd/ sarvajña.khyāpanaṁ rājñaḥ kārayan rāṣṭra.vāsiṣu //E*  
*(Arrest on suspicion, with the stolen article and by indications of the act)*  
*04.6.01/ siddha.prayogād ūrdhvaṁ śaṅkā.rūpa.karma.abhigrahaḥ //*  
*04.6.02a/ kṣīṇa.dāya.kuṭumbam, alpa.nirveśaṁ, viparīta.deśa.jāti.gotra.nāma.karma.apadeśaṁ, pracchanna.vṛtti.karmāṇaṁ,-*  
*04.6.02b/ māṁsa.surā.bhakṣya.bhojana.gandha.mālya.vastra.vibhūṣaṇeṣu prasaktam, ativyaya.kartāraṁ, puṁścalī.dyūta.śauṇḍikeṣu prasaktam,-*  
*04.6.02c/ abhīkṣṇa.pravāsinam, avijñāta.sthāna.gamanam, ekānta.araṇya.niṣkuṭa.vikāla.cāriṇaṁ, pracchanne sa.āmiṣe vā deśe bahu.mantra.saṁnipātaṁ,-*  
*04.6.02d/ sadyaḥ.kṣata.vraṇānāṁ gūḍha.pratīkāra.kārayitāram, antar.gṛha.nityam, abhyadhigantāraṁ, kāntā.paraṁ,-*  
*04.6.02e/ para.parigrahāṇāṁ para.strī.dravya.veśmanām abhīkṣṇa.praṣṭāraṁ, kutsita.karma.śāstra.upakaraṇa.saṁsargaṁ,-*  
*04.6.02f/ virātre channa.kuḍyac.chāyā.saṁcāriṇaṁ, virūpa.dravyāṇām adeśa.kāla.vikretāraṁ, jāta.vairaśayaṁ, hīna.karma.jātiṁ,-*  
*04.6.02g/ vigūhamāna.rūpaṁ, liṅgena^āliṅginaṁ, liṅginaṁ vā bhinna.ācāraṁ, pūrva.kṛta.apadānaṁ, sva.karmabhir apadiṣṭaṁ,-*  
*04.6.02h/ nāgarika.mahā.mātra.darśane guhamānam apasarantam anucchvāsa.upaveśinam āvignaṁ śuṣka.bhinna.svara.mukha.varṇaṁ,-*  
*04.6.02i/ śastra.hasta.manuṣya.sampāta.trāsinaṁ, hiṁsra.stena.nidhi.nikṣepa.apahāra.para.prayoga.gūḍha.ājīvinām anyatamaṁ śaṅketa // iti śaṅkā.abhigrahaḥ //*  
*04.6.03/ rūpa.abhigrahas tu - naṣṭa.apahṛtam avidyamānaṁ taj.jāta.vyavahāriṣu nivedayet //*  
*04.6.04/ tac cen niveditam āsādya pracchādayeyuḥ sācivya.kara.doṣam āpnuyuḥ //04.6.05/ ajānanto^asya dravyasya^atisargeṇa mucyeran //*  
*04.6.06/ na ca^anivedya saṁsthā.adhyakṣasya purāṇa.bhāṇḍānām ādhānaṁ vikrayaṁ vā kuryuḥ //*  
*04.6.07/ tac cen niveditam āsādyeta, rūpa.abhigṛhītam āgamaṁ pṛcchet "kutas te labdham" iti //*  
*04.6.08/ sa cet brūyāt "dāyādyād avāptam, amuṣmāl labdhaṁ krītaṁ kāritam ādhi.pracchannam, ayam asya deśaḥ kālaś ca^upasamprāpteḥ, ayam asya^arghaḥ pramāṇaṁ lakṣaṇaṁ mūlyaṁ ca" iti, tasya^āgama.samādhau mucyeta //*  
*04.6.09/ nāṣṭikaś cet tad eva pratisaṁdadhyāt, yasyā pūrvo dīrghaś ca paribhogaḥ śucir vā deśas tasya dravyam iti vidyāt //*  
*04.6.10/ catuṣpada.dvipadānām api hi rūpa.liṅga.sāmānyaṁ bhavati, kim aṅga punar eka.yoni.dravya.kartṛ.prasūtānāṁ kupya.ābharaṇa.bhāṇḍānām iti //*  
*04.6.11/ sa ced brūyāt "yācitakam avakrītakam āhitakaṁ nikṣepam upanidhiṁ vaiyāvṛtya.karma vā^amuṣya" iti, tasya^apasāra.pratisaṁdhānena mucyeta //*  
*04.6.12/ "na^evam" ity apasāro vā brūyāt, rūpa.abhigṛhītaḥ parasya dāna.kāraṇam ātmanaḥ pratigraha.kāraṇam upaliṅganaṁ vā dāyaka.dāpaka.nibandhaka.pratigrāhaka.upadraṣṭṛbhir upaśrotṛbhir vā pratisamānayet //*  
*04.6.13/ ujjhita.pranaṣṭa.niṣpatita.upalabdhasya deśa.kāla.lābha.upaliṅganena śuddhiḥ //*  
*04.6.14/ aśuddhas tac ca tāvac ca daṇḍaṁ dadyāt //*  
*04.6.15/ anyathā steya.daṇḍaṁ bhajeta // iti rūpa.abhigrahaḥ /*  
*04.6.16/ karma.abhigrahas tu - muṣita.veśmanaḥ praveśa.niṣkasanam advāreṇa, dvārasya saṁdhinā bījena vā vedham, uttama.agārasya jāla.vāta.ayana.nīpra.vedham, ārohaṇa.avataraṇe ca kuḍyasya vedham, upakhananaṁ vā gūḍha.dravya.nikṣepaṇa.grahaṇa.upāyam, upadeśa.upalabhyam abhyantarac.cheda.utkara.parimarda.upakaraṇam abhyantara.kṛtaṁ vidyāt //*  
*04.6.17/ viparyaye bāhya.kṛtam, ubhayata ubhaya.kṛtam //*  
*04.6.18a/ abhyantara.kṛte puruṣam āsannaṁ vyasaninaṁ krūra.sahāyaṁ taskara.upakaraṇa.saṁsargaṁ, striyaṁ vā daridra.kulām anya.prasaktāṁ vā,-*  
*04.6.18b/ paricāraka.janaṁ vā tad.vidha.ācāram, atisvapnaṁ, nidrā.klāntam, āvignaṁ. śuṣka.bhinna.svara.mukha.varṇam, anavasthitam,-*  
*04.6.18c/ atipralāpinam, ucca.ārohaṇa.saṁrabdha.gātraṁ, vilūna.nighṛṣṭa.bhinna.pāṭita.śarīra.vastraṁ, jāta.kiṇa.saṁrabdha.hasta.pādaṁ,-*  
*04.6.18d/ pāṁsu.pūrṇa.keśa.nakhaṁ vilūna.bhugna.keśa.nakhaṁ vā, samyak.snāta.anuliptaṁ taila.pramṛṣṭa.gātraṁ sadyo.dauta.hasta.pādaṁ vā,-*  
*04.6.18e/ pāṁsu.picchileṣu tulya.pāda.pada.nikṣepaṁ, praveśa.niṣkasanayor vā tulya.mālya.madya.gandha.vastrac.cheda.vilepana.svedaṁ parīkṣeta //*  
*04.6.19/ coraṁ pāradārikaṁ vā vidyāt //*

*04.7.14/ tasya paricāraka.janaṁ vāg.daṇḍa.pāruṣya.atilabdhaṁ mārgeta, duḥkha.upahatam anya.prasaktaṁ vā strī.janaṁ, dāya.vṛtti.strī.jana.abhimantāraṁ vā bandhum //*  
*04.7.15/ tad eva hata.udbaddhasya parīkṣeta //*

*04.7.17/ sarveṣāṁ vā strī.dāyādya.doṣaḥ karma.spardhā pratipakṣa.dveṣaḥ paṇya.saṁsthā.samavāyo vā vivāda.padānām anyatamad vā roṣa.sthānam //*  
*04.7.18/ roṣa.nimitto ghātaḥ //*  
*04.7.19/ svayaṁ.ādiṣṭa.puruṣair vā, corair artha.nimittaṁ, sādṛśyād anya.vairibhir vā hatasya ghātam āsannebhyaḥ parīkṣeta //*

*04.8.01/ muṣita.saṁnidhau bāhyānām abhyantarāṇāṁ ca sākṣiṇām abhiśastasya deśa.jāti.gotra.nāma.karma.sāra.sahāya.nivāsān anuyuñjīta //*  
*04.8.02/ tāṁś ca^apadeśaiḥ pratisamānayet //*  
*04.8.03/ tataḥ pūrvasya^ahnaḥ pracāraṁ rātrau nivāsaṁ ca grahaṇād ity anuyuñjīta //*  
*04.8.04/ tasya^apasāra.pratisaṁdhāne śuddhaḥ syāt, anyathā karma.prāptaḥ //*  
*04.8.05/ tri.rātrād ūrdhvam agrāhyaḥ śaṅkitakaḥ pṛcchā.abhāvād anyatra^upakaraṇa.darśanāt //*  
*04.8.06/ acoraṁ cora ity abhivyāharataś cora.samo daṇḍaḥ, coraṁ pracchādayataś ca //*  
*04.8.07/ coreṇa^abhiśasto vaira.dveṣābhyām apadiṣṭakaḥ śuddhaḥ syāt //*  
*04.8.08/ śuddhaṁ parivāsayataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.8.09/ śaṅkā.niṣpannam upakaraṇa.mantri.sahāya.rūpa.vaiyāvṛtya.karān niṣpādayet //*  
*04.8.10/ karmaṇaś ca pradeśa.dravya.ādāna.aṁśa.vibhāgaiḥ pratisamānayet //*  
*04.8.11/ eteṣāṁ kāraṇānām anabhisaṁdhāne vipralapantam acoraṁ vidyāt //*  
*04.8.12/ dṛśyate hy acoro^api cora.mārge yadṛcchayā saṁnipāte cora.veṣa.śastra.bhāṇḍa.sāmānyena gṛhyamāṇaś cora.bhāṇḍasya^upavāsena vā, yathā^aṇi.māṇḍavyaḥ karma.kleśa.bhayād acoraḥ "coro^asmi" iti bruvāṇaḥ //*  
*04.8.13/ tasmāt samāpta.karaṇaṁ niyamayet //*  
*04.8.14/ manda.aparādhaṁ bālaṁ vṛddhaṁ vyādhitaṁ mattam unmattaṁ kṣut.pipāsā.adhva.klāntam atyāśitam āmaka.aśitaṁ durbalaṁ vā na karma kārayet //*  
*04.8.15/ tulya śīla.puṁścalī.prāpāvika.kathā.avakāśa.bhojana.dātṛbhir apasarpayet //*  
*04.8.16/ evam atisaṁdadhyāt, yathā vā nikṣepa.apahāre vyākhyātam //*  
*04.8.17/ āpta.doṣaṁ karma kārayet, na tv eva striyaṁ garbhiṇīṁ sūtikāṁ vā māsa.avara.prajātām //*  
*04.8.18/ striyās tv ardha.karma, vākya.anuyogo vā //*  
*04.8.19/ brāhmaṇasya sattri.parigrahaḥ śrutavatas tapasvinaś ca //*  
*04.8.20/ tasya^atikrama uttamo daṇḍaḥ kartuḥ kārayituś ca, karmaṇā vyāpādanena ca //*  
*04.8.21/ vyāvahārikaṁ karma.catuṣkaṁ - ṣaḍ daṇḍāḥ, sapta kaśāḥ, dvāv upari.nibandhau, udaka.nālikā ca //*  
*04.8.22/ paraṁ pāpa.karmaṇāṁ nava vetra.latāḥ, dvādaśa kaśāḥ, dvāv ūru.veṣṭau, viṁśatir nakta.māla.latāḥ, dvātriṁśat.talāḥ, dvau vṛścika.bandhau, ullaṁbane ca dve, sūcī hastasya, yavāgū.pītasya eka.parva.dahanam aṅgulyāḥ, sneha.pītasya pratāpanam ekam ahaḥ, śiśira.rātrau balbaja.agra.śayyā ca //*  
*04.8.23/ ity aṣṭādaśakaṁ karma //*  
*04.8.24/ tasya.upakaraṇaṁ pramāṇaṁ praharaṇaṁ pradharaṇam avadhāraṇaṁ ca khara.paṭṭād āgamayet //*  
*04.8.25/ divasa.antaram eka.ekaṁ ca karma kārayet //*  
*04.8.26/ pūrva.kṛta.apadānaṁ pratijñāya^apaharantam eka.deśa.dṛṣṭa.dravyaṁ karmaṇā rūpeṇa vā gṛhītaṁ rāja.kośam avastṛṇantaṁ karma.vadhyaṁ vā rāja.vacanāt samastaṁ vyastam abhyastaṁ vā karma kārayet //*  
*04.8.27/ sarva.aparādheṣv apīḍanīyo brāhmaṇaḥ //*  
*04.8.28/ tasya^abhiśasta.aṅko lalāṭe syād vyavahāra.patanāya, steyo śvā, manuṣya.vadhe kabandhaḥ, guru.talpe bhagam, surā.pāne madya.dhvajaḥ //*  
*04.8.29ab/ brāhmaṇaṁ pāpa.karmāṇam udghuṣya^aṅka.kṛta.vraṇam /*  
*04.8.29cd/ kuryān nirviṣayaṁ rājā vāsayed ākareṣu vā //E*  
*(Keeping a watch over officers of all departments)*  
*04.9.01/ samāhartṛ.pradeṣṭāraḥ pūrvam adhyakṣāṇām adhyakṣa.puruṣāṇāṁ ca niyamanaṁ kuryuḥ //*  
*04.9.02/ khani.sāra.karma.antebhyaḥ sāraṁ ratnaṁ vā^apaharataḥ śuddha.vadhaḥ //*  
*04.9.03/ phalgu.dravya.karma.antebhyaḥ phalgu dravyam upaskaraṁ vā pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.9.04/ paṇya.bhūmibhyo vā rāja.paṇyaṁ māṣa.mūlyād ūrdhvam āpāda.mūlyād ity apaharato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, ā.dvi.pāda.mūlyād iti catur.viṁśati.paṇaḥ, ā.tri.pāda.mūlyād iti ṣaṭ.triṁśat.paṇaḥ, ā.paṇa.mūlyād ity aṣṭa.catvāriṁśat.paṇaḥ, ā.dvi.paṇa.mūlyād iti pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, ā.catuṣpaṇa.mūlyād iti madhyamaḥ, ā.aṣṭa.paṇa.mūlyād ity uttamaḥ, ā.daśa.paṇa.mūlyād*  
*iti vadhaḥ //*  
*04.9.05/ koṣṭha.paṇya.kupya.āyudha.agārebhyaḥ kupya.bhāṇḍa.upaskara.apahāreṣv ardha.mūlyeṣu eta eva daṇḍāḥ //*  
*04.9.06/ kośa.bhāṇḍa.agāra.akṣa.śālābhyaś catur.bhāga.mūlyeṣu eta eva dvi.guṇā daṇḍāḥ //*  
*04.9.07/ corāṇām abhipradharṣaṇe citro ghātaḥ //*  
*04.9.08/ iti rāja.parigraheṣu vyākhyātam //*  
*04.9.09/ bāhyeṣu tu - pracchannam ahani kṣetra.khala.veśma.āpaṇebhyaḥ kupya.bhāṇḍam upaskaraṁ vā māṣa.mūlyād ūrdhvam ā.pāda.mūlyād ity apaharatas tri.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, gomaya.pradehena vā pralipya^avaghoṣaṇam^ ā.dvi.pāda.mūlyād iti ṣaṭ.paṇaḥ, gomaya.bhasmanā vā pralipya^avaghoṣaṇam, ā.tri.pāda.mūlyād iti nava.paṇaḥ, gomaya.bhasmanā vā pralipya^avaghoṣaṇam,*  
*śarāva.mekhalayā vā^ ā.paṇa.mūlyād iti dvādaśa.paṇaḥ, muṇḍanaṁ pravrājanaṁ vā^ ā.dvi.paṇa.mūlyād iti catur.viṁśati.paṇaḥ, muṇḍasya^iṣṭakā.śakalena pravrājanaṁ vā^ ā.catuṣ.paṇa.mūlyād iti ṣaṭ.triṁśat.paṇah^ ā.pañca.paṇa.mūlyād*  
*ity aṣṭa.catvāriṁśat.paṇaḥ, ā.daśa.paṇa.mūlyād iti pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍah^ ā.viṁśati.paṇa.mūlyād it dviśatah^ ā.triṁśat.paṇa.mūlyād iti pañca.śatah^ ā.catvāriṁśat.paṇa.mūlyād iti sāhasrah^ ā.pañcāśat.paṇa.mūlyād iti vadhaḥ //*  
*04.9.10/ prasahya divā rātrau vā^āntaryāmikam apaharato^ardha.mūlyeṣu eta eva daṇḍāḥ //*  
*04.9.11/ prasahya divā rātrau vā sa-śastrasya^apaharataś catur.bhāga.mūlyeṣu eta eva dvi.guṇā daṇḍāḥ //*  
*04.9.12/ kuṭuṁbika.adhyakṣa.mukhya.svāmināṁ kūṭa.śāsana.mudrā.karmasu pūrva.madhya.uttama.vadhā daṇḍāḥ, yathā.aparādhaṁ vā //*  
*04.9.13/ dharmasthaś ced vivadamānaṁ puruṣaṁ tarjayati bhartsayaty apasārayaty abhigrasate vā pūrvam asmai sāhasa.daṇḍaṁ kuryāt, vāk.pāruṣye dvi.guṇam //*  
*04.9.14/ pṛcchyaṁ na pṛcchati, apṛcchyaṁ pṛcchati, pṛṣṭvā vā visṛjati, śikṣayati, smārayati, pūrvaṁ dadāti vā, iti madhyamam asmai sāhasa.daṇḍaṁ kuryāt //*  
*04.9.15/ deyaṁ deśaṁ na pṛcchati, adeyaṁ deśaṁ pṛcchati, kāryam adeśena^ativāhayati, chalena^atiharati, kāla.haraṇena śrāntam apavāhayati, mārga.āpannaṁ vākyam utkramayati, mati.sāhāyyaṁ sākṣibhyo dadāti, tārita.anuśiṣṭaṁ kāryaṁ punar api gṛhṇāti, uttamam asmai sāhasa.daṇḍaṁ kuryāt //*  
*04.9.16/ punar.aparādhe dvi.guṇaṁ sthānād vyavaropaṇaṁ ca //*  
*04.9.17/ lekhakaś ced uktaṁ na likhati, anuktaṁ likhati, duruktam upalikhati, sūktam ullikhati, artha.utpattiṁ vā vikalpayati, iti pūrvam asmai sāhasa.daṇḍaṁ kuryād, yathā.aparādhaṁ vā //*  
*04.9.18/ dharmasthaḥ pradeṣṭā vā hairaṇya.daṇḍam adaṇḍye kṣipati kṣepa.dvi.guṇam asmai daṇḍaṁ kuryāt, hīna.atirikta.aṣṭa.guṇaṁ vā //*  
*04.9.19/ śarīra.daṇḍaṁ kṣipati śārīram eva daṇḍaṁ bhajeta, niṣkraya.dvi.guṇaṁ vā //*  
*04.9.20/ yaṁ vā bhūtam arthaṁ nāśayati abhūtam arthaṁ karoti tad.aṣṭa.guṇaṁ daṇḍaṁ dadyāt //*  
*04.9.21/ dharmasthīye cārake bandhana.agāre vā śayyā.āsana.bhojana.uccāra.saṁcāra.rodha.bandhaneṣu tri.paṇa.uttarā daṇḍāḥ kartuḥ kārayituś ca //*  
*04.9.22/ cārakād abhiyuktaṁ muñcato niṣpātayato vā madhyamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, abhiyoga.dānaṁ ca, bandhana.agārāt sarva.svaṁ vadhaś ca //*  
*04.9.23/ bandhana.agāra.adhyakṣasya saṁruddhakam anākhyāya cārayataś catur.viṁśati.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, karma kārayato dvi.guṇaḥ, sthāna.anyatvaṁ gamayato^anna.pānaṁ vā rundhataḥ ṣaṇ.ṇavatir daṇḍaḥ, parikleśayata utkoṭayato vā madhyamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, ghnataḥ sāhasraḥ //*  
*04.9.24/ parigṛhītāṁ dāsīm āhitikāṁ vā saṁruddhikām adhicarataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, cora.ḍāmarika.bhāryāṁ madhyamaḥ, saṁruddhikām āryām uttamaḥ //*  
*04.9.25/ saṁruddhasya vā tatra^eva ghātaḥ //*  
*04.9.26/ tad eva^akṣaṇa.gṛhītāyām āryāyāṁ vidyāt, dāsyāṁ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.9.27/ cārakam abhittvā niṣpātayato madhyamaḥ, bhittvā vadhaḥ, bandhana.agārāt sarva.svaṁ vadhaś ca //*  
*04.9.28ab/ evam artha.carān pūrvaṁ rājā daṇḍena śodhayet /*  
*04.9.28cd/ śodhayeyuś ca śuddhās te paura.jānapadān damaiḥ //E*  
*(ṛedemption from the cutting of individual limbs)*  
*04.10.01/ tīrtha.ghāta.granthi.bheda.ūrdhva.karāṇāṁ prathame^aparādhe saṁdeśac.chedanaṁ catuṣ.pañcāśat.paṇo vā daṇḍaḥ, dvitīye chedanaṁ paṇasya śatyo vā daṇḍaḥ, tṛtīye dakṣiṇa.hasta.vadhaś catuḥ.śato vā daṇḍaḥ, caturthe yathā.kāmī vadhaḥ //*  
*04.10.02/ pañca.viṁśati.paṇa.avareṣu kukkuṭa.nakula.mārjāra.śva.sūkara.steyeṣu hiṁsāyāṁ vā catuṣ.pañcāśat.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, nāsa.agrac.chedanaṁ vā^ caṇḍāla.araṇya.carāṇām ardha.daṇḍāḥ //*  
*04.10.03/ pāśa.jāla.kūṭa.avapāteṣu baddhānāṁ mṛga.paśu.pakṣi.vyāla.matsyānām ādāne tac ca tāvac ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.10.04/ mṛga.dravya.vanān mṛga.dravya.apahāre śātyo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.10.05/ biṁba.vihāra.mṛga.pakṣi.steye hiṁsāyāṁ vā dvi.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.10.06/ kāru.śilpi.kuśīlava.tapasvināṁ kṣudraka.dravya.apahāre śatyo daṇḍaḥ, sthūlaka.dravya.apahāre dvi.śataḥ, kṛṣi.dravya.apahāre ca //*  
*04.10.07/ durgam akṛta.praveśasya praviśataḥ prākārac.chidrād vā nikṣepaṁ gṛhītvā^apasarataḥ kāṇḍarā.vadho, dvi.śāto vā daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.10.08/ cakra.yuktaṁ nāvaṁ kṣudra.paśuṁ vā^apaharata eka.pāda.vadhaḥ, tri.śato vā daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.10.09/ kūṭa.kākaṇy.akṣa.arālā.śalākā.hasta.viṣama.kāriṇa eka.hasta.vadhaḥ, catuḥ.śato vā daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.10.10/ stena.pāradārikayoḥ sācivya.karmaṇi striyāḥ saṁgṛhītāyāś ca karṇa.nāsāc.chedanam, pañca.śato vā daṇḍaḥ, puṁṣo dvi.guṇaḥ //*  
*04.10.11/ mahā.paśum ekaṁ dāsaṁ dāsīṁ vā^apaharataḥ preta.bhāṇḍaṁ vā vikrīṇānasya dvi.pāda.vadhaḥ, ṣaṭ.chato vā daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.10.12/ varṇa.uttamānāṁ gurūṇāṁ ca hasta.pāda.laṅghane rāja.yāna.vāhana.ādy.ārohaṇe ca^eka.hasta.pāda.vadhaḥ, sapta.śato vā daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.10.13/ śūdrasya brāhmaṇa.vādino deva.dravyam avastṛṇato rāja.dviṣṭam ādiśato dvi.netra.bhedinaś ca yoga.añjanena^andhatvam, aṣṭa.śato vā daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.10.14/ coraṁ pāradārikaṁ vā mokṣayato rāja.śāsanam ūnam atiriktaṁ vā likhataḥ kanyāṁ dāsīṁ vā sa-hiraṇyam aparahataḥ kūṭa.vyavahāriṇo vimāṁsa.vikrayiṇaś ca vāma.hasta.dvi.pāda.vadho, nava.śato vā daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.10.15/ mānuṣa.māṁsa.vikraye vadhaḥ //*  
*04.10.16/ deva.paśu.pratimā.manuṣya.kṣetra.gṛha.hiraṇya.suvarṇa.ratna.sasya.apahāriṇa uttamo daṇḍaḥ, śuddha.vadho vā //*  
*04.10.17ab/ puruṣaṁ ca^aparādhaṁ ca kāraṇaṁ guru.lāghavam /*  
*04.10.17cd/ anubandhaṁ tadātvaṁ ca deśa.kālau samīkṣya ca //*  
*04.10.18ab/ uttama.avara.madhyatvaṁ pradeṣṭā daṇḍa.karmaṇi /*  
*04.10.18cd/ rājñaś ca prakṛtīnāṁ ca kalpayed antarā sthitaḥ //E*  
*(ḷaw of capital punishment, simple and with torture)*  
*04.11.01/ kalahe ghnataḥ puruṣaṁ citro ghātaḥ //*  
*04.11.02/ sapta.rātrasya^antar.mṛte śuddha.vadhaḥ, pakṣasya^antar uttamaḥ, māsasya^antaḥ pañca.śataḥ samutthāna.vyayaś ca //*  
*04.11.03/ śastreṇa praharata uttamo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.11.04/ madena hasta.vadhaḥ, mohena dvi.śataḥ //*  
*04.11.05/ vadhe vadhaḥ //*  
*04.11.06/ prahāreṇa garbhaṁ pātayata uttamo daṇḍaḥ, bhaiṣajyena madhyamaḥ, parikleśena pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.11.07/ prasabha.strī.puruṣa.ghātaka.abhisāraka.nigrāhaka.avaghoṣaka.avaskandaka.upavedhakān pathi.veśma.pratirodhakān rāja.hasty.aśva.rathānāṁ hiṁsakān stenān vā śūlān ārohayeyuḥ //*  
*04.11.08/ yaś ca^enān dahed apanayed vā sa tam eva daṇḍaṁ labheta, sāhasam uttamaṁ vā //*  
*04.11.09/ hiṁsra.stenānāṁ bhakta.vāsa.upakaraṇa.agni.mantra.dāna.vaiyāvṛtya.karmasu^uttamo daṇḍaḥ, paribhāṣaṇam avijñāte //*  
*04.11.10/ hiṁsra.stenānāṁ putra.dāram asamantraṁ visṛjet, samantram ādadīta //*  
*04.11.11/ rājya.kāmukam antaḥpura.pradharṣakam aṭavy.amitra.utsāhakaṁ durga.rāṣṭra.daṇḍa.kopakaṁ vā śiro.hasta.pradīpikaṁ ghātayet //*  
*04.11.12/ brāhmaṇaṁ tamaḥ praveśayet //*  
*04.11.13/ mātṛ.pitṛ.putra.bhrātr.ācārya.tapasvi.ghātakaṁ vā^a.tvak.śiraḥ.prādīpikaṁ ghātayet //*  
*04.11.14/ teṣām ākrośe jihvāc.chedaḥ, aṅga.abhiradane tad.aṅgān mocyaḥ //*  
*04.11.15/ yadṛcchā.ghāte puṁsaḥ paśu.yūtha.steye ca śuddha.vadhaḥ //*  
*04.11.16/ daśa.avaraṁ ca yūthaṁ vidyāt //*  
*04.11.17/ udaka.dhāraṇaṁ setuṁ bhindatas tatra^eva^apsu nimajjanam, anudakam uttamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, bhagna.utsṛṣṭakaṁ madhyamaḥ //*  
*04.11.18/ viṣa.dāyakaṁ puruṣaṁ striyaṁ ca puruṣaghnīm apaḥ praveśayed agarbhiṇīm, garbhiṇīṁ māsa.avara.prajātām //*  
*04.11.19/ pati.guru.prajā.ghātikām agni.viṣadāṁ saṁdhic.chedikāṁ vā gobhiḥ pāṭayet //*  
*04.11.20/ vivīta.kṣetra.khala.veśma.dravya.hasti.vana.ādīpikam agninā dāhayet //*  
*04.11.21/ rāja.ākrośaka.mantra.bhedakayor aniṣṭa.pravṛttikasya brāhmaṇa.mahānasa.avalehinaś ca jihvām utpāṭayet //*  
*04.11.22/ praharaṇa.āvaraṇa.stenam anāyudhīyam iṣubhir ghātayet //*  
*04.11.23/ āyudhīyasya^uttamaḥ //*  
*04.11.24/ meḍhra.phala.upaghātinas tad evac^chedayet //*  
*04.11.25/ jihvā.nāsa.upaghāte saṁdaṁśa.vadhaḥ //*  
*04.11.26ab/ ete śāstreṣv anugatāḥ kleśa.daṇḍā mahātmanām /*  
*04.11.26cd/ akliṣṭānāṁ tu pāpānāṁ dharmyaḥ śuddha.vadhaḥ smṛtaḥ //E*  
*(Violation of maidens)*  
*04.12.01/ savarṇām aprāpta.phalāṁ prakurvato hasta.vadhaḥ, catuḥ.śato vā daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.12.02/ mṛtāyāṁ vadhaḥ //*  
*04.12.03/ prāpta.phalāṁ prakurvato madhyamā.pradeśinī.vadho, dvi.śato vā daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.12.04/ pituś ca^avahīnaṁ dadyāt //*  
*04.12.05/ na ca prākāmyam akāmāyāṁ labbheta //*  
*04.12.06/ sakāmāyāṁ catuṣ.pañcāśat.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, striyās tv ardha.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.12.07/ para.śulka.avaruddhāyāṁ hasta.vadhaḥ, catuḥ.śato vā daṇḍaḥ, śulka.dānaṁ ca //*  
*04.12.08/ sapta.ārtava.prajātāṁ varaṇād ūrdhvam alabhamānaḥ prakṛtya prākāmī syāt, na ca pitur avahīnaṁ dadyāt //*  
*04.12.09/ ṛtu.pratirodhibhiḥ svāmyād apakrāmati //*  
*04.12.10/ tri.varṣa.prajāta.ārtavāyās tulyo gantum adoṣaḥ, tataḥ param atulyo^apy analaṁkṛtāyāḥ //*  
*04.12.11/ pitṛ.dravya.ādāne steyaṁ bhajeta //*  
*04.12.12/ param uddiśya^anyasya vindato dvi.śato daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.12.13/ na ca prākāṁyam akāmāyāṁ labheta //*  
*04.12.14/ kanyām anyāṁ darśayitvā^anyāṁ prayacchataḥ śatyo daṇḍas tulyāyām, hīnāyāṁ dvi.guṇaḥ //*  
*04.12.15/ prakarmaṇy akumāryāś catuṣ.pañcāśat.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, śulka.vyaya.karmaṇī ca pratidadyāt //*  
*04.12.16/ avasthāya taj.jātaṁ paścāt.kṛtā dvi.guṇaṁ dadyāt //*  
*04.12.17/ anya.śoṇita.upadhāne dviśato daṇḍaḥ, mithyā.abhiśaṁsinaś ca puṁsaḥ //*  
*04.12.18/ śulka.vyaya.karmaṇī ca jīyeta //*  
*04.12.19/ na ca prākāṁyam akāmāyāṁ labheta //*  
*04.12.20/ strī.prakṛtā sakāmā samānā dvādaśa.paṇaṁ daṇḍaṁ dadyāt, prakartrī dvi.guṇam //*  
*04.12.21/ akāmāyāḥ śatyo daṇḍa ātma.rāga.artham, śulka.dānaṁ ca //*  
*04.12.22/ svayaṁ prakṛtā rāja.dāsyaṁ gacchet //*  
*04.12.23/ bahir.grāmasya prakṛtāyāṁ mithyā.abhiśaṁsane ca dvi.guṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.12.24/ prasahya kanyām apaharato dvi.śataḥ, sa-suvarṇām uttamaḥ //*  
*04.12.25/ bahūnāṁ kanyā.apahāriṇāṁ pṛthag yathā.uktā daṇḍāḥ //*  
*04.12.26/ gaṇikā.duhitaraṁ prakurvataś catuṣ.pañcāśat.paṇo daṇḍaḥ, śulkaṁ mātur bhogaḥ ṣoḍaśa.guṇaḥ //04.12.27/ dāsasya dāsyā vā duhitaram adāsīṁ prakurvataś catur.viṁśati.paṇo daṇḍaḥ śulka.ābandhya.dānaṁ ca //*  
*04.12.28/ niṣkraya.anurūpāṁ dāsīṁ prakurvato dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍo vastra.ābandhya.dānaṁ ca //*  
*04.12.29/ sācivya.avakāśa.dāne kartṛ.samo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.12.30/ proṣita.patikām apacarantīṁ pati.bandhus tat.puruṣo vā saṁgṛhṇīyāt //*  
*04.12.31/ saṁgṛhītā patim ākāṅkṣeta //*  
*04.12.32/ patiś cet kṣameta visṛjyeta^ubhayam //*  
*04.12.33/ akṣamāyāṁ striyāḥ karṇa.nāsa.ācchedanam, vadhaṁ jāraś ca prāpnuyāt //*  
*04.12.34/ jāraṁ cora ity abhiharataḥ pañca.śato daṇḍaḥ, hiraṇyena muñcatas tad.aṣṭa.guṇaḥ //*  
*04.12.35/ keśākeśikaṁ saṁgrahaṇam, upaliṅganād vā śarīra.upabhogānām, taj.jātebhyaḥ(taj.jñātebhyaḥ? cf.ṇ12.60)), strī.vacanād vā //*  
*04.12.36/ para.cakra.aṭavī.hṛtām ogha.pravyūḍhām araṇyeṣu durbhikṣe vā tyaktāṁ preta.bhāva.utsṛṣṭāṁ vā para.striyaṁ nistārayitvā yathā.saṁbhāṣitaṁ samupabhuñjīta //*  
*04.12.37/ jāti.viśiṣṭām akāmām apatyavatīṁ niṣkrayeṇa dadyāt //*  
*04.12.38ab/ cora.hastān nadī.vegād durbhikṣād deśa.vibhramāt /*  
*04.12.38cd/ nistārayitvā kāntārān naṣṭāṁ tyaktāṁ mṛtā^iti vā //*  
*04.12.39ab/ bhuñjīta striyam anyeṣāṁ yathā.saṁbhāṣitaṁ naraḥ /*  
*04.12.39cd/ na tu rāja.pratāpena pramuktāṁ svajanena vā //*  
*04.12.40ab/ na ca^uttamāṁ na ca^akāmāṁ pūrva.apatyavatīṁ na ca /*  
*04.12.40cd/ īdṛśīṁ tv anurūpeṇa niṣkrayeṇa^apavāhayet //E*  
*(Punishments for transgressions)*  
*04.13.01/ brāhmaṇam apeyam abhakṣyaṁ vā grāsayata uttamo daṇḍaḥ, kṣatriyaṁ madhyamaḥ, vaiśyaṁ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, śūdraṁ catuṣ.pañcāśat.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.13.02/ svayaṁ grasitāro nirviṣayāḥ kāryāḥ //*  
*04.13.03/ para.gṛha.abhigamane divā pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, rātrau madhyamaḥ //*  
*04.13.04/ divā rātrau vā saśastrasya praviśata uttamo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.13.05/ bhikṣuka.vaidehakau matta.unmattau balād āpadi ca^atisaṁnikṛṣṭāḥ pravṛtta.praveśāś ca^adaṇḍyāḥ, anyatra pratiṣedhāt //*  
*04.13.06/ sva.veśmano virātrād ūrdhvaṁ parivāram ārohataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, para.veśmano madhyamaḥ, grāma.ārāma.vāṭa.bhedinaś ca //*  
*04.13.07/ grāmeṣv antaḥ sārthikā jñāta.sārā vaseyuḥ //*  
*04.13.08/ muṣitaṁ pravāsitaṁ ca^eṣām anirgataṁ rātrau grāma.svāmī dadyāt //*  
*04.13.09/ grāma.antareṣu vā muṣitaṁ pravāsitaṁ vivīta.adhyakṣo dadyāt //*  
*04.13.10/ avivītānāṁ cora.rajjukaḥ //*  
*04.13.11/ tathā^apy aguptānāṁ sīma.avarodhena vicayaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*04.13.12/ asīma.avarodhe pañca.grāmī daśa.grāmī vā //*  
*04.13.13/ durbalaṁ veśma śakaṭam anuttabdham ūrdha.staṁbhaṁ śastram anapāśrayam apraticchannaṁ śvabhraṁ kūpaṁ kūṭa.avapātaṁ vā kṛtvā hiṁsāyāṁ daṇḍa.pāruṣyaṁ vidyāt //*  
*04.13.14/ vṛkṣac.chedane daṁya.raśmi.haraṇe catuṣpadānām adānta.sevane vāhane vā kāṣṭha.loṣṭa.pāṣāṇa.daṇḍa.bāṇa.bāhu.vikṣepaṇeṣu yāne hastinā ca smaghaṭṭane "apehi" iti prakośann adaṇḍyaḥ //*  
*04.13.15/ hastinā roṣitena hato droṇa.annaṁ madya.kuṁbhaṁ mālya.anulepanaṁ danta.pramārjanaṁ ca paṭaṁ dadyāt //*  
*04.13.16/ aśva.medha.avabhṛtha.snānena tulyo hastinā vadha iti pāda.prakṣālanam //*  
*04.13.17/ udāsīna.vadhe yātur uttamo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.13.18/ śṛṅgiṇā daṁṣṭriṇā vā hiṁsyamānam amokṣayataḥ svāminaḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, pratikruṣṭasya dvi.guṇaḥ //*  
*04.13.19/ śṛṅgi.daṁṣṭribhyām anyonyaṁ ghātayatas tac ca tāvac ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.13.20/ deva.paśum ṛṣabham ukṣāṇaṁ go.kumārīṁ vā vāhayataḥ pañca.śato daṇḍaḥ, pravāsayata uttamaḥ //*  
*04.13.21/ loma.doha.vāhana.prajanana.upakāriṇāṁ kṣudra.paśūnām adāne tac ca tāvac ca daṇḍaḥ, pravāsane ca, anyatra deva.pitṛ.kāryebhyaḥ //*  
*04.13.22/ chinna.nasyaṁ bhagna.yugaṁ tiryak.pratimukha.āgataṁ pratyāsarad vā cakra.yuktaṁ yātā paśu.manuṣya.saṁbādhe vā hiṁsāyām adaṇḍyaḥ //*  
*04.13.23/ anyathā yathā.uktaṁ mānuṣa.prāṇi.hiṁsāyāṁ daṇḍam abhyāvahet //*  
*04.13.24/ amānuṣa.prāṇi.vadhe prāṇi.dānaṁ ca //*  
*04.13.25/ bāle yātari yānasthaḥ svāmī daṇḍyaḥ, asvāmini yānasthaḥ, prāpta.vyavahāro vā yātā //*  
*04.13.26/ bāla.adhiṣṭhitam apuruṣaṁ vā yānaṁ rājā haret //*  
*04.13.27/ kṛtya.abhicārābhyāṁ yat.param āpādayet tad.āpādayitavyaḥ //*  
*04.13.28/ kāmaṁ bhāryāyām anicchantyāṁ kanyāyāṁ vā dāra.arthino bhartari bhāryāyā vā saṁvadana.karaṇam //*  
*04.13.29/ anyathā.hiṁsāyāṁ madhyamaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.13.30/ mātā.pitror bhaginīṁ mātulānīm ācāryāṇīṁ snuṣāṁ duhitaraṁ bhaginīṁ vā^adhicaratas tri.liṅgac.chedanaṁ vadhaś ca //*  
*04.13.31/ sakāmā tad eva labheta, dāsa.paricāraka.āhitaka.bhuktā ca //*  
*04.13.32/ brāhmaṇyām aguptāyāṁ kṣatriyasya^uttamaḥ, sarva.svaṁ vaiśyasya, śūdraḥ kaṭa.agninā dahyeta //*  
*04.13.33/ sarvatra rāja.bhāryā.gamane kuṁbhī.pākaḥ //*  
*04.13.34/ śva.pākī.gamane kṛta.kabandha.aṅkaḥ para.viṣayaṁ gacchet, śva.pākatvaṁ vā śūdraḥ //*  
*04.13.35/ śva.pākasya^āryā.gamane vadhaḥ, striyāḥ karṇa.nāsa.ācchedanam //*  
*04.13.36/ pravrajitā.gamane catur.viṁśati.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.13.37/ sakāmā tad eva labheta //*  
*04.13.38/ rūpa.ājīvāyāḥ prasahya.upabhoge dvādaśa.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.13.39/ bahūnām ekām adhicaratāṁ pṛthak catur.viṁśati.paṇo daṇḍaḥ //*  
*04.13.40/ striyam ayonau gacchataḥ pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ, puruṣam adhimehataś ca //*  
*04.13.41ab/ maithune dvādaśa.paṇas tiryag.yoniṣv anātmanaḥ /*  
*04.13.41cd/ daivata.pratimānāṁ ca gamane dvi.guṇaḥ smṛtaḥ //*  
*04.13.42ab/ adaṇḍya.daṇḍane rājño daṇḍas triṁśad.guṇo^aṁbhasi /*  
*04.13.42cd/ varuṇāya pradātavyo brāhmaṇebhyas tataḥ param //*  
*04.13.43ab/ tena tat pūyate pāpaṁ rājño daṇḍa.apacārajam /*  
*04.13.43cd/ śāstā hi varuṇo rājñāṁ mithyā vyācaratāṁ nṛṣu //E*  
*(Infliction of secret punishment)*

### 5

*05.1.01/ durga.rāṣṭrayoḥ kaṇṭaka.śodhanam uktam //*  
*05.1.02/ rāja.rājyayor vakṣyāmaḥ //*  
*05.1.03/ rājānam avagṛhya^upajīvinaḥ śatru.sādhāraṇā vā ye mukhyās teṣu gūḍha.puruṣa.praṇidhiḥ kṛtya.pakṣa.upagraho vā siddhiḥ yathā.uktaṁ purastād, upajāpo^apasarpo vā yathā pāragrāmike vakṣyāmaḥ //*  
*05.1.04/ rājya.upaghātinas tu vallabhāḥ saṁhatā vā ye mukhyāḥ prakāśam aśakyāḥ pratiṣeddhuṁ dūṣyāḥ teṣu dharma.rucir upāṁśu.daṇḍaṁ prayuñjīta //*  
*05.1.05/ dūṣya.mahā.mātra.bhrātaram asat.kṛtaṁ sattrī protsāhya rājānaṁ darśayet //*  
*05.1.06/ taṁ rājā dūṣya.dravya.upabhoga.atisargeṇa dūṣye vikramayet //*  
*05.1.07/ śastreṇa rasena vā vikrāntaṁ tatra^eva ghātayed "bhrātṛ.ghātako^ayam" iti //*  
*05.1.08/ tena pāraśavaḥ paricārikā.putraś ca vyākhyātau //*  
*05.1.09/ dūṣyaṁ.mahāmātraṁ vā sattri.protsāhito bhrātā dāyaṁ yāceta //*  
*05.1.10/ taṁ dūṣya.gṛha.pratidvāri rātrāv upaśayānam anyatra vā vasantaṁ tīkṣṇo hantā brūyād "hato^ayaṁ dāya.kāmukaḥ" iti //*  
*05.1.11/ tato hata.pakṣam upagṛhya^itaraṁ nigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*05.1.12/ dūṣya.samīpasthā vā sattriṇo bhrātaraṁ dāyaṁ yācamānaṁ ghātena paribhartsayeyuḥ //*  
*05.1.13/ taṁ rātrau iti samānam //*  
*05.1.14/ dūṣya.mahā.mātrayor vā yaḥ putraḥ pituḥ pitā vā putrasya dārān adhicarati, bhrātā vā bhrātuḥ, tayoḥ kāpaṭika.mukhaḥ kalahaḥ pūrveṇa vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*05.1.15/ dūṣya.mahā.mātra.putram ātma.saṁbhāvitaṁ vā sattrī "rāja.putras tvam, śatru.bhayād iha nyasto^asi" ity upajapet //*  
*05.1.16/ pratipannaṁ rājā rahasi pūjayet "prāpta.yauvarājya.kālaṁ tvāṁ mahā.mātra.bhayān na^abhiṣiñcāmi" iti //*  
*05.1.17/ taṁ sattrī mahā.mātra.vadhe yojayet //*  
*05.1.18/ vikrāntaṁ tatra^eva ghātayet "pitṛ.ghātako^ayam" iti //*  
*05.1.19/ bhikṣukī vā dūṣya.bhāryāṁ sāṁvadanikībhir auṣadhībhiḥ saṁvāsya rasena^atisaṁdadhyāt //*  
*05.1.20/ ity āpya.prayogaḥ //*  
*05.1.21/ dūṣya.mahā.mātram aṭavīṁ para.grāmaṁ vā hantuṁ kāntāra.vyavahite vā deśe rāṣṭra.pālam anta.pālaṁ vā sthāpayituṁ nāgara.sthānaṁ vā kupitam avagrāhituṁ sārtha.ativāhyaṁ pratyante vā sa-pratyādeyam ādātuṁ phalgu.balaṁ tīkṣṇa.yuktaṁ preṣayet //*  
*05.1.22/ rātrau divā vā yuddhe pravṛtte tīkṣṇāḥ pratirodhaka.vyañjanā vā hanyuḥ "abhiyoge hataḥ" iti //*  
*05.1.23/ yātrā.vihāra.gato vā dūṣya.mahā.mātrān darśanāya^āhvayet //*  
*05.1.24/ te gūḍha.śastrais tīkṣṇaiḥ saha praviṣṭā madhyama.kakṣyāyām ātma.vicayam antaḥ.praveśana.arthaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*05.1.25/ tato dauvārika.abhigṛhītās tīkṣṇāḥ "dūṣya.prayuktāḥ sma" iti brūyuḥ //*  
*05.1.26/ te tad.abhivikhyāpya dūṣyān hanyuḥ //*  
*05.1.27/ tīkṣṇa.sthāne ca^anye vadhyāḥ //*  
*05.1.28/ bahir.vihāra.gato vā dūṣyān āsanna.āvāsān pūjayet //*  
*05.1.29/ teṣāṁ devī.vyañjanā vā duḥstrī rātrāv āvāseṣu gṛhyeta^iti samānaṁ pūrveṇa //*  
*05.1.30/ dūṣya.mahā.mātraṁ vā "sūdo bhakṣa.kāro vā te śobhanaḥ" iti stavena bhakṣya.bhojyaṁ yāceta, bahir vā kvacid adhva.gataḥ pānīyam //*  
*05.1.31/ tad.ubhayaṁ rasena yojayitvā pratisvādane tāv eva^upayojayet //*  
*05.1.32/ tad.abhivikhyāpya "rasadau" iti ghātayet //*  
*05.1.33/ abhicāra.śīlaṁ vā siddha.vyañjano "godhā.kūrma.karkaṭaka.kūṭānāṁ lakṣaṇyānām anyatama.prāśanena manorathān avāpsyasi" iti grāhayet //*  
*05.1.34/ pratipannaṁ karmaṇi rasena loha.musalair vā ghātayet "karma.vyāpadā hataḥ" iti //*  
*05.1.35/ cikitsaka.vyañjano vā daurātmikam asādhyaṁ vā vyādhiṁ dūṣyasya sthāpayitvā bhaiṣajya.āhāra.yogeṣu rasena^atisaṁdadhyāt //*  
*05.1.36/ sūda.ārālika.vyañjanā vā praṇihitā dūṣyaṁ rasena^atisaṁdadhyuḥ //*  
*05.1.37/ ity upaniṣat.pratiṣedhaḥ //*  
*05.1.38/ ubhaya.dūṣya.pratiṣedhas tu //*  
*05.1.39/ yatra dūṣyaḥ pratiṣeddhavyas tatra dūṣyam eva phalgu.bala.tīkṣṇa.yuktaṁ preṣayet, gaccha, amuṣmin durge rāṣṭre vā sainyam utthāpaya hiraṇyaṁ vā, vallabhād vā hiraṇyam āhāraya, vallabha.kanyāṁ vā prasahya^ānaya, durga.setu.vaṇik.patha.śūnya.niveśa.khani.dravya.hasti.vana.karmaṇām anyatamad vā kāraya rāṣṭra.pālyam anta.pālyaṁ vā^ yaś ca tvā pratiṣedhayen*  
*na vā te sāhāyyaṁ dadyāt sa bandhavyaḥ syāt" iti //*  
*05.1.40/ tathaiva^itareṣāṁ preṣayed "amuṣya^avinayaḥ pratiṣeddhavyaḥ" iti //*  
*05.1.41/ tam eteṣu kalaha.sthāneṣu karma.pratighāteṣu vā vivadamānaṁ tīkṣṇāḥ śastraṁ pātayitvā pracchannaṁ hanyuḥ //*  
*05.1.42/ tena doṣeṇa^itare niyantavyāḥ //*  
*05.1.43/ purāṇāṁ grāmāṇāṁ kulānāṁ vā dūṣyāṇāṁ sīmā.kṣetra.khala.veśma.maryādāsu dravya.upakaraṇa.sasya.vāhana.hiṁsāsu prekṣā.kṛtya^utsaveṣu vā samutpanne kalahe tīkṣṇair utpādite vā tīkṣṇāḥ śastraṁ pātayitvā brūyuḥ "evaṁ kriyante ye^amunā kalahāyante: iti //*  
*05.1.44/ tena doṣeṇa^itare niyantavyāḥ //*  
*05.1.45/ yeṣāṁ vā dūṣyāṇāṁ jāta.mūlāḥ kalahās teṣāṁ kṣetra.khala.veśmāny ādīpayitvā bandhu.saṁbandhiṣu vāhaneṣu vā tīkṣṇāḥ śastraṁ pātayitvā tathaiva brūyuḥ "amunā prayuktāḥ smaḥ" iti //*  
*04.4.46/ tena doṣeṇa^itare niyantavyāḥ //*  
*05.1.47/ durga.rāṣṭra.dūṣyān vā sattriṇaḥ parasparasya^āveśanikān kārayeyuḥ //*  
*05.1.48/ tatra rasadā rasaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*05.1.49/ tena doṣeṇa^itare niyantavyāḥ //*  
*05.1.50/ bhikṣukī vā dūṣya.rāṣṭra.mukhyaṁ "dūṣya.rāṣṭra.mukhyasya bhāryā snuṣā duhitā vā kāmayate" ity upajapet //*  
*05.1.51/ pratipannasya^ābharaṇam ādāya svāmine darśayet "asau te mukhyo yauvana.utsikto bhāryāṁ snuṣāṁ duhitaraṁ vā^abhimanyate" iti //*  
*05.1.52/ tayoḥ kalaho rātrau iti samānam //*  
*05.1.53/ dūṣya.daṇḍa.upanateṣu tu - yuva.rājaḥ senā.patir vā kiṁcid apakṛtya^apakrānto vikrameta //*  
*05.1.54/ tato rājā dūṣya.daṇḍa.upanatān eva preṣayet phalgu.bala.tīkṣṇa.yuktān iti samānāḥ sarva eva yogāḥ //*  
*05.1.55/ teṣāṁ ca putreṣv anukṣiyatsu yo nirvikāraḥ sa pitṛ.dāyaṁ labheta //*  
*05.1.56/ evam asya putra.pautrān anuvartate rājyam apāsta.puruṣa.doṣam //*  
*05.1.57ab/ sva.pakṣe para.pakṣe vā tūṣṇīṁ daṇḍaṁ prayojayet /*  
*05.1.57cd/ āyatyāṁ ca tadātve ca kṣamāvān aviśaṅkitaḥ //E*  
*(ṛeplenishment of the treasury)*  
*05.2.01/ kośam akośaḥ pratyutpanna.artha.kṛcchraḥ saṁgṛhṇīyāt //*  
*05.2.02/ jana.padaṁ mahāntam alpa.pramāṇaṁ vā^adeva.mātṛkaṁ prabhūta.dhānyaṁ dhānyasya^aṁśaṁ tṛtīyaṁ caturthaṁ vā yāceta, yathā.sāraṁ madhyam avaraṁ vā //*  
*05.2.03/ durga.setu.karma.vaṇik.patha.śūnya.niveśa.khani.dravya.hasti.vana.karma.upakāriṇaṁ pratyantam alpa.pramāṇaṁ vā na yāceta //*  
*05.2.04/ dhānya.paśu.hiraṇya.ādi niviśamānāya dadyāt //*  
*05.2.05/ caturtham aṁśaṁ dhānyānāṁ bīja.bhakta.śuddhaṁ ca hiraṇyena krīṇīyāt //*  
*05.2.06/ araṇya.jātaṁ śrotriya.svaṁ ca pariharet //*  
*05.2.07/ tad apy anugraheṇa krīṇīyāt //*  
*05.2.08/ tasya^akaraṇe vā samāhartṛ.puruṣā grīṣme karṣakāṇām udvāpaṁ kārayeyuḥ //*  
*05.2.09/ pramāda.avaskannasya^atyayaṁ dvi.guṇam udāharanto bīja.kāle bīja.lekhyaṁ kuryuḥ //*  
*05.2.10/ niṣpanne harita.pakva.ādānaṁ vārayeyuḥ, anyatra śāka.kaṭa.bhaṅga.muṣṭibhyāṁ deva.pitṛ.pūjā.dāna.arthaṁ gava.arthaṁ vā //*  
*05.2.11/ bhikṣuka.grāma.bhṛtaka.arthaṁ ca rāśi.mūlaṁ parihareyuḥ //*  
*05.2.12/ sva.sasya.apahāriṇaḥ pratipāto^aṣṭa.guṇaḥ //*  
*05.2.13/ para.sasya.apahāriṇaḥ pañcāśad.guṇaḥ sītā.atyayaḥ, sva.vargasya, bāhyasya tu vadhaḥ //*  
*05.2.14/ caturtham aṁśaṁ dhānyānāṁ ṣaṣṭhaṁ vanyānāṁ tūla.lākṣā.kṣauma.valka.kārpāsa.rauma.kauśeya.kauṣadha.gandha.puṣpa.phala.śāka.paṇyānāṁ kāṣṭha.veṇu.māṁsa.vallūrāṇāṁ ca gṛhṇīyuḥ, danta.ajinasya^ardham //*  
*05.2.15/ tad anisṛṣṭaṁ vikrīṇānasya pūrvaḥ sāhasa.daṇḍaḥ //*  
*05.2.16/ iti karṣakeṣu praṇayaḥ //*  
*05.2.17/ suvarṇa.rajata.vajra.maṇi.muktā.pravāla.aśva.hasti.paṇyāḥ pañcāśat.karāḥ //*  
*05.2.18/ sūtra.vastra.tāmra.vṛtta.kaṁsa.gandha.bhaiṣajya.śīdhu.paṇyāś catvāriṁśat.karāḥ //*  
*05.2.19/ dhānya.rasa.loha.paṇyāḥ śakaṭa.vyavahāriṇaś ca triṁśat.karāḥ //*  
*05.2.20/ kāca.vyavahāriṇo mahā.kāravaś ca viṁśati.karāḥ //*  
*05.2.21/ kṣudra.kāravo bandhakī.poṣakāś ca daśa.karāḥ //*  
*05.2.22/ kāṣṭha.veṇu.pāṣāṇa.mṛd.bhāṇḍa.pakva.anna.harita.paṇyāḥ pañca.karāḥ //*  
*05.2.23/ kuśīlavā rūpa.ājīvāś ca vetana.ardhaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*05.2.24/ hiraṇya.karaṁ karmaṇyān āhārayeyuḥ, na ca^eṣāṁ kaṁcid aparādhaṁ parihareyuḥ //*  
*05.2.25/ te hy aparigṛhītam abhinīya vikrīṇīran //*  
*05.2.26/ iti vyavahāriṣu praṇayaḥ //05.2.27/ kukkuṭa.sūkaram ardhaṁ dadyāt, kṣudra.paśavaḥ ṣaḍ.bhāgam, go.mahiṣa.aśvatara.khara.uṣṭrāś ca daśa.bhāgam //*  
*05.2.28/ bandhakī.poṣakā rāja.preṣyābhiḥ parama.rūpa.yauvanābhiḥ kośaṁ saṁhareyuḥ //*  
*05.2.29/ iti yoni.poṣakeṣu praṇayaḥ //*  
*05.2.30/ sakṛd eva na dviḥ prayojyaḥ //*  
*05.2.31/ tasya^akaraṇe vā samāhartā kāryam apadiśya paura.jānapadān bhikṣeta //*  
*05.2.32/ yoga.puruṣāś ca^atra pūrvam atimātraṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*05.2.33/ etena pradeśena rājā paura.jānapadān bhikṣeta //*  
*05.2.34/ kāpaṭikāś ca^enān alpaṁ prayacchataḥ kutsayeyuḥ //*  
*05.2.35/ sārato vā hiraṇyam āḍhyān yāceta, yathā.upakāraṁ vā, sva.vaśā vā yad upahareyuḥ //*  
*05.2.36/ sthānac.chatra.veṣṭana.vibhūṣāś ca^eṣāṁ hiraṇyena prayacchet //*  
*05.2.37/ pāṣaṇḍa.saṁgha.dravyam aśrotriya.upabhogyaṁ deva.dravyaṁ vā kṛtya.karāḥ pretasya dagdha.gṛhasya vā haste nyastam ity upahareyuḥ //*  
*05.2.38/ devatā.adhyakṣo durga.rāṣṭra.devatānāṁ yathā.svam ekasthaṁ kośaṁ kuryāt, tathaiva ca^upaharet //*  
*05.2.39/ daivata.caityaṁ siddha.puṇya.sthānam aupapādikaṁ vā rātrāv utthāpya yātrā.samājābhyām ājīvet //*  
*05.2.40/ caitya.upavana.vṛkṣeṇa vā devatā.abhigamanam anārtava.puṣpa.phala.yuktena khyāpayet //*  
*05.2.41/ manuṣya.karaṁ vā vṛkṣe rakṣo.bhayaṁ prarūpayitvā siddha.vyañjanāḥ paura.jānapadānāṁ hiraṇyena pratikuryuḥ //*  
*05.2.42/ suruṅgā.yukte vā kūpe nāgam aniyata.śiraskaṁ hiraṇya.upahāreṇa darśayet //*  
*05.2.43/ nāga.pratimāyām antaś.channāyāṁ caityac.chidre valmīkac.chidre vā sarpa.darśanam āhāreṇa pratibaddha.saṁjñaṁ kṛtvā śraddadhānānāṁ darśayet //*  
*05.2.44/ aśraddadhānānām ācamana.prokṣaṇeṣu rasam upacārya devatā.abhiśāpaṁ brūyāt, abhityaktaṁ vā daṁśayitvā //*  
*05.2.45/ yoga.darśana.pratīkāreṇa vā kośa.abhisaṁharaṇaṁ kuryāt //*  
*05.2.46/ vaidehaka.vyañjano vā prabhūta.paṇya.antevāsī vyavahareta //*  
*05.2.47/ sa yadā paṇya.mūlye nikṣepa.prayogair upacitaḥ syāt tadā^enaṁ rātrau moṣayet //*  
*05.2.48/ etena rūpa.darśakaḥ suvarṇa.kāraś ca vyākhyātau //*  
*05.2.49/ vaidehaka.vyañjano vā prakhyāta.vyavahāraḥ prahavaṇa.nimittaṁ yācitakam avakrītakaṁ vā rūpya.suvarṇa.bhāṇḍam anekaṁ gṛhṇīyāt //*  
*05.2.50/ samāje vā sarva.paṇya.saṁdohena prabhūtaṁ hiraṇya.suvarṇam ṛṇaṁ gṛhṇīyāt, pratibhāṇḍa.mūlyaṁ ca //*  
*05.2.51/ tad ubhayaṁ rātrau moṣayet //*  
*05.2.52/ sādhvī.vyañjanābhiḥ strībhir dūṣyān unmādayitvā tāsām eva veśmasv abhigṛhya sarva.svāny āhareyuḥ //*  
*05.2.53/ dūṣya.kulyānāṁ vā vivāde pratyutpanne rasadāḥ praṇihitā rasaṁ dadyuḥ //*  
*05.2.54/ tena doṣeṇa^itare paryādātavyāḥ //*  
*05.2.55/ dūṣyam abhityakto vā śraddheya.apadeśaṁ paṇyaṁ hiraṇya.nikṣepam ṛṇa.prayogaṁ dāyaṁ vā yāceta //*  
*05.2.56/ dāsa.śabdena vā dūṣyam ālambeta, bhāryām asya snuṣāṁ duhitaraṁ vā dāsī.śabdena bhāryā.śabdena vā //*  
*05.2.57/ taṁ dūṣya.gṛha.pratidvāri rātrāv upaśayānam anyatra vā vasantaṁ tīkṣṇo hatvā brūyāt "hato^ayam artha.kāmukaḥ" iti //*  
*05.2.58/ tena doṣeṇa^itare paryādātavyāḥ //*  
*05.2.59/ siddha.vyañjano vā dūṣyaṁ jambhaka.vidyābhiḥ pralobhayitvā brūyāt "akṣaya.hiraṇyaṁ rāja.dvārikaṁ strī.hṛdayam ari.vyādhi.karam āyuṣyaṁ putrīyaṁ vā karma jānāmi" iti //*  
*05.2.60/ pratipannaṁ caitya.sthāne rātrau prabhūta.surā.māṁsa.gandham upahāraṁ kārayet //*  
*05.2.61/ eka.rūpaṁ ca^atra hiraṇyaṁ pūrva.nikhātaṁ preta.aṅgaṁ preta.śiśur vā yatra nihitaḥ syāt, tato hiraṇyam asya darśayed "atyalpam" iti ca brūyāt //*  
*05.2.62/ "prabhūta.hiraṇya.hetoḥ punar upahāraḥ kartavya iti svayam eva^etena hiraṇyena śvo.bhūte prabhūtam aupahārikaṁ krīṇīhi" iti //*  
*05.2.63/ sa tena hiraṇyena^aupahārika.kraye gṛhyeta //*  
*05.2.64/ mātṛ.vyañjanayā vā "putro me tvayā hataḥ" ity avakupitā syāt //*  
*05.2.65/ saṁsiddham eva^asya rātri.yāge vana.yāge vana.krīḍāyāṁ vā pravṛttāyāṁ tīkṣṇā viśasya^abhityaktam atinayeyuḥ //*  
*05.2.66/ dūṣyasya vā bhṛtaka.vyañjano vetana.hiraṇye kūṭa.rūpaṁ prakṣipya prarūpayet //*  
*05.2.67/ karma.kara.vyañjano vā gṛhe karma kurvāṇaḥ stena.kūṭa.rūpa.kāraka.upakaraṇam upanidadhyāt, cikitsaka.vyañjano vā garam agada.apadeśena //*  
*05.2.68/ pratyāsanno vā dūṣyasya sattrī praṇihitam abhiṣeka.bhāṇḍam amitra.śāsanaṁ ca kāpaṭika.mukhena^ācakṣīta, kāraṇaṁ ca brūyāt //*  
*05.2.69/ evaṁ dūṣyeṣv adhārmikeṣu ca varteta, na^itareṣu //*  
*05.2.70ab/ pakvaṁ pakvam iva^ārāmāt phalaṁ rājyād avāpnuyāt /*  
*05.2.70cd/ ātmac.cheda.bhayād āmaṁ varjayet kopa.kārakam //E*  
*(ṣalaries of state servants)*  
*05.3.01/ durga.jana.pada.śaktyā bhṛtya.karma samudaya.pādena sthāpayet, kārya.sādhana.sahena vā bhṛtya.lābhena //*  
*05.3.02/ śarīram avekṣeta, na dharma.arthau pīḍayet //*  
*05.3.03/ ṛtvig.ācārya.mantri.purohita.senā.pati.yuva.rāja.rāja.mātṛ.rāja.mahiṣyo^aṣṭa.catvāriṁśat.sāhasrāḥ //*  
*05.3.04/ etāvatā bharaṇena^anāspadyatvam akopakaṁ ca^eṣāṁ bhavati //*  
*05.3.05/ dauvārika.antar.vaṁśika.praśāstṛ.samāhartṛ.saṁnidhātāraś catur.viṁśati.sāhasrāḥ //*  
*05.3.06/ etāvatā karmaṇyā bhavanti //*  
*05.3.07/ kumāra.kumāra.mātṛ.nāyaka.paura.vyāvahārika.kārmāntika.mantri.pariṣad.rāṣṭra.anta.pālāś ca dvādaśa.sāhasrāḥ //*  
*05.3.08/ svāmi.paribandha.bala.sahāyā hy etāvatā bhavanti //*  
*05.3.09/ śreṇī.mukhyā hasty.aśva.ratha.mukhyāḥ pradeṣṭāraś ca^aṣṭa.sāhasrāḥ //*  
*05.3.10/ sva.varga.anukarṣiṇo hy etāvatā bhavanti //*  
*05.3.11/ patty.aśva.ratha.hasty.adhyakṣā dravya.hasti.vana.pālāś ca catuḥ.sāhasrāḥ //*  
*05.3.12/ rathika.anīkastha.cikitsaka.aśva.damaka.vardhakayo yoni.poṣakāś ca dvi.sāhasrāḥ //*  
*05.3.13/ kārtāntika.naimittika.mauhūrtika.paurāṇika.sūta.māgadhāḥ purohita.puruṣāḥ sarva.adhyakṣāś ca sāhasrāḥ //*  
*05.3.14/ śilpavantaḥ pādātāḥ saṁkhyāyaka.lekhaka.ādi.vargaś ca pañca.śatāḥ //*  
*05.3.15/ kuśīlavās tv ardha.tṛtīya.śatāḥ, dvi.guṇa.vetanāś ca^eṣāṁ tūrya.karāḥ //*  
*05.3.16/ kāru.śilpino viṁśati.śatikāḥ //*  
*05.3.17/ catuṣpada.dvipada.paricāraka.pārikarmika.aupasthāyika.pālaka.viṣṭi.bandhakāḥ ṣaṣṭi.vetanāḥ, ārya.yukta.ārohaka.māṇavaka.śaila.khanakāḥ sarva.upasthāyinaś ca //*  
*05.3.18/ ācāryā vidyāvantaś ca pūjā.vetanāni yathā.arhaṁ labheran pañca.śata.avaraṁ sahasra.param //*  
*05.3.19/ daśa.paṇiko yojane dūto madhyamaḥ, daśa.uttare dvi.guṇa.vetana ā.yojana.śatād iti //*  
*05.3.20/ samāna.vidyebhyas tri.guṇa.vetano rājā rāja.sūya.ādiṣu kratuṣu //*  
*05.3.21/ rājñaḥ sārathiḥ sāhasraḥ //*  
*05.3.22/ kāpaṭika.udāsthita.gṛha.patika.vaidehaka.tāpasa.vyañjanāḥ sāhasrāḥ //*  
*05.3.23/ grāma.bhṛtaka.sattri.tīkṣṇa.rasada.bhikṣukyaḥ pañca.śatāḥ //*  
*05.3.24/ cāra.saṁcāriṇo^ardha.tṛtīya.śatāḥ, prayāsa.vṛddha.vetanā vā //*  
*05.3.25/ śata.varga.sahasra.vargāṇām adhyakṣā bhakta.vetana.lābham ādeśaṁ vikṣepaṁ ca kuryuḥ //*  
*05.3.26/ avikṣepo rāja.parigraha.durga.rāṣṭra.rakṣa.avekṣaṇeṣu ca //*  
*05.3.27/ nitya.mukhyāḥ syur aneka.mukhyāś ca //*  
*05.3.28/ karmasu mṛtānāṁ putra.dārā bhakta.vetanaṁ labheran //*  
*05.3.29/ bāla.vṛddha.vyādhitāś ca^eṣām anugrāhyāḥ //*  
*05.3.30/ preta.vyādhita.sūtikā.kṛtyeṣu ca^eṣām artha.māna.karma kuryāt //*  
*05.3.31/ alpa.kośaḥ kupya.paśu.kṣetrāṇi dadyāt, alpaṁ ca hiraṇyam //*  
*05.3.32/ śūnyaṁ vā niveśayitum abhyutthito hiraṇyam eva dadyāt, na grāmaṁ grāma.saṁjāta.vyavahāra.sthāpana.artham //*  
*05.3.33/ etena bhṛtānām abhṛtānāṁ ca vidyā.karmabhyāṁ bhakta.vetana.viśeṣaṁ ca kuryāt //*  
*05.3.34/ ṣaṣṭi.vetanasya^āḍhakaṁ kṛtvā hiraṇya.anurūpaṁ bhaktaṁ kuryāt //*  
*05.3.35/ patty.aśva.ratha.dvipāḥ sūrya.udaye bahiḥ saṁdhi.divasa.varjaṁ śilpa.yogyāḥ kuryuḥ //*  
*05.3.36/ teṣu rājā nitya.yuktaḥ syāt, abhīkṣṇaṁ ca^eṣāṁ śilpa.darśanaṁ kuryāt //*  
*05.3.37/ kṛta.nara.indra.aṅkaṁ śastra.āvaraṇam āyudha.agāraṁ praveśayet //*  
*05.3.38/ aśastrāś careyuḥ, anyatra mudrā.anujñātāt //*  
*05.3.39/ naṣṭaṁ.vinaṣṭaṁ vā dvi.guṇaṁ dadyāt //*  
*05.3.40/ vidhvasta.gaṇanāṁ ca kuryāt //*  
*05.3.41/ sārthikānāṁ śastra.āvaraṇam anta.pālā gṛhṇīyuḥ, samudram avacārayeyur vā //*  
*05.3.42/ yātrām abhyutthito vā senām udyojayet //*  
*05.3.43/ tato vaidehaka.vyañjanāḥ sarva.paṇyāny āyudhīyebhyo yātrā.kāle dvi.guṇa.pratyādeyāni dadyuḥ //*  
*05.3.44/ evaṁ rāja.paṇya.yoga.vikrayo vetana.pratyādānaṁ ca bhavati //*  
*05.3.45/ evam avekṣita.āya.vyayaḥ kośa.daṇḍa.vyasanaṁ na^avāpnoti //*  
*05.3.46/ iti bhakta.vetana.vikalpaḥ //*  
*05.3.47ab/ sattriṇaś ca^āyudhīyānāṁ veśyāḥ kāru.kuśīlavāḥ /*  
*05.3.47cd/ daṇḍa.vṛddhāś ca jānīyuḥ śauca.aśaucam atandritāḥ //E*  
*(Proper conduct for a dependant)*  
*05.4.01/ loka.yātrāvid rājānam ātma.dravya.prakṛti.saṁpannaṁ priya.hita.dvāreṇa^āśrayeta //*  
*05.4.02/ yaṁ vā manyeta "yathā^aham āśraya.īpsur evam asau vinaya.īpsur ābhigāmika.guṇa.yuktaḥ" iti, dravya.prakṛti.hīnam apy enam āśrayeta, na tv eva^anātma.saṁpannam //*  
*05.4.03/ anātmavā hi nīti.śāstra.dveṣād anarthya.saṁyogād vā prāpya^api mahad aiśvaryaṁ na bhavati //*  
*05.4.04/ ātmavati labdha.avakāśaḥ śāstra.anuyogaṁ dadyāt //*  
*05.4.05/ avisaṁvādādd hi sthāna.sthairyam avāpnoti //*  
*05.4.06/ mati.karmasu pṛṣṭhas tadātve ca^āyatyāṁ ca dharma.artha.saṁyuktaṁ samarthaṁ pravīṇavad apariṣad.bhīruḥ kathayet //*  
*05.4.07/ īpsitaḥ paṇeta "dharma.artha.anuyogam aviśiṣṭeṣu balavat.saṁyukteṣu daṇḍa.dhāraṇaṁ mat.saṁyoge tadātve ca daṇḍa.dhāraṇam iti na kuryāḥ, pakṣaṁ vṛttiṁ guhyaṁ ca me na^upahanyāḥ, saṁjñayā ca tvāṁ kāma.krodha.daṇḍaneṣu vārayeyam" iti //*  
*05.4.08/ ādiṣṭaḥ pradiṣṭāyāṁ bhūmāv anujñātaḥ praviśet, upaviśec ca pārśvataḥ saṁnikṛṣṭa.viprakṛṣṭaḥ para.āsanam //*  
*05.4.09/ vigṛhya kathanam asabhyam apratyakṣam aśraddheyam anṛtaṁ ca vākyam uccair anarmaṇi hāsaṁ vāta.ṣṭhīvane ca śabdavatī na kuryāt //*  
*05.4.10/ mithaḥ kathanam anyena, jana.vāde dvandva.kathanam, rājño veṣam uddhata.kuhakānāṁ ca, ratna.atiśaya.prakāśa.abhyarthanam, eka.akṣy.oṣṭha.nirbhogaṁ bhrukuṭī.karma vākya.avakṣepaṇaṁ ca bruvati, balavat saṁyukta.virodham, strībhiḥ strī.darśibhiḥ sāmanta.dūtair dveṣya.pakṣa.avakṣiptān arthyaiś ca pratisaṁsargam eka.artha.caryāṁ saṁghātaṁ ca varjayet*  
*//*  
*05.4.11ab/ ahīna.kālaṁ rāja.arthaṁ sva.arthaṁ priya.hitaiḥ saha /*  
*05.4.11cd/ para.arthaṁ deśa.kāle ca brūyād dharma.artha.saṁhitam //*  
*05.4.12ab/ pṛṣṭaḥ priya.hitaṁ brūyān na brūyād ahitaṁ priyam /*  
*05.4.12cd/ apriyaṁ vā hitaṁ brūyāt^śṛṇvato^anumato mithaḥ //*  
*05.4.13ab/ tūṣṇīṁ vā prativākye syād veṣya.ādīṁś ca na varṇayet /*  
*05.4.13cd/ apriyā api dakṣāḥ syus tad.bhāvād ye bahiṣ.kṛtāḥ //*  
*05.4.14ab/ anarthyāś ca priyā dṛṣṭāś citta.jñāna.anuvartinaḥ /*  
*05.4.14cd/ abhihāsyeṣv abhihased ghora.hāsāṁś ca varjayet //E*  
*05.4.15ab/ parāt saṁkrāmayed ghoraṁ na ca ghoraṁ pare vadet /*  
*05.4.15cd/ titikṣeta^ātmanaś caiva kṣamāvān pṛthivī.samaḥ //*  
*05.4.16ab/ ātma.rakṣā hi satataṁ pūrvaṁ kāryā vijānatā /*  
*05.4.16cd/ agnāv iva hi saṁproktā vṛttī rājā^upajīvinām //*  
*05.4.17ab/ eka.deśaṁ dahed agniḥ śarīraṁ vā paraṁ gataḥ /*  
*05.4.17cd/ sa-putra.dāraṁ rājā tu ghātayed ardhayeta vā //E*  
*(Proper behaviour for a courtier)*  
*05.5.01/ niyuktaḥ karmasu vyaya.viśuddham udayaṁ darśayet //*  
*05.5.02/ ābhyantaraṁ bāhyaṁ guhyaṁ prakāśyam ātyayikam upekṣitavyaṁ vā kāryaṁ "idam evam" iti viśeṣayec ca //*  
*05.5.03/ mṛgayā.dyūta.madya.strīṣu prasaktaṁ na^enam anuvarteta praśaṁsābhiḥ //*  
*05.5.04/ āsannaś ca^asya vyasana.upaghāte prayateta, para.upajāpa.atisaṁdhāna.upadhibhyaś ca rakṣet //*  
*05.5.05/ iṅgita.ākārau ca^asya lakṣayet //*  
*05.5.06/ kāma.dveṣa.harṣa.dainya.vyavasāya.bhaya.dvandva.viparyāsam iṅgita.ākārābhyāṁ hi mantra.saṁvaraṇa.artham ācarati prājñaḥ //*  
*05.5.07/ darśane prasīdati, vākyaṁ pratigṛhṇāti, āsanaṁ dadāti, vivikto darśayate, śaṅkā.sthāne na^atiśaṅkate, kathāyāṁ ramate, parijñāpyeṣv avekṣate, pathyam uktaṁ sahate, smayamāno niyuṅkte, hastena spṛśati, ślāghye na^upahasati, parokṣaṁ guṇaṁ bravīti, bhakṣyeṣu smarati, saha vihāraṁ yāti, vyasane^abhyupapadyate, tad.bhaktīn pūjayati, guhyam ācaṣṭe,*  
*mānaṁ vardhayati, arthaṁ karoti, anarthaṁ pratihanti - iti tuṣṭa.jñānam //*  
*05.5.08/ etad eva viparītam atuṣṭasya, bhūyaś ca vakṣyāmaḥ //*  
*05.5.09/ saṁdarśane kopaḥ, vākyasya^aśravaṇa.pratiṣedhau, āsana.cakṣuṣor adānam, varṇa.svara.bhedaḥ, eka.akṣi.bhrukuṭy.oṣṭha.nirbhogaḥ, sveda.śvāsa.smitānām asthāna.utpattiḥ, para.mantraṇam, akasmād.vrajanam, vardhanam anyasya, bhūmi.gātra.vilekhanam, anyasya^upatodanam, vidyā.varṇa.deśa.kutsā, sama.doṣa.nindā, pratidoṣa.nindā, pratiloma.stavaḥ,*  
*sukṛta.anavekṣaṇam, duṣkṛta.anukīrtanam, pṛṣṭha.avadhānam, atityāgaḥ, mithyā.abhibhāṣaṇam, rāja.darśināṁ ca tad.vṛtta.anyatvam //*  
*05.5.10/ vṛtti.vikāraṁ ca^avekṣeta^apy amānuṣāṇām //*  
*05.5.11/ "ayam uccaiḥ siñcati" iti kātyāyanaḥ pravavrāja, "krauñco^apasavyam" iti kaṇiṅko bhāradvājaḥ, "tṛṇam" iti dīrghaś cārāyaṇaḥ, "śītā śāṭī" iti ghoṭa.mukhaḥ, "hastī pratyaukṣīt" iti kiñjalkaḥ, "ratha.aśvaṁ prāśaṁsīt" iti piśunaḥ, prati.ravaṇe śunaḥ piśuna.putraḥ //*  
*05.5.12/ artha.māna.avakṣepe ca parityāgaḥ //*  
*05.5.13/ svāmi.śīlam ātmanaś ca kilbiṣam upalabhya vā pratikurvīta //*  
*05.5.14/ mitram upakṛṣṭaṁ vā^asya gacchet //*  
*05.5.15ab/ tatrastho doṣa.nirghātaṁ mitrair bhartari ca^ācaret /*  
*05.5.15cd/ tato bhartari jīve vā mṛte vā punar āvrajet //E*  
*(Continuance of the kingdom)*  
*(Continuous sovereignty)*  
*05.6.01/ rāja.vyasanam evam amātyaḥ pratikurvīta //*  
*05.6.02/ prāg eva maraṇa.ābādha.bhayād rājñaḥ priya.hita.upagraheṇa māsa.dvi.māsa.antaraṁ darśanaṁ sthāpayed "deśa.pīḍā.apaham amitra.apaham āyuṣyaṁ putrīyaṁ vā karma rājā sādhayati" ity apadeśena //*  
*05.6.03/ rāja.vyañjanam arūpa.velāyāṁ prakṛtīnāṁ darśayet, mitra.amitra.dūtānāṁ ca //*  
*05.6.04/ taiś ca yathā.ucitāṁ saṁbhāṣām amātya.mukho gacchet //*  
*05.6.05/ dauvārika.antar.vaṁśika.mukhaś ca yathā.uktaṁ rāja.praṇidhim anuvartayet //*  
*05.6.06/ apakāriṣu ca heḍaṁ prasādaṁ vā prakṛti.kāntaṁ darśayet, prasādam eva^upakāriṣu //*  
*05.6.07/ āpta.puruṣa.adhiṣṭhitau durga.pratyantasthau vā kośa.daṇḍāv ekasthau kārayet, kulya.kumāra.mukhyāṁś ca^anya.apadeśena //*  
*05.6.08/ yaś ca mukhyaḥ pakṣavān durga.aṭavīstho vā vaiguṇyaṁ bhajeta tam upagrāhayet //*  
*05.6.09/ bahv.ābādhaṁ vā yātrāṁ preṣayet, mitra.kulaṁ vā //*  
*05.6.10/ yasmāc ca sāmantād ābādhaṁ paśyet tam utsava.vivāha.hasti.bandhana.aśva.paṇya.bhūmi.pradāna.apadeśena^avagrāhayet, sva.mitreṇa vā //*  
*05.6.11/ tataḥ saṁdhim adūṣyaṁ kārayet //*  
*05.6.12/ āṭavika.amitrair vā vairaṁ grāhayet //*  
*05.6.13/ tat.kulīnam aparuddhaṁ vā bhūṁy.eka.deśena^upagrāhayet //*  
*05.6.14/ kulya.kumāra.mukhya.upagrahaṁ kṛtvā vā kumāram abhiṣiktam eva darśayet //*  
*05.6.15/ dāṇḍa.karmikavad vā rājya.kaṇṭakān uddhṛtya rājyaṁ kārayet //*  
*05.6.16/ yadi vā kaścin mukhyaḥ sāmanta.ādīnām anyatamaḥ kopaṁ bhajeta taṁ "ehi, rājānaṁ tvā kariṣyāmi" ity āvāhayitvā ghātayet //*  
*05.6.17/ āpat.pratīkāreṇa vā sādhayet //*  
*05.6.18/ yuva.rāje vā krameṇa rājya.bhāram āropya rāja.vyasanaṁ khyāpayet //*  
*05.6.19/ para.bhūmau rāja.vyasane mitreṇa^amitra.vyañjanena śatroḥ saṁdhim avasthāpya^apagacchet //*  
*05.6.20/ sāmanta.ādīnām anyatamaṁ vā^asya durge sthāpayitvā^apagacchet //*  
*05.6.21/ kumāram abhiṣicya vā prativyūheta //*  
*05.6.22/ pareṇa^abhiyukto vā yathā.uktam āpat.pratīkāraṁ kuryāt //*  
*05.6.23/ evam eka.aiśvaryam amātyaḥ kārayed iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*05.6.24/ "na^evam" iti bhāradvājaḥ //*  
*05.6.25/ "pramriyamāṇe vā rājany amātyaḥ kulya.kumāra.mukhyān parasparaṁ mukhyeṣu vā vikramayet //*  
*05.6.26/ vikrāntaṁ prakṛti.kopena ghātayet //*  
*05.6.27/ kulya.kumāra.mukhyān upāṁśu.daṇḍena vā sādhayitvā svayaṁ rājyaṁ gṛhṇīyāt //*  
*05.6.28/ rājya.kāraṇādd hi pitā putrān putrāś ca pitaram abhidruhyanti, kim aṅga punar amātya.prakṛtir hy eka.pragraho rājyasya //*  
*05.6.29/ tat svayam upasthitaṁ na^avamanyeta //*  
*05.6.30/ "svayam ārūḍhā hi strī tyajyamānā^abhiśapati" iti loka.pravādaḥ //*  
*05.6.31ab/ kālaś ca sakṛd abhyeti yaṁ naraṁ kāla.kāṅkṣiṇam /*  
*05.6.31cd/ durlabhaḥ sa punas tasya kālaḥ karma cikīrṣataḥ //*  
*05.6.32/ prakṛti.kopakam adharmiṣṭham anaikāntikaṁ ca^etad iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*05.6.33/ rāja.putram ātma.saṁpannaṁ rājye sthāpayet //*  
*05.6.34/ saṁpanna.abhāve^avyasaninaṁ kumāraṁ rāja.kanyāṁ garbhiṇīṁ devīṁ vā puras.kṛtya mahā.mātrān saṁnipātya brūyāt "ayaṁ vo nikṣepaḥ, pitaram asya^avekṣadhvaṁ sattva.abhijanam ātmanaś ca, dhvaja.mātro^ayaṁ bhavanta eva svāminaḥ, kathaṁ vā kriyatām" iti //*  
*05.6.35/ tathā bruvāṇaṁ yoga.puruṣā brūyuḥ "ko^anyo bhavat.purogād asmād rājñaś cāturvarṇyam arhati pālayitum" iti //*  
*05.6.36/ "tathā" ity amātyaḥ kumāraṁ rāja.kanyāṁ garbhiṇīṁ devīṁ vā^adhikurvīta, bandhu.saṁbandhināṁ mitra.amitra.dūtānāṁ ca darśayet //*  
*05.6.37/ bhakta.vetana.viśeṣam amātyānām āyudhīyānāṁ ca kārayet, "bhūyaś ca^ayaṁ vṛddhaḥ kariṣyati" iti brūyāt //*  
*05.6.38/ evaṁ durga.rāṣṭra.mukhyān ābhāṣeta, yathā.arhaṁ ca mitra.amitra.pakṣam //*  
*05.6.39/ vinaya.karmaṇi ca kumārasya prayateta //*  
*05.6.40/ kanyāyāṁ samāna.jātīyād apatyam utpādya vā^abhiṣiñcet //*  
*05.6.41/ mātuś citta.kṣobha.bhayāt kulyam alpa.sattvaṁ chātraṁ ca lakṣaṇyam upanidadhyāt //*  
*05.6.42/ ṛtau ca^enāṁ rakṣet //*  
*05.6.43/ na ca^ātma.arthaṁ kaṁcid utkṛṣṭam upabhogaṁ kārayet //*  
*05.6.44/ rāja.arthaṁ tu yāna.vāhana.ābharaṇa.vastra.strī.veśma.parivāpān kārayet //*  
*05.6.45ab/ yauvanasthaṁ ca yāceta viśramaṁ citta.kāraṇāt /*  
*05.6.45cd/ parityajed atuṣyantaṁ tuṣyantaṁ ca^anupālayet //*  
*05.6.46ab/ nivedya putra.rakṣā.arthaṁ gūḍha.sāra.parigrahān /*  
*05.6.46cd/ araṇyaṁ dīrgha.sattraṁ vā seveta^ārucyatāṁ gataḥ //*  
*05.6.47ab/ mukhyair avagṛhītaṁ vā rājānaṁ tat.priya.āśritaḥ /*  
*05.6.47cd/ itihāsa.purāṇābhyāṁ bodhayed artha.śāstravit //*  
*05.6.48ab/ siddha.vyañjana.rūpo vā yogam āsthāya pārthivam /*  
*05.6.48cd/ labheta labdhvā dūṣyeṣu dāṇḍakarmikam ācaret //E*  
*((maṇḍala.yoniḥ ṣaṣṭham adhikaraṇam))*  
*(ṣaṇ.ṇavatitamaṁ prakaraṇaṁ - prakṛti.sampadah)*

### 6

*06.1.01/ svāmy.amātya.jana.pada.durga.kośa.daṇḍa.mitrāṇi prakṛtayaḥ //*  
*06.1.02/ tatra svāmi.sampat //*  
*06.1.03/ mahā.kulīno daiva.buddhi.sattva.sampanno vṛddha.darśī dhārmikaḥ satya.vāg avisaṁvādakaḥ kṛtajñaḥ sthūla.lakṣo mahā.utsāho^adīrgha.sūtraḥ śakya.sāmanto dṛḍha.buddhir akṣudra.pariṣatko vinaya.kāma ity ābhigāmikā guṇāḥ //*  
*06.1.04/ śuśrūṣā.śravaṇa.grahaṇa.dhāraṇa.vijñāna.ūha.apoha.tattva.abhiniveśāḥ prajñā.guṇāḥ //*  
*06.1.05/ śauryam amarṣaḥ śīghratā dākṣyaṁ ca^utsāha.guṇāḥ //*  
*06.1.06/ vāgmī pragalbhaḥ smṛti.mati.balavān udagraḥ sv.avagrahaḥ kṛta.śilpo^avyasano daṇḍa.nāyy upakāra.apakārayor dṛṣṭa.pratīkārī hrīmān āpat.prakṛtyor viniyoktā dīrgha.dūra.darśī deśa.kāla.puruṣa.kāra.kārya.pradhānaḥ saṁdhi.vikrama.tyāga.samyama.paṇa.parac.chidra.vibhāgī saṁvṛto^adīna.abhihāsya.jihma.bhrukuṭī.kṣaṇaḥ*  
*kāma.krodha.lobha.stambha.cāpala.upatāpa.paiśunya.hīnaḥ śaklaḥ smita.udagra.abhibhāṣī vṛddha.upadeśa.ācāra ity ātma.sampat //*  
*06.1.07/ amātya.sampad uktā purastāt //*  
*06.1.08/ madhye ca^ante ca sthānavān ātma.dhāraṇaḥ para.dhāraṇaś ca^āpadi sva.ārakṣaḥ sva.ājīvaḥ śatru.dveṣī śakya.sāmantaḥ paṅka.pāṣāṇa.uṣara.viṣama.kaṇṭaka.śreṇī.vyāla.mṛga.aṭavī.hīnaḥ kāntaḥ sītā.khani.dravya.hasti.vanavān gavyaḥ pauruṣeyo gupta.gocaraḥ paśumān adeva.mātṛko vāri.sthala.pathābhyām upetaḥ sāra.citra.bahu.paṇyo daṇḍa.kara.sahaḥ*  
*karma.śīla.karṣako^abāliśa.svāmy.avara.varṇa.prāyo bhakta.śuci.manuṣya iti jana.pada.sampat //*  
*06.1.09/ durga.sampad uktā purastāt //*  
*06.1.10/ dharma.adhigataḥ pūrvaiḥ svayaṁ vā hema.rūpya.prāyaś citra.sthūla.ratna.hiraṇyo dīrghām apy āpadam anāyatiṁ saheta^iti kośa.sampat //*  
*06.1.11/ pitṛ.paitāmaho nityo vaśyas tuṣṭa.bhṛta.putra.dāraḥ pravāseṣv avisaṁvāditaḥ sarvatra^apratihato duḥkha.saho bahu.yuddhaḥ sarva.yuddha.praharaṇa.vidyā.viśāradaḥ saha.vṛddhi.kṣayikatvād advaidhyaḥ kṣatra.prāya iti daṇḍa.sampat //*  
*06.1.12/ pitṛ.paitāmahaṁ nityaṁ vaśyam advaidhyaṁ mahal.laghu.samuttham iti mitra.sampat //*  
*06.1.13/ arāja.bījī lubdhaḥ kṣudra.pariṣatko virakta.prakṛtir anyāya.vṛttir ayukto vyasanī nirutsāho daiva.pramāṇo yat.kiṁcana.kārya.gatir ananubandhaḥ klībo nitya.apakārī ca^ity amitra.sampat //*  
*06.1.14/ evaṁ.bhūto hi śatruḥ sukhaḥ samucchettuṁ bhavati //*  
*06.1.15ab/ ari.varjāḥ prakṛtayaḥ sapta^etāḥ sva.guṇa.udayāḥ /*  
*06.1.15cd/ uktāḥ pratyaṅga.bhūtās tāḥ prakṛtā rāja.sampadaḥ //*  
*06.1.16ab/ sampādayaty asampannāḥ prakṛtīr ātmavān nṛpaḥ /*  
*06.1.16cd/ vivṛddhāś ca^anuraktāś ca prakṛtīr hanty anātmavān //*  
*06.1.17ab/ tataḥ sa duṣṭa.prakṛtiś cāturanto^apy anātmavān /*  
*06.1.17cd/ hanyate vā prakṛtibhir yāti vā dviṣatāṁ vaśam //*  
*06.1.18ab/ ātmavāṁs tv alpa.deśo^api yuktaḥ prakṛti.sampadā /*  
*06.1.18cd/nayajñaḥ pṛthivīṁ kṛtsnāṁ jayaty eva na hīyate //E*  
*(śama.vyāyāmikam)*  
*06.2.01/ śama.vyāyāmau yoga.kṣemayor yoniḥ //*  
*06.2.02/ karma.ārambhāṇāṁ yoga.ārādhano vyāyāmaḥ //*  
*06.2.03/ karma.phala.upabhogānāṁ kṣema.ārādhanaḥ śamaḥ //*  
*06.2.04/ śama.vyāyāmayor yoniḥ ṣāḍguṇyam //*  
*06.2.05/ kṣayaḥ sthānaṁ vṛddhir ity udayās tasya //*  
*06.2.06/ mānuṣaṁ naya.apanayau, daivam aya.anayau //*  
*06.2.07/ daiva.mānuṣaṁ hi karma lokaṁ yāpayati //*  
*06.2.08/ adṛṣṭa.kāritaṁ daivam //*  
*06.2.09/ tasminn iṣṭena phalena yogo^ayaḥ, aniṣṭena^anayaḥ //*  
*06.2.10/ dṛṣṭa.kāritaṁ mānuṣam //*  
*06.2.11/ tasmin yoga.kṣema.niṣpattir nayaḥ, vipattir apanayaḥ //*  
*06.2.12/ tac cintyam, acintyaṁ daivam //*  
*06.2.13/ rājā ātma.dravya.prakṛti.sampanno nayasya^adhiṣṭhānaṁ vijigīṣuḥ //*  
*06.2.14/ tasya samantato maṇḍalī.bhūtā bhūmy.anantarā ari.prakṛtiḥ //*  
*06.2.15/ tathā^eva bhūmy.eka.antarā mitra.prakṛtiḥ //*  
*06.2.16/ ari.sampad.yuktaḥ sāmantaḥ śatruḥ, vyasanī yātavyaḥ, anapāśrayo durbala.āśrayo vā^ucchedanīyaḥ, viparyaye pīḍanīyaḥ karśanīyo vā //*  
*06.2.17/ ity ari.viśeṣāḥ //*  
*06.2.18/ tasmān mitram ari.mitraṁ mitra.mitram ari.mitra.mitraṁ ca^ānantaryeṇa bhūmīnāṁ prasajyante purastāt, paścāt pārṣṇi.grāha ākrandaḥ pārṣṇi.grāha.āsāra ākranda.āsāraḥ //*  
*06.2.19/ bhūmy.anantaraḥ prakṛti.mitraḥ, tulya.abhijanaḥ sahajaḥ, viruddho virodhayitā vā kṛtrimaḥ //*  
*06.2.20/ bhūmy.eka.antaraṁ prakṛti.mitram, mātā.pitṛ.sambaddhaṁ sahajam, dhana.jīvita.hetor āśritaṁ kṛtrimam //*  
*06.2.21/ ari.vijigīṣvor bhūmy.anantaraḥ saṁhata.asaṁhatayor anugraha.samartho nigrahe ca^asaṁhatayor madhyamaḥ //*  
*06.2.22/ ari.vijigīṣu.madhyānāṁ bahiḥ prakṛtibhyo balavattaraḥ saṁhata.asaṁhatānām ari.vijigīṣu.madhyamānām anugraha.samartho nigrahe ca^asaṁhatānām udāsīnaḥ //*  
*06.2.23/ iti prakṛtayaḥ //*  
*06.2.24/ vijigīṣur mitraṁ mitra.mitraṁ vā^asya prakṛtayas tisraḥ //*  
*06.2.25/ tāḥ pañcabhir amātya.jana.pada.durga.kośa.daṇḍa.prakṛtibhir eka.ekaśaḥ samyuktā maṇḍalam aṣṭādaśakaṁ bhavati //*  
*06.2.26/ anena maṇḍala.pṛthaktvaṁ vyākhyātam ari.madhyama.udāsīnānām //*  
*06.2.27/ evaṁ catur.maṇḍala.saṁkṣepaḥ //*  
*06.2.28/ dvādaśa rāja.prakṛtayaḥ ṣaṣṭir dravya.prakṛtayaḥ, saṁkṣepeṇa dvi.saptatiḥ //*  
*06.2.29/ tāsāṁ yathā.svaṁ sampadaḥ //*  
*06.2.30/ śaktiḥ siddhiś ca //*  
*06.2.31/ balaṁ śaktiḥ //*  
*06.2.32/ sukhaṁ siddhiḥ //*  
*06.2.33/ śaktis trividhā - jñāna.balaṁ mantra.śaktiḥ, kośa.daṇḍa.balaṁ prabhu.śaktiḥ, vikrama.balam utsāha.śaktiḥ //*  
*06.2.34/ evaṁ siddhis trividhā^eva - mantra.śakti.sādhyā mantra.siddhiḥ, prabhu.śakti.sādhyā prabhu.siddhiḥ, utsāha.śakti.sādhyā utsāha.siddhiḥ //*  
*06.2.35/ tābhir abhyuccito jyāyān bhavati, apacito hīnaḥ, tulya.śaktiḥ samaḥ //*  
*06.2.36/ tasmāt^śaktiṁ siddhiṁ ca ghaṭeta^ātmany āveśayitum, sādhāraṇo vā dravya.prakṛtiṣv ānantaryeṇa śauca.vaśena vā //*  
*06.2.37/ dūṣya.amitrābhyāṁ vā^apakraṣṭuṁ yateta //*  
*06.2.38/ yadi vā paśyet "amitro me śakti.yukto vāg.daṇḍa.pāruṣya.artha.dūṣaṇaiḥ prakṛtīr upahaniṣyati, siddhi.yukto vā mṛgayā.dyūta.madya.strībhiḥ pramādaṁ gamiṣyati, sa virakta.prakṛtir upakṣīṇaḥ pramatto vā sādhyo me bhaviṣyati, vigraha.abhiyukto vā sarva.saṁdohena^ekastho^adurgastho vā sthāsyati, sa saṁhata.sainyo mitra.durga.viyuktaḥ sādhyo me bhaviṣyati,*  
*"balavān vā rājā parataḥ śatrum ucchettu.kāmaḥ tam ucchidya mām ucchindyād" iti balavatā prārthitasya me vipanna.karma.ārambhasya vā sāhāyyaṁ dāsyati", madhyama.lipsāyāṁ ca, ity evaṁ.ādiṣu kāraṇeṣv amitrasya^api śaktiṁ*  
*siddhiṁ ca^icchet //*  
*06.2.39ab/ nemim eka.antarān rājñaḥ kṛtvā ca^anantarān arān /*  
*06.2.39cd/ nābhim ātmānam āyacchen netā prakṛti.maṇḍale //*  
*06.2.40ab/ madhye hy upahitaḥ śatrur netur mitrasya ca^ubhayoḥ /*  
*06.2.40cd/ ucchedyaḥ pīḍanīyo vā balavān api jāyate //E*  
*(ṣāḍguṇya.samuddeśaḥ - kṣaya.sthāna.vṛddhi.niścayah)*

### 7

*07.1.01/ ṣāḍguṇyasya prakṛti.maṇḍalaṁ yoniḥ //*  
*07.1.02/ "saṁdhi.vigraha.āsana.yāna.saṁśraya.dvaidhī.bhāvāḥ ṣāḍguṇyam" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.1.03/ "dvaiguṇyam" iti vāta.vyādhiḥ //*  
*07.1.04/ "saṁdhi.vigrahābhyāṁ hi ṣāḍguṇyaṁ sampadyate" iti //*  
*07.1.05/ ṣāḍguṇyam eva^etad avasthā.bhedād iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.1.06/ tatra paṇa.bandhaḥ saṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.1.07/ apakāro vigrahaḥ //*  
*07.1.08/ upekṣaṇam āsanam //*  
*07.1.09/ abhyuccayo yānam //*  
*07.1.10/ para.arpaṇaṁ saṁśrayaḥ //*  
*07.1.11/ saṁdhi.vigraha.upādānaṁ dvaidhī.bhāvaḥ //*  
*07.1.12/ iti ṣaḍ.guṇāḥ //*  
*07.1.13/ parasmādd hīyamānaḥ saṁdadhīta //*  
*07.1.14/ abhyuccīyamāno vigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.1.15/ "na māṁ paro na^ahaṁ param upahantuṁ śaktaḥ" ity āsīta //*  
*07.1.16/ guṇa.atiśaya.yukto yāyāt //*  
*07.1.17/ śakti.hīnaḥ saṁśrayeta //*  
*07.1.18/ sahāya.sādhye kārye dvaidhībhāvaṁ gacchet //*  
*07.1.19/ iti guṇa.avasthāpanam //*  
*07.1.20/ teṣāṁ yasmin vā guṇe sthitaḥ paśyet "iha.sthaḥ śakṣyāmi durga.setu.karma.vaṇik.patha.śūnya.niveśa.khani.dravya.hasti.vana.karmāṇy ātmanaḥ pravartayitum, parasya ca^etāni karmāṇy upahantum" iti tam ātiṣṭhet //*  
*07.1.21/ sā vṛddhiḥ //*  
*07.1.22/ "āśutarā me vṛddhir bhūyastarā vṛddhy.udayatarā vā bhaviṣyati, viparītā parasya" iti jñātvā para.vṛddhim upekṣeta //*  
*07.1.23/ tulya.kāla.phala.udayāyāṁ vā vṛddhau saṁdhim upeyāt //*  
*07.1.24/ yasmin vā guṇe sthitaḥ sva.karmaṇām upaghātaṁ paśyen na^itarasya tasmin na tiṣṭhet //*  
*07.1.25/ eṣa kṣayaḥ //*  
*07.1.26/ "ciratareṇa^alpataraṁ vṛddhy.udayataraṁ vā kṣeṣye, viparītaṁ paraḥ" iti jñātvā kṣayam upekṣeta //*  
*07.1.27/ tulya.kāla.phala.udaye vā kṣaye saṁdhim upeyāt //*  
*07.1.28/ yasmin vā guṇe sthitaḥ sva.karma.vṛddhiṁ kṣayaṁ vā na^abhipaśyed etat.sthānam //*  
*07.1.29/ "hrasvataraṁ vṛddhy.udayataraṁ vā sthāsyāmi, viparītaṁ paraḥ" iti jñātvā sthānam upekṣeta //*  
*07.1.30/ "tulya.kāla.phala.udaye vā sthāne saṁdhim upeyād" ity ācāryāḥ //*
