*07.1.31/ na^etad vibhāṣitam iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.1.32a/ yadi vā paśyet "sandhau sthito mahā.phalaiḥ sva.karmabhiḥ para.karmāṇy upahaniṣyāmi, mahā.phalāni vā sva.karmāṇy upabhokṣye, para.karmāṇi vā, saṁdhi.viśvāsena vā yoga.upaniṣat.praṇidhibhiḥ para.karmāṇy upahaniṣyāmi, sukhaṁ vā sa.anugraha.parihāra.saukaryaṁ phala.lābha.bhūyastvena sva.karmaṇāṁ para.karma.yoga.āvahaṁ janam āsrāvayiṣyāmi - //*  
*07.1.32b/ balinā^atimātreṇa vā saṁhitaḥ paraḥ sva.karma.upaghātaṁ prāpsyati, yena vā vigṛhīto mayā.saṁdhatte tena^asya vigrahaṁ dīrghaṁ kariṣyāmi, mayā vā saṁhitasya mad.dveṣiṇo jana.padaṁ pīḍayiṣyati - //*  
*07.1.32c/ para.upahato vā^asya jana.pado mām āgamiṣyati, tataḥ karmasu vṛddhiṁ prāpsyāmi, vipanna.karma.ārambho vā viṣamasthaḥ paraḥ karmasu na me vikrameta - //*  
*07.1.32d/ parataḥ pravṛtta.karma.ārambho vā tābhyāṁ saṁhitaḥ karmasu vṛddhiṁ prāpsyāmi, śatru.pratibaddhaṁ vā śatruṇā saṁdhiṁ kṛtvā maṇḍalaṁ bhetsyāmi - //*  
*07.1.32e/ bhinnam avāpsyāmi, daṇḍa.anugraheṇa vā śatrum upagṛhya maṇḍala.lipsāyāṁ vidveṣaṁ grāhayiṣyāmi, vidviṣṭaṁ tena^eva ghātayiṣyāmi" iti saṁdhinā vṛddhim ātiṣṭhet //*  
*07.1.33a/ yadi vā paśyet "āyudhīya.prāyaḥ śreṇī.prāyo vā me jana.padaḥ śaila.vana.nadī.durga.eka.dvāra.ārakṣo vā śakṣyati para.abhiyogaṁ pratihantum, viṣaya.ante durgam aviṣahyam apāśrito vā śakṣyāmi para.karmāṇy upahantuṁ - //*  
*07.1.33b/ vyasana.pīḍa.upahata.utsāho vā paraḥ samprāpta.karma.upaghāta.kālaḥ, vigṛhītasya^anyato vā śakṣyāmi jana.padam apavāhayitum" iti vigrahe sthito vṛddhim ātiṣṭhet //*  
*07.1.34/ yadi vā manyeta "na me śaktaḥ paraḥ karmāṇy upahantuṁ na^ahaṁ tasya karma.upaghātī vā, vyasanam asya, śva.varāhayor iva kalahe vā, sva.karma.anuṣṭhāna.paro vā vardhiṣye" ity āsanena vṛddhim ātiṣṭhet //*  
*07.1.35/ yadi vā manyeta "yāna.sādhyaḥ karma.upaghātaḥ śatroḥ, prativihita.sva.karma.ārakṣaś ca^asmi" iti yānena vṛddhim ātiṣṭhet //*  
*07.1.36/ yadi vā manyeta "na^asmi śaktaḥ para.karmāṇy upahantum, sva.karma.upaghātaṁ vā trātum" iti, balavantam āśritaḥ sva.karma.anuṣṭhānena kṣayāt sthānaṁ sthānād vṛddhiṁ ca^ākāṅkṣeta //*  
*07.1.37/ yadi vā manyeta "saṁdhinā^ekataḥ sva.karmāṇi pravartayiṣyāmi, vigraheṇa^ekataḥ para.karmāṇy upahaniṣyāmi" iti dvaidhī.bhāvena vṛddhim ātiṣṭhet //*  
*07.1.38ab/ evaṁ ṣaḍbhir guṇair etaiḥ sthitaḥ prakṛti.maṇḍale /*  
*07.1.38cd/ paryeṣeta kṣayāt sthānaṁ sthānād vṛddhiṁ ca karmasu //E*  
*(saṁśraya.vṛtti)*  
*07.2.01/ saṁdhi.vigrahayos tulyāyāṁ vṛddhau saṁdhim upeyāt //*  
*07.2.02/ vigrahe hi kṣaya.vyaya.pravāsa.pratyavāyā bhavanti //*  
*07.2.03/ tena^āsana.yānayor āsanaṁ vyākhyātam //*  
*07.2.04/ dvaidhī.bhāva.saṁśrayayor dvaidhī.bhāvaṁ gacchet //*  
*07.2.05/ dvaidhī.bhūto hi sva.karma.pradhāna ātmana eva^upakaroti, saṁśritas tu parasya^upakaroti, na^ātmanaḥ //*  
*07.2.06/ yad.balaḥ sāmantas tad.viśiṣṭa.balam āśrayet //*  
*07.2.07/ tad.viśiṣṭa.bala.abhāve tam eva^āśritaḥ kośa.daṇḍa.bhūmīnām anyatamena^asya^upakartum adṛṣṭaḥ prayateta //*  
*07.2.08/ mahā.doṣo hi viśiṣṭa.bala.samāgamo rājñām, anyatra^ari.vigṛhītāt //*  
*07.2.09/ aśakye daṇḍa.upanatavad varteta //*  
*07.2.10/ yadā ca^asya prāṇa.haraṁ vyādhim antaḥ.kopaṁ śatru.vṛddhiṁ mitra.vyasanam upasthitaṁ vā tan.nimittām ātmanaś ca vṛddhiṁ paśyet tadā sambhāvya.vyādhi.dharma.kārya.apadeśena^apayāyāt //*  
*07.2.11/ sva.viṣayastho vā na^upagacchet //*  
*07.2.12/ āsanno vā^asya cchidreṣu praharet //*  
*07.2.13/ balīyasor vā madhya.gatas trāṇa.samartham āśrayeta, yasya vā^antardhiḥ syāt, ubhau vā //*  
*07.2.14/ kapāla.saṁśrayas tiṣṭhet, mūla.haram itarasya^itaram apadiśan //*  
*07.2.15/ bhedam ubhayor vā paraspara.apadeśaṁ prayuñjīta, bhinnayor upāṁśu.daṇḍam //*  
*07.2.16/ pārśvastho vā balasthayor āsanna.bhayāt pratikurvīta //*  
*07.2.17/ durga.apāśrayo vā dvaidhī.bhūtas tiṣṭhet //*  
*07.2.18/ saṁdhi.vigraha.krama.hetubhir vā ceṣṭeta //*  
*07.2.19/ dūṣya.amitra.āṭavikān ubhayor upagṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.2.20/ etayor anyataraṁ gacchaṁs tair eva^anyatarasya vyasane praharet //*  
*07.2.21/ dvābhyām upahato vā maṇḍala.apāśrayas tiṣṭhet, madhyamam udāsīnaṁ vā saṁśrayeta //*  
*07.2.22/ tena saha^ekam upagṛhya^itaram ucchindyād, ubhau vā //*  
*07.2.23/ dvābhyām ucchinno vā madhyama.udāsīnayos tat.pakṣīyāṇāṁ vā rājñāṁ nyāya.vṛttim āśrayeta //*  
*07.2.24/ tulyānāṁ vā yasya prakṛtayaḥ sukhyeyur enam, yatrastho vā śaknuyād ātmānam uddhartum, yatra vā pūrva.puruṣa.ucitā gatir āsannaḥ sambandho vā, mitrāṇi bhūyāṁsy atiśaktimanti vā bhaveyuḥ //*  
*07.2.25ab/ priyo yasya bhaved yo vā priyo^asya kataras tayoḥ /*  
*07.2.25cd/ priyo yasya sa taṁ gacched ity āśraya.gatiḥ parā //E*  
*(sama.hīna.jyāyasāṁ guṇa.abhiniveśaḥ - hīna.saṁdhayah)*  
*07.3.01/ vijigīṣuḥ śakty.apekṣaḥ ṣāḍguṇyam upayuñjīta //*  
*07.3.02/ sama.jyāyobhyāṁ saṁdhīyeta, hīnena vigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.3.03/ vigṛhīto hi jyāyasā hastinā pāda.yuddham iva^abhyupaiti //*  
*07.3.04/ samena ca^āmaṁ pātram āmena^ahatam iva^ubhayataḥ kṣayaṁ karoti //*  
*07.3.05/ kumbhena^iva^aśmā hīnena^eka.anta.siddhim avāpnoti //*  
*07.3.06/ jyāyāṁś cen na saṁdhim icched daṇḍa.upanata.vṛttam ābalīyasaṁ vā yogam ātiṣṭhet //*  
*07.3.07/ samaś cen na saṁdhim icched yāvan.mātram apakuryāt tāvan.mātram asya pratyapakuryāt //*  
*07.3.08/ tejo hi saṁdhāna.kāraṇam //*  
*07.3.09/ na^ataptaṁ lohaṁ lohena saṁdhatta iti //*  
*07.3.10/ hīnaś cet sarvatra^anupraṇatas tiṣṭhet saṁdhim upeyāt //*  
*07.3.11/ āraṇyo^agnir iva hi duḥkha.amarṣajaṁ tejo vikramayati //*  
*07.3.12/ maṇḍalasya ca^anugrāhyo bhavati //*  
*07.3.13/ saṁhitaś cet "para.prakṛtayo lubdha.kṣīṇa.apacaritāḥ pratyādāna.bhayād vā na^upagacchanti" iti paśyedd hīno^api vigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.3.14/ vigṛhītaś cet "para.prakṛtayo lubdha.kṣīṇa.apacaritā vigraha.udvignā vā māṁ na^upagacchanti" iti paśyej jyāyān api saṁdhīyeta, vigraha.udvegaṁ vā śamayet //*  
*07.3.15/ vyasana.yaugapadye^api "guru.vyasano^asmi, laghu.vyasanaḥ paraḥ sukhena pratikṛtya vyasanam ātmano^abhiyuñjyād" iti paśyej jyāyān api saṁdhīyeta //*  
*07.3.16/ saṁdhi.vigrahayoś cet para.karśanam ātma.upacayaṁ vā na^abhipaśyej jyāyān apy āsīta //*  
*07.3.17/ para.vyasanam apratikāryaṁ cet paśyedd hīno^apy abhiyāyāt //*  
*07.3.18/ apratikārya.āsanna.vyasano vā jyāyān api saṁśrayeta //*  
*07.3.19/ saṁdhinā^ekato vigraheṇa^ekataś cet kārya.siddhiṁ paśyej jyāyān api dvaidhī.bhūtas tiṣṭhet //*  
*07.3.20/ evaṁ samasya ṣāḍguṇya.upayogaḥ //*  
*07.3.21/ tatra tu prativiśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.3.22ab/ pravṛtta.cakreṇa^ākrānto rājñā balavatā^abalaḥ /*  
*07.3.22cd/ saṁdhinā^upanamet tūrṇaṁ kośa.daṇḍa.ātma.bhūmibhiḥ //*  
*07.3.23ab/ svayaṁ saṁkhyāta.daṇḍena daṇḍasya vibhavena vā /*  
*07.3.23cd/ upasthātavyam ity eṣa saṁdhir ātma.āmiṣo mataḥ //*  
*07.3.24ab/ senā.pati.kumārābhyām upasthātavyam ity ayam /*  
*07.3.24cd/ puruṣa.antara.saṁdhiḥ syān na^ātmanā^ity ātma.rakṣaṇaḥ //*  
*07.3.25ab/ ekena^anyatra yātavyaṁ svayaṁ daṇḍena vā^ity ayam /*  
*07.3.25cd/ adṛṣṭa.puruṣaḥ saṁdhir daṇḍa.mukhya.ātma.rakṣaṇaḥ //*  
*07.3.26ab/ mukhya.strī.bandhanaṁ kuryāt pūrvayoḥ paścime tv arim /*  
*07.3.26cd/ sādhayed gūḍham ity ete daṇḍa.upanata.saṁdhayaḥ //*  
*07.3.27ab/ kośa.dānena śeṣāṇāṁ prakṛtīnāṁ vimokṣaṇam /*  
*07.3.27cd/ parikrayo bhavet saṁdhiḥ sa eva ca yathā.sukham //*  
*07.3.28ab/ skandha.upaneyo bahudhā jñeyaḥ saṁdhir upagrahaḥ /*  
*07.3.28cd/ niruddho deśa.kālābhyām atyayaḥ syād upagrahaḥ //*  
*07.3.29ab/ viṣahya.dānād āyatyāṁ kṣamaḥ strī.bandhanād api /*  
*07.3.29cd/ suvarṇa.saṁdhir viśvāsād ekī.bhāva.gato bhavet //*  
*07.3.30ab/ viparītaḥ kapālaḥ syād atyādāna.abhibhāṣitaḥ /*  
*07.3.30cd/ pūrvayoḥ praṇayet kupyaṁ hasty.aśvaṁ vā gara.anvitam //*  
*07.3.31ab/ tṛtīye praṇayed arthaṁ kathayan karmaṇāṁ kṣayam /*  
*07.3.31cd/ tiṣṭhec caturtha ity ete kośa.upanata.saṁdhayaḥ //*  
*07.3.32ab/ bhūmy.eka.deśa.tyāgena śeṣa.prakṛti.rakṣaṇam /*  
*07.3.32cd/ ādiṣṭa.saṁdhis tatra^iṣṭo gūḍha.stena.upaghātinaḥ //*  
*07.3.33ab/ bhūmīnām ātta.sārāṇāṁ mūla.varjaṁ praṇāmanam /*  
*07.3.33cd/ ucchinna.saṁdhis tatra^iṣṭaḥ para.vyasana.kāṅkṣiṇaḥ //*  
*07.3.34ab/ phala.dānena bhūmīnāṁ mokṣaṇaṁ syād avakrayaḥ /*  
*07.3.34cd/ phala.atimukto bhūmibhyaḥ saṁdhiḥ sa paridūṣaṇaḥ //*  
*07.3.35ab/ kuryād avekṣaṇaṁ pūrvau paścimau tv ābalīyasam /*  
*07.3.35cd/ ādāya phalam ity ete deśa.upanata.saṁdhayaḥ //*  
*07.3.36ab/ sva.kāryāṇāṁ vaśena^ete deśe kāle ca bhāṣitāḥ /*  
*07.3.36cd/ ābalīyasikāḥ kāryās trividhā hīna.saṁdhayaḥ //E*  
*(vigṛhya.āsanaṁ - saṁdhāya.āsanaṁ - vigṛhya.yānaṁ - saṁdhāya.yānaṁ - sambhūya.prayāṇam)*  
*07.4.01/ saṁdhi.vigrahayor āsanaṁ yānaṁ ca vyākhyātam //*  
*07.4.02/ sthānam āsanam upekṣaṇaṁ ca^ity āsana.paryāyāḥ //*  
*07.4.03/ viśeṣas tu - guṇa.ekadeśe sthānam, sva.vṛddhi.prāpty.artham āsanam, upāyānām aprayoga upekṣaṇam //*  
*07.4.04/ atisaṁdhāna.kāmayor ari.vijigīṣvor upahantum aśaktayor vigṛhya^āsanaṁ saṁdhāya vā //*  
*07.4.05/ yadā vā paśyet "sva.daṇḍair mitra.aṭavī.daṇḍair vā samaṁ jyāyāṁsaṁ vā karśayitum utsahe" iti tadā kṛta.bāhya.abhyantara.kṛtyo vigṛhya^āsīta //*  
*07.4.06/ yadā vā paśyet "utsāha.yuktā me prakṛtayaḥ saṁhatā vivṛddhāḥ sva.karmāṇy avyāhatāś cariṣyanti parasya vā karmāṇy upahaniṣyanti" iti tadā vigṛhya^āsīta //*  
*07.4.07a/ yadā vā paśyet "parasya^apacaritāḥ kṣīṇā lubdhāḥ sva.cakra.stena.aṭavī.vyathitā vā prakṛtayaḥ svayam upajāpena vā mām eṣyanti, sampannā me vārttā, vipannā parasya, tasya prakṛtayo durbhikṣa.upahatā mām eṣyanti; vipannā me vārttā, sampannā parasya, - //*  
*07.4.07b/ taṁ me prakṛtayo na gamiṣyanti, vigṛhya ca^asya dhānya.paśu.hiraṇyāny āhariṣyāmi, sva.paṇya.upaghātīni vā para.paṇyāni nivartayiṣyāmi, - //*  
*07.4.07c/ para.vaṇik.pathād vā saravanti mām eṣyanti vigṛhīte, na^itaram, dūṣya.amitra.aṭavī.nigrahaṁ vā vigṛhīto na kariṣyati, tair eva vā vigrahaṁ prāpsyati, - //*  
*07.4.07d/ mitraṁ me mitra.bhāvy abhiprayāto bahv.alpa.kālaṁ tanu.kṣaya.vyayam arthaṁ prāpsyati, guṇavatīm ādeyāṁ vā bhūmim, - //*  
*07.4.07e/ sarva.saṁdohena vā mām anādṛtya prayātu.kāmaḥ kathaṁ na yāyād" iti para.vṛddhi.pratighāta.arthaṁ pratāpa.arthaṁ ca vigṛhya^āsīta //*  
*07.4.08/ "tam eva hi pratyāvṛtto grasate" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.4.09/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.4.10/ karśana.mātram asya kuryād avyasaninaḥ, para.vṛddhyā tu vṛddhaḥ samucchedanam //*  
*07.4.11/ evaṁ parasya yātavyo^asmai sāhāyyam avinaṣṭaḥ prayacchet //*  
*07.4.12/ tasmāt sarva.saṁdoha.prakṛtaṁ vigṛhya^āsīta //*  
*07.4.13/ vigṛhya.āsana.hetu.prātilomye saṁdhāya^āsīta //*  
*07.4.14/ vigṛhya.āsana.hetubhir abhyuccitaḥ sarva.saṁdoha.varjaṁ vigṛhya yāyāt //*  
*07.4.15/ yadā vā paśyet "vyasanī paraḥ, prakṛti.vyasanaṁ vā^asya śeeṣa.prakṛtibhir apratikāryam, sva.cakra.pīḍitā viraktā vā^asya prakṛtayaḥ karśitā nirutsāhāḥ parasparād vā bhinnāḥ śakyā lobhayitum, agny.udaka.vyādhi.maraka.durbhikṣa.nimittaṁ kṣīṇa.yugya.puruṣa.nicaya.rakṣā.vidhānaḥ paraḥ" iti tadā vigṛhya yāyāt //*  
*07.4.16/ yadā vā paśyet "mitram ākrandaś ca me śūra.vṛddha.anurakta.prakṛtiḥ, viparīta.prakṛtiḥ paraḥ pārṣṇi.grāhaś ca^āsāraś ca, śakṣyāmi mitreṇa^āsāram ākrandena pārṣṇi.grāhaṁ vā vigṛhya yātum" iti tadā vigṛhya yāyāt //*  
*07.4.17/ yadā vā phalam eka.hāryam alpa.kālaṁ paśyet tadā pārṣṇi.grāha.āsārābhyāṁ vigṛhya yāyāt //*  
*07.4.18/ viparyaye saṁdhāya yāyāt //*  
*07.4.19/ yadā vā paśyet "na śakyam ekena yātum avaśyaṁ ca yātavyam" iti tadā sama.hīna.jyāyobhiḥ sāmavāyikaiḥ sambhūya yāyād, ekatra nirdiṣṭena^aṁśena, anekatra^anirdiṣṭena^aṁśena //*  
*07.4.20/ teṣām asamavāye daṇḍam anyatamasmān niviṣṭa.aṁśena yāceta //*  
*07.4.21/ sambhūya.abhigamanena vā nirviśyeta, dhruve lābhe nirdiṣṭena^aṁśena, adhruve lābha.aṁśena //*  
*07.4.22ab/ aṁśo daṇḍa.samaḥ pūrvaḥ prayāsa.sama uttamaḥ /*  
*07.4.22cd/ vilopo vā yathā.lābhaṁ prakṣepa.sama eva vā //E*  
*(yātavya.amitrayor abhigraha.cintāḥ - kṣaya.lobha.virāga.hetavaḥ prakṛtīnāṁ - sāmavāyika.viparimarśah)*  
*07.5.01/ tulya.sāmanta.vyasane yātavyam amitraṁ vā^ity amitram abhiyāyāt, tat.siddhau yātavyam //*  
*07.5.02/ amitra.siddhau hi yātavyaḥ sāhāyyaṁ dadyān na^amitro yātavya.siddhau //*  
*07.5.03/ guru.vyasanaṁ yātavyaṁ laghu.vyasanam amitraṁ vā^iti "guru.vyasanaṁ saukaryato yāyād" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.5.04/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.5.05/ laghu.vyasanam amitraṁ yāyāt //*  
*07.5.06/ laghv api hi vyasanam abhiyuktasya kṛcchraṁ bhavati //*  
*07.5.07/ satyaṁ gurv api gurutaraṁ bhavati //*  
*07.5.08/ anabhiyuktas tu laghu.vyasanaḥ sukhena vyasanaṁ pratikṛtya^amitro yātavyam abhisaret, pārṣṇiṁ vā gṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.5.09/ yātavya.yaugapadye guru.vyasanaṁ nyāya.vṛttiṁ laghu.vyasanam anyāya.vṛttiṁ virakta.prakṛtiṁ vā^iti virakta.prakṛtiṁ yāyāt //*  
*07.5.10/ guru.vyasanaṁ nyāya.vṛttim abhiyuktaṁ prakṛtayo^anugṛhṇanti, laghu.vyasanam anyāya.vṛttim upekṣante, viraktā balavantam apy ucchindanti //*  
*07.5.11/ tasmād virakta.prakṛtim eva yāyāt //*  
*07.5.12/ kṣīṇa.lubdha.prakṛtim apacarita.prakṛtiṁ vā^iti kṣīṇa.lubdha.prakṛtiṁ yāyāt, kṣīṇa.lubdhā hi prakṛtayaḥ sukhena^upajāpaṁ pīḍāṁ vā^upagacchanti, na^apacaritāḥ pradhāna.avagraha.sādhyāḥ" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.5.13/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.5.14/ kṣīṇa.lubdhā hi prakṛtayo bhartari snigdhā bhartṛ.hite tiṣṭhanti, upajāpaṁ vā visaṁvādayanti, anurāge sārvaguṇyam iti //*  
*07.5.15/ tasmād apacarita.prakṛtim eva yāyāt //*  
*07.5.16/ balavantam anyāya.vṛttiṁ durbalaṁ vā nyāya.vṛttim iti balavantam anyāya.vṛttiṁ yāyāt //*  
*07.5.17/ balavantam anyāya.vṛttim abhiyuktaṁ prakṛtayo na^anugṛhṇanti, niṣpātayanti, amitraṁ vā^asya bhajante //*  
*07.5.18/ durbalaṁ tu nyāya.vṛttim abhiyuktaṁ prakṛtayaḥ parigṛhṇanti, anuniṣpatanti vā //*  
*07.5.19ab/ avakṣepeṇa hi satām asatāṁ pragraheṇa ca /*  
*07.5.19cd/ abhūtānāṁ ca hiṁsānām adharmyāṇāṁ pravartanaiḥ //*  
*07.5.20ab/ ucitānāṁ caritrāṇāṁ dharmiṣṭhānāṁ nivartanaiḥ /*  
*07.5.20cd/ adharmasya prasaṅgena dharmasya^avagraheṇa ca //*  
*07.5.21ab/ akāryāṇāṁ ca karaṇaiḥ kāryāṇāṁ ca praṇāśanaiḥ /*  
*07.5.21cd/ apradānaiś ca deyānām adeyānāṁ ca sādhanaiḥ //*  
*07.5.22ab/ adaṇḍanaiś ca daṇḍyānām adaṇḍyānāṁ ca daṇḍanaiḥ /*  
*07.5.22cd/ agrāhyāṇām upagrāhair grāhyāṇāṁ ca^anabhigrahaiḥ //*  
*07.5.23ab/ anarthyānāṁ ca karaṇair arthyānāṁ ca vighātanaiḥ /*  
*07.5.23cd/ arakṣaṇaiś ca corebhyaḥ svayaṁ ca parimoṣaṇaiḥ //*  
*07.5.24ab/ pātaiḥ puruṣa.kārāṇāṁ karmaṇāṁ guṇa.dūṣaṇaiḥ /*  
*07.5.24cd/ upaghātaiḥ pradhānānāṁ mānyānāṁ ca^avamānanaiḥ //*  
*07.5.25ab/ virodhanaiś ca vṛddhānāṁ vaiṣamyeṇa^anṛtena ca /*  
*07.5.25cd/ kṛtasya^apratikāreṇa sthitasya^akaraṇena ca //*  
*07.5.26ab/ rājñaḥ pramāda.ālasyābhyāṁ yoga.kṣema.vadhena vā /*  
*07.5.26cd/ prakṛtīnāṁ kṣayo lobho vairāgyaṁ ca^upajāyate //*  
*07.5.27ab/ kṣīṇāḥ prakṛtayo lobhaṁ lubdhā yānti virāgatām /*  
*07.5.27cd/ viraktā yānty amitraṁ vā bhartāraṁ ghnanti vā svayam //*  
*07.5.28/ tasmāt prakṛtīnāṁ kṣaya.lobha.virāga.kāraṇāni na^utpādayet, utpannāni vā sadyaḥ pratikurvīta //*  
*07.5.29/ kṣīṇā lubdhā viraktā vā prakṛtaya iti //*  
*07.5.30/ kṣīṇāḥ pīḍana.ucchedana.bhayāt sadyaḥ saṁdhiṁ yuddhaṁ niṣpatanaṁ vā rocayante //*  
*07.5.31/ lubdhā lobhena^asaṁtuṣṭāḥ para.upajāpaṁ lipsante //*  
*07.5.32/ viraktāḥ para.abhiyogam abhyuttiṣṭhante //*  
*07.5.33/ tāsāṁ hiraṇya.dhānya.kṣayaḥ sarva.upaghātī kṛcchra.pratīkāraś ca, yugya.puruṣa.kṣayo hiraṇya.dhānya.sādhyaḥ //*  
*07.5.34/ lobha aikadeśiko mukhya.āyattaḥ para.artheṣu śakyaḥ pratihantum ādātuṁ vā //*  
*07.5.35/ virāgaḥ pradhāna.avagraha.sādhyaḥ //*  
*07.5.36/ niṣpradhānā hi prakṛtayo bhogyā bhavanty anupajāpyāś ca^anyeṣām, anāpat.sahās tu //*  
*07.5.37/ prakṛti.mukhya.pragrahais tu bahudhā bhinnā guptā bhavanty āpat.sahāś ca //*  
*07.5.38/ sāmavāyikānām api saṁdhi.vigraha.kāraṇāny avekṣya śakti.śauca.yuktaiḥ sambhūya yāyāt //*  
*07.5.39/ śaktimān hi pārṣṇi.grahaṇe yātrā.sāhāyya.dāne vā śaktaḥ, śuciḥ siddhau ca^asiddhau ca yathā.sthita.kārī^iti //*  
*07.5.40/ teṣāṁ jyāyasā^ekena dvābhyāṁ samābhyāṁ vā sambhūya yātavyam iti dvābhyāṁ samābhyāṁ śreyaḥ //*  
*07.5.41/ jyāyasā hy avagṛhītaś carati, samābhyām atisaṁdhāna.ādhikye vā //*  
*07.5.42/ tau hi sukhau bhedayitum, duṣṭaś ca^eko dvābhyāṁ niyantuṁ bheda.upagrahaṁ ca^upagantum iti //*  
*07.5.43/ samena^ekena dvābhyāṁ hīnābhyāṁ vā^iti dvābhyāṁ hīnābhyāṁ śreyaḥ //*  
*07.5.44/ tau hi dvi.kārya.sādhakau vaśyau ca bhavataḥ //*  
*07.5.45ab/ kārya.siddhau tu - kṛta.arthāj jyāyaso gūḍhaḥ sa.apadeśam apasravet /*  
*07.5.45cd/ aśuceḥ śuci.vṛttāt tu pratīkṣeta^ā visarjanāt //*  
*07.5.46ab/ sattrād apasared yattaḥ kalatram apanīya vā /*  
*07.5.46cd/ samād api hi labdha.arthād viśvas tasya bhayaṁ bhavet //*  
*07.5.47ab/ jyāyastve ca^api labdha.arthaḥ samo^api parikalpate /*  
*07.5.47cd/ abhyuccitaś ca^aviśvāsyo vṛddhiś citta.vikāriṇī //*  
*07.5.48ab/ viśiṣṭād alpam apy aṁśaṁ labdhvā tuṣṭa.mukho vrajet /*  
*07.5.48cd/ anaṁśo vā tato^asya^aṅke prahṛtya dvi.guṇaṁ haret //*  
*07.5.49ab/ kṛta.arthas tu svayaṁ netā visṛjet sāmavāyikān /*  
*07.5.49cd/ api jīyeta na jayen maṇḍala.iṣṭas tathā bhavet //E*  
*(saṁhita.prayāṇikaṁ - paripaṇita.aparipaṇita.apasṛtāḥ saṁdhayah)*  
*07.6.01/ vijigīṣur dvitīyāṁ prakṛtim evam atisaṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.6.02/ sāmantaṁ saṁhita.prayāṇe yojayet "tvam ito yāhi, aham ito yāsyāmi, samāno lābhaḥ" iti //*  
*07.6.03/ lābha.sāmye saṁdhiḥ, vaiṣamye vikramaḥ //*  
*07.6.04/ saṁdhiḥ paripaṇitaś ca^aparipaṇitaś ca //*  
*07.6.05/ "tvam etaṁ deśaṁ yāhi, aham imaṁ deśaṁ yāsyāmi" iti paripaṇita.deśaḥ //*  
*07.6.06/ "tvam etāvantaṁ kālaṁ ceṣṭasva, aham etāvantaṁ kālaṁ ceṣṭiṣye" iti paripaṇita.kālaḥ //*  
*07.6.07/ "tvam etāvat.kāryaṁ sādhaya, aham idaṁ kāryaṁ sādhayiṣyāmi" iti paripaṇita.arthaḥ //*  
*07.6.08/ yadi vā manyeta "śaila.vana.nadī.durgam aṭavī.vyavahitaṁ chinna.dhānya.puruṣa.vīvadha.āsāram ayavasa.indhana.udakam avijñātaṁ prakṛṣṭam anya.bhāva.deśīyaṁ vā sainya.vyāyāmānām alabdha.bhaumaṁ vā deśaṁ paro yāsyati, viparītam aham" ity etasmin viśeṣe paripaṇita.deśaṁ saṁdhim upeyāt //*  
*07.6.09/ yadi vā manyeta "pravarṣa.uṣṇa.śītam ativyādhi.prāyam upakṣīṇa.āhāra.upabhogaṁ sainya.vyāyāmānāṁ ca^auparodhikaṁ kārya.sādhanānām ūnam atiriktaṁ vā kālaṁ paraś ceṣṭiṣyate, viparītam aham" ity etasmin viśeṣe paripaṇita.kālaṁ saṁdhim upeyāt //*  
*07.6.10/ yadi vā manyeta "pratyādeyaṁ prakṛti.kopakaṁ dīrgha.kālaṁ mahā.kṣaya.vyayam alpam anartha.anubandham akalyam adharmyaṁ madhyama.udāsīna.viruddhaṁ mitra.upaghātakaṁ vā kāryaṁ paraḥ sādhayiṣyati, viparītam aham" ity etasmin viśeṣe paripaṇita.arthaṁ saṁdhim upeyāt //*  
*07.6.11/ evaṁ deśa.kālayoḥ kāla.kāryayor deśa.kāryayor deśa.kāla.kāryāṇāṁ ca^avasthāpanāt sapta.vidhaḥ paripaṇitaḥ //*  
*07.6.12/ tasmin prāg eva^ārabhya pratiṣṭhāpya ca sva.karmāṇi para.karmasu vikrameta //*  
*07.6.13/ vyasana.tvara.avamāna.ālasya.yuktam ajñaṁ vā śatrum atisaṁdhātu.kāmo deśa.kāla.kāryāṇām anavasthāpanāt "saṁhitau svaḥ" iti saṁdhi.viśvāsena parac.chidram āsādya prahared ity aparipaṇitaḥ //*  
*07.6.14/ tatra^etad bhavati //*  
*07.6.15ab/ sāmantena^eva sāmantaṁ vidvān āyojya vigrahe /*  
*07.6.15cd/ tato^anyasya hared bhūmiṁ chittvā pakṣaṁ samantataḥ //*  
*07.6.16/ saṁdher akṛta.cikīrṣā kṛta.śleṣaṇaṁ kṛta.vidūṣaṇam avaśīrṇa.kriyā ca //*  
*07.6.17/ vikramasya prakāśa.yuddhaṁ kūṭa.yuddhaṁ tūṣṇīṁ.yuddham //*  
*07.6.18/ iti saṁdhi.vikramau //*  
*07.6.19/ apūrvasya saṁdheḥ sa.anubandhaiḥ sāma.ādibhiḥ paryeṣaṇaṁ sama.hīna.jyāyasāṁ ca yathā.balam avasthāpanam akṛta.cikīrṣā //*  
*07.6.20/ kṛtasya priya.hitābhyām ubhayataḥ paripālanaṁ yathā.sambhāṣitasya ca nibandhanasya^anuvartanaṁ rakṣaṇaṁ ca "kathaṁ parasmān na bhidyeta" iti kṛta.śleṣaṇam //*  
*07.6.21/ parasya^apasaṁdheyatāṁ dūṣya.atisaṁdhānena sthāpayitvā vyatikramaḥ kṛta.vidūṣaṇam //*  
*07.6.22/ bhṛtyena mitreṇa vā doṣa.apasṛtena pratisaṁdhānam avaśīrṇa.kriyā //*  
*07.6.23/ tasyāṁ gata.āgataś catur.vidhaḥ - kāraṇād gata.āgato, viparītaḥ, kāraṇād gato^akāraṇād āgato, viparītaś ca^iti //*  
*07.6.24/ svāmino doṣeṇa gato guṇena^āgataḥ parasya guṇena gato doṣeṇa^āgata iti kāraṇād gata.āgataḥ saṁdheyaḥ //*  
*07.6.25/ sva.doṣeṇa gata.āgato guṇam ubhayoḥ parityajya akāraṇād gata.āgataḥ cala.buddhir asaṁdheyaḥ //*  
*07.6.26/ svāmino doṣeṇa gataḥ parasmāt sva.doṣeṇa^āgata iti kāraṇād gato^akāraṇād āgataḥ tarkayitavyaḥ "para.prayuktaḥ svena vā doṣeṇa^apakartu.kāmaḥ, parasya^ucchettāram amitraṁ me jñātvā pratighāta.bhayād āgataḥ, paraṁ vā mām ucchettu.kāmaṁ parityajya^ānṛśaṁsyād āgataḥ" iti //*  
*07.6.27/ jñātvā kalyāṇa.buddhiṁ pūjayed, anyathā.buddhim apakṛṣṭaṁ vāsayet //*  
*07.6.28/ sva.doṣeṇa gataḥ para.doṣeṇa^āgata ity akāraṇād gataḥ kāraṇād āgataḥ tarkayitavyaḥ " chidraṁ me pūrayiṣyati, ucito^ayam asya vāsaḥ, paratra^asya jano na ramate, mitrair me saṁhitaḥ, śatrubhir vigṛhītaḥ, lubdha.krūrād āvignaḥ śatru.saṁhitād vā parasmāt" iti //*  
*07.6.29/ jñātvā yathā.buddhy avasthāpayitavyaḥ //*  
*07.6.30/ "kṛta.praṇāśaḥ śakti.hānir vidyā.paṇyatvam āśā.nirvedo deśa.laulyam aviśvāso balavad.vigraho vā parityāga.sthānam" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.6.31/ bhayam avṛttir amarṣa iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.6.32/ iha^apakārī tyājyaḥ, para.apakārī saṁdheyaḥ, ubhaya.apakārī tarkayitavya iti samānam //*  
*07.6.33/ asaṁdheyena tv avaśyaṁ saṁdhātavye yataḥ prabhāvas tataḥ pratividadhyāt //*  
*07.6.34ab/ sa.upakāraṁ vyavahitaṁ guptam āyuḥ.kṣayād iti /*  
*07.6.34cd/ vāsayed ari.pakṣīyam avaśīrṇa.kriyā.vidhau //*  
*07.6.35ab/ vikramayed bhartari vā siddhaṁ vā daṇḍa.cāriṇam //*  
*07.6.35cd/ kuryād amitra.aṭavīṣu pratyante vā^anyataḥ kṣipet //*  
*07.6.36ab/ paṇyaṁ kuryād asiddhaṁ vā siddhaṁ vā tena saṁvṛtam //*  
*07.6.36cd/ tasya^eva doṣeṇa.adūṣya para.saṁdheya.kāraṇāt //*  
*07.6.37ab/ atha vā śamayed enam āyaty.artham upāṁśunā //*  
*07.6.37cd/ āyatyāṁ ca vadha.prepsuṁ dṛṣṭvā hanyād gata.āgatam //*  
*07.6.38ab/ arito^abhyāgato doṣaḥ śatru.saṁvāsa.kāritaḥ //*  
*07.6.38cd/ sarpa.saṁvāsa.dharmitvān nitya.udvegena dūṣitaḥ //*  
*07.6.39ab/ jāyate plakṣa.bīja.āśāt kapotād iva śālmaleḥ //*  
*07.6.39cd/ udvega.janano nityaṁ paścād api bhaya.āvahaḥ //*  
*07.6.40ab/ prakāśa.yuddhaṁ nirdiṣṭe deśe kāle ca vikramaḥ //*  
*07.6.40cd/ vibhīṣaṇam avaskandaḥ pramāda.vyasana.ardanam //*  
*07.6.41ab/ ekatra tyāga.ghātau ca kūṭa.yuddhasya mātṛkā //*  
*07.6.41cd/ yoga.gūḍha.upajāpa.arthaṁ tūṣṇīṁ.yuddhasya lakṣaṇam //E*  
*(dvaidhī.bhāvikāḥ saṁdhi.vikramāh)*  
*07.7.01/ vijigīṣur dvitīyāṁ prakṛtim evam upagṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.7.02/ sāmantaṁ sāmantena sambhūya yāyāt, yadi vā manyeta "pārṣṇiṁ me na grahīṣyati, pārṣṇi.grāhaṁ vārayiṣyati, yātavyaṁ na^abhisariṣyati, bala.dvaiguṇyaṁ me bhaviṣyati, vīvadha.āsārau me pravartayiṣyati, parasya vārayiṣyati, bahv.ābādhe me pathi kaṇṭakān mardayiṣyati, durga.aṭavy.apasāreṣu daṇḍena cariṣyati, yātavyam aviṣahye doṣe saṁdhau vā sthāpayiṣyati,*  
*labdha.lābha.aṁśo vā śatrūn anyān me viśvāsayiṣyati" iti //*  
*07.7.03/ dvaidhī.bhūto vā kośena daṇḍaṁ daṇḍena kośaṁ sāmantānām anyatamāl lipseta //*  
*07.7.04/ teṣāṁ jyāyaso^adhikena^aṁśena samāt samena hīnādd hīnena^iti sama.saṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.7.05/ viparyaye viṣama.saṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.7.06/ tayor viśeṣa.lābhād atisaṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.7.07/ vyasaninam apāya.sthāne saktam anarthinaṁ vā jyāyāṁsaṁ hīno bala.samena lābhena paṇeta //*  
*07.7.08/ paṇitas tasya^apakāra.samartho vikrameta, anyathā saṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.7.09/ evaṁ.bhūto vā hīna.śakti.pratāpa.pūraṇa.arthaṁ sambhāvya.artha.abhisārī mūla.pārṣṇi.trāṇa.arthaṁ vā jyāyāṁsaṁ hīno bala.samād viśiṣṭena lābhena paṇeta //*  
*07.7.10/ paṇitaḥ kalyāṇa.buddhim anugṛhṇīyāt, anyathā vikrameta //*  
*07.7.11/ jāta.vyasana.prakṛti.randhram upasthita.anarthaṁ vā jyāyāṁsaṁ hīno durga.mitra.pratiṣṭabdho vā hrasvam adhvānaṁ yātu.kāmaḥ śatrum ayuddham eka.anta.siddhiṁ vā lābham ādātu.kāmo bala.samādd hīnena lābhena paṇeta //*  
*07.7.12/ paṇitas tasya^apakāra.samartho vikrameta, anyathā saṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.7.13/ arandhra.vyasano vā jyāyān dur.ārabdha.karmāṇaṁ bhūyaḥ kṣaya.vyayābhyāṁ yoktu.kāmo dūṣya.daṇḍaṁ pravāsayitu.kāmo dūṣya.daṇḍam āvāhayitu.kāmo vā pīḍanīyam ucchedanīyaṁ vā hīnena vyathayitu.kāmaḥ saṁdhi.pradhāno vā kalyāṇa.buddhir hīnaṁ lābhaṁ pratigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.7.14/ kalyāṇa.buddhinā sambhūya^arthaṁ lipseta, anyathā vikrameta //*  
*07.7.15/ evaṁ samaḥ samam atisaṁdadhyād anugṛhṇīyād vā //*  
*07.7.16/ para.anīkasya pratyanīkaṁ mitra.aṭavīnāṁ vā, śatror vibhūmīnāṁ deśikaṁ mūla.pārṣṇi.trāṇa.arthaṁ vā samo bala.samena lābhena paṇeta //*  
*07.7.17/ paṇitaḥ kalyāṇa.buddhim anugṛhṇīyāt, anyathā vikrameta //*  
*07.7.18/ jāta.vyasana.prakṛti.randhram aneka.viruddham anyato labhamāno vā samo bala.samādd hīnena lābhena paṇeta //*  
*07.7.19/ paṇitas tasya^apakāra.samartho vikrameta, anyathā saṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.7.20/ evaṁ.bhūto vā samaḥ sāmanta.āyatta.kāryaḥ kartavya.balo vā bala.samād viśiṣṭena lābhena paṇeta //*  
*07.7.21/ paṇitaḥ kalyāṇa.buddhim anugṛhṇīyāt anyathā vikrameta //*  
*07.7.22/ jāta.vyasana.prakṛti.randhram abhihantu.kāmaḥ sv.ārabdham eka.anta.siddhiṁ vā^asya karma.upahantu.kāmo mūle yātrāyāṁ vā prahartu.kāmo yātavyād.bhūyo labhamāno vā jyāyāṁsaṁ hīnaṁ samaṁ vā bhūyo yāceta //*  
*07.7.23/ bhūyo vā yācitaḥ sva.bala.rakṣā.arthaṁ durdharṣam anya.durgam āsāram aṭavīṁ vā para.daṇḍena marditu.kāmaḥ prakṛṣṭe^adhvani kāle vā para.daṇḍaṁ kṣaya.vyayābhyāṁ yoktu.kāmaḥ para.daṇḍena vā vivṛddhas tam eva^ucchettu.kāmaḥ para.daṇḍam ādātu.kāmo vā bhūyo dadyāt //*  
*07.7.24/ jyāyān vā hīnaṁ yātavya.apadeśena haste kartu.kāmaḥ param ucchidya vā tam eva^ucchettu.kāmaḥ, tyāgaṁ vā kṛtvā pratyādātu.kāmo bala.samād viśiṣṭena lābhena paṇeta //*  
*07.7.25/ paṇitas tasya^apakāra.samartho vikrameta, anyathā saṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.7.26/ yātavya.saṁhito vā tiṣṭhet, dūṣya.amitra.aṭavī.daṇḍaṁ vā^asmai dadyāt //*  
*07.7.27/ jāta.vyasana.prakṛti.randhro vā jyāyān hīnaṁ bala.samena lābhena paṇeta //*  
*07.7.28/ paṇitas tasya^apakāra.samartho vikrameta, anyathā saṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.7.29/ evaṁ.bhūtaṁ hīnaṁ jyāyān bala.samādd hīnena lābhena paṇeta //*  
*07.7.30/ paṇitas tasya^apakāra.samartho vikrameta, anyathā saṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.7.31ab/ ādau budhyeta paṇitaḥ paṇamānaś ca kāraṇam //*  
*07.7.31cd/ tato vitarkya.ubhayato yataḥ śreyaś tato vrajet //E*  
*(yātavya.vṛttiḥ - anugrāhya.mitra.viśeṣāh)*  
*07.8.01/ yātavyo^abhiyāsyamānaḥ saṁdhi.kāraṇam ādātu.kāmo vihantu.kāmo vā sāmavāyikānām anyatamaṁ lābha.dvaiguṇyena paṇeta //*  
*07.8.02/ paṇamānaḥ kṣaya.vyaya.pravāsa.pratyavāya.para.upakāra.śarīra.ābādhāṁś ca^asya varṇayet //*  
*07.8.03/ pratipannam arthena yojayet //*  
*07.8.04/ vairaṁ vā parair grāhayitvā visaṁvādayet //*  
*07.8.05/ durārabdha.karmāṇaṁ bhūyaḥ kṣaya.vyayābhyāṁ yoktu.kāmaḥ sv.ārabdhāṁ vā yātrā.siddhiṁ vighātayitu.kāmo mūle yātrāyāṁ vā prahartu.kāmo yātavya.saṁhitaḥ punar yācitu.kāmaḥ pratyutpanna.artha.kṛcchras tasminn aviśvasto vā tadātve lābham alpam icchet, āyatyāṁ prabhūtam //*  
*07.8.06/ mitra.upakāram amitra.upaghātam artha.anubandham avekṣamāṇaḥ pūrva.upakārakaṁ kārayitu.kāmo bhūyas tadātve mahāntaṁ lābham utsṛjya^āyatyām alpam icchet //*  
*07.8.07/ dūṣya.amitrābhyāṁ mūla.hareṇa vā jyāyasā vigṛhītaṁ trātu.kāmas tathā.vidham upakāraṁ kārayitu.kāmaḥ sambandha.avekṣī vā tadātve ca^āyatyāṁ ca lābhaṁ na pratigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.8.08/ kṛta.saṁdhir atikramitu.kāmaḥ parasya prakṛti.karśanaṁ mitra.amitra.saṁdhi.viśleṣaṇaṁ vā kartu.kāmaḥ para.abhiyogāt^śaṅkamāno lābham aprāptam adhikaṁ vā yāceta //*  
*07.8.09/ tam itaras tadātve ca^āyatyāṁ ca kramam avekṣeta //*  
*07.8.10/ tena pūrve vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*07.8.11/ ari.vijigīṣvos tu svaṁ svaṁ mitram anugṛhṇatoḥ śakya.kalya.bhavya.ārambhi.sthira.karma.anurakta.prakṛtibhyo viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.8.12/ śakya.ārambhī viṣahyaṁ karma^ārabhate, kalya.ārambhī nirdoṣam, bhavya.ārambhī kalyāṇa.udayam //*  
*07.8.13/ sthira.karmā na^asamāpya karma^uparamate //*  
*07.8.14/ anurakta.prakṛtiḥ susahāyatvād alpena^apy anugraheṇa kāryaṁ sādhayati //*  
*07.8.15/ ta ete kṛta.arthāḥ sukhena prabhūtaṁ ca^upakurvanti //*  
*07.8.16/ ataḥ pratilomā na^anugrāhyāḥ //*  
*07.8.17/ tayor eka.puruṣa.anugrahe yo mitraṁ mitra.taraṁ vā^anugṛhṇāti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.8.18/ mitrād ātma.vṛddhiṁ hi prāpnoti, kṣaya.vyaya.pravāsa.para.upakārān itaraḥ //*  
*07.8.19/ kṛta.arthaś ca śatrur vaiguṇyam eti //*  
*07.8.20/ madhyamaṁ tv anugṛhṇator yo madhyamaṁ mitraṁ mitrataraṁ vā^anugṛhṇāti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.8.21/ mitrād ātma.vṛddhiṁ hi prāpnoti, kṣaya.vyaya.pravāsa.para.upakārān itaraḥ //*  
*07.8.22/ madhyamaś ced anugṛhīto viguṇaḥ syād amitro^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.8.23/ kṛta.prayāsaṁ hi madhyama.amitram apasṛtam eka.artha.upagataṁ prāpnoti //*  
*07.8.24/ tena^udāsīna.anugraho vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*07.8.25/ madhyama.udāsīnayor bala.aṁśa.dāne yaḥ śūraṁ kṛta.astraṁ duḥkha.saham anuraktaṁ vā daṇḍaṁ dadāti so^atisaṁdhīyate //*  
*07.8.26/ viparīto^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.8.27/ yatra tu daṇḍaḥ prahitas taṁ vā ca^artham anyāṁś ca sādhayati tatra maula.bhṛta.śreṇī.mitra.aṭavī.balānām anyatamam upalabdha.deśa.kālaṁ daṇḍaṁ dadyāt, amitra.aṭavī.balaṁ vā vyavahita.deśa.kālam //*  
*07.8.28/ yaṁ tu manyeta "kṛta.artho me daṇḍaṁ gṛhṇīyād, amitra.aṭavy.abhūmy.anṛtuṣu vā vāsayed, aphalaṁ vā kuryād" iti, daṇḍa.vyāsaṅga.apadeśena na^enam anugṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.8.29/ evam avaśyaṁ tv anugrahītavye tat.kāla.saham asmai daṇḍaṁ dadyāt //*  
*07.8.30/ ā.samāpteś ca^enaṁ vāsayed yodhayec ca bala.vyasanebhyaś ca rakṣet //*  
*07.8.31/ kṛta.arthāc ca sa.apadeśam apasrāvayet //*  
*07.8.32/ dūṣya.amitra.aṭavī.daṇḍaṁ vā^asmai dadyāt //*  
*07.8.33/ yātavyena vā saṁdhāya^enam atisaṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.8.34ab/ same hi lābhe saṁdhiḥ syād viṣame vikramo mataḥ //*  
*07.8.34cd/ sama.hīna.viśiṣṭānām ity uktāḥ saṁdhi.vikramāḥ //E*  
*(mitra.hiraṇya.bhūmi.karma.saṁdhayaḥ, tatra mitra.saṁdhiḥ hiraṇya.saṁdhiś ca)*  
*07.9.01/ saṁhita.prayāṇe mitra.hiraṇya.bhūmi.lābhānām uttara.uttaro lābhaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.9.02/ mitra.hiraṇye hi bhūmi.lābhād bhavataḥ, mitraṁ hiraṇya.lābhāt //*  
*07.9.03/ yo vā lābhaḥ siddhaḥ śeṣayor anyataraṁ sādhayati //*  
*07.9.04/ "tvaṁ ca^ahaṁ ca mitraṁ labhāvahe" ity evaṁ.ādidh sama.saṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.9.05/ "tvaṁ mitram" ity evaṁ.ādir viṣama.saṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.9.06/ tayor viśeṣa.lābhād atisaṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.9.07/ sama.saṁdhau tu yaḥ sampannaṁ mitraṁ mitra.kṛcchre vā mitram avāpnoti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.9.08/ āpadd hi sauhṛda.sthairyam utpādayati //*  
*07.9.09/ mitra.kṛcchre^api nityam avaśyam anityaṁ vaśyaṁ vā^iti "nityam avaśyaṁ śreyaḥ, tadd hi anupakurvad api na^apakaroti" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.9.10/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.9.11/ vaśyam anityaṁ śreyaḥ //*  
*07.9.12/ yāvad upakaroti tāvan mitraṁ bhavati, upakāra.lakṣaṇaṁ mitram iti //*  
*07.9.13/ vaśyayor api mahā.bhogam anityam alpa.bhogaṁ vā nityam iti // mahā.bhogam anityaṁ śreyaḥ, mahā.bhogam anityam alpa.kālena mahad.upakurvan mahānti vyaya.sthānāni pratikaroti" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.9.14/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.9.15/ nityam alpa.bhogaṁ śreyaḥ //*  
*07.9.16/ mahā.bhogam anityam upakāra.bhayād apakrāmati, upakṛtya vā pratyādātum īhate //*  
*07.9.17/ nityam alpa.bhogaṁ sātatyād alpam upakurvan mahatā kālena mahad upakaroti //*  
*07.9.18/ guru.samutthaṁ mahan mitraṁ laghu.samuttham alpaṁ vā^iti "guru.samutthaṁ mahan mitraṁ pratāpa.karaṁ bhavati, yadā ca^uttiṣṭhate tadā kāryaṁ sādhayati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.9.19/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.9.20/ laghu.samuttham alpaṁ śreyaḥ //*  
*07.9.21/ lagu.samuttham alpaṁ mitraṁ kārya.kālaṁ na^atipātayati daurbalyāc ca yathā.iṣṭa.bhogyaṁ bhavati, na^itarat prakṛṣṭa.bhaumam //*  
*07.9.22/ vikṣipta.sainyam avaśya.sainyaṁ vā^iti "vikṣiptaṁ sainyaṁ śakyaṁ pratisaṁhartuṁ vaśyatvād" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.9.23/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.9.24/ avaśya.sainyaṁ śreyaḥ //*  
*07.9.25/ avaśyaṁ hi śakyaṁ sāma.ādibhir vaśyaṁ kartum, na^itarat kārya.vyāsaktaṁ pratisaṁhartum //*  
*07.9.26/ puruṣa.bhogaṁ hiraṇya.bhogaṁ vā mitram iti "puruṣa.bhogaṁ mitraṁ śreyaḥ, pruṣa.bhogaṁ mitraṁ pratāpa.karaṁ bhavati, yadā ca^uttiṣṭhate tadā kāryaṁ sādhayati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.9.27/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.9.28/ hiraṇya.bhogaṁ mitraṁ śreyaḥ //*  
*07.9.29/ nityo hi hiraṇyena yogaḥ kadācid daṇḍena //*  
*07.9.30/ daṇḍaś ca hiraṇyena^anye ca kāmāḥ prāpyanta iti //*  
*07.9.31/ hiraṇya.bhogaṁ bhūmi.bhogaṁ vā mitram iti "hiraṇya.bhogaṁ gatimattvāt sarva.vyaya.pratīkāra.karam" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.9.32/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.9.33/ mitra.hiraṇye hi bhūmi.lābhād bhavata ity uktaṁ purastād //*  
*07.9.34/ tasmād bhūmi.bhogaṁ mitraṁ śreya iti //*  
*07.9.35/ tulye puruṣa.bhoge vikramaḥ kleśa.sahatvam anurāgaḥ sarva.bala.lābho vā mitra.kulād viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.9.36/ tulye hiraṇya.bhoge prārthita.arthatā prābhūtyam alpa.prayasatā sātatyaṁ ca viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.9.37/ tatra^etad bhavati //*  
*07.9.38ab/ nityaṁ vaśyaṁ laghu.utthānaṁ pitṛ.paitāmahaṁ mahat /*  
*07.9.38cd/ advaidhyaṁ ca^iti sampannaṁ mitraṁ ṣaḍ.guṇam ucyate //*  
*07.9.39ab/ ṛte yad arthaṁ praṇayād rakṣyate yac ca rakṣati /*  
*07.9.39cd/ pūrva.upacita.sambandhaṁ tan mitraṁ nityam ucyate //*  
*07.9.40ab/ sarva.citra.mahā.bhogaṁ trividhaṁ vaśyam ucyate /*  
*07.9.40cd/ ekato.bhogy ubhayataḥ sarvato.bhogi ca^aparam //*  
*07.9.41ab/ ādātṛ vā dātr.api vā jīvaty ariṣu hiṁsayā /*  
*07.9.41cd/ mitraṁ nityam avaśyaṁ tad.durga.aṭavy.apasāri ca //*  
*07.9.42ab/ anyato vigṛhītaṁ yal laghu.vyasanam eva vā /*  
*07.9.42cd/ saṁdhatte ca^upakārāya tan mitraṁ vaśyam adhruvam //*  
*07.9.43ab/ eka.arthena^atha sambaddham upakārya.vikāri ca /*  
*07.9.43cd/ mitra.bhāvi bhavaty etan mitram advaidhyam āpadi //*  
*07.9.44ab/ mitra.bhāvād dhruvaṁ mitraṁ śatru.sādhāraṇāc calam /*  
*07.9.44cd/ na kasyacid udāsīnaṁ dvayor ubhaya.bhāvi tat //*  
*07.9.45ab/ vijigīṣor amitraṁ yan mitram antardhitāṁ gatam /*  
*07.9.45cd/ upakāre^aniviṣṭaṁ vā^aśaktaṁ vā^anupakāri tat //*  
*07.9.46ab/ priyaṁ parasya vā rakṣyaṁ pūjyaṁ sambaddham eva vā /*  
*07.9.46cd/ anugṛhṇāti yan mitraṁ śatru.sādhāraṇaṁ hi tat //*  
*07.9.47ab/ prakṛṣṭa.bhaumaṁ saṁtuṣṭaṁ balavac ca^ālasaṁ ca yat /*  
*07.9.47cd/ udāsīnaṁ bhavaty etad vyasanād avamānitam //*  
*07.9.48ab/ arer netuś ca yad vṛddhiṁ daurbalyād anuvartate /*  
*07.9.48cd/ ubhayasya^apy avidviṣṭaṁ vidyād ubhaya.bhāvi tat //*  
*07.9.49ab/ kāraṇa.akāraṇa.dhvastaṁ kāraṇa.akāraṇa.āgatam /*  
*07.9.49cd/ yo mitraṁ samupekṣeta sa mṛtyum upagūhati //*  
*07.9.50/ kṣipram alpo lābhaś cirān mahān iti vā "kṣipram alpo lābhaḥ kārya.deśa.kāla.saṁvādakaḥ śreyān" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.9.51/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.9.52/ cirād avinipātī bīja.sadharmā mahāml lābhaḥ śreyān, viparyaye pūrvaḥ //*  
*07.9.53ab/ evaṁ dṛṣṭvā dhruve lābhe lābha.aṁśe ca guṇa.udayam /*  
*07.9.53cd/ sva.artha.siddhi.paro yāyāt saṁhitaḥ sāmavāyikaiḥ //E*  
*(mitra.hiraṇya.bhūmi.karma.saṁdhayaḥ, tatra bhūmi.saṁdhih)*  
*07.10.01/ "tvaṁ ca^ahaṁ ca bhūmiṁ labhāvahe" iti bhūmi.saṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.10.02/ tayor yaḥ pratyupasthita.arthaḥ sampannāṁ bhūmim avāpnoti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.10.03/ tulye sampanna.alābhe yo balavantam ākramya bhūmim avāpnoti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.10.04/ bhūmi.lābhaṁ śatru.karśanaṁ pratāpaṁ ca hi prāpnoti //*  
*07.10.05/ durbalād.bhūmi.lābhe satyaṁ saukaryaṁ bhavati //*  
*07.10.06/ durbala eva ca bhūmi.lābhaḥ, tat.sāmantaś ca mitram amitra.bhāvaṁ gacchati //*  
*07.10.07/ tulye balīyastve yaḥ sthita.śatrum utpāṭya bhūmim avāpnoti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.10.08/ durga.avāptir hi sva.bhūmi.rakṣaṇam amitra.aṭavī.pratiṣedhaṁ ca karoti //*  
*07.10.09/ cala.amitrād.bhūmi.lābhe śakya.sāmantato viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.10.10/ durbala.sāmantā hi kṣipra.āpyāyana.yoga.kṣemā bhavati //*  
*07.10.11/ viparītā balavat sāmantā kośa.daṇḍa.avacchedanī ca bhūmir bhavati //*  
*07.10.12/ sampannā nitya.amitrā manda.guṇā vā bhūmir anitya.amitrā^iti "sampannā nitya.amitrā śreyasī bhūmiḥ sampannā hi kośa.daṇḍau sampādayati, tau ca^amitra.pratighātakau ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.10.13/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.10.14/ nitya.amitra.alābhe bhūyān śatru.lābho bhavati //*  
*07.10.15/ nityaś ca śatrur upakṛte ca^apakṛte ca śatrur eva bhavati, anityas tu śatrur upakārād anapakārād vā śāmyati //*  
*07.10.16/ yasyā hi bhūmer bahu.durgāś cora.gaṇair mleccha.aṭavībhir vā nitya.avirahitāḥ pratyantāḥ sā nitya.amitrā, viparyaye tv anitya.amitrā //*  
*07.10.17/ alpā pratyāsannā mahatī vyavahitā vā bhūmir iti alpā pratyāsannā śreyasī //*  
*07.10.18/ sukhā hi prāptuṁ pālayitum abhisārayituṁ ca bhavati //*  
*07.10.19/ viparītā vyavahitā //*  
*07.10.20/ vyavahitayor api daṇḍa.dhāraṇā^ātma.dhāraṇā vā bhūmir iti ātma.dhāraṇā śreyasī //*  
*07.10.21/ sā hi sva.samutthābhyāṁ kośa.daṇḍābhyāṁ dhāryate //*  
*07.10.22/ viparītā daṇḍa.dhāraṇā daṇḍa.sthānam //*  
*07.10.23/ bāliśāt prājñād vā bhūmi.lābha iti bāliśād.bhūmi.lābhaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.10.24/ suprāpyā^anupālyā hi bhavati, apratyādeyā ca //*  
*07.10.25/ viparītā prājñād anuraktā //*  
*07.10.26/ pīḍanīya.ucchedanīyayor ucchedanīyād bhūmi.lābhaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.10.27/ ucchedanīyo hy anapāśrayo durbala.apāśrayo vā^abhiyuktaḥ kośa.daṇḍāv ādāya^apasartu.kāmaḥ prakṛtibhis tyajyate, na pīḍanīyo durga.mitra.pratiṣṭabdhaḥ //*  
*07.10.28/ durga.pratiṣṭabdhayor api sthala.nadī.durgīyābhyāṁ sthala.durgīyād bhūmi.lābhaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.10.29/ sthāleyaṁ hi surodha.avamarda.avaskandam anihśrāvi.śatru ca //*  
*07.10.30/ nadī.durgaṁ tu dvi.guṇa.kleśa.karam, udakaṁ ca pātavyaṁ vṛtti.karaṁ ca^amitrasya //*  
*07.10.31/ nadī.parvata.durgīyābhyāṁ nadī.durgīyād bbhūmi.lābhaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.10.32/ nadī.durgaṁ hi hasti.stambha.saṁkrama.setu.bandha.naubhiḥ sādhyam anitya.gāmbhīryam avasrāvy udakaṁ ca //*  
*07.10.33/ pārvataṁ tu sv.ārakṣaṁ duruparodhi kṛcchra.ārohaṇam, bhagne ca^ekasmin na sarva.vadhaḥ, śilā.vṛkṣa.pramokṣaś ca mahā.apakāriṇām //*  
*07.10.34/ nimna.sthala.yodhibhyo nimna.yodhibhyo bhūmi.lābhaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.10.35/ nimna.yodhino hy uparuddha.deśa.kālāḥ, sthala.yodhinas tu sarva.deśa.kāla.yodhinaḥ //*  
*07.10.36/ khanaka.ākāśa.yodhibhyaḥ khanakebhyo bhūmi.lābhaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.10.37/ khanakā hi khātena śastreṇa ca^ubhayathā yudhyante, śastreṇa^eva^ākāśa.yodhinaḥ //*  
*07.10.38ab/ evaṁ.vidhyebhyaḥ pṛthivīṁ labhamāno^artha.śāstravit /*  
*07.10.38cd/ saṁhitebhyaḥ parebhyaś ca viśeṣam adhigacchati //E*  
*(mitra.hiraṇya.bhūmi.karma.samdhayah - tatra anavasita.samdhih)*  
*07.11.01/ "tvaṁ ca^ahaṁ ca śūnyaṁ niveśayāvahe" ity anavasita.saṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.11.02/ tayor yaḥ pratyupasthita.artho yathā.ukta.guṇāṁ bhūmiṁ niveśayati so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.11.03/ tatra^api sthalam audakaṁ vā^iti mahataḥ sthalād alpam audakaṁ śreyaḥ, sātatyād avasthitatvāc ca phalānām //*  
*07.11.04/ sthalayor api prabhūta.pūrva.apara.sasyam alpa.varṣa.pākam asakta.ārambhaṁ śreyaḥ //*  
*07.11.05/ audakayor api dhānya.vāpam adhānya.vāpāt^śreyaḥ //*  
*07.11.06/ tayor alpa.bahutve dhānya.kāntād alpān mahad adhānya.kāntaṁ śreyaḥ //*  
*07.11.07/ mahaty avakāśe hi sthālyāś ca^anūpyāś ca^oṣadhayo bhavanti //*  
*07.11.08/ durga.ādīni ca karmāṇi prabhūtyena kriyante //*  
*07.11.09/ kṛtrimā hi bhūmi.guṇāḥ //*  
*07.11.10/ khani.dhānya.bhogayoḥ khani.bhogaḥ kośa.karaḥ, dhānya.bhogaḥ kośa.koṣṭha.agāra.karaḥ //*  
*07.11.11/ dhānya.mūlā hi durga.ādīnāṁ karmaṇām ārambhāḥ //*  
*07.11.12/ mahā.viṣaya.vikrayo vā khani.bhogaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.11.13/ "dravya.hasti.vana.bhogayor dravya.vana.bhogaḥ sarva.karmaṇāṁ yoniḥ prabhūta.nidhāna.kṣamaś ca, viparīto hasti.vana.bhogaḥ" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.11.14/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.11.15/ śakyaṁ dravya.vanam anekam anekasyāṁ bhūmau vāpayitum, na hasti.vanam //*  
*07.11.16/ hasti.pradhāno hi para.anīka.vadha iti //*  
*07.11.17/ vāri.sthala.patha.bhogayor anityo vāri.patha.bhogaḥ, nityaḥ sthala.patha.bhogaḥ //*  
*07.11.18/ bhinna.manuṣyā śreṇī.manuṣyā vā bhūmir iti bhinna.manuṣyā śreyasī //*  
*07.11.19/ bhinna.manuṣyā bhogyā bhavati, anupajāpyā ca^anyeṣām, anāpat.sahā tu //*  
*07.11.20/ viparītā śreṇī.manuṣyā, kope mahā.doṣā //*  
*07.11.21/ tasyāṁ cāturvarṇya.niveśe sarva.bhoga.sahatvād avara.varṇa.prāyā śreyasī, bāhulyād dhruvatvāc ca kṛṣyāḥ karṣakavatī, kṛṣyāś ca^anyeṣāṁ ca^ārambhāṇāṁ prayojakatvāt go.rakṣakavatī, paṇya.nicaya.ṛṇa.anugrahād āḍhya.vaṇigvatī //*  
*07.11.22/ bhūmi.guṇānām apāśrayaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.11.23/ durga.apāśrayā puruṣa.apāśrayā vā bhūmir iti puruṣa.apāśrayā śreyasī //*  
*07.11.24/ puruṣavad dhi rājyam //*  
*07.11.25/ apuruṣā gaur vandhy eva kiṁ duhīta //*  
*07.11.26/ mahā.kṣaya.vyaya.niveśāṁ tu bhūmim avāptu.kāmaḥ pūrvam eva kretāraṁ paṇeta durbalam arāja.bījinaṁ nirutsāham apakṣam anyāya.vṛttiṁ vyasaninaṁ daiva.pramāṇaṁ yat.kiṁcana.kāriṇaṁ vā //*  
*07.11.27/ mahā.kṣaya.vyaya.niveśāyāṁ hi bhūmau durbalo rāja.bījī niviṣṭaḥ sagandhābhiḥ prakṛtibhiḥ saha kṣaya.vyayena^avasīdati //*  
*07.11.28/ balavān arāja.bījī kṣaya.vyaya.bhayād asagandhābhiḥ prakṛtibhis tyajyate //*  
*07.11.29/ nirutsāhas tu daṇḍavān api daṇḍasya^apraṇetā sadaṇḍaḥ kṣaya.vyayena^avabhajyate //*  
*07.11.30/ kośavān apy apakṣaḥ kṣaya.vyaya.anugraha.hīnatvān na kutaścit prāpnoti //*  
*07.11.31/ anyāya.vṛttir niviṣṭam apy utthāpayet //*  
*07.11.32/ sa katham aniviṣṭaṁ niveśayet //*  
*07.11.33/ tena vyasanī vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*07.11.34/ daiva.pramāṇo mānuṣa.hīno nirārambho vipanna.karma.ārambho vā^avasīdati //*  
*07.11.35/ yat.kiṁcana.kārī na kiṁcid āsādayati //*  
*07.11.36/ sa ca^eṣāṁ pāpiṣṭhatamo bhavati //*  
*07.11.37/ "yat.kiṁcid.ārabhamāṇo hi vijigīṣoḥ kadācic chidram āsādayed" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.11.38/ yathā chidraṁ tathā vināśam apy āsādayed iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.11.39/ teṣām alābhe yathā pārṣṇi.grāha.upagrahe vakṣyāmas tathā bhūmim avasthāpayet //*  
*07.11.40/ ity abhihita.saṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.11.41/ guṇavatīm ādeyāṁ vā bhūmiṁ balavatā krayeṇa yācitaḥ saṁdhim avasthāpya dadyāt //*  
*07.11.42/ ity anibhṛta.saṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.11.43/ samena vā yācitaḥ kāraṇam avekṣya dadyāt "pratyādeyā me bhūmir vaśyā vā, anayā pratibaddhaḥ paro me vaśyo bhaviṣyati^ bhūmi.vikrayād vā mitra.hiraṇya.lābhaḥ kārya.sāmarthya.karo me bhaviṣyati" iti //*  
*07.11.44/ tena hīnaḥ kretā vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*07.11.45ab/ evaṁ mitraṁ hiraṇyaṁ ca sajanām ajanāṁ ca gām /*  
*07.11.45cd/ labhamāno^atisaṁdhatte śāstravit sāmavāyikān //E*  
*(mitra.hiraṇya.bhūmi.karma.samdhayah, tatra karma.samdhih)*  
*07.12.01/ "tvaṁ ca^ahaṁ ca durgaṁ kārayāvahe" iti karma.saṁdhiḥ //*  
*07.12.02/ tayor yo daiva.kṛtam aviṣahyam alpa.vyaya.ārambhaṁ durgaṁ kārayati so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.12.03/ tatra^api sthala.nadī.parvata.durgāṇām uttara.uttaraṁ śreyaḥ //*  
*07.12.04/ setu.bandhayor apy āhārya.udakāt saha.udakaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.12.05/ saha.udakayor api prabhūta.vāpa.sthānaḥ śreyān //*  
*07.12.06/ dravya.vanayor api yo mahat.sāravad.dravya.aṭavīkaṁ viṣaya.ante nadī.mātṛkaṁ dravya.vanaṁ chedayati so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.12.07/ nadī.mātṛkaṁ hi sv.ājīvam apāśrayaś ca^āpadi bhavati //*  
*07.12.08/ hasti.vanayor api yo bahu.śūra.mṛgaṁ durbala.prativeśaṁ.ananta.avakleśi viṣaya.ante hasti.vanaṁ badhnāti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.12.09/ tatra^api "bahu.kuṇṭha.alpa.śūrayoḥ alpa.śūraṁ śreyaḥ, śūreṣu hi yuddham, alpāḥ śūrā bahūn aśūrān bhañjanti, te bhagnāḥ sva.sainya.avaghātino bhavanti" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.12.10/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.12.11/ kuṇṭhā bahavaḥ śreyāṁsaḥ, skandha.viniyogād anekaṁ karma kurvāṇāḥ sveṣām apāśrayo yuddhe, pareṣāṁ durdharṣā vibhīṣaṇāś ca //*  
*07.12.12/ bahuṣu hi kuṇṭheṣu vinaya.karmaṇā śakyaṁ śauryam ādhātum, na tv eva^alpeṣu śūreṣu bahutvam iti //*  
*07.12.13/ khanyor api yaḥ prabhūta.sārām adurga.mārgām alpa.vyaya.ārambhāṁ khaniṁ khānayati, so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.12.14/ tatra^api mahā.sāram alpam alpa.sāraṁ vā prabhūtam iti "mahā.sāram alpaṁ śreyaḥ, vajra.maṇi.muktā.pravāla.hema.rūpya.dhātur hi prabhūtam alpa.sāram atyargheṇa grasate" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.12.15/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.12.16/ cirād alpo mahā.sārasya kretā vidyate, prabhūtaḥ sātatyād alpa.sārasya //*  
*07.12.17/ etena vaṇik.patho vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*07.12.18/ tatra^api "vāri.sthala.pathayor vāri.pathaḥ śreyān, alpa.vyaya.vyāyāmaḥ prabhūta.paṇya.udayaś ca" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.12.19/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.12.20/ samruddha.gatir asārvakālikaḥ prakṛṣṭa.bhaya.yonir niṣpratīkāraś ca vāri.pathaḥ, viparītaḥ sthala.pathaḥ //*  
*07.12.21/ vāri.pathe tu kūla.samyāna.pathayoḥ kūla.pathaḥ paṇya.pattana.bāhulyāt^śreyān, nadī.patho vā, sātatyād viṣahya.ābādhatvāc ca //*  
*07.12.22/ sthala.pathe^api "haimavato dakṣiṇā.pathāt^śreyān, hasty.aśva.gandha.danta.ajina.rūpya.suvarṇa.paṇyāḥ sāravattarāḥ" ity ācāryāḥ" //*  
*07.12.23/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.12.24/ kambala.ajina.aśva.paṇya.varjāḥ śaṅkha.vajra.maṇi.muktā.suvarṇa.paṇyāś ca prabhūtatarā dakṣiṇā.pathe //*  
*07.12.25/ dakṣiṇā.pathe^api bahu.khaniḥ sāra.paṇyaḥ prasiddha.gatir alpa.vyaya.vyāyāmo vā vaṇik.pathaḥ śreyān, prabhūta.viṣayo vā phalgu.puṇyaḥ //*  
*07.12.26/ tena pūrvaḥ paścimaś ca vaṇik.patho vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*07.12.27/ tatra^api cakra.pāda.pathayoś cakra.patho vipula.ārambhatvāt^śreyān, deśa.kāla.sambhāvano vā khara.uṣṭra.pathaḥ //*  
*07.12.28/ ābhyām aṁsa.patho vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*07.12.29/ para.karma.udayo netuḥ kṣayo vṛddhir viparyaye //*  
*07.12.30/ tulye karma.pathe sthānaṁ jñeyaṁ svaṁ vijigīṣuṇā //*  
*07.12.31/ alpa.āgama.ativyayatā kṣayo vṛddhir viparyaye //*  
*07.12.32/ samāya.vyayatā sthānaṁ karmasu jñeyam ātmanaḥ //*  
*07.12.33/ tasmād alpa.vyaya.ārambhaṁ durga.ādiṣu mahā.udayam //*  
*07.12.34/ karma labdhvā viśiṣṭaḥ syād ity uktāḥ karma.saṁdhayaḥ //E*  
*(pārṣṇi.grāh.cintā)*  
*07.13.01/ saṁhatya^ari.vijigīṣvor amitrayoḥ para.abhiyoginoḥ pārṣṇiṁ gṛhṇator yaḥ śakti.sampannasya pārṣṇiṁ gṛhṇāti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.02/ śakti.sampanno hy amitram ucchidya pārṣṇi.grāham ucchindyāt, na hīna.śaktir alabdha.lābhaḥ //*  
*07.13.03/ śakti.sāmye yo vipula.ārambhasya pārṣṇiṁ gṛhṇāti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.04/ vipula.ārambho hy amitram ucchidya pārṣṇi.grāham ucchindyāt, na^alpa.ārambhaḥ sakta.cakraḥ //*  
*07.13.05/ ārambha.sāmye yaḥ sarva.saṁdohena prayātasya pārṣṇiṁ gṛhṇāti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.06/ śūnya.mūlo hy asya sukaro bhavati, naika.deśa.bala.prayātaḥ kṛta.pārṣṇi.pratividhānaḥ //*  
*07.13.07/ bala.upādāna.sāmye yaś cala.amitraṁ prayātasya pārṣṇiṁ gṛhṇāti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.08/ cala.amitraṁ prayāto hi sukhena^avāpta.siddhiḥ pārṣṇi.grāham ucchindyāt, na sthita.amitraṁ prayātaḥ //*  
*07.13.09/ asau hi durga.pratihataḥ pārṣṇi.grāhe ca pratinivṛttaḥ sthitena^amitreṇa^avagṛhyate //*  
*07.13.10/ tena pūrve vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*07.13.11/ śatru.sāmye yo dhārmika.abhiyoginaḥ pārṣṇiṁ gṛhṇāti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.12/ dhārmika.abhiyogī hi sveṣāṁ pareṣāṁ ca dveṣyo bhavati, adhārmika.abhiyogī sampriyaḥ //*  
*07.13.13/ tena mūla.hara.tādātvika.kadarya.abhiyogināṁ pārṣṇi.grahaṇaṁ vyākhyātam //*  
*07.13.14/ mitra.abhiyoginoḥ pārṣṇi.grahaṇe ta eva hetavaḥ //*  
*07.13.15/ mitram amitraṁ ca^abhiyuñjānayor yo mitra.abhiyoginaḥ pārṣṇiṁ gṛhṇāti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.16/ mitra.abhiyogī hi sukhena^avāpta.siddhiḥ pārṣṇi.grāham ucchindyāt //*  
*07.13.17/ sukaro hi mitreṇa saṁdhir na^amitreṇa //*  
*07.13.18/ mitram amitraṁ ca^uddharator yo^amitra.uddhāriṇaḥ pārṣṇiṁ gṛhṇāti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.19/ vṛddha.mitro hy amitra.uddhārī pārṣṇi.grāham ucchindyāt, na^itaraḥ sva.pakṣa.upaghātī //*  
*07.13.20/ tayor alabdha.lābha.apagamane yasya.amitro mahato lābhād viyuktaḥ kṣaya.vyaya.adhiko vā sa pārṣṇi.grāho^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.21/ labdha.lābha.apagamane yasya^amitro lābhena śaktyā hīnaḥ sa pārṣṇi.grāho^atisaṁdhatte, yasya vā yātavyaḥ śatror vigraha.apakāra.samarthaḥ syāt //*  
*07.13.22/ pārṣṇi.grāhayor api yaḥ śakya.ārambha.bala.upādāna.adhikaḥ sthita.śatruḥ pārśva.sthāyī vā so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.23/ pārśva.sthāyī hi yātavya.abhisāro mūla.ābādhakaś ca bhavati, mūla.ābādhaka eva paścāt.sthāyī //*  
*07.13.24ab/ pārṣṇi.grāhās trayo jñeyāḥ śatroś ceṣṭā.nirodhakāḥ /*  
*07.13.24cd/ sāmantaḥ pṛṣṭhato vargaḥ prativeśau ca pārśvayoḥ //*  
*07.13.25ab/ arer netuś ca madhyastho durbalo^antardhir ucyate /*  
*07.13.25cd/ pratighāto balavato durga.aṭavy.apasāravān //*  
*07.13.26/ madhyamaṁ tvari.vijigīṣvor lipsamānayor madhyamasya pārṣṇiṁ gṛhṇator labdha.lābha.apagamane yo madhyamaṁ mitrād viyojayaty amitraṁ ca mitram āpnoti so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.27/ saṁdheyaś ca śatrur upakurvāṇo, na mitraṁ mitra.bhāvād utkrāntam //*  
*07.13.28/ tena^udāsīna.lipsā vyākhyātā //*  
*07.13.29/ "pārṣṇi.grahaṇa.abhiyānayos tu mantra.yuddhād abhyuccayaḥ //*  
*Kaś07.13.30/ vyāyāma.yuddhe hi kṣaya.vyayābhyām ubhayor avṛddhiḥ //*  
*07.13.31/ jitvā^api hi kṣiṇa.daṇḍa.kośaḥ parājito bhavati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.13.32/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.13.33/ sumahatā^api kṣaya.vyayena śatru.vināśo^abhyupagantavyaḥ //*  
*07.13.34/ tulye kṣaya.vyaye yaḥ purastād dūṣya.balaṁ ghātayitvā nihśalyaḥ paścād vaśya.balo yudhyeta so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.35/ dvayor api purastād dūṣya.bala.ghātinor yo bahulataraṁ śaktimattaram atyanta.dūṣyaṁ ca ghātayet so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.13.36/ tena^amitra.aṭavī.bala.ghāto vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*07.13.37ab/ pārṣṇi.grāho^abhiyoktā vā yātavyo vā yadā bhavet /*  
*07.13.37cd/ vijigīṣus tadā tatra netram etat samācaret //*  
*07.13.38ab/ pārṣṇi.grāho bhaven netā śatror mitra.abhiyoginaḥ /*  
*07.13.38cd/ vigrāhya pūrvam ākrandaṁ pārṣṇi.grāha.abhisāriṇā //*  
*07.13.39ab/ ākrandena^abhiyuñjānaḥ pārṣṇi.grāhaṁ nivārayet /*  
*07.13.39cd/ tathā^ākranda.abhisāreṇa pārṣṇi.grāha.abhisāriṇam //*  
*07.13.40ab/ ari.mitreṇa mitraṁ ca purastād avaghaṭṭayet /*  
*07.13.40cd/ mitra.mitram areś ca^api mitra.mitreṇa vārayet //*  
*07.13.41ab/ mitreṇa grāhayet pārṣṇim abhiyukto^abhiyoginaḥ /*  
*07.13.41cd/ mitra.mitreṇa ca^ākrandaṁ pārṣṇi.grāhān nivārayet //*  
*07.13.42ab/ evaṁ maṇḍalam ātma.arthaṁ vijigīṣur niveśayet /*  
*07.13.42cd/ pṛṣṭhataś ca purastāc ca mitra.prakṛti.sampadā //*  
*07.13.43ab/ kṛtsne ca maṇḍale nityaṁ dūtān gūḍhāṁś ca vāsayet /*  
*07.13.43cd/ mitra.bhūtaḥ sapatnānāṁ hatvā hatvā ca saṁvṛtaḥ //*  
*07.13.44ab/ asaṁvṛtasya kāryāṇi prāptāny api viśeṣataḥ /*  
*07.13.44cd/ nihsaṁśayaṁ vipadyante bhinna.plava iva^udadhau //E*  
*(hīna.śakti.pūraṇam)*  
*07.14.01/ sāmavāyikair evam abhiyukto vijigīṣur yas teṣāṁ pradhānas taṁ brūyāt "tvayā me saṁdhiḥ, idaṁ hiraṇyam, ahaṁ ca mitram, dvi.guṇā te vṛddhiḥ, na^arhasy ātma.kṣayeṇa mitra.mukhān amitrān vardhayitum, ete hi vṛddhās tvām eva paribhaviṣyanti" iti //*  
*07.14.02/ bhedaṁ vā brūyāt "anapakāro yathā^aham etaiḥ sambhūya^abhiyuktas tathā tvām apy ete saṁhita.balāḥ svasthā vyasane vā^abhiyokṣyante, balaṁ hi cittaṁ vikaroti, tad eṣāṁ vighātaya" iti //*  
*07.14.03/ bhinneṣu pradhānam upagṛhya hīneṣu vikramayet, hīnān anugrāhya vā pradhāne, yathā vā śreyo^abhimanyeta tathā //*  
*07.14.04/ vairaṁ vā parair grāhayitvā visaṁvādayet //*  
*07.14.05/ phala.bhūyastvena vā pradhānam upajāpya saṁdhiṁ kārayet //*  
*07.14.06/ atha^ubhaya.vetanāḥ phala.bhūyastvaṁ darśayantaḥ sāmavāyikān "atisaṁhitāḥ stha" ity udddūṣayeyuḥ //*  
*07.14.07/ duṣṭeṣu saṁdhiṁ dūṣayet //*  
*07.14.08/ atha^ubhaya.vetanā bhūyo bhedam eṣāṁ kuryuḥ "evaṁ tad yad asmābhir darśitam" iti //*  
*07.14.09/ bhinneṣv anyatama.upagraheṇa ceṣṭeta //*  
*07.14.10/ pradhāna.abhāve sāmavāyikānām utsāhayitāraṁ sthira.karmāṇam anurakta.prakṛktiṁ lobhād bhayād vā saṁghātam upāgataṁ vijigīṣor bhītaṁ rājya.pratisambaddhaṁ mitraṁ cala.amitraṁ vā pūrvān uttara.abhāve sādhayet - utsāhayitāram ātma.nisargeṇa, sthira.karmāṇaṁ sāntva.praṇipātena, anurakta.prakṛtiṁ kanyā.dāna.yāpanābhyām, lubdham aṁśa.dvaiguṇyena,*  
*bhītam ebhyaḥ kośa.daṇḍa.anugraheṇa, svato bhītaṁ viśvāsya pratibhū.pradānena, rājya.pratisambaddham ekī.bhāva.upagamanena, mitram ubhayataḥ priya.hitābhyām, upakāra.tyāgena vā, cala.amitram avadhṛtam anapakāra.upakārābhyām*  
*//*  
*07.14.11/ yo vā yathā^ayogaṁ bhajeta taṁ tathā sādhayet, sāma.dāna.bheda.daṇḍair vā yathā^āpatsu vyākhyāsyāmaḥ //*  
*07.14.12/ vyasana.upaghāta.tvarito vā kośa.daṇḍābhyāṁ deśe kāle kārye vā^avadhṛtaṁ saṁdhim upeyāt //*  
*07.14.13/ kṛta.saṁdhir hīnam ātmānaṁ pratikurvīta //*  
*07.14.14/ pakṣe hīno bandhu.mitra.pakṣaṁ kurvīta, durgam aviṣahyaṁ vā //*  
*07.14.15/ durga.mitra.pratiṣṭabdho hi sveṣāṁ pareṣāṁ ca pūjyo bhavati //*  
*07.14.16/ mantra.śakti.hīnaḥ prājña.puruṣa.upacayaṁ vidyā.vṛddha.samyogaṁ vā kurvīta //*  
*07.14.17/ tathā hi sadyaḥ śreyaḥ prāpnoti //*  
*07.14.18/ prabhāva.hīnaḥ prakṛti.yoga.kṣema.siddhau yateta //*  
*07.14.19/ jana.padaḥ sarva.karmaṇāṁ yoniḥ, tataḥ prabhāvaḥ //*  
*07.14.20/ tasya sthānam ātmanaś ca^āpadi durgam //*  
*07.14.21/ setu.bandhaḥ sasyānāṁ yoniḥ //*  
*07.14.22/ nitya.anuṣakto hi varṣa.guṇa.lābhaḥ setu.vāpeṣu //*  
*07.14.23/ vaṇik.pathaḥ para.atisaṁdhānasya yoniḥ //*  
*07.14.24/ vaṇik.pathena hi daṇḍa.gūḍha.puruṣa.atinayanaṁ śastra.āvaraṇa.yāna.vāhana.krayaś ca kriyate, praveśo nirṇayanaṁ ca //*  
*07.14.25/ khaniḥ saṁgrāma.upakaraṇānāṁ yoniḥ, dravya.vanaṁ durga.karmaṇāṁ yāna.rathayoś ca, hasti.vanaṁ hastinām, gava.aśva.khara.uṣṭrāṇāṁ ca vrajaḥ //*  
*07.14.26/ teṣām alābhe bandhu.mitra.kulebhyaḥ samārjanam //*  
*07.14.27/ utsāha.hīnaḥ śreṇī.pravīra.puruṣāṇāṁ cora.gaṇa.āṭavika.mleccha.jātīnāṁ para.apakāriṇāṁ gūḍha.puruṣāṇāṁ ca yathā.lābbham upacayaṁ kurvīta //*  
*07.14.28/ para.miśra.apratīkāram ābalīyasaṁ vā pareṣu prayuñjīta //*  
*07.14.29ab/ evaṁ pakṣeṇa mantreṇa dravyeṇa ca balena ca /*  
*07.14.29cd/ sampannaḥ pratinirgacchet para.avagraham ātmanaḥ //E*  
*(vigṛhya.uparodha.hetavah - daṇḍa.upanata.vṛttam)*  
*07.15.01/ durbalo rājā balavatā^abhiyuktas tad.viśiṣṭa.balam āśrayeta yam itaro mantra.śaktyā na^atisaṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.15.02/ tulya.mantra.śaktīnām āyatta.sampado vṛddha.samyogād vā viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.15.03/ viśiṣṭa.bala.abhāve sama.balais tulya.bala.saṁghair vā balavataḥ sambhūya tiṣṭhed yān na mantra.prabhāva.śaktibhyām atisaṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.15.04/ tulya.mantra.prabhāva.śaktīnāṁ vipula.ārambhato viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.15.05/ sama.bala.abhāve hīna.balaiḥ śucibhir utsāhibhiḥ pratyanīka.bhūtair balavataḥ sambhūya tiṣṭhed yān na mantra.prabhāva.utsāha.śaktibhir atisaṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.15.06/ tulya.utsāha.śaktīnāṁ sva.yuddha.bhūmi.lābhād viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.15.07/ tulya.bhūmīnāṁ sva.yuddha.kāla.lābhād viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.15.08/ tulya.deśa.kālānāṁ yugya.śastra.āvaraṇato viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.15.09/ sahāya.abhāve durgam āśrayeta yatra^amitraḥ prabhūta.sainyo^api bhakta.yavasa.indhana.udaka.uparodhaṁ na kuryāt svayaṁ ca kṣaya.vyayābhyāṁ yujyeta //*  
*07.15.10/ tulya.durgāṇāṁ nicaya.apasārato viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.15.11/ nicaya.apasāra.sampannaṁ hi manuṣya.durgam icched iti kauṭilyaḥ //07.15.12a/tad ebhiḥ kārṇair āśrayeta - "pārṣṇi.grāham āsāraṁ madhyamam udāsīnaṁ vā pratipādayiṣyāmi, sāmanta.āṭavika.tat.kulīna.aparuddhānām anyatamena^asya rājyaṁ hārayiṣyāmi ghātayiṣyāmi vā - //*  
*07.15.12b/ kṛtya.pakṣa.upagraheṇa vā^asya durge rāṣṭre skandha.āvāre vā kopaṁ samutthāpayiṣyāmi, śastra.agni.rasa.praṇidhānair aupaniṣadikair vā yathā.iṣṭam āsannaṁ haniṣyāmi - //*  
*07.15.12c/ svayaṁ.adhiṣṭhitena vā yoga.praṇidhānena kṣaya.vyayam enam upaneṣyāmi, kṣaya.vyaya.pravāsa.upatapte vā^asya mitra.varge sainye vā krameṇa^upajāpaṁ prāpsyāmi - //*  
*07.15.12d/ vīvadha.āsāra.prasāra.vadhena vā^asya skandha.āvāra.avagrahaṁ kariṣyāmi, daṇḍa.upanayena vā^asya randhram utthāpya sarva.saṁdohena prahariṣyāmi, pratihata.utsāhena vā yathā.iṣṭaṁ saṁdhim avāpsyāmi, mayi pratibaddhasya vā sarvataḥ kopāḥ samutthāsyanti - //*  
*07.15.12e/ nirāsāraṁ vā^asya mūlaṁ mitra.aṭavī.daṇḍair uddhātayiṣyāmi, mahato vā deśasya yoga.kṣemam ihasthaḥ pālayiṣyāmi, sva.vikṣiptaṁ mitra.vikṣiptaṁ vā me sainyam ihasthasya^ekastham aviṣahyaṁ bhaviṣyati, nimna.khāta.rātri.yuddha.viśāradaṁ vā me sainyaṁ pathya.ābādha.muktam āsanne karma kariṣyati - //*  
*07.15.12f/ viruddha.deśa.kālam iha.āgato vā svayam eva kṣaya.vyayābhyāṁ na bhaviṣyati, mahā.kṣaya.vyaya.abhigamyo^ayaṁ deśo durga.aṭavy.apasāra.bāhulyāt - //*  
*07.15.12g/ pareṣāṁ vyādhi.prāyaḥ sainya.vyāyāmānām alabdha.bhaumaś ca, tam āpad.gataḥ pravekṣyati, praviṣṭo vā na nirgamiṣyati" iti //*  
*07.15.13/ "kāraṇa.abhāve bala.samucchraye vā parasya durgam unmucya^apagacchet //*  
*07.15.14/ agni.pataṅgavad amitre vā praviśet //*  
*07.15.15/ anyatara.siddhir hi tyakta.ātmano bhavati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.15.16/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.15.17/ saṁdheyatām ātmanaḥ parasya ca^upalabhya saṁdadhīta //*  
*07.15.18/ viparyaye vikrameṇa saṁdhim apasāraṁ vā lipseta //*  
*07.15.19/ saṁdheyasya vā dūtaṁ preṣayet //*  
*07.15.20/ tena vā preṣitam artha.mānābhyāṁ satkṛtya brūyāt "idaṁ rājñaḥ paṇya.agāram, idaṁ devī.kumārāṇām, devī.kumāra.vacanāt, idaṁ rājyam ahaṁ ca tvad.arpaṇaḥ" iti //*  
*07.15.21/ labdha.saṁśrayaḥ samaya.ācārikavad bhartari varteta //*  
*07.15.22/ durga.ādīni ca karmāṇi āvāha.vivāha.putra.abhiṣeka.aśva.paṇya.hasti.grahaṇa.sattra.yātrā.vihāra.gamanāni ca^anujñātaḥ kurvīta //*  
*07.15.23/ sva.bhūmy.avasthita.prakṛti.saṁdhim upaghātam apasṛteṣu vā sarvam anujñātaḥ kurvīta //*  
*07.15.24/ duṣṭa.paura.jānapado vā nyāya.vṛttir anyāṁ bhūmiṁ yāceta //*  
*07.15.25/ duṣyavad upāṁśu.daṇḍena vā pratikurvīta //*  
*07.15.26/ ucitāṁ vā mitrād bhūmiṁ dīyamānāṁ na pratigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.15.27/ mantri.purohita.senā.pati.yuva.rājānām anyatamam adṛśyamāne bhartari paśyet, yathā.śakti ca^upakuryāt //*  
*07.15.28/ daivata.svasti.vācaneṣu tat.parā āśiṣo vācayet //*  
*07.15.29/ sarvatra^ātma.nisargaṁ guṇaṁ brūyāt //*  
*07.15.30ab/ samyukta.balavat.sevī viruddhaḥ śaṅkita.ādibhiḥ /*  
*07.15.30cd/ varteta daṇḍa.upanato bhartary evam avasthitaḥ //E*  
*(daṇḍa.upanāyi.vṛttam)*  
*07.16.01/ anujñāta.saṁdhi.paṇa.udvega.karaṁ balavān vijigīṣamāṇo yataḥ sva.bhūmiḥ sva.ṛtu.vṛttiś ca sva.sainyānām, adurga.apasāraḥ śatrur.apārṣṇir anāsāraś ca, tato yāyāt //*  
*07.16.02/ viparyaye kṛta.pratīkāro yāyāt //*  
*07.16.03/ sāma.dānābhyāṁ durbalān upanamayet, bheda.daṇḍābhyāṁ balavataḥ //*  
*07.16.04/ niyoga.vikalpa.samuccayaiś ca^upāyānām anantara.eka.antarāḥ prakṛtīḥ sādhayet //*  
*07.16.05/ grāma.araṇya.upajīvi.vraja.vaṇik.patha.anupālanam ujjhita.apasṛta.apakāriṇāṁ ca^arpaṇam iti sāntvam ācaret //*  
*07.16.06/ bhūmi.dravya.kanyā.dānam abhayasya ca^iti dānam ācaret //*  
*07.16.07/ sāmanta.āṭavika.tat.kulīna.aparuddhānām anyatama.upagraheṇa kośa.daṇḍa.bhūmi.dāya.yācanam iti bhedam ācaret //*  
*07.16.08/ prakāśa.kūṭa.tūṣṇīṁ.yuddha.durga.lambha.upāyair amitra.pragrahaṇam iti daṇḍam ācaret //*  
*07.16.09/ evam utsāhavato daṇḍa.upakāriṇaḥ sthāpayet, sva.prabhāvavataḥ kośa.upakāriṇaḥ, prajñāvato bhūmy.upakāriṇaḥ //*  
*07.16.10/ teṣāṁ paṇya.pattana.grāma.khani.saṁjātena ratna.sāra.phalgu.kupyena dravya.hasti.vana.vraja.samutthena yāna.vāhanena vā yad bahuśa upakaroti tac citra.bhogam //*  
*07.16.11/ yad daṇḍena kośena vā mahad upakaroti tan mahā.bhogam //*  
*07.16.12/ yad daṇḍa.kośa.bhūmībhir upakaroti tat sarva.bhogam //*  
*07.16.13/ yad amitram ekataḥ pratikaroti tad ekato.bhogi //*  
*07.16.14/ yad amitram āsāraṁ ca^ubhayataḥ pratikaroti tad ubhayato.bhogi //*  
*07.16.15/ yad amitra.āsāra.prativeśa.āṭavikān sarvataḥ pratikaroti tat sarvato.bhogi //*  
*07.16.16a/ pārṣṇi.grāhaś ca^āṭavikaḥ śatru.mukhyaḥ śatrur vā bhūmi.dāna.sādhyaḥ kaścid āsādyeta, nirguṇayā bhūmyā^enam upagrāhayet, apratisambaddhayā durgastham, nirupajīvyayā^āṭavikaṁ - //*  
*07.16.16b/ pratyādeyayā tat.kulīnaṁ śatroḥ, apacchinnayā śatror aparuddhaṁ nitya.amitrayā śreṇī.balam, balavat.sāmantayā saṁhata.balam, ubhābhyāṁ yuddhe pratilomam, - //*  
*07.16.16c/ alabdha.vyāyāmayā^utsāhinam, śūyayā^ari.pakṣīyam, karśitayā^apavāhitam, mahā.kṣaya.vyaya.niveśayā gata.pratyāgatam, anapāśrayayā pratyapasṛtam, pareṇa^anadhivāsyayā svayam eva bhartāram upagrāhayet //*  
*07.16.17/ teṣāṁ mahā.upakāraṁ nirvikāraṁ ca^anuvartayet //*  
*07.16.18/ pratilomam upāṁśunā sādhayet //*  
*07.16.19/ upakāriṇam upakāra.śaktyā toṣayet //*  
*07.16.20/ prayāsataś ca^artha.mānau kuryād, vyasaneṣu ca^anugraham //*  
*07.16.21/ svayaṁ.āgatānāṁ yathā.iṣṭa.darśanaṁ pratividhānaṁ ca kuryāt //*  
*07.16.22/ paribhava.upaghāta.kutsa.ativādāṁś ca^eṣu na prayuñjīta //*  
*07.16.23/ dattvā ca^abhayaṁ pitā^iva^anugṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.16.24/ yaś ca^asya^apakuryāt tad doṣam abhivikhyāpya prakāśam enaṁ ghātayet //*  
*07.16.25/ para.udvega.kāraṇād vā dāṇḍakarmikavac ceṣṭeta //*  
*07.16.26/ na ca hatasya bhūmi.dravya.putra.dārān abhimanyeta //*  
*07.16.27/ kulyān apy asya sveṣu pātreṣu sthāpayet //*  
*07.16.28/ karmaṇi mṛtasya putraṁ rājye sthāpayet //*  
*07.16.29/ evam asya daṇḍa.upanatāḥ putra.pautrān anuvartante //*  
*07.16.30/ yas tu^upanatān hatvā baddhvā vā bhūmi.dravya.putra.dārān abhimanyeta tasya^udvignaṁ maṇḍalam abhāvāya^uttiṣṭhate //*  
*07.16.31/ ye ca^asya^amātyāḥ sva.bhūmiṣv āyattās te ca^asya^udvignā maṇḍalam āśrayante //*  
*07.16.32/ svayaṁ vā rājyaṁ prāṇān vā^asya^abhimanyante //*  
*07.16.33ab/ sva.bhūmiṣu ca rājānas tasmāt sāmnā^anupālitāḥ /*  
*07.16.33cd/ bhavanty anuguṇā rājñaḥ putra.pautra.anuvartinaḥ //E*  
*(samdhi.karma -samādhi.mokṣah)*  
*07.17.01/ śamaḥ saṁdhiḥ samādhir ity eko^arthaḥ //*  
*07.17.02/ rājñāṁ viśvāsa.upagamaḥ śamaḥ saṁdhiḥ samādhir iti //*  
*07.17.03/ "satyaṁ śapatho vā calaḥ saṁdhiḥ, pratibhūḥ pratigraho vā sthāvaraḥ" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*07.17.04/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*07.17.05/ satyaṁ śapatho vā paratra^iha ca sthāvaraḥ saṁdhiḥ, iha.artha eva pratibhūḥ pratigraho vā bala.apekṣaḥ //*  
*07.17.06/ "saṁhitāḥ smaḥ" iti satya.saṁdhāḥ pūrve rājānaḥ satyena saṁdadhire //*  
*07.17.07/ tasya^atikrame śapathena agny.udaka.sītā.prākāra.loṣṭa.hasti.skandha.aśva.pṛṣṭa.ratha.upastha.śastra.ratna.bīja.gandha.rasa.suvarṇa.hiraṇyāny ālebhire "hanyur etāni tyajeyuś ca^enaṁ yaḥ śapatham atikrāmet" iti //*  
*07.17.08/ śapatha.atikrame mahatāṁ tapasvināṁ mukhyānāṁ vā prātibhāvya.bandhaḥ pratibhūḥ //*  
*07.17.09/ tasmin yaḥ para.avagraha.samarthān pratibhuvo gṛhṇāti, so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.17.10/ viparīto^atisaṁdhīyate //*  
*07.17.11/ bandhu.mukhya.pragrahaḥ pratigrahaḥ //*  
*07.17.12/ tasmin yo dūṣya.amātyaṁ dūṣya.apatyaṁ vā dadāti, so^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.17.13/ viparīto^atisaṁdhīyate*  
*07.17.14/ pratigraha.grahaṇa.viśvastasya hi paraś chidreṣu nirapekṣaḥ praharati //*  
*07.17.15/ apatya.samādhau tu kanyā.putra.dāne dadat tu kanyām atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.17.16/ kanyā hy adāyādā pareṣām eva^arthāya^ākleśyā(?) ca //*  
*07.17.17/ viparītaḥ putraḥ //*  
*07.17.18/ putrayor api yo jātyaṁ prājñaṁ śūraṁ kṛta.astram eka.putraṁ vā dadāti so^atisaṁdhīyate //*  
*07.17.19/ viparīto^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.17.20/ jātyād ajātyo hi lupta.dāyāda.saṁtānatvād ādhātuṁ śreyān, prājñād aprājño mantra.śakti.lopāt, śūrād aśūra utsāha.śakti.lopāt, kṛta.astrād akṛta.astraḥ prahartavya.sampal.lopāt, eka.putrād aneka.putro nirapekṣatvāt //*  
*07.17.21/ jātya.prājñayor jātyam aprājñam aiśvarya.prakṛtir anuvartate, prājñam ajātyaṁ mantra.adhikāraḥ //*  
*07.17.22/ mantra.adhikāre^api vṛddha.samyogāj jātyaḥ prājñam atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.17.23/ prājña.śūrayoḥ prājñam aśūraṁ mati.karmaṇāṁ yogo^anuvartate, śūram aprājñaṁ vikrama.adhikāraḥ //*  
*07.17.24/ vikrama.adhikāre^api hastinam iva lubdhakaḥ prājñaḥ śūram atisaṁdhatteś //*  
*07.17.25/ śūra.kṛta.astrayoḥ śūram akṛta.astraṁ vikrama.vyavasāyo^anuvartate, kṛta.astram aśūraṁ lakṣya.lambha.adhikāraḥ //*  
*07.17.26/ lakṣya.lambha.adhikāre^api sthairya.pratipatty.asammoṣaiḥ śūraḥ kṛta.astram atisaṁdhatte //*  
*07.17.27/ bahv.eka.putrayor bahu.putra ekaṁ dattvā śeṣa.pratiṣṭabdhaḥ saṁdhim atikrāmati, na^itaraḥ //*  
*07.17.28/ putra.sarva.sva.dāne saṁdhiś cet putra.phalato viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.17.29/ sama.phalayoḥ śakta.prajananato viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.17.30/ śakta.prajananayor apy upasthita.prajananato viśeṣaḥ //*  
*07.17.31/ śaktimaty eka.putre tu lupta.putra.utpattir ātmānam ādadhyāt, na ca^eka.putram iti //*  
*07.17.32/ abhyuccīyamānaḥ samādhi.mokṣaṁ kārayet //*  
*07.17.33/ kumāra.āsannāḥ sattriṇaḥ kāru.śilpi.vyañjanāḥ karmāṇi kurvāṇāḥ suruṅgayā rātrāv upakhānayitvā kumāram apahareyuḥ //*  
*07.17.34/ naṭanartaka.gāyana.vādaka.vāg.jīvana.kuśīlava.plavaka.saubhikā vā pūrva.praṇihitāḥ param upatiṣṭheran //*  
*07.17.35/ te kumāraṁ paraṁ.parayā^upatiṣṭheran //*  
*07.17.36/ teṣām aniyata.kāla.praveśa.sthāna.nirgamanāni sthāpayet //*  
*07.17.37/ tatas tad.vyañjano vā rātrau pratiṣṭheta //*  
*07.17.38/ tena rūpa.ājīvā bhāryā.vyañjanāś ca vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*07.17.39/ teṣāṁ vā tūrya.bhāṇḍa.phelāṁ gṛhītvā nirgacchet //*  
*07.17.40/ sūda.ārālika.snāpaka.saṁvāhaka.āstaraka.kalpaka.prasādhaka.udaka.paricārakair vā dravya.vastra.bhāṇḍa.phelā.śayana.āsana.sambhogair nirhriyeta //*  
*07.17.41/ paricārakac.chadmanā vā kiṁcid arūpa.velāyām ādāya nirgacchet, suruṅgā.mukhena vā niśā.upahāreṇa //*  
*07.17.42/ toya.āśaye vā vāruṇaṁ yogam ātiṣṭhet //*  
*07.17.43/ vaidehaka.vyañjanā vā pakva.anna.phala.vyavahāreṇa^ārakṣiṣu rasam upacārayeyuḥ //*  
*07.17.44/ daivata.upahāra.śrāddha.prahavaṇa.nimittam ārakṣiṣu madana.yoga.yuktam anna.pānaṁ rasaṁ vā prayujya^apagacchet, ārakṣaka.protsāhanena vā //*  
*07.17.45/ nāgaraka.kuśīlava.cikitsaka.āpūpika.vyañjanā vā rātrau samṛddha.gṛhāṇy ādīpayeyuḥ ārakṣiṇāṁ vā //*  
*07.17.46/ vaidehaka.vyañjanā vā paṇya.saṁsthām ādīpayeyuḥ //*  
*07.17.47/ anyad vā śarīraṁ nikṣipya sva.gṛham ādīpayed anupāta.bhayāt //*  
*07.17.48/ tataḥ saṁdhic.cheda.khāta.suruṅgābhir apagacchet //*  
*07.17.49/ kāca.kumbha.bhāṇḍa.bhāra.vyañjano vā rātrau pratiṣṭheta //*  
*07.17.50/ muṇḍa.jaṭilānāṁ pravāsanāny anupraviṣṭo vā rātrau tad.vyañjanaḥ pratiṣṭheta, virūpa.vyādhi.karaṇa.araṇya.carac.chadmanām anyatamena vā //07.17.51/ preta.vyañjano vā gūḍhair nirhriyeta //*  
*07.17.52/ pretaṁ vā strī.veṣeṇa^anugacchet //*  
*07.17.53/ vana.cara.vyañjanāś ca^enam anyato yāntam anyato^apadiśeyuḥ //*  
*07.17.54/ tato^anyato gacchet //*  
*07.17.55/ cakra.carāṇāṁ vā śakaṭa.vāṭair apagacchet //*  
*07.17.56/ āsanne ca^anupāte sattraṁ vā gṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.17.57/ sattra.abhāve hiraṇyaṁ rasa.viddhaṁ vā bhakṣya.jātam ubhayataḥ.panthānam utsṛjet //*  
*07.17.58/ tato^anyato^apagacchet //*  
*07.17.59/ gṛhīto vā sāma.ādibhir anupātam atisaṁdadhyāt, rasa.viddhena vā pathy.adanena //*  
*07.17.60/ vāruṇa.yoga.agni.dāheṣu vā śarīram anyad ādhāya śatrum abhiyuñjīta "putro me tvayā hataḥ" iti //*  
*07.17.61a/ upāttac.channa.śastro vā rātrau vikramya rakṣiṣu //*  
*07.17.61b/ śīghra.pātair apasared gūḍha.praṇihitaiḥ saha //E*  
*(madhyama.caritam - udāsīna.caritam - maṇḍala.caritam)*  
*07.18.01/ madhyamasya^ātmā tṛtīyā pañcamī ca prakṛtī prakṛtayaḥ //*  
*07.18.02/ dvitīyā caturthī ṣaṣṭhī ca vikṛtayaḥ //*  
*07.18.03/ tac ced ubhayaṁ madhyamo^anugṛhṇīyāt, vijigīṣur madhyama.anulomaḥ syāt //*  
*07.18.04/ na ced anugṛhṇīyāt, prakṛty.anulomaḥ syāt //*  
*07.18.05/ madyamaś ced vijigīṣor mitraṁ mitra.bhāvi lipseta, mitrasya^ātmanaś ca mitrāṇy utthāpya madhyamāc ca mitrāṇi bhedayitvā mitraṁ trāyeta //*  
*07.18.06/ maṇḍalaṁ vā protsāhayet "atipravṛddho^ayaṁ madhyamaḥ sarveṣāṁ no vināśāya^abhyutthitaḥ, sambhūya^asya yātrāṁ vihanāma" iti //*  
*07.18.07/ tac cen maṇḍalam anugṛhṇīyāt, madhyama.avagraheṇa^ātmānam upabṛṁhayet //*  
*07.18.08/ na ced anugṛhṇīyāt, kośa.daṇḍābhyāṁ mitram anugṛhya ye madhyama.dveṣiṇo rājānaḥ paraspara.anugṛhītā vā bahavas tiṣṭheyuḥ, eka.siddhau vā bahavaḥ sidhyeyuḥ, parasparād vā śaṅkitā na^uttiṣṭheran, teṣāṁ pradhānam ekam āsannaṁ vā sāma.dānābhyāṁ labheta //*  
*07.18.09/ dvi.guṇo dvitīyaṁ tri.gunas tṛtīyam //*  
*07.18.10/ evam abhyuccito madhyamam avagṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.18.11/ deśa.kāla.atipattau vā saṁdhāya madhyamena mitrasya sācivyaṁ kuryāt, dūṣyeṣu vā karma.saṁdhim //*  
*07.18.12/ karśanīyaṁ vā^asya mitraṁ madhyamo lipseta, pratistambhayed enaṁ "ahaṁ tvā trāyeya" iti ā karśanāt //*  
*07.18.13/ karśitam enaṁ trāyeta //*  
*07.18.14/ ucchedanīyaṁ vā^asya mitraṁ madhyamo lipseta, karśitam enaṁ trāyeta madhyama.vṛddhi.bhayāt //*  
*07.18.15/ ucchinnaṁ vā bhūmy.anugraheṇa haste kuryād anyatra^apasāra.bhayāt //*  
*07.18.16/ karśanīya.ucchedanīyayoś cen mitrāṇi madhyamasya sācivya.karāṇi syuḥ, puruṣa.antareṇa saṁdhīyeta //*  
*07.18.17/ vijigīṣor vā tayor mitrāṇy avagraha.samarthāni syuḥ, saṁdhim upeyāt //*  
*07.18.18/ amitraṁ vā^asya madhyamo lipseta, saṁdhim upeyāt //*  
*07.18.19/ evaṁ sva.arthaś ca kṛto bhavati madhyamasya priyaṁ ca //*  
*07.18.20/ madhyamaś cet sva.mitraṁ mitra.bhāvi lipseta, puruṣa.antareṇa saṁdadhyāt //*  
*07.18.21/ sa.apekṣaṁ vā "na^arhasi mitram ucchettum" iti vārayet //*  
*07.18.22/ upekṣeta vā "maṇḍalam asya kupyatu sva.pakṣa.vadhāt" iti //*  
*07.18.23/ amitram ātmano vā madhyamo lipseta, kośa.daṇḍābhyām enam adṛśyamāno^anugṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.18.24/ udāsīnaṁ vā madhyamo lipseta, asmai sāhāyyaṁ dadyād "udāsīnād bhidyatām" iti //*  
*07.18.25/ madhyama.udāsīnayor yo maṇḍalasya^abhipretas tam āśrayeta //*  
*07.18.26/ madhyama.caritena^udāsīna.caritaṁ vyākhyātam //*  
*07.18.27/ udāsīnaś cen madhyamaṁ lipseta, yataḥ śatrum atisaṁdadhyān mitrasya^upakāraṁ kuryād udāsīnaṁ vā daṇḍa.upakāriṇaṁ labheta tataḥ pariṇameta //*  
*07.18.28/ evam upabṛhya^ātmānam ari.prakṛtiṁ karśayen mitra.prakṛtiṁ ca^upagṛhṇīyāt //*  
*07.18.29a/ saty apy amitra.bhāve tasya^anātmavān nitya.apakārī śatruḥ śatru.saṁhitaḥ pārṣṇi.grāho vā vyasanī yātavyo vyasane vā netur abhiyoktā ity ari.bhāvinaḥ, eka.artha.abhiprayātaḥ pṛthag.artha.abhiprayātaḥ sambhūya.yātrikaḥ saṁhita.prayāṇikaḥ sva.artha.abhiprayātaḥ sāmutthāyikaḥ kośa.daṇḍayor anyatarasya kretā vikretā vā dvaidhī.bhāvika iti mitra.bhāvinaḥ,*  
*- //*  
*07.18.29b/ sāmanto balavataḥ pratighāto^antardhiḥ prativeśo vā balavataḥ pārṣṇi.grāho vā svayam upanataḥ pratāpa.upanato vā daṇḍa.upanata iti bhṛtya.bhāvinaḥ sāmantāḥ //*  
*07.18.30/ tair bhūmy.eka.antarā vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*07.18.31ab/ teṣāṁ śatru.virodhe yan mitram eka.arthatāṁ vrajet /*  
*07.18.31cd/ śaktyā tad.anugṛhṇīyād viṣaheta yayā param //*  
*07.18.32ab/ prasādhya śatruṁ yan mitraṁ vṛddhaṁ gacched avaśyatām /*  
*07.18.32cd/ sāmanta.eka.antarābhyāṁ tat.prakṛtibhyāṁ virodhayet //*  
*07.18.33ab/ tat.kulīna.aparuddhābhyāṁ bhūmiṁ vā tasya hārayet /*  
*07.18.33cd/ yathā vā^anugraha.apekṣaṁ vaśyaṁ tiṣṭhet tathā caret //*  
*07.18.34ab/ na^upakuryād amitraṁ vā gacched yad atikarśitam /*  
*07.18.34cd/ tad ahīnam avṛddhaṁ ca sthāpayen mitram arthavit //*  
*07.18.35ab/ artha.yuktyā calaṁ mitraṁ saṁdhiṁ yad upagacchati /*  
*07.18.35cd/ tasya^apagamane hetuṁ vihanyān na caled yathā //*  
*07.18.36ab/ ari.sādhāraṇaṁ yad vā tiṣṭhet tad aritaḥ śaṭham /*  
*07.18.36cd/ bhedayed bhinnam ucchindyāt tataḥ śatrum anantaram //*  
*07.18.37ab/ udāsīnaṁ ca yat tiṣṭhet sāmantais tad virodhayet /*  
*07.18.37cd/ tato vigraha.saṁtaptam upakāre niveśayet //*  
*07.18.38ab/ amitraṁ vijigīṣuṁ ca yat saṁcarati durbalam /*  
*07.18.38cd/ tad balena^anugṛhṇīyād yathā syān na parān.mukham //*  
*07.18.39ab/ apanīya tato^anyasyāṁ bhūmau vā samniveśayet /*  
*07.18.39cd/ niveśya pūrvaṁ tatra^anyad daṇḍa.anugraha.hetunā //*  
*07.18.40ab/ apakuryāt samarthaṁ vā na^upakuryād yad āpadi /*  
*07.18.40cd/ ucchindyād eva tan.mitraṁ viśvasya^aṅkam upasthitam //*  
*07.18.41ab/ mitra.vyasanato vā^arir uttiṣṭhed yo^anavagrahaḥ /*  
*07.18.41cd/ mitreṇa^eva bhavet sādhyaś chādita.vyasanena saḥ //*  
*07.18.41ab/ amitra.vyasanān mitram utthitaṁ yad virajyati /*  
*07.18.41cd/ ari.vyasana.siddhyā tat.śatruṇā^eva prasidhyati //*  
*07.18.42ab/ vṛddhiṁ kṣayaṁ ca sthānaṁ ca karśana.ucchedanaṁ tathā //*  
*07.18.42cd/ sarva.upāyān samādadhyād etān yaś ca^artha.śāstravit /*  
*07.18.43ab/ evam anyonya.saṁcāraṁ ṣāḍguṇyaṁ yo^anupaśyati //*  
*07.18.43cd/ sa buddhi.nigalair baddhair iṣṭaṁ krīḍati pārthivaiḥ //E*  
*(prakṛti.vyasana.vargah)*

### 8

*08.1.01/ vyasana.yaugapadye saukaryato yātavyaṁ rakṣitavyaṁ vā^iti vyasana.cintā //*  
*08.1.02/ daivaṁ mānuṣaṁ vā prakṛti.vyasanam anaya.apanayābhyāṁ sambhavati //*  
*08.1.03/ guṇa.prātilomyam abhāvaḥ pradoṣaḥ prasaṅgaḥ pīḍā vā vyasanam //*  
*08.1.04/ vyasyaty enaṁ śreyasa iti vyasanam //*  
*08.1.05/ "svāmy.amātya.jana.pada.durga.kośa.daṇḍa.mitra.vyasanānāṁ pūrvaṁ pūrvaṁ garīyaḥ" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.1.06/ na^iti bharadvājaḥ //*  
*08.1.07/ "svāmy.amātya.vyasanayor amātya.vyasanaṁ garīyaḥ //*  
*08.1.08/ mantro mantra.phala.avāptiḥ karma.anuṣṭhānam āya.vyaya.karma daṇḍa.praṇayanam amitra.aṭavī.pratiṣedho rājya.rakṣaṇaṁ vyasana.pratīkāraḥ kumāra.rakṣaṇam abhiṣekaś ca kumārāṇām āyattam amātyeṣu //*  
*08.1.09/ teṣām abhāve tad.abhāvaḥ, chinna.pakṣasya^iva rājñaś ceṣṭā.nāśaś ca //*  
*08.1.10/ vyasaneṣu ca^āsannaḥ para.upajāpaḥ //*  
*08.1.11/ vaiguṇye ca prāṇa.ābādhaḥ prāṇa.antika.caratvād rājñaḥ" iti //*  
*08.1.12/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.1.13/ mantri.purohita.ādi.bhṛtya.vargam adhyakṣa.pracāraṁ puruṣa.dravya.prakṛti.vyasana.pratīkāram edhanaṁ ca rājā^eva karoti //*  
*08.1.14/ vyasaniṣu vā^amātyeṣv anyān avyasaninaḥ karoti //*  
*08.1.15/ pūjya.pūjane dūṣya.avagrahe ca nitya.yuktas tiṣṭhati //*  
*08.1.16/ svāmī ca sampannaḥ sva.sampadbhiḥ prakṛtīḥ sampādayati //*  
*08.1.17/ sa yat.śīlas tat.śīlāḥ prakṛtayo bhavanti, utthāne pramāde ca tad.āyattatvāt //*  
*08.1.18/ tat.kūṭa.sthānīyo hi svāmī^iti //*  
*08.1.19/ "amātya.jana.pada.vyasanayor jana.pada.vyasanaṁ garīyaḥ" iti viśāla.akṣaḥ //*  
*08.1.20/ "kośo daṇḍaḥ kupyaṁ viṣṭir vāhanaṁ nicayāś ca jana.padād uttiṣṭhante //*  
*08.1.21/ teṣām abhāvo jana.pada.abhāve, svāmy.amātyayoś ca^anantaraḥ" iti //*  
*08.1.22/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.1.23/ amātya.mūlāḥ sarva.ārambhāḥ - jana.padasya karma.siddhayaḥ svataḥ parataś ca yoga.kṣema.sādhanaṁ vyasana.pratīkāraḥ śūnya.niveśa.upacayau daṇḍa.kara.anugrahaś ca^iti //*  
*08.1.24/ "jana.pada.durga.vyasanayor durga.vyasanam" iti pārāśarāḥ //*  
*08.1.25/ "durge hi kośa.daṇḍa.utpattir āpadi sthānaṁ ca jana.padasya //*  
*08.1.26/ śaktimattarāś ca paurā jānapadebhyo nityāś ca^āpadi sahāyā rājñaḥ //*  
*08.1.27/ jānapadās tv amitra.sādhāraṇāḥ" iti //*  
*08.1.28/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.1.29/ jana.pada.mūlā durga.kośa.daṇḍa.setu.vārttā.ārambhāḥ //*  
*08.1.30/ śauryaṁ sthairyaṁ dākṣyaṁ bāhulyaṁ ca jānapadeṣu //*  
*08.1.31/ parvata.antar.dvīpāś ca durgā na^adhyuṣyante jana.pada.abhāvāt //*  
*08.1.32/ karṣaka.prāye tu durga.vyasanam, āyudhīya.prāye tu jana.pade jana.pada.vyasanam iti //*  
*08.1.33/ "durga.kośa.vyasanayoḥ kośa.vyasanam" iti piśunaḥ //*  
*08.1.34/ "kośa.mūlo hi durga.saṁskāro durga.rakṣaṇaṁ jana.pada.mitra.amitra.nigraho deśa.antaritānām utsāhanaṁ daṇḍa.bala.vyavahāraś ca //*  
*08.1.35/ durgaḥ kośād upajāpyaḥ pareṣām //*  
*08.1.36/ kośam ādāya ca vyasane śakyam apayātum, na durgam" iti //*  
*08.1.37/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.1.38/ durga.arpaṇaḥ kośo daṇḍas tūṣṇīṁ.yuddhaṁ sva.pakṣa.nigraho daṇḍa.bala.vyavahāra āsāra.pratigrahaḥ para.cakra.aṭavī.pratiṣedhaś ca //*  
*08.1.39/ durga.abhāve ca kośaḥ pareṣām //*  
*08.1.40/ dṛśyate hi durgavatām anucchittir iti //*  
*08.1.41/ "kośa.daṇḍavyasanayor daṇḍa.vyasanam" iti kauṇapadantaḥ //*  
*08.1.42/ "daṇḍa.mūlo hi mitra.amitra.nigrahaḥ para.daṇḍa.utsāhanaṁ sva.daṇḍa.pratigrahaś ca //*  
*08.1.43/ daṇḍa.abhāve ca dhruvaḥ kośa.vināśaḥ //*  
*08.1.44/ kośa.abhāve ca śakyaḥ kupyena bhūmyā para.bhūmi.svayaṁ.grāheṇa vā daṇḍaḥ piṇḍayitum, daṇḍavatā ca kośaḥ //*  
*08.1.45/ svāminaś ca^āsanna.vṛttitvād amātya.sadharmā daṇḍaḥ" iti //*  
*08.1.46/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.1.47/ kośa.mūlo hi daṇḍaḥ //*  
*08.1.48/ kośa.abhāve daṇḍaḥ paraṁ gacchati, svāminaṁ vā hanti //*  
*08.1.49/ sarva.abhiyoga.karaś ca kośo dharma.kāma.hetuḥ //*  
*08.1.50/ deśa.kāla.kārya.vaśena tu kośa.daṇḍayor anyataraḥ pramāṇī.bhavati //*  
*08.1.51/ lambha.pālano hi daṇḍaḥ kośasya, kośaḥ kośasya daṇḍasya ca bhavati //*  
*08.1.52/ sarva.dravya.prayojakatvāt kośa.vyasanaṁ garīya iti //*  
*08.1.53/ "daṇḍa.mitra.vyasanayor mitra.vyasanam" iti vātavyādhiḥ //*  
*08.1.54/ "mitram abhṛtaṁ vyavahitaṁ ca karma karoti, pārṣṇi.grāham āsāram amitram āṭavikaṁ ca pratikaroti, kośa.daṇḍa.bhūmibhiś ca^upakaroti vyasana.avasthā.yogam" iti //*  
*08.1.55/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.1.56/ daṇḍavato mitraṁ mitra.bhāve tiṣṭhati, amitro vā mitra.bhāve //*  
*08.1.57/ daṇḍa.mitrayos tu sādhāraṇe kārye sārataḥ sva.yuddha.deśa.kāla.lābhād viśeṣaḥ //*  
*08.1.58/ śīghra.abhiyāne tv amitra.āṭavika.anabhyantara.kope ca na mitraṁ vidyate //*  
*08.1.59/ vyasana.yaugapadye para.vṛddhau ca mitram artha.yuktau tiṣṭhati //*  
*08.1.60/ iti prakṛti.vyasana.sampradhāraṇam uktam //*  
*08.1.61ab/ prakṛty.avayavānāṁ tu vyasanasya viśeṣataḥ /*  
*08.1.61cd/ bahu.bhāvo^anurāgo vā sāro vā kārya.sādhakaḥ //*  
*08.1.62ab/ dvayos tu vyasane tulye viśeṣo guṇataḥ kṣayāt /*  
*08.1.62cd/ śeṣa.prakṛti.sādguṇyaṁ yadi syān na^avidheyakam //*  
*08.1.63ab/ śeṣa.prakṛti.nāśas tu yatra^eka.vyasanād bhavet /*  
*08.1.63cd/ vyasanaṁ tad garīyaḥ syāt pradhānasya^itarasya vā //E*  
*(rāja.rājyayor vyasana.cintā)*  
*08.2.01/ rājā rājyam iti prakṛti.saṁkṣepaḥ //*  
*08.2.02/ rājño^abhyantaro bāhyo vā kopa iti //*  
*08.2.03/ ahi.bhayād abhyantaraḥ kopo bāhya.kopāt pāpīyān, antar.amātya.kopaś ca^antaḥ.kopāt //*  
*08.2.04/ tasmāt kośa.daṇḍa.śaktim ātma.saṁsthāṁ kurvīta //*  
*08.2.05/ "dvairājya.vairājyayor dvairājyam anyonya.pakṣa.dveṣa.anurāgābhyāṁ paraspara.saṁgharṣeṇa vā vinaśyati, vairājyaṁ tu prakṛti.citta.grahaṇa.apekṣi yathā.sthitam anyair bhujyate" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.2.06/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.2.07/ pitā.putrayor bhrātror vā dvairājyaṁ tulya.yoga.kṣemam amātya.avagrahaṁ vartayati //*  
*08.2.08/ vairājyaṁ tu jīvataḥ parasya^ācchidya "na^etan mama" iti manyamānaḥ karśayati, apavāhayati, paṇyaṁ vā karoti, viraktaṁ vā parityajya^apagacchati^iti //*  
*08.2.09/ andhaś calita.śāstro vā rājā^iti "aśāstra.cakṣur andho yat.kiṁcana.kārī dṛḍha.abhiniveśī para.praṇeyo vā rājyam anyāyena^upahanti, calita.śāstras tu yatra śāstrāc calita.matir bhavati śakya.anunayo bhavati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.2.10/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.2.11/ andho rājā śakyate sahāya.sampadā yatra tatra vā paryavasthāpayitum //*  
*08.2.12/ calita.śāstras tu śāstrād anyathā.abhiniviṣṭa.buddhir anyāyena rājyam ātmānaṁ ca^upahanti^iti //*  
*08.2.13/ vyādhito navo vā rājā^iti "vyādhito rājā rājya.upaghātam amātya.mūlaṁ prāṇa.ābādhaṁ vā rājya.mūlam avāpnoti, navas tu rājā sva.dharma.anugraha.parihāra.dāna.māna.karmabhiḥ prakṛti.rañjana.upakāraiś carati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.2.14/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.2.15/ vyādhito rājā yathā.pravṛttaṁ rāja.praṇidhim anuvartayati //*  
*08.2.16/ navas tu rājā bala.āvarjitaṁ "mama^idaṁ rājyam" iti yathā.iṣṭam anavagrahaś carati //*  
*08.2.17/ sāmutthāyikair avagṛhīto vā rājya.upaghātaṁ marṣayati //*  
*08.2.18/ prakṛtiṣv arūḍhaḥ sukham ucchettuṁ bhavati^iti //*  
*08.2.19/ vyādhite viśeṣaḥ pāpa.rogya.pāpa.rogī ca //*  
*08.2.20/ nave^apy abhijāto^anabhijāta iti //*  
*08.2.21/ durbalo^abhijāto balavān anabhijāto rājā^iti "durbalasya^abhijātasya^upajāpaṁ daurbalya.apekṣāḥ prakṛtayaḥ kṛcchreṇa^upagacchanti, balavataś ca^anabhijātasya bala.apekṣāḥ sukhena" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.2.22/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.2.23/ durbalam abhijātaṁ prakṛtayaḥ svayam upanamanti, jātyam aiśvarya.prakṛtir anuvartata iti //*  
*08.2.24/ balavataś ca^anabhijātasya^upajāpaṁ visaṁvādayanti, anurāge sārvaguṇyam iti //*  
*08.2.25/ prayāsa.vadhāt sasya.vadho muṣṭi.vadhāt pāpīyān, nirājīvatvād avṛṣṭir ativṛṣṭitaḥ //*  
*08.2.26ab/ dvayor dvayor vyasanayoḥ prakṛtīnāṁ bala.abalam /*  
*08.2.26cd/ pāramparya.krameṇa^uktaṁ yāne sthāne ca kāraṇam //E*  
*(puruṣa.vyasana.vargah)*  
*08.3.01/ avidyā.vinayaḥ puruṣa.vyasana.hetuḥ //*  
*08.3.02/ avinīto hi vyasana.doṣān na paśyati //*  
*08.3.03/ tān upadekṣyāmaḥ //*  
*08.3.04/ kopajas tri.vargaḥ, kāmajaś catur.vargaḥ //*  
*08.3.05/ tayoḥ kopo garīyān //*  
*08.3.06/ sarvatra hi kopaś carati //*  
*08.3.07/ prāyaśaś ca kopa.vaśā rājānaḥ prakṛti.kopair hatāḥ śrūyante, kāma.vaśāḥ kṣaya.nimittam ari.vyādhibhir iti //*  
*08.3.08/ na^iti bhāradvājaḥ //*  
*08.3.09/ "sat.puruṣa.ācāraḥ kopo vaira.yātanam avajñā.vadho bhīta.manuṣyatā ca //*  
*08.3.10/ nityaś ca kopena sambandhaḥ pāpa.pratiṣedha.arthaḥ //*  
*08.3.11/ kāmaḥ siddhi.lābhaḥ sāntvaṁ tyāga.śīlatā sampriya.bhāvaś ca //*  
*08.3.12/ nityaś ca kāmena sambandhaḥ kṛta.karmaṇaḥ phala.upabhoga.arthaḥ" iti //*  
*08.3.13/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.3.14/ dveṣyatā śatru.vedanaṁ duḥkha.āsaṅgaś ca kopaḥ //*  
*08.3.15/ paribhavo dravya.nāśaḥ pāṭaccara.dyūtakāra.lubdhaka.gāyana.vādakaiś ca^anarthyaiḥ samyogaḥ kāmaḥ //*  
*08.3.16/ tayoḥ paribhavād dveṣyatā garīyasī //*  
*08.3.17/ paribhūtaḥ svaiḥ paraiś ca^avagṛhyate, dveṣyaḥ samucchidyata iti //08.3.18/ dravya.nāśāt^śatru.vedanaṁ garīyaḥ //*  
*08.3.19/ dravya.nāśaḥ kośa.ābādhakaḥ, śatru.vedanaṁ prāṇa.ābādhakam iti //*  
*08.3.20/ anarthya.samyogād duḥkha.samyogo garīyān //*  
*08.3.21/ anarthya.samyogo muhūrta.pratīkāro, dīrgha.kleśa.karo duḥkhānām āsaṅga iti //*  
*08.3.22/ tasmāt kopo garīyān //*  
*08.3.23/ vāk.pāruṣyam artha.dūṣaṇaṁ daṇḍa.pāruṣyam iti //*  
*08.3.24/ "vāk.pāruṣya.artha.dūṣaṇayor vāk.pāruṣyaṁ garīyaḥ" iti viśāla.akṣaḥ //*  
*08.3.25/ "paruṣa.mukto hi tejasvī tejasā pratyārohati //*  
*08.3.26/ durukta.śalyaṁ hṛdi nikhātaṁ tejaḥ.saṁdīpanam indriya.upatāpi ca" iti //*  
*08.3.27/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*
