*08.3.28/ artha.pūjā vāk.śalyam apahanti, vṛtti.vilopas tv artha.dūṣaṇam //*  
*08.3.29/ adānam ādānaṁ vināśaḥ parityāgo vā^arthasya^ity artha.dūṣaṇam //*  
*08.3.30/ "artha.dūṣaṇa.daṇḍa.pāruṣyayor artha.dūṣaṇaṁ garīyaḥ" iti pārāśarāḥ //*  
*08.3.31/ "artha.mūlau dharma.kāmau //*  
*08.3.32/ artha.pratibaddhaś ca loko vartate //*  
*08.3.33/ tasya.upaghāto garīyān" iti //*  
*08.3.34/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.3.35/ sumahatā^apy arthena na kaścana śarīra.vināśam icchet //*  
*08.3.36/ daṇḍa.pāruṣyāc ca tam eva doṣam anyebhyaḥ prāpnoti //*  
*08.3.37/ iti kopajas tri.vargaḥ //*  
*08.3.38/ kāmajas tu mṛgayā dyūtaṁ striyaḥ pānam iti catur.vargaḥ //*  
*08.3.39/ tasya "mṛgayā.dyūtayor mṛgayā garīyasī" iti piśunaḥ //*  
*08.3.40/ "stena.amitra.vyāla.dāva.praskhalana.bhaya.din.mohāḥ kṣut.pipāse ca prāṇa.ābādhas tasyām //*  
*08.3.41/ dyūte tu jitam eva^akṣa.viduṣā yathā jayat.sena.duryodhanābhyām" iti //*  
*08.3.42/ na^ity kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.3.43/ tayor apy anyatara.parājayo^asti^iti nala.yudhiṣṭhirābhyāṁ vyākhyātam //*  
*08.3.44/ tad eva vijita.dravyam āmiṣaṁ vaira.anubandhaś ca //*  
*08.3.45/ sato^arthasya vipratipattir asataś ca^arjanam apratibhukta.nāśo mūtra.purīṣa.dhāraṇa.bubhukṣā.ādibhiś ca vyādhi.lābha iti dyūta.doṣāḥ //*  
*08.3.46/ mṛgayāyāṁ tu vyāyāmaḥ śleṣma.pitta.medaḥ.sveda.nāśaś cale sthite ca kāye lakṣa.paricayaḥ kopa.bhaya.sthāneṣu ca mṛgāṇāṁ citta.jñānam anitya.yānaṁ ca^iti //*  
*08.3.47/ "dyūta.strī.vyasanayoḥ kaitava.vyasanam" iti kauṇapadantaḥ //*  
*08.3.48/ "sātatyena hi niśi pradīpe mātari ca mṛtāyāṁ dīvyaty eva kitavaḥ //*  
*08.3.49/ kṛcchre ca pratipṛṣṭaḥ kupyati //*  
*08.3.50/ strī.vyasane tu snāna.pratikarma.bhojana.bhūmiṣu bhavaty eva dharma.artha.paripraśnaḥ //*  
*08.3.51/ śakyā ca strī rājahite.niyoktum, upāṁśu.daṇḍena vyādhinā vā vyāvartayitum avasrāvayituṁ vā" iti //*  
*08.3.52/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.3.53/ sapratyādeyaṁ dyūtaṁ niṣpratyādeyaṁ strī.vyasanam //*  
*08.3.54/ adarśanaṁ kārya.nirvedaḥ kāla.atipātanād anartho dharma.lopaś ca tantra.daurbalyaṁ pāna.anubandhaś ca^iti //*  
*08.3.55/ "strī.pāna.vyasanayoḥ strī.vyasanam" iti vātavyādhiḥ //*  
*08.3.56/ "strīṣu hi bāliśyam aneka.vidhaṁ niśānta.praṇidhau vyākhyātam //*  
*08.3.57/ pāne tu śabda.ādīnām indriya.arthānām upabhogaḥ prīti.dānaṁ parijana.pūjanaṁ karma.śrama.vadhaś ca" iti //*  
*08.3.58/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.3.59/ strī.vyasane bhavaty apatya.utpattir ātma.rakṣaṇaṁ ca^antar.dāreṣu, viparyayo vā bāhyeṣu, agamyeṣu sarva.ucchittiḥ //*  
*08.3.60/ tad ubhayaṁ pāna.vyasane //*  
*08.3.61/ pāna.sampat - saṁjñā.nāśo^anunmattasya^unmattatvam apretasya pretatvaṁ kaupīna.darśanaṁ śruta.prajñā.prāṇa.vitta.mitra.hāniḥ sadbhir viyogo^anarthya.samyogas tantrī.gīta.naipuṇyeṣu ca^arthaghneṣu prasaṅga iti //*  
*08.3.62/ dyūta.madyayor dyūtam //*  
*08.3.63/ ekeṣāṁ paṇa.nimitto jayaḥ parājayo vā prāṇiṣu niścetaneṣu vā pakṣa.dvaidhena prakṛti.kopaṁ karoti //*  
*08.3.64/ viśeṣataś ca saṁghānāṁ saṁgha.dharmiṇāṁ ca rāja.kulānāṁ dyūta.nimitto bhedas tan.nimitto vināśa ity asat.pragrahaḥ pāpiṣṭhatamo vyasanānāṁ tantra.daurbalyād iti //*  
*08.3.65ab/ asatāṁ pragrahaḥ kāmaḥ kopaś ca^avagrahaḥ satām /*  
*08.3.65cd/ vyasanaṁ doṣa.bāhulyād atyantam ubhayaṁ matam //*  
*08.3.66ab/ tasmāt kopaṁ ca kāmaṁ ca vyasana.ārambham ātmavān /*  
*08.3.66cd/ parityajen mūla.haraṁ vṛddha.sevī jita.indriyaḥ //E*  
*(pīḍana.vargah - stambha.vargah - kośa.sanga.vargah)*  
*08.4.01/ daiva.pīḍanaṁ - agnir udakaṁ vyādhir durbhikṣaṁ maraka iti //*  
*08.4.02/ "agny.udakayor agni.pīḍanam apratikāryaṁ sarva.dāhi ca, śakya.apagamanaṁ tārya.ābādham udaka.pīḍanam" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.03/ na^it kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.04/ agnir grāmam ardha.grāmaṁ vā dahati, udaka.vegas tu grāma.śata.pravāhī^iti //*  
*08.4.05/ "vyādhi.durbhikṣayor vyādhiḥ preta.vyādhita.upasṛṣṭa.paricāraka.vyāyāma.uparodhena karmāṇy upahanti, durbhikṣaṁ punar akarma.upaghāti hiraṇya.paśu.kara.dāyi ca" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.06/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.07/ eka.deśa.pīḍano vyādhiḥ śakya.pratīkāraś ca, sarva.deśa.pīḍanaṁ durbhikṣaṁ prāṇinām ajīvanāya^iti //*  
*08.4.08/ tena marako vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*08.4.09/ "kṣudraka.mukhya.kṣayayoḥ kṣudraka.kṣayaḥ karmaṇām ayoga.kṣemaṁ karoti, mukhya.kṣayaḥ karma.anuṣṭhāna.uparodha.dharmā" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.10/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.11/ śakyaḥ kṣudraka.kṣayaḥ pratisaṁdhātuṁ bāhulyāt kṣudrakāṇām, na mukhya.kṣayaḥ //*  
*08.4.12/ sahasreṣu hi mukhyo bhavaty eko na vā sattva.prajñā.ādhikyāt tad.āśrayatvāt kṣudrakāṇām iti //*  
*08.4.13/ "sva.cakra.para.cakrayoḥ sva.cakram atimātrābhyāṁ daṇḍa.karābhyāṁ pīḍayaty aśakyaṁ ca vārayitum, para.cakraṁ tu śakyaṁ praiyoddhum upasāreṇa saṁdhinā vā mokṣayitum" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.14/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.15/ sva.cakra.pīḍanaṁ prakṛti.puruṣa.mukhya.upagraha.vighātābhyāṁ śakyate vārayitum eka.deśaṁ vā pīḍayati, sarva.deśa.pīḍanaṁ tu para.cakraṁ vilopa.ghāta.dāha.vidhvaṁsana.apavāhanaiḥ pīḍayati^iti //*  
*08.4.16/ "prakṛti.rāja.vivādayoḥ prakṛiti.vivādaḥ prakṛtīnāṁ bhedakaḥ para.abhiyogān āvahati, rāja.vivādas tu prakṛtīnāṁ dvi.guṇa.bhakta.vetana.parihāra.karo bhavati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.17/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.18/ śakyaḥ prakṛti.vivādaḥ prakṛti.mukhya.upagraheṇa kalaha.sthāna.apanayanena vā vārayitum //*  
*08.4.19/ vivadamānās tu prakṛtayaḥ paraspara.saṁgharṣeṇa^upakurvanti //*  
*08.4.20/ rāja.vivādas tu pīḍana.ucchedanāya prakṛtīnāṁ dvi.guṇa.vyāyāma.sādhya iti //*  
*08.4.21/ "deśa.rāja.vihārayor deśa.vihāras traikālyena karma.phala.upaghātaṁ karoti, rāja.vihāras tu kāru.śilpi.kuśīlava.vāg.jīvana.rūpa.ājīvā.vaidehaka.upakāraṁ karoti" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.22/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.23/ deśa.vihāraḥ karma.śramam avadhā.artham alpaṁ bhakṣayati bhakṣayitvā ca bhūyaḥ karmasu yogaṁ gacchati, rāja.vihāras tu svayaṁ vallabhaiś ca svayaṁ.grāha.praṇaya.paṇya.agāra.kārya.upagrahaiḥ pīḍayati^iti //*  
*08.4.24/ "subhagā.kumārayoḥ kumāraḥ svayaṁ vallabhaiś ca svayaṁ.grāha.praṇaya.paṇya.agāra.kārya.upagrahaiḥ pīḍayati, subhagā vilāsa.upabhogena" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.25/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.26/ śakyaḥ kumāro mantri.purohitābhyāṁ vārayitum, na subhagā bāliśyād anarthya.jana.samyogāc ca^iti //*  
*08.4.27/ "śreṇī.mukhyayoḥ śreṇī bāhulyād anavagrahā steya.sāhasābhyāṁ pīḍayati, mukhyaḥ kārya.anugraha.vighātābhyām" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.28/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.29/ suvyāvartyā śreṇī samāna.śīla.vyasanatvāt, śreṇī.mukhya.eka.deśa.upagraheṇa vā //*  
*08.4.30/ stambha.yukto mukhyaḥ para.prāṇa.dravya.upaghātābhyāṁ pīḍayati^iti //*  
*08.4.31/ "samnidhātṛ.samāhartroḥ samnidhātā kṛta.vidūṣaṇa.atyayābhyāṁ pīḍayati, samāhartā karaṇa.adhiṣṭhitaḥ pradiṣṭa.phala.upabhogī bhavati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.32/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.33/ samnidhātā kṛta.avastham anyaiḥ kośa.praveśyaṁ pratigṛhṇāti, samāhartā tu pūrvam artham ātmanaḥ kṛtvā paścād rāja.arthaṁ karoti praṇāśayati vā, para.sva.ādāne ca sva.pratyayaś carati^iti //*  
*08.4.34/ "anta.pāla.vaidehakayor anta.pālaś cora.prasarga.deya.atyādānābhyāṁ vaṇik.pathaṁ pīḍayati, vaidehakās tu paṇya..pratipaṇya.anugrahaiḥ prasādhayanti" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.35/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.36/ anta.pālaḥ paṇya.sampāta.anugraheṇa vartayati, vaidehakās tu sambhūya paṇyānām utkarṣa.apakarṣaṁ kurvāṇāḥ paṇe paṇa.śataṁ kumbhe kumbha.śatam ity ājīvanti //*  
*08.4.37/ abhijāta.uparuddhā bhūmiḥ paśu.vraja.uparuddhā vā^iti "abhijāta.uparuddhā bhūmiḥ mahā.phalā^apy āyudhīya.upakāriṇī na kṣamā mokṣayituṁ vyasana.ābādha.bhayāt, paśu.vraja.uparuddhā tu kṛṣi.yogyā kṣamā mokṣayitum //*  
*08.4.38/ vivītaṁ hi kṣetreṇa bādhyate" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.39/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.40/ abhijāta.uparuddhā bhūmir atyanta.mahā.upakārā^api kṣamā mokṣayituṁ vyasana.ābādha.bhayāt, paśu.vraja.uparuddhā tu kośa.vāhana.upakāriṇī na kṣamā mokṣayitum, anyatra sasya.vāpa.uparodhād iti //*  
*08.4.41/ "pratirodhaka.āṭavikayoḥ pratirodhakā rātri.sattra.carāḥ śarīra.ākramiṇo nityāḥ śata.sahasra.ahapāriṇaḥ pradhāna.kopakāś ca vyavahitāḥ pratyantara.araṇya.carāś ca^āṭavikāḥ prakāśā dṛsyāś caranti, eka.deśa.ghātakāś ca" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*08.4.42/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*08.4.43/ pratirodhakāḥ pramattasya^aparahanti, alpāḥ kuṇṭhāḥ sukhā jñātuṁ grahītuṁ ca, sva.deśasthāḥ prabhūtā vikrāntāś ca^āṭavikāḥ prakāśa.yodino^apahartāro hantāraś ca deśānāṁ rāja.sadharmāṇa iti //*  
*08.4.44/ mṛga.hasti.vanayoḥ mṛgāḥ prabhūtāḥ prabhūta.māṁsa.carma.upakāriṇo manda.grāsa.avakleśinaḥ suniyamyāś ca //*  
*08.4.45/ viparītā hastino gṛhyamāṇā duṣṭāś ca deśa.vināśāya^iti //*  
*08.4.46/ sva.para.sthānīya.upakārayoḥ sva.sthānīya.upakāro dhānya.paśu.hiraṇya.kupya.upakāro jānapadānām āpady ātma.dhāraṇaḥ //*  
*08.4.47/ viparītaḥ para.sthānīya.upakāraḥ //*  
*08.4.48/ iti pīḍanāni - ābhyantaro mukhya.stambho bāhyo^amitra.aṭavī.stambhaḥ // \[iti stambha.vargah\]*  
*08.4.49/ tābhyāṁ pīḍanair yathā.uktaiś ca pīḍitaḥ, sakto mukhyeṣu, parihāra.upahataḥ, prakīrṇo, mithyā.saṁhṛtaḥ, sāmanta.aṭavī.hṛta iti kośa.saṅga.vargaḥ //*  
*08.4.50ab/ pīḍanānām anutpattāv utpannānāṁ ca vāraṇe /*  
*08.4.50cd/ yateta deśa.vṛddhy.arthaṁ nāśe ca stambha.saṅgayoḥ //E*  
*(bala.vyasana.vargah - mitra.vyasana.vargah)*  
*08.5.01/ bala.vyasanāni - amānitam, vimānitam, abhṛtam, vyādhitam, nava.āgatam, dūra.āyātam, pariśrāntam, parikṣīṇam, pratihatam, hata.agra.vegam, anṛtu.prāptam, abhūmi.prāptam, āśā.nirvedi, parisṛptam, kalatra.garbhi, antaḥ.śalyam, kupita.mūlam, bhinna.garbham, apasṛtam, atikṣiptam, upaniviṣṭam, samāptam, uparuddham, parikṣiptam, chinna.dhānya.puruṣa.vīvadham,*  
*sva.vikṣiptam, mitra.vikṣiptam, dūṣya.yuktam, duṣṭa.pārṣṇi.grāham, śūnya.mūlam, asvāmi.saṁhatam, bhinna.kūṭam, andham iti //*  
*08.5.02/ teṣām amānita.vimānita.aniyatayor amānitaṁ kṛta.artha.mānaṁ yudhyeta, na vimānitam antaḥ.kopam //*  
*08.5.03/ abhṛta.vyādhitayor abhṛtaṁ tadātva.kṛta.vetanaṁ yudhyeta, na vyādhitam akarmaṇyam //*  
*08.5.04/ nava.āgata.dūra.āyātayor nava.āgatam anyata upalabdha.deśam anava.miśraṁ yudhyeta, na dūra.āyatam āyata.gata.parikleśam //*  
*08.5.05/ pariśrānta.parikṣīṇayoḥ pariśrāntaṁ snāna.bhojana.svapna.labdha.viśrāmaṁ yudhyeta, na parikṣīṇam anyatra^āhave kṣīṇayugya.puruṣam //*  
*08.5.06/ pratihata.hata.agra.vegayoḥ pratihatam agra.pāta.bhagnaṁ pravīra.puruṣa.saṁhataṁ yudhyeta, na hata.agra.vegam agra.pāta.hata.vīram //*  
*08.5.07/ anṛtv.abhūmi.prāptayor anṛtu.prāptaṁ yatha.ṛtu.yugya.śastra.āvaraṇaṁ yudhyeta, na^abhūmi.prāptam avaruddha.prasāra.vyāyāmam //*  
*08.5.08/ āśā.nirvedi.parisṛptayor āśā.nirvedi labdha.abhiprāyaṁ yudhyeta, na parisṛptam apasṛta.mukhyam //*  
*08.5.09/ kalatra.garbhy.antaḥ.śalyayoḥ kalatra.garbhi unmucya kalatraṁ yudhyeta, na^antaḥ.śalyam antara.mitram //*  
*08.5.10/ kupita.mūla.bhinna.garbhayoḥ kupita.mūlaṁ praśamita.kopaṁ sāma.ādibhir yudhyeta, na bhinna.garbham anyonyasmād bhinnam //*  
*08.5.11/ apasṛta.atikṣiptayor apasṛtam eka.rājya.atikrāntaṁ mantra.vyāyāmābhyāṁ sattra.mitra.apāśrayaṁ yudhyeta, na^atikṣiptam aneka.rājya.atikrāntaṁ bahv.ābādhatvāt //*  
*08.5.12/ upaniviṣṭa.samāptayor upaniviṣṭaṁ pṛthag.yāna.sthānam atisaṁdhāya^ariṁ yudhyeta, na samāptam ariṇā^eka.sthāna.yānam //*  
*08.5.13/ uparuddha.parikṣiptayor uparuddham anyato niṣkramya^uparoddhāraṁ pratiyudhyeta, na parikṣiptaṁ sarvataḥ pratiruddham //*  
*08.5.14/ chinna.dhānya.puruṣa.vīvadhayoḥ chinna.dhānyam anyato dhānyam ānīya jaṅgama.sthāvara.āhāraṁ vā yudhyeta, na chinna.puruṣa.vīvadham anabhisāram //*  
*08.5.15/ sva.vikṣipta.mitra.vikṣiptayoḥ sva.vikṣiptaṁ sva.bhūmau vikṣiptaṁ sainyam āpadi śakyam āvāhayitum, na mitra.vikṣiptaṁ viprakṛṣṭa.deśa.kālatvāt //*  
*08.5.16/ dūṣya.yukta.duṣṭa.pārṣṇi.grāhayor dūṣya.yuktam āpta.puruṣa.adhiṣṭhitam asaṁhataṁ yudhyeta, na duṣṭa.pārṣṇi.grāhaṁ pṛṣṭha.abhighāta.trastam //*  
*08.5.17/ śūnya.mūla.asvāmi.saṁhatayoḥ śūnya.mūlaṁ kṛta.paura.jānapada.ārakṣaṁ sarva.saṁdohena yudhyeta, na^asvāmi.saṁhataṁ rāja.senā.pati.hīnam //*  
*08.5.18/ bhinna.kūṭa.andhayor bhinna.kūṭam anya.adhiṣṭhitaṁ yudhyeta, na^andham adeśikaṁ - iti //*  
*08.5.19ab/ doṣa.śuddhir bala.āvāpaḥ sattra.sthāna.atisaṁhitam /*  
*08.5.19cd/ saṁdhiś ca^uttara.pakṣasya bala.vyasana.sādhanam //*  
*08.5.20ab/ rakṣet sva.daṇḍaṁ vyasane śatrubhyo nityam utthitaḥ /*  
*08.5.20cd/ prahared daṇḍa.randhreṣu śatrūṇāṁ nityam utthitaḥ //*  
*08.5.21ab/ yato nimittaṁ vyasanaṁ prakṛtīnām avāpnuyāt /*  
*08.5.21cd/ prāg eva pratikurvīta tan nimittam atandritaḥ /*  
*08.5.22ab/ abhiyātaṁ svayaṁ mitraṁ sambhūya^anya.vaśena vā //*  
*08.5.22cd/ parityaktam aśaktyā vā lobhena praṇayena vā /*  
*08.5.23ab/ vikrītam abhiyuñjāne saṁgrāme vā^apavartinā //*  
*08.5.23cd/ dvaidhī.bhāvena vā^amitraṁ yāsyatā vā^anyam anyataḥ /*  
*08.5.24ab/ pṛthag vā saha.yāne vā viśvāsena^atisaṁhitam //*  
*08.5.24cd/ bhaya.avamāna.ālasyair vā vyasanān na pramokṣitam /*  
*08.5.25ab/ avaruddhaṁ sva.bhūmibhyaḥ samīpād vā bhayād gatam //*  
*08.5.25cd/ ācchedanād adānād vā dattvā vā^apy avamānitam /*  
*08.5.26ab/ atyāhāritam arthaṁ vā svayaṁ para.mukhena vā //*  
*08.5.26cd/ atibhāre niyuktaṁ vā bhaṅktvā param upasthitam /*  
*08.5.27ab/ upekṣitam aśaktyā vā prārthayitvā virodhitam //*  
*08.5.27cd/ kṛcchreṇa sādhyate mitraṁ siddhaṁ ca^āśu virajyati /*  
*08.5.28ab/ kṛta.prayāsaṁ mānyaṁ vā mohān mitram amānitam /*  
*08.5.28cd/ mānitaṁ vā na sadṛśaṁ śaktito vā nivāritam //*  
*08.5.29ab/ mitra.upaghāta.trastaṁ vā śaṅkitaṁ vā^ari.saṁhitāt /*  
*08.5.29cd/ dūṣyair vā bhedituṁ mitraṁ sādhyaṁ siddhaṁ ca tiṣṭhati //*  
*08.5.30ab/ tasmān na^utpādayed enān doṣān mitra.upaghātakān /*  
*08.5.30cd/ utpannān vā praśamayed guṇair doṣa.upaghātibhiḥ //E*  
*(śakti.deśa.kāla.bala.abala.jnānam - yātrā.kālāh)*

### 9

*09.1.01/ vijigīṣur ātmanaḥ parasya ca bala.abalaṁ śakti.deśa.kāla.yātrā.kāla.bala.samuddāna.kāla.paścāt.kopa.kṣaya.vyaya.lābha.āpadāṁ jñātvā viśiṣṭa.balo yāyāt, anyathā^āsīta //*  
*09.1.02/ "utsāha.prabhāvayor utsāhaḥ śreyān //*  
*09.1.03/ svayaṁ hi rājā śūro balavān arogaḥ kṛta.astro daṇḍa.dvitīyo^api śaktaḥ prabhāvavantaṁ rājānaṁ jetum //*  
*09.1.04/ alpo^api ca^asya daṇḍas tejasā kṛtya.karo bhavati //*  
*09.1.05/ nirutsāhas tu prabhāvavān rājā vikrama.abhipanno naśyati" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*09.1.06/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*09.1.07/ prabhāvavān utsāhavantaṁ rājānaṁ prabhāvena^atisaṁdhatte tad.viśiṣṭam anyaṁ rājānam āvāhya bhṛtvā krītvā pravīra.puruṣān //*  
*09.1.08/ prabhūta.prabhāva.haya.hasti.ratha.upakaraṇa.sampannaś ca^asya daṇḍaḥ sarvatra^apratihataś carati //*  
*09.1.09/ utsāhavataś ca prabhāvavanto jitvā krītvā ca striyo bālāḥ paṅgavo^andhāś ca pṛthivīṁ jigyur iti //*  
*09.1.10/ "prabhāva.mantrayoḥ prabhāvaḥ śreyān //*  
*09.1.11/ mantra.śakti.sampanno hi vandhya.buddhir aprabhāvo bhavati //*  
*09.1.12/ mantra.karma ca^asya niścitam aprabhāvo garbha.dhānyam avṛṣṭir iva^upahanti" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*09.1.13/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*09.1.14/ mantra.śaktiḥ śreyasī //*  
*09.1.15/ prajñā.śāstra.cakṣur hi rājā^alpena^api prayatnena mantram ādhātuṁ śaktaḥ parān utsāha.prabhāvavataś ca sāma.ādibhir yoga.upaniṣadbhyāṁ ca^atisaṁdhātum //*  
*09.1.16/ evam utsāha.prabhāva.mantra.śaktīnām uttara.uttara.adhiko^atisaṁdhatte //*  
*09.1.17/ deśaḥ pṛthivī //*  
*09.1.18/ tasyāṁ himavat.samudra.antaram udīcīnaṁ yojana.sahasra.parimāṇaṁ tiryak cakra.varti.kṣetram //*  
*09.1.19/ tatra^araṇyo grāmyaḥ parvata audako bhaumaḥ samo viṣama iti viśeṣāḥ //*  
*09.1.20/ teṣu yathā.sva.bala.vṛddhi.karaṁ karma prayuñjīta //*  
*09.1.21/ yatra.ātmanaḥ sainya.vyāyāmānāṁ bhūmiḥ, abhūmiḥ parasya, sa uttamo deśaḥ, viparīto^adhamaḥ, sādhāraṇo madhyamaḥ //*  
*09.1.22/ kālaḥ śīta.uṣṇa.varṣa.ātmā //*  
*09.1.23/ tasya rātrir ahaḥ pakṣo māsa ṛtur ayanaṁ saṁvatsaro yugam iti viśeṣāḥ //*  
*09.1.24/ teṣu yathā.sva.bala.vṛddhi.karaṁ karma.prayuñjīta //*  
*09.1.25/ yatra.ātmanaḥ sainya.vyāyāmānām ṛtuḥ anṛtuḥ parasya, sa uttamaḥ kālaḥ, viparīto^adhamaḥ, sādhāraṇo madhyamaḥ //*  
*09.1.26/ "śakti.deśa.kālānāṁ tu śaktiḥ śreyasī" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*09.1.27/ śaktimān hi nimna.sthalavato deśasya śīta.uṣṇa.varṣavataś ca kālasya śaktaḥ pratīkāre bhavati //*  
*09.1.28/ "deśaḥ śreyān" ity eke //*  
*09.1.29/ "sthala.gato hi śvā nakraṁ vikarṣati, nimna.gato nakraḥ śvānam" iti //*  
*09.1.30/ "kālaḥ śreyān" ity eke //*  
*09.1.31/ "divā kākaḥ kauśikaṁ hanti, rātrau kauśikaḥ kākam" iti //*  
*09.1.32/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*09.1.33/ paraspara.sādhakā hi śakti.deśa.kālāḥ //*  
*09.1.34/ tair abhyuccitas tṛtīyaṁ caturthaṁ vā daṇḍasya^aṁśaṁ mūle pārṣṇyāṁ pratyanta.aṭavīṣu ca rakṣā vidhāya kārya.sādhana.sahaṁ kośa.daṇḍaṁ ca^ādāya kṣīṇa.purāṇa.bhaktam agṛhīta.nava.bhaktam asaṁskṛta.durgama.mitraṁ vārṣikaṁ ca^asya sasyaṁ haimanaṁ ca muṣṭim upahantuṁ mārgaśīrṣīṁ yātrāṁ yāyāt //*  
*09.1.35/ haimānaṁ ca^asya sasyaṁ vāsantikaṁ ca muṣṭim upahantuṁ caitrīṁ yātrāṁ yāyāt //*  
*09.1.36/ kṣīṇa.kṛṇa.kāṣṭha.udakam asaṁskṛta.durgama.mitraṁ vāsantikaṁ ca^asya sasyaṁ vārṣikīṁ ca muṣṭim upahantuṁ jyeṣṭhāmūlīyāṁ yātrāṁ yāyāt //*  
*09.1.37/ atyuṣṇam alpa.yavasa.indhana.udakaṁ vā deśaṁ hemante yāyāt //*  
*09.1.38/ tuṣāra.durdinam agādha.nimna.prāyaṁ gahana.tṛṇa.vṛkṣaṁ vā deśaṁ grīṣme yāyāt //*  
*09.1.39/ sva.sainya.vyāyāma.yogyaṁ parasya^ayogyaṁ varṣati yāyāt //*  
*09.1.40/ mārgaśīrṣīṁ taiṣīṁ ca^antareṇa dīrgha.kālāṁ yātrāṁ yāyāt, caitrīṁ vaiśākhīṁ ca^antareṇa madhyama.kālām, jyeṣṭhāmūlīyām āṣāḍhīṁ ca^antareṇa hrasva.kālām, upoṣiṣyan vyasane caturthīm //*  
*09.1.41/ vyasana.abhiyānaṁ vigṛhya.yāne vyākhyātam //*  
*09.1.42/ prāyaśaś ca^ācāryāḥ "para.vyasane yātavyam" ity upadiśanti //*  
*09.1.43/ śakty.udaye yātavyam anaikānntikatvād vyasanānām iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*09.1.44/ yadā vā prayātaḥ karśayitum ucchetuṁ vā śaknuyād amitraṁ tadā yāyāt //*  
*09.1.45/ atyuṣṇa.upakṣīṇe kāle hasti.bala.prāyo yāyāt //*  
*09.1.46/ hastino hy antaḥ.svedāḥ kuṣṭhino bhavanti //*  
*09.1.47/ anavagāhamānās toyam apibantaś ca^antar.avakṣārāc ca^andhī.bhavanti //*  
*09.1.48/ tasmāt prabhūta.udake deśe varṣati ca hasti.bala.prāyo yāyāt //*  
*09.1.49/ viparyaye khara.uṣṭra.aśva.bala.prāyo deśam alpa.varṣa.paṅkam //*  
*09.1.50/ varṣati maru.prāyaṁ catur.aṅga.balo yāyāt //*  
*09.1.51/ sama.viṣama.nimna.sthala.hrasva.dīrgha.vaśena vā^adhvano yātrāṁ vibhajet //*  
*09.1.52ab/ sarvā vā hrasva.kālāḥ syur yātavyāḥ kārya.lāghavāt /*  
*09.1.52cd/ dīrghāḥ kārya.gurutvād vā varṣā.vāsaḥ paratra ca //E*  
*(bala.upādāna.kālāh -samnāha.guṇāh -pratibala.karma)*  
*09.2.01/ maula.bhṛtaka.śreṇī.mitra.amitra.aṭavī.balānāṁ samuddāna.kālāḥ //*  
*09.2.02/ mūla.rakṣaṇād atiriktaṁ maula.balam, atyāvāpa.yuktā vā maulā mūle vikurvīran, bahula.anurakta.maula.balaḥ sāra.balo vā pratiyoddhā, vyāyāmena yoddhavyam, prakṛṣṭe^adhvani kāle vā kṣaya.vyaya.sahatvān maulānām, bahula.anurakta.sampāte ca yātavyasya^upajāpa.bhayād anya.sainyānāṁ bhṛta.ādīnām aviśvāse, bala.kṣaye vā sarva.sainyānāṁ - iti maula.bala.kālaḥ*  
*//*  
*09.2.03/ "prabhūtaṁ me bhṛta.balam alpaṁ ca maula.balamṇ" "parasya^alpaṁ viraktaṁ vā maula.balam, phalgu.prāyam asāraṁ vā bhṛta.sainyamṇ" "mantreṇa yoddhavyam alpa.vyāyāmenaṇ" "hrasvo deśaḥ kālo vā tanu.kṣaya.vyayahṇ" "alpa.āvāpaṁ śānta.upajāpaṁ viśvastaṁ vā me sainyamṇ" "parasya^alpaḥ prasāro hantavyahṇ" - iti bhṛta.bala.kālaḥ //*  
*09.2.04/ "prabhūtaṁ me śreṇī.balam, śakyaṁ mūle yātrāyāṁ ca^ādhātumṇ" hrasvaḥ pravāsaḥ, śreṇī.bala.prāyaḥ pratiyoddhā mantra.vyāyāmābhyāṁ pratiyoddhu.kāmaḥ, daṇḍa.bala.vyavahāraḥ - iti śreṇī.bala.kālaḥ //*  
*09.2.05/ "prabhūtaṁ me mitra.balaṁ śakyaṁ mūle yātrāyāṁ ca^ādhātumṇ" "alpaḥ pravāso mantra.yuddhāc ca bhūyo vyāyāma.yuddhamṇ" "mitra.balena vā pūrvam aṭavīṁ nagara.sthānam āsāraṁ vā yodhayitvā paścāt sva.balena yoddhayiṣyāmiṇ" "mitra.sādhāraṇaṁ vā me kāryamṇ" "mitra.āyattā vā me kārya.siddhihṇ" "āsannam anugrāhyaṁ vā me mitramṇ" "atyāvāpaṁ vā^asya sādayiṣyāmi"*  
*- iti mitra.bala.kālaḥ //*  
*09.2.06/ "prabhūtaṁ me śatru.balam, śatru.balena yodhayiṣyāmi nagara.sthānam aṭavīṁ vā, tatra me śva.varāhayoḥ kalahe caṇḍālasya^iva^anyatara.siddhir bhaviṣyatiṇ" "āsārāṇām aṭavīnāṁ vā kaṇṭaka.mardanam etat kariṣyāmiṇ" - atyupacitaṁ vā kopa.bhayān nityam āsannam ari.balaṁ vāsayed, anyatra.abhyantara.kopa.śaṅkāyāḥ - śatru.yuddha.avara.yuddha.kālaś ca*  
*- ity amitra.bala.kālaḥ //*  
*09.2.07/ tena^aṭavī.bala.kālo vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*09.2.08/ mārga.ādeśikam, para.bhūmi.yogyam, ari.yuddha.pratilomam, aṭavī.bala.prāyaḥ śatrur vā, "bilvaṁ bilvena hanyatāmṇ" alpaḥ prasāro hantavyaḥ - ity aṭavī.bala.kālaḥ //*  
*09.2.09/ sainyam anekam anekastham uktam anuktaṁ vā vilopa.arthaṁ yad uttiṣṭhati tad autsāhikaṁ - abhakta.vetanaṁ vilopa.viṣṭi.pratāpa.karaṁ bhedyaṁ pareṣām, abhedyaṁ tulya.deśa.jāti.śilpa.prāyaṁ saṁhataṁ mahat // \[iti bala.upādāna.kālāh\]*  
*09.2.10/ teṣāṁ kupya.bhṛtam amitra.aṭavī.balaṁ vilopa.bhṛtaṁ vā kuryāt //*  
*09.2.11/ amitrasya vā bala.kāle pratyutpanne śatru.balam avagṛhṇīyāt, anyatra vā preṣayet, aphalaṁ vā kuryāt, vikṣiptaṁ vā vāsayet, kāle vā^atikrānte visṛjet //*  
*09.2.12/ parasya ca^etad bala.samuddānaṁ vighātayet, ātmanaḥ sampādayet //*  
*09.2.13/ pūrvaṁ pūrvaṁ ca^eṣāṁ śreyaḥ samnāhayitum //*  
*09.2.14/ tad.bhāva.bhāvitvān nitya.satkāra.anugamāc ca maula.balaṁ bhṛta.balāt^śreyaḥ //*  
*09.2.15/ nitya.anantaraṁ kṣipra.utthāyi vaśyaṁ va bhṛta.balaṁ śreṇī.balāt^śreyaḥ //*  
*09.2.16/ jānapadam eka.artha.upagataṁ tulya.saṁgharṣa.amarṣa.siddhi.lābhaṁ ca śreṇī.balaṁ mitra.balāt^śreyaḥ //*  
*09.2.17/ aparimita.deśa.kālam eka.artha.upagamāc ca mitra.balam amitra.balāt^śreyaḥ //*  
*09.2.18/ ārya.adhiṣṭhitam amitra.balam aṭavī.balāt^śreyaḥ //*  
*09.2.19/ tad ubhayaṁ vilopa.artham //*  
*09.2.20/ avilope vyasane ca tābhyām ahi.bhayaṁ syāt //*  
*09.2.21/ "brāhmaṇa.kṣatriya.vaiśya.śūdra.sainyānāṁ tejaḥ.prādhānyāt pūrvaṁ pūrvaṁ śreyaḥ samnāhayitum" ity ācāryāḥ //*  
*09.2.22/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*09.2.23/ praṇipātena brāhmaṇa.balaṁ paro^abhihārayet //*  
*09.2.24/ praharaṇa.vidyā.vinītaṁ tu kṣatriya.balaṁ śreyaḥ, bahula.sāraṁ vā vaiśya.śūdra.balam iti //*  
*09.2.25/ tasmād evaṁ.balaḥ paraḥ, tasya^etat pratibalam iti bala.samuddānaṁ kuryāt //*  
*09.2.26/ hasti.yantra.śakaṭa.garbha.kunta.prāsa.hāṭaka.veṇu.śalyavad hasti.balasya pratibalam //*  
*09.2.27/ tad eva pāṣāṇa.laguḍa.āvaraṇa.aṅkuśa.kaca.grahaṇī.prāyaṁ ratha.balasya pratibalam //*  
*09.2.28/ tad eva^aśvānāṁ pratibalam, varmiṇo vā hastino^aśvā vā varmiṇaḥ //*  
*09.2.29/ kavacino rathā āvaraṇinaḥ pattayaś ca catur.aṅga.balasya pratibalam //*  
*09.2.30ab/ evaṁ bala.samuddānaṁ para.sainya.nivāraṇam /*  
*09.2.30cd/ vibhavena sva.sainyānāṁ kuryād aṅga.vikalpaśaḥ //E*  
*(paścāt.kopa.cintā - bāhya.abhyantara.prakṛti.kopa.pratīkārah)*  
*09.3.01/ alpaḥ paścāt.kopo mahān purastāl.lābha iti alpaḥ paścāt.kopo garīyān //*  
*09.3.02/ alpaṁ paścāt.kopaṁ prayātassya dūṣya.amitra.āṭavikā hi sarvataḥ samedhayanti, prakṛti.kopo vā //*  
*09.3.03/ labdham api ca mahāntaṁ purastāl.lāham evaṁ.bhūte bhṛtya.mitra.kṣaya.vyayā grasante //*  
*09.3.04/ tasmāt sahasra.ekīyaḥ purastāl.lābhasya^ayogaḥ śata.ekīyo vā paścāt.kopa iti na yāyāt //*  
*09.3.05/ sūcī.mukhā hy anarthā iti loka.pravādaḥ //*  
*09.3.06/ paścāt.kope sāma.dāna.bheda.daṇḍān prayuñjīta //*  
*09.3.07/ purastāl.lābhe senā.patiṁ kumāraṁ vā daṇḍa.cāriṇaṁ kurvīta //*  
*09.3.08/ balavān vā rājā paścāt.kopa.avagraha.samarthaḥ purastāl.lābham ādātuṁ yāyāt //*  
*09.3.09/ abhyantara.kopa.śaṅkāyāṁ śaṅkitān ādāya yāyāt, bāhya.kopa.śaṅkāyāṁ vā putra.dāram eṣām //*  
*09.3.10/ abhyantara.avagrahaṁ kṛtvā śūnya.pālam aneka.bala.vargam aneka.mukhyaṁ ca sthāpayitvā yāyāt, na vā yāyāt //*  
*09.3.11/ abhyantara.kopo bāhya.kopāt pāpīyān ity uktaṁ purastāt //*  
*09.3.12/ mantra.purohita.senā.pati.yuva.rājānām anyatama.kopo^abhyantara.kopaḥ //*  
*09.3.13/ tam ātma.doṣa.tyāgena para.śakty.aparādha.vaśena vā sādhayet //*  
*09.3.14/ mahā.aparādhe^api purohite samrodhanam avasrāvaṇaṁ vā siddhiḥ, yuva.rāje samrodhanaṁ nigraho vā guṇavaty anyasmin sati putre //*  
*09.3.15/ putraṁ bhrātaram anyaṁ vā kulyaṁ rāja.grāhiṇam utsāhena sādhayet, utsāha.abbhāve gṛhīta.anuvartana.saṁdhi.karmabhyām ari.saṁdhāna.bhayāt //*  
*09.3.16/ anyebhyas tad.vidhebhyo vā bhūmi.dānair viśvāsayed enam //*  
*09.3.17/ tad.viśiṣṭaṁ svayaṁ.grāhaṁ daṇḍaṁ vā preṣayet, sāmanta.āṭavikān vā, tair vigṛhītam atisaṁdadhyāt //*  
*09.3.18/ aparuddha.ādānaṁ pāragrāmikaṁ vā yogam ātiṣṭhet //*  
*09.3.19/ etena mantra.senā.patī vyākhyātau //*  
*09.3.20/ mantry.ādi.varjānām antar.amātyānām anyatama.kopo^antar.amātya.kopaḥ //*  
*09.3.21/ tatra^api yathā.arham upāyān prayuñjīta //*  
*09.3.22/ rāṣṭra.mukhya.anta.pāla.āṭavika.daṇḍa.upanatānām anyatama.kopo bāhya.kopaḥ //*  
*09.3.23/ tam anyonyena^avagrāhayet //*  
*09.3.24/ atidurga.pratiṣṭabdhaṁ vā sāmanta.āṭavika.tat.kulīna.aparuddhānām anyatamena^avagrāhayet //*  
*09.3.25/ mitreṇa^upagrāhayed vā yathā na^amitraṁ gacchet //*  
*09.3.26/ amitrād vā sattrī bhedayed enaṁ - "ayaṁ tvā yoga.puruṣaṁ manyamāno bhartary eva vikramayiṣyati, avāpta.artho daṇḍa.cāriṇam amitra.āṭavikeṣu kṛcchre vā prayāse yokṣyati, viputra.dāram ante vā vāsayiṣyati //*  
*09.3.27/ pratihata.vikramaṁ tvāṁ bhartary paṇyaṁ kariṣyati, tvayā vā saṁdhiṁ kṛtvā bhartāram eva prasādayiṣyati //*  
*09.3.28/ mitram upakṛṣṭaṁ vā^asya gaccha" iti //*  
*09.3.29/ pratipannam iṣṭa.abhiprāyaiḥ pūjayet //*  
*09.3.30/ apratipannasya saṁśrayaṁ bhedayed "asau te yoga.puruṣaḥ praṇihitaḥ" iti //*  
*09.3.31/ sattrī ca^enam abhityakta.śāsanair ghātayet, gūḍha.puruṣair vā //*  
*09.3.32/ saha.prasthāyino vā^asya pravīra.puruṣān yathā.abhiprāya.karaṇena^āvāhayet //*  
*09.3.33/ tena praṇihitān sattrī brūyāt //*  
*09.3.34/ iti siddhiḥ //*  
*09.3.35/ parasya ca^enān kopān utthāpayet, ātmanaś ca śamayet //*  
*09.3.36/ yaḥ kopaṁ kartuṁ śamayituṁ vā śaktas tatra^upajāpaḥ kāryaḥ //*  
*09.3.37/ yaḥ satya.saṁdhaḥ śaktaḥ karmaṇi phala.avāptau ca^anugrahītuṁ vinipāte ca trātuṁ tatra pratijāpaḥ kāryaḥ, tarkayitavyaś ca kalyāṇa.buddhir uta^aho śaṭha iti //*  
*09.3.38/ śaṭho hi bāhyo^abhyantaram evam upajapati - "bhartāraṁ cedd hatvā māṁ pratipādayiṣyati śatru.vadho bhūmi.lābhaś ca me dvividho lābho bhaviṣyati, atha vā śatrur enam āhaniṣyati^iti hata.bandhu.pakṣas tulya.doṣa.daṇḍena^udvignaś ca me bhūyān akṛtya.pakṣo bhaviṣyati, tad.vidhe vā^anyasminn api śaṅkito bhaviṣyati, anyam anyaṁ ca^asya mukhyam*  
*abhityakta.śāsanena ghātayiṣyāmi" iti //*  
*09.3.39/ abhyantaro vā śaṭho bāhyam evam upajapati - "kośam asya hariṣyāmi, daṇḍaṁ vā^asya haniṣyāmi, duṣṭaṁ vā bhartāram anena ghātayiṣyāmi, pratipannaṁ bāhyam amitra.āṭavikeṣu vikramayiṣyāmi "cakram asya sajyatām, vairam asya prasajyatām, tataḥ sva.adhīno me bhaviṣyati, tato bhartāram eva prasādayiṣyāmi, svayaṁ vā rājyaṁ grahīṣyāmiṇ" baddhvā vā bāhya.bhūmiṁ*  
*bhartṛ.bhūmiṁ ca^ubhayam avāpsyāmi, viruddhaṁ vā^āvāhayitvā bāhyaṁ viśvastaṁ ghātayiṣyāmi, śūnyaṁ vā^asya mūlaṁ hariṣyāmi" iti //*  
*09.3.40/ kalyāṇa.buddhis tu saha.jīvy artham upajapati //*  
*09.3.41/ kalyāṇa.buddhinā saṁdadhīta, śaṭhaṁ "tathā" iti pratigṛhya^atisaṁdadhyāt - iti //*  
*09.3.42ab/ evam upalabhya - pare parebhyaḥ sve svebhyaḥ sve parebhyaḥ svataḥ pare /*  
*09.3.42cd/ rakṣyāḥ svebhyaḥ parebhyaś ca nityam ātmā vipaścitā //E*  
*(kṣaya.vyaya.lābha.viparimarśah)*  
*09.4.01/ yugya.puruṣa.apacayaḥ kṣayaḥ //*  
*09.4.02/ hiraṇya.dhānya.apacayo vyayaḥ //*  
*09.4.03/ tābhyāṁ bahu.guṇa.viśiṣṭe lābhe yāyāt //*  
*09.4.04/ ādeyaḥ pratyādeyaḥ prasādakaḥ prakopako hrasva.kālas tanu.kṣayo^alpa.vyayo mahān vṛddhy.udayaḥ kalyo dharmyaḥ purogaś ca^iti lābha.sampat //*  
*09.4.05/ suprāpya.anupālyaḥ pareṣām apratyādeya ity ādeyaḥ //*  
*09.4.06/ viparyaye pratyādeyaḥ //*  
*09.4.07/ tam ādadānas tatrastho vā vināśaṁ prāpnoti //*  
*09.4.08/ yadi vā paśyet "pratyādeyam ādāya kośa.daṇḍa.nicaya.rakṣā.vidhānāny avasrāvayiṣyāmi, khani.dravya.hasti.vana.setu.bandha.vaṇik.pathān uddhṛta.sārān kariṣyāmi, prakṛtīr asya karśayiṣyāmi, apavāhayiṣyāmi, āyogena^ārādhayiṣyāmi vā, tāḥ paraṁ pratiyogena kopayiṣyati, pratipakṣe vā^asya paṇyam enaṁ kariṣyāmi, mitram aparuddhaṁ vā^asya pratipādayiṣyāmi,*  
*mitrasya svasya vā deśasya pīḍām atrasthas taskarebhyaḥ parebhyaś ca pratikariṣyāmi, mitram āśrayaṁ vā^asya vaiguṇyaṁ grāhayiṣyāmi, tad amitra.viraktaṁ tat.kulīnaṁ pratipatsyate, satkṛtya vā^asmai bhūmiṁ dāsyāmi iti*  
*saṁhita.samutthitaṁ mitraṁ me cirāya bhaviṣyati" iti pratyādeyam api lābham ādadīta //*  
*09.4.09/ ity ādeya.pratyādeyau vyākhyātau //*  
*09.4.10/ adhārmikād dhārmikasya lābho labhyamānaḥ sveṣāṁ pareṣāṁ ca prasādako bhavati //*  
*09.4.11/ viparītaḥ prakopaka iti //*  
*09.4.12/ mantriṇām upadeśāl lābho^alabhyamānaḥ kopako bhavati "ayam asmābhiḥ kṣaya.vyayau grāhitaḥ" iti //*  
*09.4.13/ dūṣya.mantriṇām anādarāl lābho labhyamānaḥ kopako bhavati "siddha.artho^ayam asmān vināśayiṣyati" iti //*  
*09.4.14/ viparītaḥ prasādakaḥ //*  
*09.4.15/ iti prasādaka.kopakau vyākhyātau*  
*09.4.16/ gamana.mātra.sādhyatvād hrasva.kālah*  
*09.4.17/ mantra.sādhyatvāt tanu.kṣayaḥ //*  
*09.4.18/ bhakta.mātra.vyayatvād alpa.vyayah*  
*09.4.19/ tadātva.vaipulyān mahān //*  
*09.4.20/ artha.anubandhakatvād vṛddhy.udayaḥ //*  
*09.4.21/ nirābādhakatvāt kalyaḥ //*  
*09.4.22/ praśasta.upādānād dharmyaḥ //*  
*09.4.23/ sāmavāyikānām anirbandha.gāmitvāt purogaḥ - iti //*  
*09.4.24/ tulye lābhe deśa.kālau śakty.upāyau priya.apriyau java.ajavau sāmīpya.viprakarṣau tadātva.anubandhau sāratva.sātatye bāhulya.bāhu.guṇye ca vimṛśya bahu.guṇa.yuktaṁ lābham ādadīta //*  
*09.4.25/ lābha.vighnāḥ - kāmaḥ kopaḥ sādhvasaṁ kāruṇyaṁ hrīr anārya.bhāvo mānaḥ sānukrośatā para.loka.apekṣā dhārmikatvam atyāgitvaṁ dainyam asūyā hasta.gata.avamāno daurātmyam aviśvāso bhayam apratīkāraḥ śīta.uṣṇa.varṣāṇām ākṣamyaṁ maṅgala.tithi.nakṣatra.iṣṭitvam iti //*  
*09.4.26ab/ nakṣatram ati pṛcchantaṁ bālam artho^ativartate /*  
*09.4.26cd/ artho hy arthasya nakṣatraṁ kiṁ kariṣyanti tārakāḥ //*  
*09.4.27ab/ na^adhanāḥ prāpnuvanty arthān narā yatna.śatair api /*  
*09.4.27cd/ arthair arthā prabadhyante gajāḥ prajigajair iva //E*  
*(bāhya.abhyantarāś ca^āpadah)*  
*09.5.01/ saṁdhy.ādīnām ayathā.uddeśa.avasthāpanam apanayaḥ //*  
*09.5.02/ tasmād āpadaḥ sambhavanti //*  
*09.5.03/ bāhya.utpattir abhyantara.pratijāpā, abhyantara.utpattir bāhya.pratijāpā, bāhya.utpattir bāhya.pratijāpā, abhyantara.utpattir abhyantara.pratijāpā - ity āpadaḥ //*  
*09.5.04/ yatra bāhyā abhyantara.anupajapanti, abhyantarā vā bāhyān, tatra.ubhaya.yoge pratijapataḥ siddhir viśeṣavatī //*  
*09.5.05/ suvyājā hi pratijapitāro bhavanti, na^upajapitāraḥ //*  
*09.5.06/ teṣu praśānteṣu na^anyān^śaknuyur upajapitum upajapitāraḥ //*  
*09.5.07/ kṛcchra.upajāpā hi bāhyānām abhyantarās teṣām itare vā //*  
*09.5.08/ mahataś ca prayatnasya vadhaḥ pareṣām, artha.anubandhaś ca^ātmana iti //*  
*09.5.09/ abhyantareṣu pratijapatsu sāma.dāne prayuñjīta //*  
*09.5.10/ sthāna.māna.karma sāntvam //*  
*09.5.11/ anugraha.parihārau karmasv āyogo vā dānam //*  
*09.5.12/ bāhyeṣu pratijapatsu bheda.daṇḍau prayuñjīta //*  
*09.5.13/ sattriṇo mitra.vyañjanā vā bāhyānāṁ cāram eṣāṁ brūyuḥ "ayaṁ vo rājā dūṣya.vyañjanair atisaṁdhātu.kāmaḥ, budhyadhvam" iti //*  
*09.5.14/ dūṣyeṣu vā dūṣya.vyañjanāḥ praṇihitā dūṣyān bāhyair bhedayeyuḥ, bāhyān vā dūṣyaiḥ //*  
*09.5.15/ dūṣyān anupraviṣṭā vā tīkṣṇāḥ śastra.rasābhyāṁ hanyuḥ //*  
*09.5.16/ āhūya vā bāhyān ghātayeyuḥ //*  
*09.5.17/ yatra bāhyā bāhyān upajapanti, abhyantarān abhyantarā vā, tatra^ekānta.yoga upajapituḥ siddhir viśeṣavatī //*  
*09.5.18/ doṣa.śuddhau hi dūṣyā na vidyante //*  
*09.5.19/ dūṣya.śuddhau hi doṣaḥ punar anyān dūṣayati //*  
*09.5.20/ tasmād bāhyeṣu^upajapatsu bheda.daṇḍau prayuñjīta //*  
*09.5.21/ sattriṇo mitra.vyañjanā vā brūyuḥ "ayaṁ vo rājā svayam ādātu.kāmaḥ, vigṛhītāḥ sthānena rājñā, budhyadhvam" iti //*  
*09.5.22/ pratijapitur vā dūta.daṇḍān anupraviṣṭās tīkṣṇāḥ śastra.rasa.ādibhir eṣāṁ chidreṣu prahareyuḥ //*  
*09.5.23/ tataḥ sattriṇaḥ pratijapitāram abhiśaṁseyuḥ //*  
*09.5.24/ abhyantarān abhyantareṣu^upajapatsu yathā.arham upāyaṁ prayuñjīta //*  
*09.5.25/ tuṣṭa.liṅgam atuṣṭaṁ viparītaṁ vā sāma prayuñjīta //*  
*09.5.26/ śauca.sāmarthya.apadeśena vyasana.abhyudaya.avekṣaṇena vā pratipūjanam iti dānam //*  
*09.5.27/ mitra.vyañjano vā brūyād etān "citta.jñāna.artham upadhāsyati vo rājā, tad asya^ākhyātavyam iti //*  
*09.5.28/ parasparād vā bhedayed enān "asau ca^asau ca vo rājany evam upajapati" - iti bhedaḥ //*  
*09.5.29/ dāṇḍakarmikavac ca daṇḍaḥ //*  
*09.5.30/ etāsāṁ catasṛṇām āpadām abhyantarām eva pūrvaṁ sādhayet //*  
*09.5.31/ ahi.bhayād abhyantara.kopo bāhya.kopāt pāpīyān ity uktaṁ purastād //*  
*09.5.32ab/ pūrvāṁ pūrvāṁ vijānīyāl laghvīm āpadam āpadām /*  
*09.5.32cd/ utthitāṁ balavadbhyo vā gurvīṁ laghvīṁ viparyaye //E*  
*(dūṣya.śatru.samyuktāh "āpadah")*  
*09.6.01/ dūṣyebhyaḥ śatrubhyaś ca dvividhā śuddhā //*  
*09.6.02/ dūṣya.śuddhāyāṁ paureṣu jānapadeṣu vā daṇḍa.varjān upāyān prayuñjīta //*  
*09.6.03/ daṇḍo hi mahā.jane kṣeptum aśakyaḥ //*  
*09.6.04/ kṣipto vā taṁ ca^arthaṁ na kuryāt, anyaṁ ca^anartham utpādayet //*  
*09.6.05/ mukhyeṣu tv eṣāṁ dāṇḍa.karmikavac ceṣṭeta //*  
*09.6.06/ śatru.śuddhāyāṁ yataḥ śatruḥ pradhānaḥ kāryo vā tataḥ sāma.ādibhiḥ siddhiṁ lipseta //*  
*09.6.07/ svāminy āyattā pradhāna.siddhiḥ, mantriṣv āyattā^āyatta.siddhiḥ, ubhaya.āyattā pradhāna.āyatta.siddhiḥ //*  
*09.6.08/ dūṣya.adūṣyāṇām āmiśritatvād āmiśrā //*  
*09.6.09/ āmiśrāyām adūṣyataḥ siddhiḥ //*  
*09.6.10/ ālambana.abhāve hy ālambitā na vidyante //*  
*09.6.11/ mitra.amitrāṇām ekī.bhāvāt para.miśrā //*  
*09.6.12/ para.miśrāyāṁ mitrataḥ siddhiḥ //*  
*09.6.13/ sukaro hi mitreṇa saṁdhiḥ, na^amitreṇa^iti //*  
*09.6.14/ mitraṁ cen na saṁdhim icched abhīkṣṇam upajapet //*  
*09.6.15/ tataḥ sattribhir amitrād bhedayitvā mitraṁ labheta //*  
*09.6.16/ mitra.saṁghasya vā yo^anta.sthāyī taṁ labheta //*  
*09.6.17/ anta.sthāyini labdhe madhya.sthāyino bhidyante //*  
*09.6.18/ madhya.sthāyinaṁ vā labheta //*  
*09.6.19/ madhya.sthāyini labdhe na^anta.sthāyinaḥ saṁhanyante //*  
*09.6.20/ yathā ca^eṣām āśraya.bhedas tān upāyān prayuñjīta //*  
*09.6.21/ dhārmikaṁ jāti.kula.śruta.vṛtta.stavena sambandhena pūrveṣāṁ traikālya.upakārān apakārābhyāṁ vā sāntvayet //*  
*09.6.22/ nivṛtta.utsāhaṁ vigraha.śrāntaṁ pratihata.upāyaṁ kṣaya.vyayābhyāṁ pravāsena ca^upataptaṁ śaucena^anyaṁ lipsamānam anyasmād vā śaṅkamānaṁ maitrī.pradhānaṁ vā kalyāṇa.buddhiṁ sāmnā sādhayet //*  
*09.6.23/ lubdhaṁ kṣīṇaṁ vā tapasvi.mukhya.avasthāpanā.pūrvaṁ dānena sādhayet //*  
*09.6.24/ tat pañca.vidhaṁ - deya.visargo gṛhīta.anuvartanam ātta.pratidānaṁ sva.dravya.dānam apūrvaṁ para.sveṣu svayaṁ.grāha.dānaṁ ca //*  
*09.6.25/ iti dāna.karma //*  
*09.6.26/ paraspara.dveṣa.vaira.bhūmi.haraṇa.śaṅkitam ato^anyatamena bhedayet //*  
*09.6.27/ bhīruṁ vā pratighātena "kṛta.saṁdhir eṣa tvayi karma.kariṣyati, mitram asya nisṛṣṭam, saṁdhau vā na^abhyantaraḥ" iti //*  
*09.6.28/ yasya vā sva.deśād anya.deśād vā paṇyāni paṇya.agāratayā^āgaccheyuḥ tāni asya "yātavyāl labdhāni" iti sattriṇaś cārayeyuḥ //*  
*09.6.29/ bahulī.bhūte śāsanam abhityaktena preṣayet "etat te paṇyaṁ paṇya.agāraṁ vā mayā te preṣitam, sāmavāyikeṣu vikramasva, apagaccha vā, tataḥ paṇa.śeṣam avāpsyasi" iti //*  
*09.6.30/ tataḥ sattriṇaḥ pareṣu grāhayeyuḥ "etad ari.pradattam" iti //*  
*09.6.31/ śatru.prakhyātaṁ vā paṇyam avijñātaṁ vijigīṣuṁ gacchet //*  
*09.6.32/ tad asya vaidehaka.vyañjanāḥ śatru.mukhyeṣu vikrīṇīran //*  
*09.6.33/ tataḥ sattriṇaḥ pareṣu grāhayeyuḥ "etat paṇyam ari.pradattam" iti //*  
*09.6.34/ mahā.aparādhān artha.mānābhyām upagṛhya vā śastra.rasa.agnibhir amitre praṇidadhyāt //*  
*09.6.35/ atha^ekam amātyaṁ niṣpātayet //*  
*09.6.36/ tasya putra.dāram upagṛhya rātrau hatam iti khyāpayet //*  
*09.6.37/ atha^amātyaḥ śatros tān eka.ekaśaḥ prarūpayet //*  
*09.6.38/ te ced yathā.uktaṁ kuryur na ca^enān grāhayet //*  
*09.6.39/ aśaktimato vā grāhayet //*  
*09.6.40/ āpta.bhāva.upagato mukhyād asya^ātmānaṁ rakṣaṇīyaṁ kathayet //*  
*09.6.41/ atha^amitra.śāsanaṁ mukhya.upaghātāya preṣitam ubhaya.vetano grāhayet //*  
*09.6.42/ utsāha.śaktimato vā preṣayet "amuṣya rājyaṁ gṛhāṇa, yathā.asthito naḥ saṁdhiḥ" iti //*  
*09.6.43/ tataḥ sattriṇaḥ pareṣu grāhayeyuḥ //*  
*09.6.44/ ekasya skandha.āvāraṁ vīvadham āsāraṁ vā ghātayeyuḥ //*  
*09.6.45/ itareṣu maitrīṁ bruvāṇāḥ "tvam eteṣāṁ ghātayitavyaḥ" ity upajapeyuḥ //*  
*09.6.46/ yasya vā pravīra.puruṣo hastī hayo vā mriyeta gūḍha.puruṣair hanyeta hriyeta vā sattriṇaḥ paraspara.upahataṁ brūyuḥ //*  
*09.6.47/ tataḥ śāsanam abhiśastasya preṣayet "bhūyaḥ kuru tataḥ paṇa.śeeṣam avāpsyasi" iti //*  
*09.6.48/ tad ubhaya.vetanā grāhayeyuḥ //*  
*09.6.49/ bhinneṣv anyatamaṁ labheta //*  
*09.6.50/ tena senā.pati.kumāra.daṇḍa.cāriṇo vyākhyātāḥ //*  
*09.6.51/ sāṁdhikaṁ ca bhedaṁ prayuñjīta //*  
*09.6.52/ iti bheda.karma //*  
*09.6.53/ tīkṣṇam utsāhinaṁ vyasaninaṁ sthita.śatruṁ vā gūḍha.puruṣāḥ śastra.agni.rasa.ādibhiḥ sādhayeyuḥ, saukaryato vā teṣām anyatamaḥ //*  
*09.6.54/ tīkṣṇo hy ekaḥ śastra.rasa.agnibhiḥ sādhayet //*  
*09.6.55/ ayaṁ sarva.saṁdoha.karma viśiṣṭaṁ vā karoti //*  
*09.6.56/ ity upāya.catur.vargaḥ //*  
*09.6.57/ pūrvaḥ pūrvaś ca^asya laghiṣṭhaḥ //*  
*09.6.58/ sāntvam eka.guṇam //*  
*09.6.59/ dānaṁ dvi.guṇaṁ sāntva.pūrvam //*  
*09.6.60/ bhedas tri.guṇaḥ sāntva.dāna.pūrvaḥ //*  
*09.6.61/ daṇḍaś catur.guṇaḥ sāntva.dāna.bheda.pūrvaḥ //*  
*09.6.62/ ity abhiyuñjāneṣu^uktam //*  
*09.6.63/ sva.bhūmiṣṭheṣu tu ta eva^upāyāḥ //*  
*09.6.64/ viśeṣas tu //*  
*09.6.65/ sva.bhūmiṣṭhānām anyatamasya paṇya.agārair abhijñātān dūta.mukhyān abhīkṣṇaṁ preṣayet //*  
*09.6.66/ ta enaṁ saṁdhau para.hiṁsāyāṁ vā yojayeyuḥ //*  
*09.6.67/ apratipadyamānaṁ "kṛto naḥ saṁdhiḥ" ity āvedayeyuḥ //*  
*09.6.68/ tam itareṣām ubhaya.vetanāḥ saṁkrāmayeyuḥ "ayaṁ vo rājā duṣṭaḥ" iti //*  
*09.6.69/ yasya vā yasmād bhayaṁ vairaṁ dveṣo vā taṁ tasmād bhedayeyuḥ "ayaṁ te śatruṇā saṁdhatte, purā tvām atisaṁdhatte, kṣiprataraṁ saṁdhīyasva, nigrahe ca^asya prayatasva" iti //*  
*09.6.70/ āvāha.vivāhābhyāṁ vā kṛtvā samyogam asamyuktān bhedayet //*  
*09.6.71/ sāmanta.āṭavika.tat.kulīna.aparuddhaiś ca^eṣāṁ rājyāni ghātayet, sārtha.vraja.aṭavīr vā, daṇḍaṁ vā^abhisṛtam //*  
*09.6.72/ paraspara.apāśrayāś ca^eṣāṁ jāti.saṁghāś chidreṣu prahareyuḥ, gūḍhāś ca^agni.rasa.śastreṇa //*  
*09.6.73ab/ vītaṁsa.gilavac ca^arīn yogair ācaritaiḥ śaṭhaḥ /*  
*09.6.73cd/ ghātayet para.miśrāyāṁ viśvāsena^āmiṣeṇa ca //E*  
*(artha.anartha.samśaya.yuktāh "āpadah" - tāsām upāya.vikalpajāh siddhayah)*  
*09.7.01/ kāma.ādir utsekaḥ svāḥ prakṛtīḥ kopayati, apanayo bāhyāḥ //*  
*09.7.02/ tad ubhayam āsurī vṛttiḥ //*  
*09.7.03/ sva.jana.vikāraḥ kopaḥ //*  
*09.7.04/ para.vṛddhi.hetuṣu āpad.artho^anarthaḥ saṁśaya iti //*  
*09.7.05/ yo^arthaḥ śatru.vṛddhim aprāptaḥ karoti, prāptaḥ pratyādeyaḥ pareṣāṁ bhavati, prāpyamāṇo vā kṣaya.vyaya.udayo bhavati, sa bhavaty āpad.arthaḥ //*  
*09.7.06/ yathā sāmantānām āmiṣa.bhūtaḥ sāmanta.vyasanajo lābhaḥ, śatru.prārthito vā sva.bhāva.adhigamyo lābhaḥ, paścāt kopena pārṣṇi.grāheṇa vā vigṛhītaḥ purastāl.lābhaḥ, mitra.ucchedena saṁdhi.vyatikrameṇa vā maṇḍala.viruddho lābhaḥ ity āpad.arthaḥ //*  
*09.7.07/ svataḥ parato vā bhaya.utpattir ity anarthaḥ //*  
*09.7.08/ tayoḥ artho na vā^iti, anartho na vā^iti, artho^anartha iti, anartho^artha iti saṁśayaḥ //*  
*09.7.09/ śatru.mitram utsāhayitum artho na vā^iti saṁśayaḥ //*  
*09.7.10/ śatru.balam artha.mānābhyām āvāhayitum anartho na vā^iti saṁśayaḥ //*  
*09.7.11/ balavat.sāmantāṁ bhūmim ādātum artho^anartha iti saṁśayaḥ //*  
*09.7.12/ jāyasā sambhūyayānam anartho^artha iti saṁśayaḥ //*  
*09.7.13/ teṣām artha.saṁśayam upagacchet //*  
*09.7.14/ artho^artha.anubandhaḥ, artho niranubandhaḥ, artho^anartha.anubandhaḥ, anartho^artha.anubandhaḥ, anartho niranubandhaḥ, anartho^anartha.anubandhaḥ ity anubandha.ṣaḍ.vargaḥ //*  
*09.7.15/ śatrum utpāṭya pārṣṇi.grāha.ādānam artho^anartha.anubandhaḥ //*  
*09.7.16/ udāsīnasya daṇḍa.anugrahaḥ phalena artho niranubandhaḥ //*  
*09.7.17/ parasya^antar.ucchedanam artho^anartha.anubandhaḥ //*  
*09.7.18/ śatru.prativeśasya^anugrahaḥ kośa.daṇḍābhyām anartho^anartha.anubandhaḥ //*  
*09.7.19/ hīna.śaktim utsāhya nivṛttir anartho niranubandhaḥ //*  
*09.7.20/ jyāyāṁsam utthāpya nivṛttir anartho^anartha.anubandhaḥ //*  
*09.7.21/ teṣāṁ pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyān upasamprāptum //*  
*09.7.22/ iti kārya.avasthāpanam //*  
*09.7.23/ samantato yugapad.artha.utpattiḥ samantato^artha.āpad bhavati //*  
*09.7.24/ sā^eva pārṣṇi.grāha.vigṛhītā samantato^artha.saṁśaya.āpad bhavati //*  
*09.7.25/ tayor mitra.ākranda.upagrahāt siddhiḥ //*  
*09.7.26/ samantataḥ śatrubhyo bhaya.utpattiḥ samantto^anartha.āpad bhavati //*  
*09.7.27/ sā^eva mitra.vigṛhītā samantato^anartha.saṁśaya.āpad bhavati //*  
*09.7.28/ tayoś cala.amitra.ākranda.upagrahāt siddhiḥ, para.miśra.apratīkāro vā //*  
*09.7.29/ ito lābha itarato lābha ity ubhayato^artha.āpad bhavati //*  
*09.7.30/ tasyāṁ samantato^arthāyāṁ ca lābha.guṇa.yuktam artham ādātuṁ yāyāt //*  
*09.7.31/ tulye lābha.guṇe pradhānam āsannam anatipātinam ūno vā yena bhavet tam ādātuṁ yāyāt //*  
*09.7.32/ ito^anartha itarato^anartha ity ubhayato^anartha.āpat //*  
*09.7.33/ tasyāṁ samantato^anarthāyāṁ ca mitrebhyaḥ siddhiṁ lipseta //*  
*09.7.34/ mitra.abhāve prakṛtīnāṁ laghīyasya^ekato^anarthāṁ sādhayet, ubhayato^anarthāṁ jyāyasyā, samantato^anarthāṁ mūlena pratikuryāt //*  
*09.7.35/ aśakye sarvam utsṛjya^apagacchet //*  
*09.7.36/ dṛṣṭā hi jīvataḥ punar.āvṛttir yathā suyātrā.udayanābhyām //*  
*09.7.37/ ito lābha itarato rājya.abhimarśa ity ubhayato^artha.anartha.āpad bhavati //*  
*09.7.38/ tasyām anartha.sādhako yo^arthas tam ādātuṁ yāyāt //*  
*09.7.39/ anyathā hi rājya.abhimarśaṁ vārayet //*  
*09.7.40/ etayā samantato^artha.anartha.āpad vyākhyātā //*  
*09.7.41/ ito^anartha itarato^artha.saṁśaya ity ubhayato^anartha.artha.saṁśayā //*  
*09.7.42/ tasyāṁ pūrvam anarthaṁ sādhayet, tat.siddhāv artha.saṁśayam //*  
*09.7.43/ etayā samantato^anartha.artha.saṁśayā vyākhyātā //*  
*09.7.44/ ito^artha itarato^anartha.saṁśaya ity ubhayato^artha.anartha.saṁśaya.āpad //*  
*09.7.45/ etayā samantato^artha.anartha.saṁśayā vyākhyātā //*  
*09.7.46/ tasyāṁ pūrvāṁ pūrvāṁ prakṛtīnām anartha.saṁśayān mokṣayituṁ yateta //*  
*09.7.47/ śreyo hi mitram anartha.saṁśaye tiṣṭhan na daṇḍaḥ, daṇḍo vā na kośa iti //*  
*09.7.48/ samagra.mokṣaṇa.abhāve prakṛtīnām avayavān mokṣayituṁ yateta //*  
*09.7.49/ tatra puruṣa.prakṛtīnāṁ bahulam anuraktaṁ vā tīkṣṇa.lubdha.varjam, dravya.prakṛtīnāṁ sāraṁ mahā.upakāraṁ vā //*  
*09.7.50/ saṁdhinā^āsanena dvaidhī.bhāvena vā laghūni, viparyayair gurūṇi //*  
*09.7.51/ kṣaya.sthāna.vṛddhīnāṁ ca^uttara.uttaraṁ lipseta //*  
*09.7.52/ prātilomyena vā kṣaya.ādīnām āyatyāṁ viśeṣaṁ paśyet //*  
*09.7.53/ iti deśa.avasthāpanam //*  
*09.7.54/ etena yātrā.ādi.madhya.anteṣv artha.anartha.saṁśayānām upasamprāptir vyākhyātā //*  
*09.7.55/ nirantara.yogitvāc ca^artha.anartha.saṁśayānāṁ yātrā.ādāv arthaḥ śreyān upasamprāptuṁ pārṣṇi.grāha.āsāra.pratighāte kṣaya.vyaya.pravāsa.pratyādeye mūla.rakṣaṇeṣu ca bhavati //*  
*09.7.56/ tathā^anarthaḥ saṁśayo vā sva.bhūmiṣṭhasya viṣahyo bhavati //*  
*09.7.57/ etena yātrā.madhye^artha.anartha.saṁśayānām upasamprāptir vyākhyātā //*  
*09.7.58/ yātrā.ante tu karśanīyam ucchedanīyaṁ vā karśayitvā^ucchidya vā^arthaḥ śreyān upasamprāptuṁ na^anarthaḥ saṁśayo vā para.ābādha.bhayāt //*  
*09.7.59/ sāmavāyikānām apurogasya tu yātrā.madhya.antago^anarthaḥ saṁśayo vā śreyān upasamprāptum anirbandha.gāmitvāt //*  
*09.7.60/ artho dharmaḥ kāma ity artha.tri.vargaḥ //*  
*09.7.61/ tasya pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyān upasamprāptum //*  
*09.7.62/ anartho^adharmaḥ śoka ity anartha.tri.vargaḥ //*  
*09.7.63/ tasya pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyān pratikartum //*  
*09.7.64/ artho^anartha iti, dharmo^adharma iti, kāmaḥ śoka iti saṁśaya.tri.vargaḥ //*  
*09.7.65/ tasya^uttara.pakṣa.siddhau pūrva.pakṣaḥ śreyān upasamprāptum //*  
*09.7.66/ iti kāla.avasthāpanam //*  
*09.7.67/ ity āpadaḥ - tāsāṁ siddhiḥ //*  
*09.7.68/ putra.bhrātṛ.bandhuṣu sāma.dānābhyāṁ siddhir anurūpā, paura.jānapada.daṇḍa.mukhyeṣu dāna.bhedābhyām, sāmanta.āṭavikeṣu bheda.daṇḍābhyām //*  
*09.7.69/ eṣā^anulomā, viparyaye pratilomā //*  
*09.7.70/ mitra.amitreṣu vyāmiśrā siddhiḥ //*  
*09.7.71/ paraspara.sādhakā hy upāyāḥ //*  
*09.7.72/ śatroḥ śaṅkita.amātyeṣu sāntvaṁ prayuktaṁ śeṣa.prayogaṁ nivartayati, dūṣya.amātyeṣu dānam, saṁghāteṣu bhedaḥ, śaktimatsu daṇḍa iti //*  
*09.7.73/ guru.lāghava.yogāc ca^āpadāṁ niyoga.vikalpa.samuccayā bhavanti //*  
*09.7.74/ "anena^eva^upāyena na^anyena" iti niyogaḥ //*  
*09.7.75/ "anena vā^anyena vā" iti vikalpaḥ //*  
*09.7.76/ "anena^anyena ca" iti samuccayaḥ //*  
*09.7.77/ teṣām eka.yogāś catvāras tri.yogāś ca, dvi.yogāḥ ṣaṭ, ekaś catur.yogaḥ //*  
*09.7.78/ iti pañca.daśa.upāyāḥ //*  
*09.7.79/ tāvantaḥ pratilomāḥ //*  
*09.7.80/ teṣām ekena^upāyena siddhir eka.siddhiḥ, dvābhyāṁ dvi.siddhiḥ, tribhis tri.siddhiḥ, caturbhiś catuḥ.siddhir iti //*  
*09.7.81/ dharma.mūlatvāt kāma.phalatvāc ca^arthasya dharma.artha.kāma.anubandhā yā^arthasya siddhiḥ sā sarva.artha.siddhiḥ //*  
*09.7.82/ daivād agnir udakaṁ vyādhiḥ pramāro vidravo durbhikṣam āsurī sṛṣṭir ity āpadaḥ //*  
*09.7.83/ tāsāṁ daivata.brāhmaṇa.rpaṇipātataḥ siddhiḥ //*  
*09.7.84ab/ ativṛṣṭir avṛṣṭir vā sṛṣṭir vā yā^āsurī bhavet /*  
*09.7.84cd/ tasyām ātharvaṇaṁ karma siddha.ārambhāś ca siddhayaḥ //E*  
*(skandha.āvāra.niveśah)*

### 10

*10.1.01/ vāstuka.praśaste vāstuni nāyaka.vardhaki mauhūrtikāḥ skandha.āvāram, vṛttaṁ dīrghaṁ catur.aśraṁ vā bhūmi.vaśena vā, catur.dvāraṁ ṣaṭ.pathaṁ nava.saṁsthānaṁ māpayeyuḥ khāta.vapra.sāla.dvāra.aṭṭālaka.sampannaṁ bhaye sthāne ca //*  
*10.1.02/ madhyamasya^uttare nava.bhāge rāja.vāstukaṁ dhanuḥ.śata.āyāmam ardha.vistāram, paścima.ardhe tasya^antaḥ.puram //*  
*10.1.03/ antar.vaṁśika.sainyaṁ ca^ante niviśeta //*  
*10.1.04/ purastād upasthānam, dakṣiṇataḥ kośa.śāsana.kārya.karaṇāni, vāmato rāja.aupavāhyānāṁ hasty.aśva.rathānāṁ sthānam //*  
*10.1.05/ ato dhanuḥ.śata.antarāś catvāraḥ śakaṭa.methī.pratati.stambha.sāla.parikṣepāḥ //*  
*10.1.06/ prathame purastān mantri.purohitau, daṇṣiṇataḥ koṣṭha.agāraṁ mahānasaṁ ca, vāmataḥ kupya.āyudha.agāram //*  
*10.1.07/ dvitīye maula.bhṛtānāṁ sthānam aśva.rathānāṁ senā.pateś ca //*  
*10.1.08/ tṛtīye hastinaḥ śreṇyaḥ praśāstā ca //*  
*10.1.09/ caturthe viṣṭir nāyako mitra.amitra.aṭavī.balaṁ sva.puruṣa.adhiṣṭhitam //*  
*10.1.10/ vaṇijo rūpa.ājīvāś ca^anu.mahā.patham //*  
*10.1.11/ bāhyato lubdhaka.śva.gaṇinaḥ satūrya.agnayaḥ, gūḍhāś ca^ārakṣāḥ //*  
*10.1.12/ śatrūṇām āpāte kūpa.kūṭa.avapāta.kaṇṭakinīś ca sthāpayet //*  
*10.1.13/ aṣṭādaśa.vargāṇām ārakṣa.viparyāsaṁ kārayet //*  
*10.1.14/ diva.āyāmaṁ ca kārayed apasarpa.jñāna.artham //*  
*10.1.15/ vivāda.saurika.samāja.dyūta.vāraṇaṁ ca kārayet, mudrā.rakṣaṇaṁ ca //*  
*10.1.16/ senā.nivṛttam āyudhīyam aśāsanaṁ śūnya.pālo badhnīyāt //*  
*10.1.17ab/ purastād adhvanaḥ samyak.praśāstā rakṣaṇāni ca /*  
*10.1.17cd/ yāyād vardhaki.viṣṭibhyām udakāni ca kārayet //E*  
*(skandha.āvāra.prayāṇam - bala.vyasana.avaskanda.kāla.rakṣaṇam)*  
*10.2.01/ grāma.araṇyānām adhvani niveśān yavasa.indhana.udaka.vaśena parisaṁkhyāya sthāna.āsana.gamana.kālaṁ ca yātrāṁ yāyāt //*  
*10.2.02/ tat.pratīkāra.dvi.guṇaṁ bhakta.upakaraṇaṁ vāhayet //*  
*10.2.03/ aśakto vā sainyeṣv āyojayet, antareṣu vā nicinuyāt //*  
*10.2.04/ purastān nāyakaḥ, madhye kalatraṁ svāmī ca, pārśvayor aśvā bāhu.utsāraḥ, cakra.anteṣu hastinaḥ prasāra.vṛddhir vā, paścāt senā.patir yāyāt niviśeta //*  
*10.2.05/ sarvato vana.ājīvaḥ prasāraḥ //*  
*10.2.06/ sva.deśād anvāyatir vīvadhaḥ //*  
*10.2.07/ mitra.balam āsāraḥ //*  
*10.2.08/ kalatra.sthānam apasāraḥ //*  
*10.2.09/ purastād adhyāghāte makareṇa yāyāt, paścāt^śakaṭena, pārśvayor vajreṇa, samantataḥ sarvato.bhadreṇa, eka.ayane sūcyā //*  
*10.2.10/ pathi.dvaidhī.bhāve svabhūmito yāyāt //*  
*10.2.11/ abhūmiṣṭhānāṁ hi sva.bhūmiṣṭhā yuddhe pratilomā bhavanti //*  
*10.2.12/ yojanam adhamā, adhyardhaṁ madhyamā, dvi.yojanam uttamā, sambhāvyā vā gatiḥ //*  
*10.2.13/ āśraya.kārī sampanna.ghātī pārṣṇir āsāro madhyama udāsīno vā pratikartavyaḥ, saṁkaṭo mārgaḥ śodhayitavyaḥ, kośo daṇḍo mitra.amitra.aṭavī.balaṁ viṣṭi.ṛtur vā pratīkṣyāḥ, kṛta.durga.karma.nicaya.rakṣā.kṣayaḥ krīta.bala.nirvedo mitra.bala.nirvedaś ca^āgamiṣyati, upajapitāro vā na^atitvarayanti, śatrur abhiprāyaṁ vā pūrayiṣyati, iti śanair yāyāt,*  
*viparyaye śīghram //*  
*10.2.14/ hasti.stambha.saṁkrama.setu.bandha.nau.kāṣṭha.veṇu.saṁghātair alābu.carma.karaṇḍa.dṛti.plava.gaṇḍikā.veṇikābhiś ca^udakāni tārayet //*  
*10.2.15/ tīrtha.abhigrahe hasty.aśvair anyato rātrāv uttārya sattraṁ gṛhṇīyāt //*  
*10.2.16/ anudake cakri.catuṣpadaṁ ca^adhva.pramāṇena śaktyā^udakaṁ vāhayet //*  
*10.2.17/ dīrgha.kāntāram anudakaṁ yavasa.indhana.udaka.hīnaṁ vā kṛcchra.adhvānam abhiyoga.praskannaṁ kṣut.pipāsā.adhva.klāntaṁ paṅka.toya.gambhīrāṇāṁ vā nadī.darī.śailānām udyāna.apayāne vyāsaktam eka.ayana.mārge śaila.viṣame saṁkaṭe vā bahulī.bhūtaṁ niveśe prasthite visamnāhaṁ bhojana.vyāsaktam āyata.gata.pariśrāntam avasuptaṁ vyādhi.maraka.durbhikṣa.pīḍitaṁ*  
*vyādhita.patty.aśva.dvipam abhūmiṣṭhaṁ vā bala.vyasaneṣu vā sva.sainyaṁ rakṣet, para.sainyaṁ ca^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.2.18/ eka.ayana.mārga.prayātasya senā.niścāra.grāsa.āhāra.śayyā.prastāra.agni.nidhāna.dhvaja.āyudha.saṁkhyānena para.bala.jñānam //*  
*10.2.19/ tadā^ātmāno gūhayet //*  
*10.2.20ab/ pārvataṁ vana.durgaṁ vā sāpasāra.pratigraham /*  
*10.2.20cd/ sva.bhumau pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā yudhyeta niviśeta ca //E*  
*(kūṭa.yuddha.vikalpāh - sva.sainya.utsāhanam - sva.balāny abala.vyāyogah)*  
*10.3.01/ bala.viśiṣṭaḥ kṛta.upajāpaḥ prativihita.ṛtuḥ sva.bhūmyāṁ prakāśa.yuddham upeyāt //*  
*10.3.02/ viparyaye kūṭa.yuddham //*  
*10.3.03/ bala.vyasana.avaskanda.kāleṣu param abhihanyāt, abhūmiṣṭhaṁ vā sva.bhūmiṣṭhaḥ, prakṛti.pragraho vā sva.bhūmiṣṭham //*  
*10.3.04/ dūṣya.amitra.aṭavī.balair vā bhaṅgaṁ dattvā vibhūmi.prāptaṁ hanyāt //*  
*10.3.05/ saṁhata.anīkaṁ hastibhir bhedayet //*  
*10.3.06/ pūrvaṁ bhaṅga.pradānena^anupralīnaṁ bhinnam abhinnaḥ pratinivṛtya hanyāt //*  
*10.3.07/ purastād abhihatya pracalaṁ vimukhaṁ vā pṛṣṭhato hasty.aśvena^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.08/ pṛṣṭhato^abhihatyā pracalaṁ vimukhaṁ vā purastāt sāra.balena^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.09/ tābhyāṁ pārśva.abhigātau vyākhyātau //*  
*10.3.10/ yato vā dūṣya.phalgu.balaṁ tato^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.11/ purastād viṣamāyāṁ pṛṣṭhato^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.12/ pṛṣṭhato viṣamāyāṁ purastād abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.13/ pārśvato viṣamāyām itarato^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.14/ dūṣya.amitra.aṭavī.balair vā pūrvaṁ yodhayitvā śrāntam aśrāntaḥ param abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.15/ dūṣya.balena vā svayaṁ bhaṅgaṁ dattvā "jitam" iti viśvastam aviśvastaḥ sattra.apāśrayo^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.16/ sārtha.vraja.skandha.āvāra.saṁvāha.vilopa.pramattam apramatto^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.17/ phalgu.bala.avacchanna.sāra.balo vā para.vīrān anupraviśya hanyāt //*  
*10.3.18/ go.grahaṇena śvā.pada.vadhena vā para.vīrān ākṛṣya sattrac.channo^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.19/ rātrāv avaskandena jāgarayitvā nidrā.klāntān avasuptān vā divā hanyāt //*  
*10.3.20/ sapāda.carma.kośair vā hastibhiḥ sauptikaṁ dadyāt //*  
*10.3.21/ ahaḥ.samnāha.pariśrāntān apara.ahne^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.22/ śuṣka.carma.vṛtta.śarkara.ākośakair go.mahiṣa.uṣṭra.yūthair vā trasnubhir akṛta.hasty.aśvaṁ bhinnam abhinnaḥ pratinivṛttaṁ hanyāt //*  
*10.3.23/ pratisūrya.vātaṁ vā sarvam abhihanyāt //*  
*10.3.24/ dhānvana.vana.saṁkaṭa.paṅka.śaila.nimna.viṣama.nāvo gāvaḥ śakaṭavyūho nīhāro rātrir iti sattrāṇi //*  
*10.3.25/ pūrve ca praharaṇa.kālāḥ kūṭa.yuddha.hetavaḥ //*  
*10.3.26/ saṁgrāmas tu nirdiṣṭa.deśa.kālo dharmiṣṭhaḥ //*  
*10.3.27/ saṁhatya daṇḍaṁ brūyāt "tulya.vetano^asmi, bhavadbhiḥ saha bhogyam idaṁ rājyam, mayā^abhihitaiḥ paro^abhihantavyaḥ" iti //*  
*10.3.28/ vedeṣv apy anuśrūyate samāpta.dakṣiṇānāṁ yajñānām avabhṛtheṣu "sā te gatir yā śūrāṇām" iti //*  
*10.3.29/ api^iha ślokau bhavataḥ //*  
*10.3.30ab/ "yān yajña.saṁghais tapasā ca viprāḥ svarga.eṣiṇaḥ pātra.cayaiś ca yānti /*  
*10.3.30cd/ kṣaṇena tān apy atiyānti śūrāḥ prāṇān suyuddheṣu parityajantaḥ //*  
*10.3.31ab/ "navaṁ śarāvaṁ salilasya pūrṇaṁ susaṁskṛtaṁ darbha.kṛta.uttarīyam /*  
*10.3.31cd/ tat tasya mā bhūn narakaṁ ca gacched yo bhartṛ.piṇḍasya kṛte na yudhyet - iti //*  
*10.3.32/ mantri.purohitābhyām utsāhayed yodhān vyūha.sampadā //*  
*10.3.33/ kārtāntika.ādiś ca^asya vargaḥ sarvajña.daivata.samyoga.khyāpanābhyāṁ sva.pakṣam uddharṣayet, para.pakṣaṁ ca^udvejayet //*  
*10.3.34/ "śvo yuddham" iti kṛta.upavāsaḥ śastra.vāhanaṁ ca^anuśayīta //*  
*10.3.35/ atharvabhiś ca juhuyāt //*  
*10.3.36/ vijaya.yuktāḥ svargīyāś ca^āśiṣo vācayet //*  
*10.3.37/ brāhmaṇebhyaś ca^ātmānam atisṛjet //*  
*10.3.38/ śaurya.śilpa.abhijana.anurāga.yuktam artha.mānābhyām avisaṁvāditam anīka.garbhaṁ kurvīta //*  
*10.3.39/ pitṛ.putra.bhrātṛkāṇām āyudhīyānām adhvajaṁ muṇḍa.anīkaṁ rāja.sthānam //*  
*10.3.40/ hastī ratho vā rāja.vāhanam aśva.anubandhaḥ //*  
*10.3.41/ yat prāya.sainyo yatra vā vinītaḥ syāt t(ad) adhirohayet //*  
*10.3.42/ rāja.vyañjano vyūha.adhiṣṭhānam āyojyaḥ //*  
*10.3.43/ sūta.māgadhāḥ śūrāṇāṁ svargam asvargaṁ bhīrūṇāṁ jāti.saṁgha.kula.karma.vṛtta.stavaṁ ca yodhānāṁ varṇayeyuḥ //*  
*10.3.44/ purohita.puruṣāḥ kṛtya.abhicāraṁ brūyuḥ, yantrika.vardhaki.mauhūrtikāḥ sva.karma.siddhim asiddhiṁ pareṣām //*  
*10.3.45/ senā.patir artha.mānābhyām abhisaṁskṛtam anīkam ābhāṣeta - "śata.sāhasro rāja.vadhaḥ, pañcāśat.sāhasraḥ senā.pati.kumāra.vadhaḥ, daśa.sāhasraḥ pravīra.mukhya.vadhaḥ, pañca.sāhasro hasti.ratha.vadhaḥ, sāhasro^aśva.vadhaḥ, śatyaḥ patti.mukhya.vadhaḥ, śiro viṁśatikaṁ bhoga.dvaiguṇyaṁ svayaṁ.grāhaś ca" iti //*  
*10.3.46/ tad eṣāṁ daśa.varga.adhipatayo vidyuḥ //*  
*10.3.47/ cikitsakāḥ śastra.yantra.agada.sneha.vastra.hastāḥ striyaś ca^anna.pāna.rakṣiṇyaḥ puruṣāṇām uddharṣaṇīyāḥ pṛṣṭhatas tiṣṭheyuḥ //*  
*10.3.48/ adakṣiṇā.mukhaṁ pṛṣṭhataḥ.sūryam anuloma.vātam anīkaṁ sva.bhūmau vyūheta //*  
*10.3.49/ para.bhūmi.vyūhe ca^aśvāṁś cārayeyuḥ //*  
*10.3.50/ yatra sthānaṁ prajavaś ca^abhūmir vyūhasya tatra sthitaḥ prajavitaś ca^ubhayathā jīyeta // viparyaye jayati, ubhayathā sthāne prajave ca //*  
*10.3.52/ samā viṣamā vyāmiśrā vā bhūmir iti purastāt pārśvābhyāṁ paścāc ca jñeyā //*  
*10.3.53/ samāyāṁ daṇḍa.maṇḍala.vyūhāḥ, viṣamāyāṁ bhoga.asaṁhata.vyūhāḥ, vyāmiśrāyāṁ viṣama.vyūhāḥ //*  
*10.3.54/ viśiṣṭa.balaṁ bhaṅktvā saṁdhiṁ yāceta //*  
*10.3.55/ sama.balena yācitaḥ saṁdadhīta //*  
*10.3.56/ hīnam anuhanyāt, na tv eva sva.bhūmi.prāptaṁ tyakta.ātmānaṁ vā //*  
*10.3.57ab/ punar.āvartamānasya nirāśasya ca jīvite /*  
*10.3.57cd/ adhāryo jāyate vegas tasmād bhagnaṁ na pīḍayet //E*  
*(yuddha.bhūmayah - patty.aśva.ratha.hasti.karmāṇi)*  
*10.4.01/ sva.bhūmiḥ patty.aśva.ratha.dvipānām iṣṭā yuddhe niveśe ca //*  
*10.4.02/ dhānvana.vana.nimna.sthala.yodhināṁ khanaka.ākāśa.divā.rātri.yodhināṁ ca puruṣāṇāṁ nādeya.pārvata.ānūpa.sārasānāṁ ca hastinām aśvānāṁ ca yathā.svam iṣṭā yuddha.bhūmayaḥ kālāś ca //*  
*10.4.03/ samā sthirā^abhikāśā nirutkhātinya.cakra.khurā^anakṣa.grāhiṇya.vṛkṣa.gulma.vratatī.stambha.kedāra.śvabhra.valmīka.sikatā.paṅka.bhaṅgurā daraṇa.hīnā ca ratha.bhūmiḥ, hasty.aśvayor manuṣyāṇāṁ ca same viṣame hitā yuddhe niveśe ca //*  
*10.4.04/ aṇv.aśma.vṛkṣā hrasva.laṅghanīya.śvabhrā manda.daraṇa.doṣā ca^aśva.bhūmiḥ //*  
*10.4.05/ sthūla.sthāṇv aśma.vṛkṣa.vratatī.valmīka.gulmā padāti.bhūmiḥ //*  
*10.4.06/ gamya.śaila.nimna.viṣamā mardanīya.vṛkṣā chedanīya.vratatī paṅka.bhaṅgurā daraṇa.hīnā ca hasti.bhūmiḥ //*  
*10.4.07/ akaṇṭakiny abahu.viṣamā pratyāsāravatī^iti padātīnām atiśayaḥ //*  
*10.4.08/ dvi.guṇa.pratyāsārā kardama.udaka.khañjana.hīnā nihśarkarā^iti vājinām atiśayaḥ //*  
*10.4.09/ pāṁsu.kardama.udaka.nala.śara.ādhānavatī śva.daṇṣṭra.hīnā mahā.vṛkṣa.śākhā.ghāta.viyuktā^iti hastinām atiśayaḥ //*  
*10.4.10/ toya.āśaya.apāśrayavatī nirutkhātinī kedāra.hīnā vyāvartana.samarthā^iti rathānām atiśayaḥ //*  
*10.4.11/ uktā sarveṣāṁ bhūmiḥ //*  
*10.4.12/ etayā sarva.bala.niveśā yuddhāni ca vyākhyātāni bhavanti //*  
*10.4.13/ bhūmi.vāsa.vana.vicayo^aviṣama.toya.tīrtha.vāta.raśmi.grahaṇaṁ vīvadha.āsārayor ghāto rakṣā vā viśuddhiḥ sthāpanā ca balasya prasāra.vṛddhir bāhu.utsāraḥ pūrva.prahāro vyāveśanaṁ vyāvedhanam āśvāso grahaṇaṁ mokṣaṇaṁ mārga.anusāra.vinimayaḥ kośa.kumāra.abhiharaṇaṁ jaghana.koṭy.abhighāto hīna.anusāraṇam anuyānaṁ samāja.karma^ity aśva.karmāṇi //*  
*10.4.14/ puro.yānam akṛta.mārga.vāsa.tīrtha.karma bāhu.utsāras toya.taraṇa.avataraṇe sthāna.gamana.avataraṇaṁ viṣama.sambādha.praveśo^agni.dāna.śamanam eka.aṅga.vijayo bhinna.saṁdhānam abhinna.bhedanaṁ vyasane trāṇam abhighāto vibhīṣikā trāsanaṁ.audāryaṁ grahaṇaṁ mokṣaṇaṁ sāla.dvāra.aṭṭālaka.bhañjanaṁ kośa.vāhana.apavāhanam iti hasti.karmāṇi //*  
*10.4.15/ sva.bala.rakṣā catur.aṅga.bala.pratiṣedhaḥ saṁgrāme grahaṇaṁ mokṣaṇaṁ bhinna.saṁdhānam abhinna.bhedanaṁ trāsanam audāryaṁ bhīma.ghoṣaś ca^iti ratha.karmāṇi //*  
*10.4.16/ sarva.deśa.kāla.śastra.vahanaṁ vyāyāmaś ca^iti padāti.karmāṇi //*  
*10.4.17/ śibira.mārga.setu.kūpa.tīrtha.śodhana.karma yantra.āyudha.āvaraṇa.upakaraṇa.grāsa.vahanam āyodhanāc ca praharaṇa.āvaraṇa.pratividdha.apanayanam iti viṣṭi.karmāṇi //*  
*10.4.18ab/ kuryād gava.aśva.vyāyogaṁ ratheṣv alpa.hayo nṛpaḥ /*  
*10.4.18cd/ khara.uṣṭra.śakaṭānāṁ vā garbham alpa.gajas tathā //E*  
*(pakṣa.kakṣa.urasyānām bala.agrato vyūha.vibhāgah - sāra.phalgu.bala.vibhāgah - patty.aśva.ratha.hasti.yuddhāni)*  
*10.5.01/ pañca.dhanuḥ.śata.apakṛṣṭaṁ durgam avasthāpya yuddham upeyāt, bhūmi.vaśena vā //*  
*10.5.02/ vibhakta.mukhyām acakṣur.viṣaye mokṣayitvā senāṁ senā.pati.nāyakau vyūheyātām //*  
*10.5.03/ śama.antaraṁ pattiṁ sthāpayet, tri.śama.antaram aśvam, pañca.śama.antaraṁ rathaṁ hastinaṁ vā //*  
*10.5.04/ dvi.guṇa.antaraṁ tri.guṇa.antaraṁ vā vyūheta //*  
*10.5.05/ evaṁ yathā.sukham asambādhaṁ yudhyeta //*  
*10.5.06/ pañca.aratni dhanuḥ //*  
*10.5.07/ tasmin dhanvinaṁ sthāpayet, tri.dhanuṣy aśvam, pañca.dhanuṣi rathaṁ hastinaṁ vā //*  
*10.5.08/ pañca.dhanur anīka.saṁdhiḥ pakṣa.kakṣa.urasyānām //*  
*10.5.09/ aśvasya trayaḥ puruṣāḥ pratiyoddhāraḥ //*  
*10.5.10/ pañca.daśa rathasya hastino vā, pañca ca^aśvāḥ //*  
*10.5.11/ tāvantaḥ pāda.gopā vāji.ratha.dvipānāṁ vidheyāḥ //*  
*10.5.12/ trīṇi trikāṇy anīkaṁ rathānām urasyaṁ sthāpayet, tāvat kakṣaṁ pakṣaṁ ca^ubhayataḥ //*  
*10.5.13/ pañca.catvāriṁśad evaṁ rathā ratha.vyūhe bhavanti, dve śate pañca.viṁśatiś ca^aśvāḥ, ṣaṭ.śatāni pañca.saptatiś ca puruṣāḥ pratiyodhāraḥ, tāvantaḥ pāda.gopāḥ //*  
*10.5.14/ eṣa sama.vyūhaḥ //*  
*10.5.15/ tasya dvi.ratha.uttarā vṛddhir ā.eka.viṁśati.rathād iti //*  
*10.5.16/ evam ojā daśa sama.vyūha.prakṛtayo bhavanti //*  
*10.5.17/ pakṣa.kakṣa.urasyānāṁ mitho viṣama.saṁkhyāne viṣama.vyūhaḥ //*  
*10.5.18/ tasya^api dvi.ratha^uttarā vṛddhir ā.eka.viṁśati.rathād iti //*  
*10.5.19/ evam ojā daśa viṣama.vyūha.prakṛtayo bhavanti //*  
*10.5.20/ ataḥ sainyānāṁ vyūha.śeṣam āvāpaḥ kāryaḥ //*  
*10.5.21/ rathānāṁ dvau tri.bhāgāv aṅgeṣv āvāpayet, śeṣam urasyaṁ sthāpayet //*  
*10.5.22/ evaṁ tri.bhāga.ūno rathānām āvāpaḥ kāryaḥ //*  
*10.5.23/ tena hastinām aśvānām āvāpo vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*10.5.24/ yāvad.aśva.ratha.dvipānāṁ yuddha.sambādhanm na kuryāt tāvad āvāpaḥ kāryaḥ //*  
*10.5.25/ daṇḍa.bāhulyam āvāpaḥ //*  
*10.5.26/ patti.bāhulyaṁ pratyāpāvaḥ //*  
*10.5.27/ eka.aṅga.bāhulyam anvāvāpaḥ //*  
*10.5.28/ dūṣya.bāhulyam atyāvāpaḥ //*  
*10.5.29/ para.āvāpāt pratyāvāpāc ca catur.guṇād ā.aṣṭa.guṇād iti vā vibhavataḥ sainyānām āvāpaḥ //*  
*10.5.30/ ratha.vyūhena hasti.vyūho vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*10.5.31/ vyāmiśro vā hasti.ratha.aśvānāṁ - cakra.anteṣu hastinaḥ pārśvayor aśvā rathā urasye //*  
*10.5.32/ hastinām urasyaṁ rathānāṁ kakṣāv aśvānāṁ pakṣāv iti madhya.bhedī //*  
*10.5.33/ viparīto^anta.bhedī //*  
*10.5.34/ hastinām eva tu śuddhaḥ - sāmnāhyānām urasyam aupavāhyānāṁ jaghanaṁ vyālānāṁ koṭyāv iti //*  
*10.5.35/ aśva.vyūho - varmiṇām urasyaṁ śuddhānāṁ kakṣa.pakṣāv iti //*  
*10.5.36/ patti.vyūhaḥ - purastād āvaraṇinaḥ pṛṣṭhato dhanvinaḥ //*  
*10.5.37/ iti śuddhāḥ //*  
*10.5.38/ pattayaḥ pakṣayor aśvāḥ pārśvayoḥ hastinaḥ pṛṣṭhato rathāḥ purastāt, para.vyūha.vaśena vā viparyāsaḥ //*  
*10.5.39/ iti dvy.aṅga.bala.vibhāgaḥ //*  
*10.5.40/ tena tr.aṅga.bala.vibhāgo vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*10.5.41/ daṇḍa.sampat sāra.balaṁ puṁsāṁ*  
*10.5.42/ hasty.aśvayor viśeṣaḥ kulaṁ jātiḥ sattvaṁ vayaḥ.sthatā prāṇo varṣma javas tejaḥ śilpaṁ stairyam udagratā vidheyatvaṁ suvyañjana.ācāratā^iti //*  
*10.5.43/ patty.aśva.ratha.dvipānāṁ sāra.tri.bhāgam urasyaṁ sthāpayet, dvau tri.bhāgau kakṣaṁ pakṣaṁ ca^ubhayataḥ, anulomam anusāram, pratilomaṁ tṛtīya.sāram, phalgu pratilomam //*  
*10.5.44/ evaṁ sarvam upayogaṁ gamayet //*  
*10.5.45/ phalgu.balam anteṣv avadhāya vega.abhihūliko bhavati //*  
*10.5.46/ sāra.balam agrataḥ kṛtvā koṭīṣv anusāraṁ kuryāt, jaghane tṛtiyiya.sāram, madhye phalgu.balam //*  
*10.5.47/ evam etat sahiṣṇu bhavati //*  
*10.5.48/ vyūhaṁ tu sthāpayitvā pakṣa.kakṣa.urasyānām ekena dvābhyāṁ vā praharet, śeṣaiḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt //*  
*10.5.49/ yat parasya durbalaṁ vīta.hasty.aśvaṁ dūṣya.amātyaṁ kṛta.upajāpaṁ vā tat.prabhūta.sāreṇa^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.5.50/ yad vā parasya sāriṣṭhaṁ tad.dvi.guṇa.sāreṇa^abhihanyāt //*  
*10.5.51/ yad aṅgam alpa.sāram ātmanas tad bahunā^upacinuyāt //*  
*10.5.52/ yataḥ parasya^apacayas tato^abhyāśe vyūheta, yatot vā bhayaṁ syāt //*  
*10.5.53/ abhisṛtaṁ parisṛtam atisṛtam apasṛtam unmathya.avadhānaṁ valayo go.mūtrikā maṇḍalaṁ prakīrṇikā vyāvṛtta.pṛṣṭham anuvaṁśam agrataḥ pārśvābhyāṁ pṛṣṭhato bhagna.rakṣā bhagna.anupāta ity aśva.yuddhāni //*  
*10.5.54/ prakīrṇika.āvarjāny etāny eva caturṇām aṅgānāṁ vyasta.samastānāṁ vā ghātaḥ, pakṣa.kakṣa.urasyānāṁ ca prabhañjanam avaskandaḥ sauptikaṁ ca^iti hasit.yuddhāni //*  
*10.5.55/ unmathya.avadhāna.varjāny etāny eva sva.bhūmāv abhiyāna.apayāna.sthita.yuddhāni^iti ratha.yuddhāni //*  
*10.5.56/ sarva.deśa.kāla.praharaṇam upāṁśu.daṇḍaś ca^iti patti.yuddhāni //*  
*10.5.57ab/ etena vidhinā vyūhān ojān yugmāṁś ca kārayet /*  
*10.5.57cd/ vibhavo yāvad aṅgānāṁ caturṇāṁ sadṛśo bhavet //*  
*10.5.58ab/ dve śate dhanuṣāṁ gatvā rājā tiṣṭhet pratigrahe /*  
*10.5.58cd/ bhinna.saṁghātanaṁ tasmān na yudhyeta^apratigrahaḥ //E*  
*(daṇḍa.bhoga.maṇḍala.asamhata.vyūha.vyūhanam - tasya prativyūha.sthānam)*  
*10.6.01/ pakṣāv urasyaṁ pratigraha ity auśanaso vyūha.vibhāgaḥ //*  
*10.6.02/ pakṣau kakṣāv urasym pratigraha iti bārhasptyaḥ //*  
*10.6.03/ prapakṣa.kakṣa.urasyā ubhayoḥ daṇḍa.bhoga.maṇḍala.asaṁhatāḥ prakṛti.vyūhāḥ //*  
*10.6.04/ tatra tiryag.vṛttir daṇḍaḥ //*  
*10.6.05/ samastānām anvāvṛttir bhogaḥ //*  
*10.6.06/ saratāṁ sarvato.vṛttir maṇḍalaḥ //*  
*10.6.07/ sthitānāṁ pṛthag.anīka.vṛttir asaṁhataḥ //*  
*10.6.08/ pakṣa.kakṣa.urasyaiḥ samaṁ vartamāno daṇḍaḥ //*  
*10.6.09/ sa kakṣa.atikrāntaḥ pradaraḥ //*  
*10.6.10/ sa eva pakṣa.kakṣābhyāṁ pratikrānto dṛḍhakaḥ //*  
*10.6.11/ sa eva^atikrāntaḥ pakṣābhyām asahyaḥ //*  
*10.6.12/ pakṣāv avasthāpya^urasya.atikrāntaḥ śyenaḥ //*  
*10.6.13/ viparyaye cāpaṁ cāpa.kukuṣiḥ pratiṣṭhaḥ supratiṣṭhaś ca //*  
*10.6.14/ cāpa.pakṣaḥ saṁjayaḥ //*  
*10.6.15/ sa eva^urasya.atikrānto vijayaḥ //*  
*10.6.16/ sthūla.karṇa.pakṣaḥ sthūṇa.akarṇaḥ //*  
*10.6.17/ dvi.guṇa.pakṣa.sthūṇo viśāla.vijayaḥ //*  
*10.6.18/ try.abhikrānta.pakṣaś camū.mukhaḥ //*  
*10.6.19/ viparyaye jhaṣa.āsyaḥ //*  
*10.6.20/ ūrdhva.rājir daṇḍaḥ sūcī //*  
*10.6.21/ dvau daṇḍau valayaḥ //*  
*10.6.22/ catvāro durjayaḥ //*  
*10.6.23/ iti daṇḍa.vyūhāḥ //*  
*10.6.24/ pakṣa.kakṣa.urasyair viṣamaṁ vartamāno bhogaḥ //*  
*10.6.25/ sa sarpa.sārī go.mūtrikā vā //*  
*10.6.26/ sa yugma.urasyo daṇḍa.pakṣaḥ śakaṭaḥ //*  
*10.6.27/ viparyaye makaraḥ //*  
*10.6.28/ hasty.aśva.rathair vyatikīrṇaḥ śakaṭaḥ pāripatantakaḥ //*  
*10.6.29/ iti bhoga.vyūhāḥ //*  
*10.6.30/ pakṣa.kakṣa.urasyānām ekī.bhāve maṇḍalaḥ //*  
*10.6.31/ sa sarvato.mukhaḥ sarvato.bhadraḥ //*  
*10.6.32/ aṣṭa.anīko durjayaḥ //*  
*10.6.33/ iti maṇḍala.vyūhāḥ //*  
*10.6.34/ pakṣa.kakṣa.urasyānām asaṁhatād asaṁhataḥ //*  
*10.6.35/ sa pañca.anīkānām ākṛti.sthāpanād vajro godhā vā //*  
*10.6.36/ caturṇām uddhānakaḥ kākapadī vā //*  
*10.6.37/ trayāṇām ardha.candrakaḥ karkaṭaka.śṛṅgī vā //*  
*10.6.38/ ity asaṁhata.vyūhāḥ //*  
*10.6.39/ ratha.urasyo hasti.kakṣo^aśva.pṛṣṭho^ariṣṭaḥ //*  
*10.6.40/ pattayo^aśvā rathā hastinaś ca^anupṛṣṭham acalaḥ //*  
*10.6.41/ hastino^aśvā rathāḥ pattayaś ca^anupṛṣṭham apratihataḥ //*  
*10.6.42/ teṣāṁ pradaraṁ dṛḍhakena ghātayet, dṛḍhakam asahyena, śyenaṁ cāpena, pratiṣṭhaṁ supratiṣṭhena, saṁjayaṁ vijayena, sthūṇa.ākarṇaṁ viśāla.vijayena, pāripatantakaṁ sarvato.bhadreṇa //*  
*10.6.43/ durjayena sarvān prativyūheta //*  
*10.6.44/ patty.aśva.ratha.dvipānāṁ pūrvaṁ pūrvam uttareṇa ghātayet, hīna.aṅgam adhika.aṅgena ca^iti //*  
*10.6.45/ aṅga.daśakasya^ekaḥ patiḥ patikaḥ, patika.daśakasya^ekaḥ senā.patiḥ, tad.daśakasya^eko nāyaka iti //*  
*10.6.46/ sa tūrya.ghoṣa.dhvaja.patākābhir vyūha.aṅgānāṁ saṁjñāḥ sthāpayed aṅga.vibhāge saṁghāte sthāne gamane vyāvartane praharaṇe ca //*  
*10.6.47/ same vyūhe deśa.kāla.sāra.yogāt siddhiḥ //*  
*10.6.48ab/ yantrair upaniṣad.yogais tīkṣṇair vyāsakta.ghātibhiḥ /*  
*10.6.48cd/ māyābhir deva.samyogaiḥ śakaṭair hasti.bhīṣaṇaiḥ //*  
*10.6.49ab/ dūṣya.prakopair go.yūthaiḥ skandha.āvāra.pradīpanaiḥ /*  
*10.6.49cd/ koṭī.jaghana.ghātair vā dūta.vyañjana.bhedanaiḥ //*  
*10.6.50ab/ "durgaṁ dagdhaṁ hṛtaṁ vā te kopaḥ kulyaḥ samutthitaḥ /*  
*10.6.50cd/ śatrur āṭaviko vā" iti parasya^udvegam ācaret //*  
*10.6.51ab/ ekaṁ hanyān na vā hanyād iṣuḥ kṣipto dhanuṣmatā /*  
*10.6.51cd/ prajñānena tu matiḥ kṣiptā hanyād garbha.gatān api //E*  
*(bheda.upādānāni - upāmśu.daṇḍāh)*

### 11

*11.1.01/ saṁgha.lābho daṇḍa.mitra.lābhānām uttamaḥ //*  
*11.1.02/ saṁghā hi saṁhatatvād adhṛṣyāḥ pareṣām //*  
*11.1.03/ tān anuguṇān bhuñjīta sāma.dānābhyām, viguṇān bheda.daṇḍābhyām //*  
*11.1.04/ kāmboja.surāṣṭra.kṣatriya.śreṇy.ādayo vārtta.śastra.upajīvinaḥ //*  
*11.1.05/ licchivika.vṛjika.mallaka.madraka.kukura.kuru.pāñcāla.ādayo rāja.śabda.upajīvinaḥ //*  
*11.1.06/ sarveṣām āsannāḥ sattriṇaḥ saṁghānāṁ paraspara.nyaṅga.dveṣa.vaira.kalaha.sthānāny upalabhya krama.abhinītaṁ bhedam upacārayeyuḥ "asau tvā vijalpati" iti //*  
*11.1.07/ evam ubhayato.baddha.roṣāṇāṁ vidyā.śilpa.dyūta.vaihārikeṣv ācārya.vyañjanā bāla.kalahān utpādayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.08/ veśa.śauṇḍikeṣu vā pratiloma.praśaṁsābhiḥ saṁgha.mukhya.manuṣyāṇāṁ tīkṣṇāḥ kalahān utpādayeyuḥ, kṛtya.pakṣa.upagraheṇa vā //*  
*11.1.09/ kumārakān viśiṣṭac.chindikayā hīnac.chindikān utsāhayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.10/ viśiṣṭānāṁ ca^eka.pātraṁ vivāhaṁ vā hīnebhyo vārayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.11/ hīnān vā viśiṣṭair eka.pātre vivāhe vā yojayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.12/ avahīnān vā tulya.bhāva.upagamane kulataḥ pauruṣataḥ sthāna.viparyāsato vā //*  
*11.1.13/ vyavahāram avasthitaṁ vā pratiloma.sthāpanena niśāmayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.14/ vivāda.padeṣu vā dravya.paśu.manuṣya.abhighātena rātrau tīkṣṇāḥ kalahān utpādayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.15/ sarveṣu ca kalaha.sthāneṣu hīna.pakṣaṁ rājā kośa.daṇḍābhyām upagṛhya pratipakṣa.vadhe yojayet //*  
*11.1.16/ bhinnān apavāhayed vā //*  
*11.1.17/ bhūmau ca^eṣāṁ pañca.kulīṁ daśa.kulīṁ vā kṛṣyāyāṁ niveśayet //*  
*11.1.18/ ekasthā hi śastra.grahaṇa.samarthāḥ syuḥ //*  
*11.1.19/ samavāye ca^eṣām atyayaṁ sthāpayet //*  
*11.1.20/ rāja.śabdibhir avaruddham avakṣiptaṁ vā kulyam abhijātaṁ rāja.putratve sthāpayet //*  
*11.1.21/ kārtāntika.ādiś ca^asya vargo rāja.lakṣaṇyatāṁ saṁgheṣu prakāśayet //*  
*11.1.22/ saṁgha.mukhyāṁś ca dharmiṣṭhān upajapet "sva.dharmam amuṣya rājñaḥ putre bhrātari vā pratipadyadhvam" iti //*  
*11.1.23/ pratipanneṣu kṛtya.pakṣa.upagraha.artham arthaṁ daṇḍaṁ ca preṣayet //*  
*11.1.24/ vikrama.kāle śauṇḍika.vyañjanāḥ putra.dāra.preta.apadeśena "naiṣecanikam" iti madana.rasa.yuktān madya.kumbhān^śataśaḥ prayaccheyuḥ //*  
*11.1.25/ caitya.daivata.dvāra.rakṣā.sthāneṣu ca sattriṇaḥ samaya.karma.nikṣepaṁ sahiraṇya.abhijñāna.mudrāṇi hiraṇya.bhājanāni ca prarūpayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.26/ dṛśyamāneṣu ca saṁgheṣu "rājakīyāḥ" ity āvedayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.27/ atha^avaskandaṁ dadyāt //*  
*11.1.28/ saṁghānāṁ vā vāhana.hiraṇye kālike gṛhītvā saṁgha.mukhyāya prakhyātaṁ dravyaṁ prayacchet //*  
*11.1.29/ tad eṣāṁ yācite "dattam amuṣmai mukhyāya" iti brūyāt //*  
*11.1.30/ etena skandha.āvāra.aṭavī.bhedo vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*11.1.31/ saṁgha.mukhya.putram ātma.sambhāvitaṁ vā sattrī grāhayet "amuṣya rājñaḥ putras tvam, śatru.bhayād iha nyasto^asi" iti //*  
*11.1.32/ pratipannaṁ rājā kośa.daṇḍābhyām upagṛhya saṁgheṣu vikramayet //*  
*11.1.33/ avāpta.arthas tam api pravāsayet //*  
*11.1.34/ bandhakī.poṣakāḥ plavaka.naṭa.nartaka.saubhikā vā praṇihitāḥ strībhiḥ parama.rūpa.yauvanābhiḥ saṁgha.mukhyān unmādayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.35/ jāta.kāmānām anyatamasya pratyayaṁ kṛtvā^anyatra gamanena prasabha.haraṇena vā kalahān utpādayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.36/ kalahe tīkṣṇāḥ karma kuryuḥ "hato^ayam itthaṁ kāmukaḥ" iti //*  
*11.1.37/ visaṁvāditaṁ vā marṣayamāṇam abhisṛtya strī brūyāt "asau māṁ mukhyas tvayi jāta.kāmāṁ bādhate, tasmin^jīvati na^iha sthāsyāmi" iti ghātam asya prayojayet //*  
*11.1.38/ prasahya.apahṛtā vā vana.ante kriḍā.gṛhe vā^apahartāraṁ rātrau tīkṣṇena ghātayet, svayaṁ vā rasena //*  
*11.1.39/ tataḥ prakāśayet "amunā me priyo hataḥ" iti //*  
*11.1.40/ jāta.kāmaṁ vā siddha.vyañjanaḥ sāṁvadanikībhir auṣadhībhiḥ saṁvāsya rasena^atisaṁdhāya^apagacchet //*  
*11.1.41/ tasminn apakrānte sattriṇaḥ para.prayogam abhiśaṁseyuḥ //*  
*11.1.42/ āḍhya.vidhavā gūḍha.ājīvā yoga.striyo vā dāya.nikṣepa.arthaṁ vivadamānāḥ saṁgha.mukhyān unmādayeyuḥ, aditi.kauśika.striyo nartakī.gāyanā vā //*  
*11.1.43/ pratipannān gūḍha.veśmasu rātri.samāgama.praviṣṭāṁs tīkṣṇā hanyur baddhvā hareyur vā //*  
*11.1.44/ sattrī vā strī.lolupaṁ saṁgha.mukhyaṁ prarūpayet "amuṣmin grāme daridra.kullam apasṛtam, tasya strī rāja.arhā, gṛhāṇa^enām" iti //*  
*11.1.45/ gṛhītāyām ardha.māsa.anantaraṁ siddha.vyañjano dūṣya.saṁgha.mukhya.madhye prakrośet "asau me mukhyo bhāryāṁ snuṣāṁ bhaginīṁ duhitaraṁ vā^adhicarati" iti //*  
*11.1.46/ taṁ cet saṁgho nigṛhṇīyāt, rājā^enam upagṛhya viguṇeṣu vikramayet //*  
*11.1.47/ anigṛhīte siddha.vyañjanaṁ rātrau tīkṣṇāḥ pravāsayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.48/ tatas tad.vyañjanāḥ prakrośeyuḥ "asau brahmahā brāhmaṇī.jāraś ca" iti //*  
*11.1.49/ kārtāntika.vyañjano vā kanyām anyena vṛtām anyasya prarūpayet "amuṣya kanyā rāja.patnī rāja.prasavinī ca bhaviṣyati, sarva.svena prasahya vā^enāṁ labhasva" iti //*  
*11.1.50/ alabhyamānāyāṁ para.pakṣam uddharṣayet //*  
*11.1.51/ labdhāyāṁ siddhaḥ kalahaḥ //*  
*11.1.52/ bhikṣukī vā priya.bhāryaṁ mukhyaṁ brūyāt "asau te mukhyo yauvana.utsikto bhāryāyāṁ māṁ prāhiṇot, tasya^ahaṁ bhayāl lekhyam ābharaṇaṁ gṛhītvā^āgatā^asmi, nirdoṣā te bhāryā, gūḍham asmin pratikartavyam, aham api tāvat pratipatsyāmi" iti //*  
*11.1.53/ evaṁ.ādiṣu kalaha.sthāneṣu svayam utpanne vā kalahe tīkṣṇair utpādite vā hīna.pakṣaṁ rājā kośa.daṇḍābhyām upagṛhya viguṇeṣu vikramayed apavāhayed vā //*  
*11.1.54/ saṁgheṣv evam eka.rājo varteta //*  
*11.1.55/ saṁghāś ca^apy evam eka.rājād etebhyo^atisaṁghān ebhyo rakṣayeyuḥ //*  
*11.1.56ab/ saṁgha.mukhyaś ca saṁgheṣu nyāya.vṛttir hitaḥ priyaḥ /*  
*11.1.56cd/ dānto yukta.janas tiṣṭhet sarva.citta.anuvartakaḥ //E*  
*(dūta.karma)*

### 12

*12.1.01/ "balīyasā^abhiyukto durbalaḥ sarvatra^anupraṇato vetasa.dharmā tiṣṭhet //*  
*12.1.02/ indrasya hi sa praṇamati yo balīyaso namati" iti bhāradvājaḥ //*  
*12.1.03/ "sarva.saṁdohena balānāṁ yudhyeta //*  
*12.1.04/ parākramo hi vyasanam apahanti //*  
*12.1.05/ sva.dharmaś ca^eṣa kṣatriyasya, yuddhe jayaḥ parājayo vā" iti viśāla.akṣaḥ //*  
*12.1.06/ na^iti kauṭilyaḥ //*  
*12.1.07/ sarvatra^anupraṇataḥ kulaiḍaka iva nirāśo jīvite vasati //*  
*12.1.08/ yudhyamānaś ca^alpa.sainyaḥ samudram iva^aplavo^avagāhamānaḥ sīdati //*  
*12.1.09/ tad.viśiṣṭaṁ tu rājānam āśrito durgam aviṣahyaṁ vā ceṣṭeta //*  
*12.1.10/ trayo^abhiyoktāro dharma.lobha.asura.vijayina iti //*  
*12.1.11/ teṣām abhyavapattyā dharma.vijayī tuṣyati //*  
*12.1.12/ tam abhyavapadyeta, pareṣām api bhayāt //*  
*12.1.13/ bhūmi.dravya.haraṇena lobha.vijayī tuṣyati //*  
*12.1.14/ tam arthena^abhyavapadyeta //*  
*12.1.15/ bhūmi.dravya.putra.dāra.prāṇa.haraṇena^asura.vijayī //*  
*12.1.16/ taṁ bhūmi.dravyābhyām upagṛhya^agrāhyaḥ pratikurvīta //*  
*12.1.17/ teṣām anyatamam uttiṣṭhamānaṁ saṁdhinā mantra.yuddhena kūṭa.yuddhena vā prativyūheta //*  
*12.1.18/ śatru.pakṣam asya sāma.dānābhyām, sva.pakṣaṁ bheda.daṇḍābhyām //*  
*12.1.19/ durgaṁ rāṣṭraṁ skandha.āvāraṁ vā^asya gūḍhāḥ śastra.rasa.agnibhiḥ sādhayeyuḥ //*  
*12.1.20/ sarvataḥ pārṣṇim asya grāhayet //*  
*12.1.21/ aṭavībhir vā rājyaṁ ghātayet, tat.kulīna.aparuddhābhyāṁ vā hārayet //*  
*12.1.22/ apakāra.anteṣu ca^asya dūṭaṁ preṣayet //*  
*12.1.23/ anapakṛtya vā saṁdhānam //*  
*12.1.24/ tathā^apy abhiprayāntaṁ kośa.daṇḍayoḥ pāda.uttaram aho.rātra.uttaraṁ vā saṁdhiṁ yāceta //*  
*12.1.25/ sa ced daṇḍa.saṁdhiṁ yāceta, kuṇṭham asmai hasty.aśvaṁ dadyād, utsāhitaṁ vā gara.yuktam //*  
*12.1.26/ puruṣa.saṁdhiṁ yāceta, dūṣya.amitra.aṭavī.balam asmai dadyād yoga.puruṣa.adhiṣṭhitam //*  
*12.1.27/ tathā kuryād yathā^ubhaya.vināśaḥ syāt //*  
*12.1.28/ tīkṣṇa.balaṁ vā^asmai dadyād yad avamānitaṁ vikurvīta, maulam anuraktaṁ vā yad asya vyasane^apakuryāt //*  
*12.1.29/ kośa.saṁdhiṁ yāceta, sāram asmai dadyād yasya kretāraṁ na^adhigacchet, kupyam ayuddha.yogyaṁ vā //*  
*12.1.30/ bhūmi.saṁdhiṁ yāceta, pratyādeyāṁ nitya.amitrām anapāśrayāṁ mahā.kṣaya.vyaya.niveśāṁ vā^asmai bhūmiṁ dadyāt //*  
*12.1.31/ sarva.svena vā rāja.dhānī.varjena saṁdhiṁ yāceta balīyasaḥ //*  
*12.1.32ab/ yat prasahya hared anyas tat prayacced upāyataḥ /*  
*12.1.32cd/ rakṣet sva.dehaṁ na dhanaṁ kā hy anitye dhane dayā //E*  
*(mantra.yuddha)*  
*12.2.01/ sa cet saṁdhau na^avatiṣṭheta, brūyād enaṁ - "ime śatru.ṣaḍ.varga.vaśagā rājāno vinaṣṭāḥ, teṣām anātmavatāṁ na^arhasi mārgam anugantum //*  
*12.2.02/ dharmam arthaṁ ca^avekṣasva //*  
*12.2.03/ mitra.mukhā hy amitrās te ye tvā sāhasam adharmam artha.atikramaṁ ca grāhayanti //*  
*12.2.04/ śūrais tyakta.ātmabhiḥ saha yoddhuṁ sāhasam, jana.kṣayam ubhayataḥ kartum adharmaḥ, dṛṣṭam arthaṁ mitram aduṣṭaṁ ca tyaktum artha.atikramaḥ //*  
*12.2.05/ mitravāṁś ca sa rājā, bhūyaś ca^etena^arthena mitrāṇy udyojayiṣyati yāni tvā sarvato^abhiyāsyanti //*  
*12.2.06/ na ca madhyama.udāsīnayor maṇḍalasya vā parityaktaḥ, bhavāṁs tu parityaktaḥ yattvā samudyuktam upaprekṣante "bhūyaḥ kṣaya.vyayābhyāṁ yujyatām, mitrāc ca bhidyatām, atha^enaṁ parityakta.mūlaṁ sukhena^ucchetsyāmaḥ" iti //*  
*12.2.07/ sa bhavān na^arhati mitra.mukhānām amitrāṇāṁ śrotum, mitrāṇy udvejayitum amitrāṁś ca śreyasā yoktum, prāṇa.saṁśayam anarthaṁ ca^upagantum" iti yacchet //*  
*12.2.08/ tathā^api pratiṣṭhamānasya prakṛti.kopam asya kārayed yathā saṁgha.vṛtte vyākhyātaṁ yoga.vāmane ca //*  
*12.2.09/ tīkṣṇa.rasada.prayogaṁ ca //*  
*12.2.10/ yad uktam ātma.rakṣitake rakṣyaṁ tatra tīkṣṇān rasadāṁś ca prayuñjīta //*  
*12.2.11/ bandhakī.poṣakāḥ parama.rūpa.yauvanābhiḥ strībhiḥ senā.mukhyān unmādayeyuḥ //*  
*12.2.12/ bahūnām ekasyāṁ dvayor vā mukhyayoḥ kāme jāte tīkṣṇāḥ kalahān utpādayeyuḥ //*  
*12.2.13/ kalahe parājita.pakṣaṁ paratra.apagamane yātrā.sāhāyya.dāne vā bhartur yojayeyuḥ //*  
*12.2.14/ kāma.vaśān vā siddha.vyañjanāḥ sāṁvadanikībhir oṣadhībhir atisaṁdhānāya mukhyeṣu rasaṁ dāpayeyuḥ //*  
*12.2.15/ vaidehaka.vyañjane vā rāja.mahiṣyāḥ subhagāyāḥ preṣyām āsannāṁ kāma.nimittam arthena^abhivṛṣya parityajet //*  
*12.2.16/ tasya^eva paricāraka.vyañjana.upadiṣṭaḥ siddha.vyañjanaḥ sāṁvadanikīm oṣadhīṁ dadyāt "vaidehaka.śarīre^avaghātavyā" iti //*  
*12.2.17/ siddhe subhagāyā apy enaṁ yogam upadiśet "rāja.śarīre^avadhātavyā" iti //*  
*12.2.18/ tato rasena^atisaṁdadhyāt //*  
*12.2.19/ kārtāntika.vyañjano vā mahā.mātraṁ "rāja.lakṣaṇa.sampannam" krama.abhinītaṁ brūyāt //*  
*12.2.20/ bhāryām asya bhikṣukī "rāja.patnī rāja.prasavinī vā bhaviṣyasi" iti //*  
*12.2.21/ bhāryā.vyañjanā vā mahā.mātraṁ brūyāt "rājā kila mām avarodhayiṣyati, tava^antikāya pattra.lekhyam ābharaṇaṁ ca^idaṁ parivrājikayā^āhṛtam" iti //*  
*12.2.22/ sūda.ārālika.vyañjano vā rasa.prayoga.arthaṁ rāja.vacanam arthaṁ ca^asya lobhanīyam abhinayet //*  
*12.2.23/ tad asya vaidehaka.vyañjanaḥ pratisaṁdadhyāt, kārya.siddhiṁ ca brūyāt //*  
*12.2.24/ evam ekena dvābhyāṁ tribhir ity upāyair eka.ekam asya mahā.mātraṁ vikramāya^apagamanāya vā yojayet - iti //*  
*12.2.25/ durgeṣu ca^asya śūnya.pāla.āsannāḥ sattriṇaḥ paura.jānapadeṣu maitrī.nimittam āvedayeyuḥ - "śūnya.pālena^uktā yodhāś ca^adhikaraṇasthāś ca "kṛcchra.gato rājā jīvann āgamiṣyati, na vā, prasahya vittam ārjayadhvam, amitrāṁś ca hata" iti //*  
*12.2.26/ bahulī.bhūte tīkṣṇāḥ paurān niśāsv āhārayeyuḥ, mukhyāṁś ca^abhihanyuḥ "evaṁ kriyante ye śūnya.pālasya na śuśrūṣante" iti //*  
*12.2.27/ śūnya.pāla.sthāneṣu ca saśoṇitāni śastra.vitta.bandhanāny utsṛjeyuḥ //*  
*12.2.28/ tataḥ sattriṇaḥ "śūnya.pālo ghātayati vilopayati ca" ity āvedayeyuḥ //*  
*12.2.29/ evaṁ jānapadān samāhartur bhedayeyuḥ //*  
*12.2.30/ samāhartṛ.puruṣāṁs tu grāma.madhyeṣu rātrau tīkṣṇā hatvā brūyuḥ "evaṁ kriyante ye jana.padam adharmeṇa bādhante" iti //*  
*12.2.31/ samutpanne doṣe śūnya.pālaṁ samāhartāraṁ vā prakṛti.kopena ghātayeyuḥ //*  
*12.2.32/ tat.kulīnam aparuddhaṁ vā pratipādayeyuḥ //*  
*12.2.33ab/ antaḥ.pura.pura.dvāraṁ dravya.dhānya.parigrahān /*  
*12.2.33cd/ daheyus tāṁś ca hanyur vā brūyur asya^ārta.vādinaḥ //E*  
*(senā.mukhya.vadhah - maṇḍala.protsāhanam)*  
*12.3.01/ rājño rāja.vallabhānāṁ ca^āsannāḥ sattriṇaḥ patty.aśva.ratha.dvipa.mukhyānāṁ "rājā kruddhaḥ" iti suhṛd.viśvāsena mitra.sthānīyeṣu kathayeyuḥ //*  
*12.3.02/ bahulī.bhūte tīkṣṇāḥ kṛta.rātri.cāra.pratīkārā gṛheṣu "svāmi.vacanena^āgamyatām" iti brūyuḥ //*  
*12.3.03/ tānnirgacchata eva^abhihanyuḥ, "svāmi.saṁdeśaḥ" iti ca^āsannān brūyuḥ //*  
*12.3.04/ ye ca^apravāsitās tān sattriṇo brūyuḥ "etat tad yad asmābhiḥ kathitam, jīvitu.kāmena^apakrāntavyam" iti //*  
*12.3.05/ yebhyaś ca rājā yācito na dadāti tān sattriṇo brūyuḥ - "uktaḥ śūnya.pālo rājñā "ayācyam artham asau ca^asau ca mā yācate, mayā pratyākhyātāḥ śatru.saṁhitāḥ, teṣām uddharaṇe prayatasva" iti //*  
*12.3.06/ tataḥ pūrvavad ācaret //*  
*12.3.07/ yebhyaś ca rājā yācito dadāti tān sattriṇo brūyuḥ - "uktaḥ śūnya.pālo rājñā "ayācyam artham asau ca^asau ca mā yācate, tebhyo mayā so^artho viśvāsa.arthaṁ dattaḥ, śatru.saṁhitāḥ, teṣām uddharaṇe prayatasva" iti //*  
*12.3.08/ tataḥ pūrvavad ācaret //*  
*12.3.09/ ye ca^enaṁ yācyam arthaṁ na yācante tān sattriṇo brūyuḥ - "uktaḥ śūnya.pālo rājñā "yācyam artham asau ca^asau ca mā na yācate, kim anyat sva.doṣa.śaṅkitatvāt, teṣām uddharaṇe prayatasva" iti //*  
*12.3.10/ tataḥ pūrvavad ācaret //*  
*12.3.11/ etena sarvaḥ kṛtya.pakṣo vyākhyātaḥ //*  
*12.3.12/ pratyāsanno vā rājānaṁ sattrī grāhayet "asau ca^asau ca te mahā.mātraḥ śatru.puruṣaiḥ sambhāṣate" iti //*  
*12.3.13/ pratipanne dūṣyān asya śāsana.harān darśayet "etat tat" iti //*  
*12.3.14/ senā.mukhya.prakṛti.puruṣān vā bhūmyā hiraṇyena vā lobhayitvā sveṣu vikramayed apavāhayed vā //*  
*12.3.15/ yo^asya putraḥ samīpe durge vā prativasati taṁ sattriṇā^upajāpayet "ātma.sampannataras tvaṁ putraḥ, tathā^apy antar.hitaḥ, tat.kim upekṣase vikramya gṛhāṇa, purā tvā yuva.rājo vināśayati" iti //*  
*12.3.16/ tat.kulīnam aparuddhaṁ vā hiraṇyena pratilobhya brūyāt "antar.balaṁ pratyanta.skandham antaṁ vā^asya pramṛdnīhi" iti //*  
*12.3.17/ āṭavikān artha.mānābhyām upagṛhya rājyam asya ghātayet //*  
*12.3.18/ pārṣṇi.grāhaṁ vā^asya brūyāt "eṣa khalu rājā mām ucchidya tvām ucchetsyati, pārṣṇim asya gṛhāṇa, tvayi nivṛttasya^ahaṁ pārṣṇiṁ grahīṣyāmi" iti //*  
*12.3.19/ mitrāṇi vā^asya brūyāt "ahaṁ vaḥ setuḥ, mayi vibhinne sarvān eṣa vo rājā plāvayiṣyati, sambhūya vā^asya yātrāṁ vihanāma" iti //*  
*12.3.20/ tat.saṁhatānām asaṁhatānāṁ ca preṣayet "eṣa khalu rājā mām utpāṭya bhavatsu karma kariṣyati, budhyadhvam, ahaṁ vaḥ śreyān abhyupapattum" iti //*  
*12.3.21ab/ madhyamasya prahiṇuyād udāsīnasya vā punaḥ /*  
*12.3.21cd/ yathā^āsannasya mokṣa.arthaṁ sarva.svena tad.arpaṇam //E*  
*(śastra.agni.rasa.praṇidhayah - vīvadhā.sāra.prrasāra.vadhah)*  
*12.4.01/ ye ca^asya durgeṣu vaidehakavyañjanāḥ, grāmesu gṛhapatika.vyañjanāḥ, jana.pada.saṁdhiṣu go.rakṣaka.tāpasa.vyañjanāḥ, te sāmanta.āṭavika.tat.kulīna.aparuddhānāṁ paṇya.āgāra.pūrvaṁ preṣayeyuḥ "ayaṁ deśo hāryaḥ" iti //*  
*12.4.02/ āgatāṁś ca^eṣāṁ durge gūḍha.puruṣān artha.mānābhyām abhisatkṛtya prakṛtic.chidrāṇi pradarśayeyuḥ //*  
*12.4.03/ teṣu taiḥ saha prahareyuḥ //*
